Chapter 451: First Round of Transactions

Endless bloodlines, all races die!

This was You Shen’s, the founder of necromancy, catchphrase.

The meaning it in resembled the change that occurred in the painting Cang Xu held – the undead did not belong to the creation myth. And the blood river was endless, for after the bloodlines mixed with each other and mutated, further and more complex bloodlines were formed. But regardless of how many bloodlines there were or how many new races were created, they could all become undead beings. Being undead was the final destination of all life!

By sampling this slogan somewhat, one could sense the necromancers’ arrogance as well as the terrifying ambition of the one who created it, Founder You Shen.

It’s no wonder the undead sect suffered the entire world’s pursuit and attacks.

Although Cang Xu had heard this slogan before, with his field of vision and lifetime knowledge, he wouldn’t be so easily agitated.

Right now, most of his attention was on the advanced inheritance.

This was a typical soul aspect necromancer inheritance.

It had many magic spells, most of which were used for transforming ghosts and making them better in certain fields.

Cang Xu quickly grasped the main points – Burden Alleviation spell and Karma Reduction spell.

The Burden Alleviation spell could lighten the burden ghosts had on a necromancer’s soul.

With this magic, a necromancer could control more ghost subordinates, it was crucial for a necromancer to form an army!

As for the Karma Reduction spell, one could see from its name that it was named after the eastern monks.

The creation of this magic indeed involved a few monk theories.

A portion of the eastern monks believed that whenever a person sinned, they would create karma, and this karma was a crucial element that would torment the living being after death. The more karma, the more one suffered after dying.

The Karma Reduction spell was literal; it could reduce karma and lessen the torment after death. When used on a necromancer, it had greater importance – it could help a necromancer reduce negative energy erosion!

This inheritance greatly pleased Cang Xu.

He nodded at once, gave thanks to Jian Gui, then asked how much he needed to pay to acquire this inheritance.

“Oh ho ho.” Jian Gua once again magnanimously laughed, he acted very stylishly.

Not only did he let Cang Xu examine the inheritance ahead of time, but he also answered with, “Undead lifeforms of outstanding quality, money, valuable magic or information, and one’s own magic crystals can all be regarded as currency.”

He opened the transaction with a very relaxed manner.

Cang Xu had three silver level ghosts, this amount was a difficult burden on him.

He originally planned to peddle the three silver level ghosts during the gathering. But he didn’t expect that at the gathering, he would get an advanced inheritance without a hitch.

With the Burden Alleviation and Karma Reduction spells, he was now confident he could control three, no even more silver level ghosts.

Therefore, Cang Xu didn’t want to sell them.

He altered his plan at the last moment and stated, “Then I will use gold coins to pay. Lord Jian Gui, name your price.”

Jian Gui deliberated for a bit, then declared a number.

Cang Xu was surprised.

The price was very cheap, about half what it was worth.

He once again sensed Jian Gui’s benevolence.

Cang Xu paid with gold coins then had another request, “I also need a large quantity of undead materials, all kinds of alchemy materials, the most important of which are soul crystals.”

This provoked Xiao Wang Zi’s resentment, “Oi, newcomer, we let you do one transaction, you’re already taken care of.”

“Your first round is finished, don’t win an inch and want a foot!”

Cang Xu paused, then hastily apologized.

Ku Feng then directed, “Please then, the second one is Xiao Wang Zi.”

The skeleton child magician raised his skull, “I want soul crystals, the more the better.”

He raised his voice, but he was mainly looking at Jian Gui.

Ku Feng and Qi Ling didn’t say anything.

Jian Gui nodded, “This time, I brought a lot of soul crystals.”

Xiao Wang Zi immediately cheered.

He rejoiced when he completed the transaction with an exquisite bone tool to purchase sixty soul crystals.

Soul crystals were very important to necromancers.

Or to be more accurate, soul crystals were very beneficial to any undead life form.

It could grow one’s soul and substantially improve their spirit.

The hearts of the undead had long become necrotic, and were nonexistent, let alone having any blood.

The force moving them outside of negative energy was soul power.

Soul crystals were the most valuable food for undead lifeforms.

The third transaction was with Qi Ling; she had the same request as the skeleton magician – soul crystals.

Jian Gui and she reached a deal.

The fourth was Ku Feng, he asked this: did anyone have any outstanding blade skills?

War Merchant’s database had collected sword battle skills.

But Cang Xu kept silent.

It wasn’t convenient for him to take them out because those kinds of battle skills were incongruous with his public persona.

Finally, it was Jian Gui and Ku Feng who came to a deal.

He traded Ku Feng a memory crystal.

Seeing this caused Cang Xu’s mind to shake, it proved that Jian Gui might have a memory modification device on him.

Cang Xu gazed at the memory crystal, feigning hesitation while actually probing information, “Could that be…”

Xiao Wang Zi proudly answered once again, “That’s right, that is the famous memory crystal.”

Before Founder You Shen created necromancy, he profanely sacrificed to the gods, and the memory crystal device was something he developed.”

Cang Xu nodded, “That I understand, but to see one with my own eyes, it is my first time.”

This was another probe.

Cang Xu deliberately lied.

He was testing to see if Jian Gui and the others could really measure his lies, whether the Deceptive Disguise spell was working.

Cang Xu was put at ease, the others didn’t notice anything peculiar.

Jian Gui explained further, “Memory crystals aren’t as useful as the rumors say, there are many systemic problems with misusing them. It is liable to create memory derangement, causing people to have errors in self-awareness. If this derangement happens, returning them back is very difficult.”

“Under most circumstances when I use memory crystals, it is to cultivate a particular ghost.”

“You also know that after we successfully call back a soul, the ghost will lack memories, unless they cultivated necromancy while they were still alive to a certain degree.”

“A lack of memories will cause a ghost’s value to tumble greatly.”

“For example, a swordsman ghost lacking memories on how to use sword skills will disappoint others with its combat strength.”

“With memory crystals filling in the blanks, the ghost can regain its strength and even improve it.”

“But one cannot use too many, and if one misuses them, it will cause the ghost to go mad, go out of control, and even crumble into nothing.”

Ku Feng issued a promise, “I will engrave Lord Jian Gui’s warning into my heart, and will never directly use the memory crystal. I will just pour battle qi into it and use my spirit to access the memory inside and treat it as a book I read.”

“The path of the blade has no shortcuts to walk, each step needs practice.”

“If one takes shortcuts, it will instead lead far away from the legendary peak!”

Ku Feng carefully accepted the memory crystal.

Jian Gui was the last person in the first round of transactions.

“Oh ho ho, this time I have many wants.”

“The first one is an old one, if anyone has a corpse or bone aspect necromancer inheritance, I can trade for them.”

“Apart from that, any secret information on the Charming Blue Goddess is of great value to me.”

“Finally, I want an effective way to contact the legend level magician War Merchant.”

Cang Xu remained undisturbed, while his thoughts were being jolted.

“What is Jian Gui up to?”

“Why is he interested in Charming Blue Goddess and War Merchant?”

“He put forward that he wants an effective way to contact War Merchant, this conversely indicates that he once maintained contact with War Merchant?”

The survivors had been on Mystifying Monster Island and discovered War Merchant’s many identities. War Merchant used various identities to continue to deliver large amounts of product for the munitions market.

War Merchant was dead, and Mystifying Monster Island had completely disappeared, he had left a gigantic vacancy in the market.

Someone surely would have noticed and naturally looked into it.

This was what the survivors had surmised earlier.

Jian Gui seemed to be looking for War Merchant’s people.

But if it was just that, it wouldn’t make Cang Xu so nervous and bewildered.

Jian Gui also wanted information on Charming Blue Goddess.

Charming Blue Goddess’ divine artifact was the Mermaid Fairy Tale, which was currently on the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

“During the battle, the naga priestess treated the Deep Sea Monster Fish as a divine envoy, Charming Blue Goddess’ divine artifact was also kept in War Merchant’s central tower, perhaps the two of them really were collaborating in secret?”

“Why is Jian Gui looking for them?”

“He is no ordinary necromancer, he is an orthodox necromancer, his teacher is Captain Gou Hun, one of You Shen’s twelve disciples.”

“Maybe his request is not his own notion…”

“As a third party, he is simultaneously looking for War Merchant and Charming Blue Goddess, what does he know? What does he desire?”

Cang Xu was very vigilant.

“Don’t tell me he found out about Mystifying Monster Island’s disappearance?”

“Do I have some clues on me?”

“No, calm down. Jian Gui is just a gold level; the possibility that he can see through the Deceptive Disguise and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spells is miniscule.”

Cang Xu strived to calm his agitated mind while secretly growing more alert.

If Jian Gui knew Cang Xu had a close connection to what he requested, one would fear he wouldn’t be so good-natured.

Although his mind had been moved very intensely, on the face of it, Cang Xu remained calm and collected.

He didn’t feign questions, he merely kept silent.

Xiao Wang Zi hung his head, “Those are rare things Lord Jian Gui wants, but I really can’t do anything about them, oh dear, I am too useless!”

Qi Ling shook her head, “I cannot betray my clan or my beliefs!”

Ku Feng shrugged his shoulders, “You know Lord Jian Gui, I have only been moving around Two Eyes Island for the last few years.”

Cang Xu was the last to respond, “Perhaps I can use Wisteria Merchant Alliance channels to make discreet inquiries for the pertinent information. But I cannot guarantee it will succeed, just as Lord Jian Gui has said, the current Wisteria Merchant Alliance has been broken utterly.”

Jian Gui nodded, expressing his thanks to Cang Xu, then consoled Xiao Wang Zi, before finally saying to Qi Ling, “You don’t need to struggle so, small fish, I understand you and respect your beliefs.”

The first round of transactions came to an end.

The strongest necromancer in the cave, Jian Gui, was actually the sole loser in these transactions.

Chapter 452: A Big Purchase

The undead gathering’s first round of transactions had concluded.

Cang Xu also saw that the transactions with each other was not equal, simply put, it was Jian Gui playing with the others.

Jian Gui controlled unrivalled resources. Regardless of whether it was soul or memory crystals, the other necromancers rushed like ducks for them, they were things they needed.

Jian Gui monopolized these resources; therefore, he naturally held the dominant position.

In light of his power and resources, he could completely depress the prices and cheaply acquire the others’ common resources.

But Jian Gui didn’t do this, he traded based on market value, and even a bit cheaper than market value.

“It’s no wonder these people respect Jian Gui so much.” Cang Xu’s thoughts flashed.

This kind of respect had a reason, every undead was shown consideration by Jian Gui, Jian Gui was a gold level; he was the strongest and monopolized resources.

Ku Feng said, “The second round of transactions begins, following the established order.”

This time, Cang Xu immediately asked for soul crystals.

Both he and Zi Di urgently needed them.

Jian Gui was winning to supply them at the previous price.

Cang Xu bought two hundred soul crystals.

Jian Gui laughed, “Oh ho ho, I didn’t expect such a big customer, I only brought so many, I don’t have any more now.”

It wasn’t that Cang Xu didn’t want to buy more, rather Jian Gui only had so many on him.

Everyone was surprised.

“Newcomer, I have a whole new level of respect for you, you are wealthy!” Xiao Wang Zi bluntly stated, to purchase ten soul crystals, he had painstakingly saved for a year.

Qi Ling gave an indistinct look but remained silent.

Qing Xin looked pensive.

Ku Feng clicked his tongue and guessed, “It seems during the battle for Two Eyes Island, you dredged out longs of treasures from the seafloor?”

Cang Xu humbly nodded and candidly admitted, “Indeed.”

Qing Xin’s eyes flashed and her heart shook, startled by Cang Xu’s plunder. Because that was the wealth of an entire island, and she didn’t know how much of it Cang Xu had fished up.

Cang Xu admitted this was intended.

The survivors considered Hun Tong’s reaction, and it was likely the latter would launch an investigation and pursuit. Therefore, Cang Xu decided to admit it here to leave behind a trail.

Because after this undead gathering ended, Weeping Pine Island would have a naval battle, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would be victorious, and the Iron Lump would join their fleet. The necromancer Cang Xu would also “die in battle”, while actually using the name of “Lao Zhong” to return to everyone else.

Thus, the treasures the survivors secretly salvaged could be washed clean.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would get spoils of war that surpassed imagination, and according to imperial law, Hun Tong couldn’t respond with force to it.

The survivors could even give the antiques and other things to Hun Tong if Hun Tong gave them a suitable bid.

The second round of transactions continued.

Xiao Wang Zi shouted and stood up, “Lord Jian Gui, did you make the thing I ordered?”

Jian Gui nodded, “You pretty lucky, more than a month ago, I killed an incautious water path magician and made a water casting memory crystal.”

Xiao Wang Zi jumped up happily, “That’s wonderful! If that happened, my ‘generalissimo’ will get much stronger.”

He wanted to give the memory crystal to his mount, the three headed sea serpent.

After the three headed sea serpent had become undead, its memory had almost completely dissipated, thus it couldn’t use its water path racial magics. During battle, it could only serve as a meat shield and a mount.

Jian Gui reminded, “You can look at the memory crystal’s contents well. If it is not to your satisfaction, I can adjust it a bit for you right now. But not by much, only in a few subtle ways.”

When Cang Xu heard this, his heart had a thought, “It seems that Jian Gui possesses a memory crystal making device and has some methods of altering memories.”

The survivors also had a memory crystal making device in their inventory.

“If I could trade for those things, that would be splendid.” Cang Xu was merely daydreaming, and didn’t have a practical plan to implement.

Transaction requests couldn’t be made wantonly, because it would subsequently reveal many things.

If Cang Xu put forth such a request, perhaps everyone would be wondering if he had a memory making device on him?

The second round of transactions quickly finished.

Cang Xu obtained many soul crystals.

Xiao Wang Zi obtained a memory crystal.

Qi Ling and Ku Feng did a trade, she obtained Weeping Pine Island’s special wood. It was good material to make ships out of.

Ku Feng wanted to buy a weapon, and Jian Gui stated he didn’t have any.

Xiao Wang Zi giggled and declared arrogantly, “I have one!”

Cang Xu easily guessed that Ku Feng would make such a request during this gathering.

As a result, Xiao Wang Zi had intentionally made a white bone cutlass that reached silver level quality.

This caused Cang Xu to secretly have more respect for Xiao Wang Zi, “He was the one who made the Eulogy Tombstone, and he also has that silver level work, don’t tell me it is because he is an alchemy master?”

Necromancers were also magicians and weren’t prevented from doing alchemy.

Finally, it was Jian Gui’s turn, his prior trade hadn’t been reached, and he simply gave up on it.

The third round of transactions began.

Cang Xu Mainly looked at Ku Feng, he asked to buy a corpse aspect necromancer inheritance.

“Although I obtained an advanced soul aspect inheritance, my body has been transformed through my research, and I don’t plan on abandoning that.”

Cang Xu gave a simple explanation to everyone.

But Ku Feng replied, “I am not a necromancer, I am a fighter. Although Lord Doctor Sui Shi roused me, I didn’t get a corpse aspect necromancer inheritance from him.”

“However, I can research for you. But you’ll need to pay for it to my satisfaction, furthermore, that research cannot disturb my pursuit of the blade path.”

Ku Feng’s words surprised Cang Xu.

However, the others didn’t seem surprised.

Seeing Cang Xu’s puzzled look, Ku Feng shrugged his shoulders, “Don’t be baffled, I have done research for Lord Jian Gui.”

Jian Gui laughed, “Oh ho ho, craving for knowledge is an outstanding characteristic of a magician. I have a basic corpse aspect inheritance that I can sell to you.”

Although Cang Xu was very interested in Ku Feng’s body, he knew he didn’t have the opportunity to research it.

Ku Feng could not be brought back to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, and research would take a lot of time, he didn’t have such leisure.

Thus, Cang Xu looked at Jiang Gui, and after a period of silence, audaciously said, “Lord Jian Gui, if you can sell me your necromancer inheritances, I want to buy them all, please name your price.”

As a result, Cang Xu obtained a basic inheritance for both the corpse and bone aspects.

Cang Xu acutely took note that Jian Gui did not sell his research results on Ku Feng’s body.

Even so, he had an ample harvest.

After a mere skim over, he obtained much assistance from the corpse aspect necromancer inheritance that would allow him to continue transforming his own body.

The third round concluded and the fourth began.

Cang Xu took a deep breath, then boldly requested a deal with Jian Gui, “Lord Jian Gui, if I may be so bold, are you willing to sell the method of how to make soul crystals?”

Jian Gui stared at him.

Xiao Wang Zi bellowed, “What? Newcomer, you’re dreaming.”

Cang Xu deeply bowed, “With money, anything can be discussed.”

Qing Xin looked at Cang Xu, covering her concern.

As the proverb says, don’t flaunt your wealth.

When Cang Xu showed off his money, was he not worried that these necromancers would besiege and rob him?

This transaction gathering was unlike Two Eyes Island’s auction hall, it was easy for dirty deeds to happen here.

Cang Xu also admitted to his own boldness.

However, he didn’t carelessly take risks.

First, he had his judgement. Although this was the first time he had met Jian Gui and the others, with his life experience and communication skills, he could determine the innate character of these people.

Second, Cang Xu believed that this audaciousness would instead be beneficial for him, it was called bluffing.

As expected, outside of Xiao Wang Zi’s reprimand, the others kept silent.

Qi Ling looked at Cang Xu faintly.

Anyone with basic intelligence could guess, “This newcomer is so bold, he must have something he is relying on, we have to be vigilant!”

Of course, Cang Xu was displaying a silver level life aura.

Even if silver levels were not the gold level Jian Gui’s match, they could still escape.

Cang Xu also had another magician, Qing Xin, of the same level by his side.

They didn’t know that Cang Xu wasn’t relying on himself or Qing Xin, but the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps in the distance.

Finally, Cang Xu believed that taking this risk was worth it.

If Jian Gui agreed to this transaction, wouldn’t it be a bloody profit?

Even if the price paid was high, it was worth the money.

One egg and one egg laying hen, the latter was naturally worth far more in the long term.

“Oh ho ho.” Jian Gui laughed and shook his head, “So sorry, the soul crystal creation method cannot be sold.”

“Even though I lack money greatly.”

“You understand, all magicians lack money!”

After Jiang Gui’s small jest, he turned serious, “The soul crystal creation method is a treasure of the soul aspect necromancer inheritance, its most valuable part.”

“This cannot be taught to outsiders.”

Cang Xu’s expression changed, “What do you mean?”

“Very simple, become one of us.” Jian Gui solemnly said, “You need to pass an audit and complete some missions, then you’ll be qualified to obtain the pertinent inheritance.”

“This is also the rules set by Founder You Shen!”

Cang Xu bowed again, expressing his apology, then he continued to ask about the audit and investigation, along with what kind of missions needed to be completed.

“I became a necromancer to gain greater power.”

“Since I am already wanted and not tolerated by the world, then I only have one road, isn’t that so?”

“Oh ho ho, you are very aware.” Jian Gui exclaimed in admiration, then he changed his tone, “But unfortunately, you cannot pass the audit.”

“I can see that your potential is limited, the limits of your talent isn’t high.”

“The first step of this audit is inspecting this.”

“So sorry.”

Cang Xu stared blankly in place.

For a split second, he was a bit absent minded.

It was like returning to the past, when the authorities condemned his cultivation aptitude to death.

Chapter 453: Ship Spirit Restoration Scroll

Qi Ling whispered, “You don’t think we too want to gain a deeper level inheritance? We are not qualified.”

Ku Feng murmured, “Being undead is just another life form. Thus, talent is still an essential factor.”

“Some people were never blessed by the heavens, but after becoming an undead and having their life shape altered, their talents would rise. For others, it would go down instead."

Xiao Wang Zi looked at Cang Xu in a daze, sympathizing with Cang Xu, consoling him with pity, “Newcomer, we are the same.”

“In fact, those soul crystals to us are the same too.”

“When our spirits and souls reach their limits, more soul crystals won’t be useful.”

“Buying soul crystals is actually very cost effective to us.”

“No, we are not the same.” Cang Xu thought to himself, but outwardly, he still seemed disappointed.

Several rounds of transactions followed.

After confirming that the soul crystal creation method couldn’t be bought, Cang Xu naturally didn’t ask for the memory crystal magic.

He mainly bought a large quantity of necromancy materials.

These materials were generally controlled by every country and were very difficult to buy in normal markets.

But at this undead gathering, Cang Xu’s difficulty in acquiring them tumbled immensely.

The transactions continued until all of the participants no longer asked for anything.

“Next is the information exchange. May I ask everyone to declare what they think is valuable information and exchange it with us.” Ku Feng directed.

“Oh ho ho, don’t be nervous, you can chat casually.” Jian Gui soothed Cang Xu, then looked at Qing Xin, “You didn’t gain anything from our transactions, so don’t bother.”

Qing Xin quickly expressed her thanks.

Ku Feng spoke first about the battle for Two Eyes Island, and informed Qi Ling that Dao Hen had been taken prisoner and might be executed publicly.

Qi Ling sighed, “That silver level fishman fighter is very famous and also very strong.”

“I was invited by the Blue Green Coral Clan to fight with them; I didn’t expect that it would happen on Two Eyes Island.”

The Blue Green Coral Clan is pretty strong; a pity they wanted to retake the coral ball cage tree. That was the wrong strategy.”

“The ocean surface will belong to the Sheng Ming Empire.”

“We will only have hope of counterattacking and retaking the ocean when the humans and beastmen have been depleted gravely.”

When Cang Xu heard this, he couldn’t help but raise an eyebrow towards Qi Ling.

This fishman ghost magician had been quite uncommunicative, but her words now had insight.

Qi Ling continued to state her information: large quantities of ocean beasts migrated into White Hair Trench, forming a temporary seafloor hunting ground, and currently, there were many fishmen clans hunting there.

Xiao Wang Zi said, “Ice Sculpture Island is preparing a celebration this year, they have invited many powers. The celebration is very grand, a once in a century occasion. It will be very bustling then, and it is even said that a beastman clan emissary will show up.”

Xiao Wang Zi’s parents were also visiting Ice Sculpture Island at this time.

It was Cang Xu’s turn now, and after he deliberated for a bit, he declared some inside information, “You know I was also at the battle for Two Eyes Island, and I happened to learn some inside scoops.”

“I discovered a giant fishman on the seafloor attacking Hun Tong’s Golden Flash, it almost caused it to fall to the seabed.”

“When the giant fishman on the seafloor and the naga priestess met up, the naga priestess addressed it as ‘divine envoy’, the former might be a divine envoy from the fishmen’s Holy City!”

“Furthermore, the fishmen’s goal was not just to take back Two Eyes Island, but they also had a more hidden agenda.”

“They seemed to be searching for something on Two Eyes Island, then smuggled it back to the naga priestess.”

“So that’s what happened.” Ku feng looked pensive, “The Blue Green Coral Clan attacking Two Eyes Island was indeed only a part of the fishmen troops.”

“That was the innate reason why the naga priestess retreated so candidly.”

Although Ku Feng had become an undead, he was only a fighter.

Even a necromancer might not have been paying attention to what was happening on the seafloor.

The information Cang Xu stated also had to do with him.

First, although the information seemed valuable, it really wasn’t, and as time passed, it might be discovered as so.

Second, by providing this information, Cang Xu could perfectly complete the link.

Finally, the justification he gave were sufficient. Because beforehand, he had exposed his wealth and admitted that he took much of the seafloor valuables away. Since he moved through the seafloor, observing the fishman divine envoy was very reasonable.

Xiao Wang Zi tapped his chin, then considered, “A divine envoy and a naga priestess, don’t tell me they are both of the fishmen’s Holy City?”

“Holy City…I once made a pilgrimage there.” Qi Ling sighed regretfully, revealing her deep yearning and longing, “A pity that I never got a chance to go ever again.”

It was Jian Gui’s turn.

He took out a scroll.

“I have spent a lot of energy, time, and even fought a fierce battle to acquire this scroll.”

“This is also why I was late today.”

“Don’t underestimate it, it is very important to our entire undead faction!”

“This is a ship spirit restoration scroll; its writer is Ta Ke.”

“He is a saint level alchemist, an important member of the Gang Bang Clan, 45 years old and already a great alchemist scholar.”

“According to my information, Ta Ke developed this scroll and has the ability to manufacture batches of them.”

Jian Gui looked grave, his tone heavy.

Xiao Wang Zi shook his head, “Ta Ke? I heard of him. He is the greatest alchemist genius in the Sheng Ming Empire as of recent years.”

“A ship spirit restoration scroll? Ku Feng was puzzled, “Are you saying it can restore ship spirits? Why is it so important to the entire undead faction?”

Qi Ling was a magician, and she thought of the crucial point to this, and she said with bewilderment, “Are you saying this scroll uses the undead faction’s methods?”

“Correct.” Jian Gui nodded, “We all understand that ship spirits and tower spirits exist, the essence of them are soul fragments of magicians, which bestow them with artificial intelligence.”

“The ship spirit restoration scroll is named after its effect; it can heal a ship spirit’s injuries. It is a healing scroll.”

“In other words, this scroll can be used on the soul and heal it.”

“This is one of our undead faction’s personal strengths.”

“This scroll contains knowledge, I have already made out some of it, it contains our undead faction’s unique substance.”

Qi Ling whispered, “Since the undead faction was created, its new faction superiority has been rapidly diminishing. Every great power is both publicly hunting us down while also researching it secretly. The ship spirit restoration scroll is evidence that proves the Sheng Ming Empire has acquired enough of our undead faction’s research to have a fundamental breakthrough!”

Jian Gui nodded with Qi Ling’s analysis, throwing an encouraging and appreciative gaze, “This is what I want to say to you all, in the future, surviving will be harder, I hope everyone can vigorously prepare for this.”

Cang Xu stared at the scroll in Jian Gui’s hand, his mind suddenly flashing, “A ship spirit restoration scroll? Can it be used to an extent on a tower spirit?”

In theory, this was possible.

Ship spirits and tower spirits had the same origin, they were made from a severed part of a magician’s soul.

After some consideration, Cang Xu decided to take another risk!

“Lord Jian Gui.” Cang Xu said directly, “This is the first time I’ve seen such a scroll, is your Lordship willing to part with it?”

“We can discuss the price.”

“To tell the truth, I also want to research it.”

“Oh?” Jian Gui looked at Cang Xu with astonishment, then he laughed, “Oh ho ho ho, I am not mistaken, you are an excellent magician who won’t let any chance to acquire new knowledge slip by.”

“But I am very sorry, I just got ahold of this scroll, I haven’t had time to genuinely study it yet.”

Cang Xu faintly nodded, and immediately settled for the next best thing, “When your research finishes, is Lord Jian Gui willing to sell it to this one?”

But Jian Gui still shook his head, “So sorry, I can’t sell that either. This thing is very significant, after I research it a little, I will have to immediately hand it over to my teacher, and it might even be delivered to Founder You Shen’s hands!”

“I understand, I was rude.” Cang Xu sighed, looking very regretful.

If the ship spirit restoration scroll was effective on tower spirits, then that new kind of magic scroll was very important to the survivors.

And so, the gathering continued.

The undead exchanged more information, then entered the third section.

It was exchanging cultivation insights and experiences.

Knowledge was priceless, and exchanging knowledge was mutually profitable. The difference was that one side won more, and the other won less.

Cang Xu felt he had a great gain.

Until now, he had cultivated alone, and although he had the ghost girl Zi Di by him now after escaping Mystifying Monster Island, the latter’s necromancer inheritance had come from Cang Xu.

Zi Di had been learning from Cang Xu.

This time, Cang Xu was communicating with veteran necromancers. Even Ku Feng, an undead fighter, talking about his cultivation experience and how he dealt with negative energy had a big impact on Cang Xu’s knowledge.

To say nothing of the necromancers Qi Ling and Xiao Wang Zi.

As for Jian Gui, although he didn’t speak much, each comment was very insightful and caused Cang Xu to ponder over them for a good while.

Finally, as the undead gathering concluded, and each of them was bidding farewell, Cang Xu wasn’t yet satisfied and inquired about the time and location of the next gathering.

Ku Feng looked at Jian Gui, and after the latter nodded, the former stated that if nothing unexpected happened, a year later, it would be at the same time and place.

Cang Xu then asked, “Is it possible to exchange contact details with each other?”

If they were close enough, necromancers could use alchemy communication devices.

At the same time, they could also have specific places and detailed countersigns to leave behind messages for each other.

After Cang Xu exchanged those with the others, including Jian Gui’s, he departed.

Chapter 454: Weeping Pine Naval Battle (1)

The Iron Lump’s captain’s room.

After Cang Xu boarded the Iron Lump, the first thing he did was to contact the dragonman youngster to alter the battle plan.

Cang Xu had an enormous gain from this undead gathering.

He didn’t want to execute the prior plan.

In the previous plan, the battlefield would be near Weeping Pine Island.

“If battle takes place here, the undead gathering will be held at a different place and time, they might even exclude me, resulting in a significant loss.”

“In the short term, this kind of gathering is valuable, it cannot be replaced!”

With this opinion in mind, he activated the alchemy communication device.

Upon activation, the dragonman youngster transmitted, “Cang Xu, you finally appeared!”

“The situation has changed, we have discovered a large group of pirates. They are headed straight for Weeping Pine Island, stall as long as possible, we are rushing over at top speed to provide assistance!”

The transmission shocked Cang Xu.

A group of pirates suddenly appearing had upended his original plan and had also prevented him from changing to a new one.

Cang Xu thought of Ku Feng’s words – “if nothing unexpected happened, a year later, it would be at the same time and place.”

“As expected, the unexpected happens often.” Cang Xu bitterly laughed.

The dragonman youngster continued to give him information, “This group of pirates are our old friends.”

“All of them were pirate groups involved in the battle for Two Eyes Island. The Stench Pirates, the Applaud Pirates, and the Lighting Pirates.”

“All of their ships are covered in camouflage magic and were concealed from the Deep Sea Monster Fish’s detection for some time.”

“If you were just relying on your own detection methods, you wouldn’t have noticed the pirate ships.”

“Maybe we can make use of this.”

“In short, please procrastinate as much as possible until we arrive.”

“Zong Ge has suggested that you can abandon ship and go onto the island, flee into the pine forest and fight for time.”

Cang Xu became heavy, and he took a deep breath, “All three pirate groups are silver level, but I don’t want to abandon the Iron Lump before the battle starts!”

The dragonman youngster was silent for a moment, “Everything will stem from your decision, Cang Xu. I trust you.”

“I understand, Lord Commander.” Cang Xu severed the communication.

The dragonman youngster trusted Cang Xu and had given him full authority to handle it.

Similarly, the necromancer also trusted the dragonman youngster completely, he didn’t doubt the dragonman youngster would support him with all he had!

According to common sense, retreating to the island was the dependable option.

But because of this trust, Cang Xu decided to take a risk.

“Running away before battle and surrendering the Iron Lump without resistance is inappropriate.”

“A large number of people going into the pine forest will be very difficult to conceal, I am not good at that kind of magic, nor is Qing Xin.”

“If we abandon these dwarfs, then the dwarfs will be massacred by pirates or the undead, how will Men Shi react?”

“If I abandon those humans, what will Qing Xin think in the future?”

“Calm, keep calm, I can ponder over the best strategy.”

Cang Xu was inevitably nervous.

The enemy was too strong.

And their side was too weak.

Cang Xu was only an iron level, and with the fire path rings, he could only pretend to be a silver level for a short time.

Deceptive Disguise and the other spells could not amplify his military might.

When he fought the summoner Ya Ma on Two Eyes Island, this became apparent.

Qing Xin was actually the strongest person on the Iron Lump.

But she had barely any equipment, despite the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps doing their best to make up for that deficiency.

What made Cang Xu most worried was that they only had one ship.

The Iron Lump was a bronze level magic ship, but its maritime military strength was very low.

Because they didn’t have suitable sailors, and after the battle for Two Eyes Island, the Iron Lump had left the island without getting the chance to repair the cannons and other parts.

The Iron Lump was essentially a cruise ship.

“In an open battle, the Iron Lump will be crushed!”

“The swordsmen guards on board can be relied on, but they can only be effective when we are boarded.”

“If this kind of battle happens, the humans and dwarfs on board will suffer disastrous casualties.”

Cang Xu really wanted to prevent this situation.

Cang Xu tightly scowled, and felt puzzled, “Why did these pirate groups unite and head towards Weeping Pine Island?”

“This island has pine trees everywhere but lacks common resources.”

“To cause pirates to band together, it must be a major interest. What can it be?”

“If the empire had dispatched troops to encircle and annihilate the undead gathering, then that would be expected. How come pirates came?”

Cang Xu had limited information.

After thinking deeply, he had a feeling, “The pirates might be following me.”

“I exposed myself on Two Eyes Island, outsiders know I purchased many good things from the auction.”

“Although I seized the Iron Lump, its naval battle capabilities are very limited, and not worth it to these pirate groups.”

“Therefore, they might not know there was an undead gathering being held here!”

“Can I take advantage of this though?”

Cang Xu was not the dragonman youngster, he wasn’t strong enough to fight them all alone.”

Cang Xu wasn’t Zong Ge either, he couldn’t lead troops and didn’t have an uncompromising nature for battle.

Cang Xu was himself.

He had a magician’s calmness and had the vicissitudes and the experience of the old, he had served as a noble steward for many years, and he had been very good at it.

He had outstanding communication skills and political acumen.

Everyone handled the same problem differently.

Zong Ge proposed that he abandon the Iron Lump and travel further into Weeping Pine Island, using space to obtain more time; it was a military strategy.

Cang Xu decided to use a political stratagem.

He moved his brain, imagining, “Can I use the people from the undead gathering?”

“How can I exploit them without making them disgusted with me and make it possible for me to continue participating in this undead gathering?”

After a few seconds of hurried pondering, Cang Xu immediately acted.

He first gave the order to set sail, then informed Qing Xin that his ghosts detected a pirate attack.

After that, he used an alchemy communication device to connect to the other necromancers.

Fortunately, the necromancers hadn’t teleported to other islands with the Eulogy Tombstone yet.

But Cang Xu didn’t offer a high price, he was asking for help.

He informed them of the urgent military situation.

“These people might have followed the Iron Lump all the way here.”

“If this is true, I will find the opportunity to compensate everyone for this in the future.”

“So sorry for bringing trouble to everyone.”

“I have already set sail and look forward to seeing you next time!”

Cang Xu didn’t provide any help, instead after giving a warning, he immediately retreated.

The Iron Lump became chaotic.

After Qing Xin was informed by Cang Xu, she instantly unlocked the ship cabins, allowing the humans and dwarfs to move freely.

Qing Xin spoke forthrightly about the situation and hoped that everyone would work together to overcome this crisis.

The humans and dwarfs didn’t want to fall into pirate hands either, and although they didn’t trust the necromancer, in this cruel situation, they were in this together, for better or worse.

Cang Xu promised to everyone that he and Qing Xin were friends. Although he was a necromancer, he would never slaughter living beings, and after getting through this crisis, he would set everyone free.

Despite encouraging everyone, unfortunately none of the humans on board were able to steer the ship.

The dwarfs came from the same clan and had always lived on a mountain.

The humans were also from inland areas and had scant chances to sail ships.

The dwarfs still had the star iron hand axes, and if boarding actions took place, they could fight.

The Iron Lump set out and left its berthing.

It moved to the western side of Weeping Pine Island.

The reason why they were moving this direction was because the dragonman youngster had given Cang Xu information.

The three silver level pirate groups weren’t all coming in from the same direction.

The Applaud Pirates were coming in from the north east, the Stench Pirate from the east, and the Lighting Pirates were approaching from the south east.

This military deployment had an obvious opening to the west.

Therefore, from being berthed on Weeping Pine Island’s eastern shore, the Iron Lump’s best route was winding around the island and going west of the island to make it more likely to break out o the pirates’ encirclement.

The Applaud Pirates’ new commander Bei Xiang was on top of the ship’s bow, holding a telescope and watching where the Iron Lump was moving towards.

He immediately said, “Not good, the target is escaping.”

“It seems our opponent is not simple; they can at least see through our camouflage!”

“Damn it, can’t you just let me rob you.”

Bei Xiang scratched his back.

He knew this would be a crucial battle for him.

Because he had just ascended to leadership and needed this victory and its generous spoils of war to stabilize his authority.

He quickly shouted, “Top speed, chase them down!”

The new first mate hurried to ask for instructions, “Commander, do we need to use the battle squad?”

Bei Xiang hesitated, then waved his hand in refusal, “It is too early to use it now, don’t separate the flagship from the fleet rashly. The target is nothing to worry about, but we need to be on guard against the other pirates.”

Although the pirate groups had reached a temporary alliance, they were vigilant against each other.

Meanwhile, in the Lightning Pirates’ fleet, Dian Wan also discovered the Iron Lump was moving abnormally.

He raised his head and laughed, appearing very confident, “Based on the Iron Lump’s speed, how can you escape? Hehehe.”

To the east, in the Stench Pirates’ fleet, Yang Cong Tou narrowed his eyes, his heart still worried, “It would be best to adopt a blitzkrieg strategy. If we delay any further, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps will notice, and they’ll stir things up terribly!”

“Perhaps my worries are superfluous, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is a bit tactful, they won’t oppose an alliance of three silver level pirate groups.”

When the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps discovered the three pirate fleets, the pirates had already found them first.

They had been in the battle for Two Eyes Island, and although they hadn’t fought the dragonman youngster personally, they could see this mercenary corps was very strong.

The pirates sought riches and wouldn’t fight the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to the death.

Therefore, they took the long way to avoid the island the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps stopped at.

Chapter 455: Weeping Pine Naval Battle (2)

As the Iron Lump left the island, the pirates tore away their camouflage and sailed at top speed, officially opening the prologue to the Weeping Pine Island naval battle.

Cang Xu did his best to sail the Iron Lump as he pondered their course.

“To the east is the Stench Pirates, with 13 ships in total. The flagship is the Stinky Tofu and isn’t very fast, no need to consider it for the time being.”

“To the northeast is the Applaud Pirates. Their commander Hu Li and the Applaud battle squad working in tandem have air warfare capabilities, a thorny problem. At the same time, their flagship, the Love Triangle, is a long distance voyage type ship. It possesses a silver level triangle sail and can even maintain its speed in a headwind.”

“As for the southeast, the Lightning Pirates are there. They have a total of 7 ships, and they have excellent speed. Their flagship the Electric Fish Net is a remolded fishing vessel; it can put down electric nets.”

The information on these three pirate groups was easy to gather.

In addition, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps fought on Two Eyes Island and naturally had full and accurate information on them.

After Cang Xu thought it through, he chose to head south of Weeping Pine Island.

“Damn it!” Upon seeing the Iron Lump, Bei Xiang exploded in anger.

It was obvious that the Iron Lump had chosen to avoid the Applaud Pirates and would rather face the Lightning Pirates.

This was the correct choice.

The Lightning Pirates’ ships were all fast, but they were ordinary ships. Their only iron level magic ship, the Electric Fish Net, was not as speedy.

The Applaud Pirates had no choice but to go around the island to continue pursuing. Furthermore, due to Weeping Pine Island blocking their line of sight, they effectively lost vision of the Iron Lump.

Bei Xiang was very worried that if the other pirate groups captured the Iron Lump, he would get less spoils of war.

Dian Wan was a sharp contrast to the indignant Bei Xiang.

The pirate commander burst into laughter, “The prey is delivering themselves to us!”

His fleet attacking from the southeast was the closest to the Iron Lump’s route.

The Iron Lump didn’t have a qualified helmsman on board, only Cang Xu reluctantly taking on the job.

This magic ship wasn’t confident in turning around the island, it could only sail in a straight line and scrape past Weeping Pine Island on its way out.

“Chase it down!” Dian Wan excitedly shouted.

The Iron Lump wasn’t that fast and was like an old ox being hunted down by a pack of wolves.

The distance between both sides quickly closed.

Qing Xin was nervous, “We must activate the sail now!”

The sail she was talking about was the Feather Snake Sail.

A gold level magic ship component.

—

Currently, Cang Xu and Qing Xin had to work together to form a magic power reservoir that was channeled into the sail.

Not only that, but they had also wrapped the sail in a layer of plain white cloth, a cursory disguise as an ordinary sail.

But when the gold sail was activated, its gold aura would be emitted, and its disguise would vanish in an instant.

Cang Xu shook his head at Qing Xin’s suggestion, “No, wait a bit.”

Soon after, the distance between both sides had halved.

Qing Xin suggested again, “Activate the sail!”

“No, not yet!” Cang Xu was indifferent, his tone was tranquil, but he was growing increasingly nervous.

Several minutes later.

The distance had nearly been closed, and both sides could see the people on each other’s ships.

Qing Xin was anxious, “Activate it, or else this ship is finished!”

Cang Xu tightened his lips and didn’t say anything as he looked towards Weeping Pine Island.

Crack.

Suddenly, an echoing gunshot caused both sides to quiver.

Dian Wan flew into a rage, “Which idiot opened fire?!”

“What would you do if you shot my ship?”

“If you shot my things, what would you do to compensate for it?!”

When he said that, he had treated such a presumption as truth.

Not just Dian Wan, the others were also tenaciously looking for the shooter.

A pitiful pirate quickly threw down his firearm and raised both hands, “No, it isn’t my fault. This wretched firearm accidently went off!”

Just as they said that a bone arrow whooshed through the air and shot the pirate in the throat.

The pirate went stiff, then fell face first to the deck, dying tragically on the spot.

Whoosh, whoosh, whoosh, whoosh…

A moment later, a large amount of wretched bone white arrows flew out, forming a tight rain of arrows.

Many human skeleton archers came out of the pine trees.

Meanwhile, Cang Xu’s communication device transmitted Xiao Wang Zi’s voice, “Newcomer, don’t be afraid, I’m here to help you.”

“Get out of here!” Said Ku Feng.

“We will cover for you.” Qi Ling echoed too.

Xiao Wang Zi ended with, “Don’t forget that Weeping Pine Island has my Eulogy Tombstone, we can teleport out, don’t be so worried!”

“Success!” Cang Xu’s eyes flashed as he let out a big breath.

He successfully made use of the members of the undead gathering.

Furthermore, this method was very ingenious.

He didn’t ask them for help directly, because officially requesting it might not really work. Because Cang Xu was very suspicious and needed both a long time and due course of events to gradually earn their trust.

Therefore, Cang Xu chose to issue a warning and that he didn’t wish to encumber them, he didn’t want to look like he wanted to draw support from the others.

By doing this, it was instead easier to gain their trust.

These undead, would they really stay out of it and watch on unfeeling as Cang Xu was captured?

Of course not.

If Cang Xu was captured, he might confess things about them.

Cang Xu was a newcomer, and this was the first time they had met him. Even if one knew each other for many years, they too would confess under torture and the threat of death and betray their former friendship.

In Jian Gui’s point of view, he was extremely likely to help out.

The first reason was that he was a gold level and the pirates’ strongest member was just silver.

The second reason was that Jian Gui was considering his prestige. He had helped Ku Feng, Xiao Wang Zi, and Qi Ling many times, regardless of how sincere he really was, it was still within his benefits to set out and not watch on as Cang Xu was arrested before his very eyes.

If Jian Gui was this “cold blooded”, how would Ku Feng, Xiao Wang Zi, Qi Ling, and others see him?

The three would have similar apprehensions, all of them were undead, all of them were pushed aside by the world, every great power was hunting them, and if Jian Gui didn’t save one of them when he could, was he really so magnanimous?

Absolutely not, these apprehensions were a blow to Jian Gui’s prestige.

Cang Xu knew the hearts of men, and with this foundation, he put his stratagem into action.

First, he knew the other undead had a good opinion of him. Because during the gathering, he had done the most transactions. Everyone had exchanged benefits. Who didn’t like a scholar and trading partner with abundant capital?

If the gold sail had been activated earlier, the undead would have hesitated if they saw what was going on the Iron Lump.

Cang Xu chose to take a risk as he watched the Lightning Pirates approach, nearly risking a boarding action in order to put himself in danger.

When Jian Gui and the others saw one of their own caught up in danger, how could they not act?

The probability of them acting was quite high.

When one of them acted, it would undoubtedly spur on the others to do so too.

Not even Jian Gui would watch with folded arms as this happened.

He was a gold level necromancer!

Cang Xu’s gamble paid off, he had utilized his own intelligence to successfully influence the other undead into attacking!

The skeleton archers must be Xiao Wang Zi’s troops.

Their volleys were pretty good, and they caught the Lightning Pirates off guard, inflicting heavy casualties.

Dian Wan became furious, “What’s going on?”

His first mate had already taken command.

Bang, bang, bang.

The Lightning Pirates’ cannons fired, aiming at the skeleton arches in the trees.

The artillery shells exploded into the pine trees, blowing branches and leaves into the sky.

The skeleton archers were turned into bone fragments under the bombardment, unable to withstand it.

However, Xiao Wang Zi, who was hidden in some secret place, was not fretful, on the contrary, he was enjoying himself quietly and mumbled, “Kill them, kill them, I want more of these small skeletons, I have many of them.”

After the Lightning Pirates did their bombardment, they found that although many skeleton archers were slain, there were more coming from all directions and filling in the gaps without delay.

The Lightning Pirates were constantly enveloped by arrows, and their casualties were steadily mounting.

The first mate shouted to Dian Wan, “Commander, the enemy’s military power is unknown, this cost is too disadvantageous to us!”

Dian Wan clenched his fists and bit his teeth, “Necromancers are so annoying! I didn’t expect that he’d leave arrangements here.”

Because of Xiao Wang Zi’s assistance, the Lightning Pirates were forced to retreat from Weeping Pine Island.

Xiao Wang Zi’s skeleton archers couldn’t shoot as far as cannons, and very quickly, they couldn’t threaten the Lightning Pirates anymore.

Anyways, due to this delay, the distance between the Lightning Pirates and the Iron Lump had increased.

Dian Wan was incessantly furious as he watched the distance between them grow. His impulses bubbled up and he decided to do something on the spot, “I will charge in!”

“No, hold on!” The first mate rushed to stop him, “The enemy’s power is unknown, don’t be reckless…”

The first mate stopped talking.

Because Dian Wan had already run out.

He had alchemy equipment, the water walking boots, and as a result, he could treat the ocean surface as soft land that he could run across.

When the first mate saw Dian Wan running out, he deeply sighed and quickly ordered, “Discharge the electric nets now!”

The Flagship Electric Fish Net immediately shot its bow cannon.

It shot a special type of artillery shell.

After the artillery shell flew into the sky, it instantly spread open and formed a large iron net.

The large iron net fell onto the ocean surface and flattened itself across it, then burst with dazzling blue lightning.

Dian Wan’s eyes shone, and he strode onto the momentarily floating electric net.

The electric net expedited Dian Wan’s battle qi, causing it to suddenly explode with all of its might.

Dian Wan focused the electric current, and his pointed blue hair became even more straight as lightning twisted around his entire body.

Battle skill – Electric Current Overload.

“Ah ah ah, I feel power brimming from head to toe!” Dian Wan’s muscles swelled somewhat, and his spry body immediately became thick and strong.

Whoosh!

Dian Wan ferociously stomped on the iron net crackling with electricity, causing it to sink into the seawater while he shot towards the Iron Lump with speed comparable to that of an arrow.

Cang Xu and Qing Xin had been prepared for this, and both used their magic.

Dian Wan nimbly moved, dodging Qing Xin’s wind blades, but Cang Xu’s ghost came, throwing itself into his body.

The large amount of negative energy instantly eroded at Dian Wan, while simultaneously, the ghost’s memories also poured into Dian Wan’s sea of consciousness, blurring his original memory!

Dian Wan’s fierce charge was immediately routed.

Plop.

He fell down, head first into the seawater.

But his water walking boots were still working, so he was floating upside down, his feet on the surface, and his head underwater.

Chapter 456: Weeping Pine Naval Battle (3)

Dian Wan fell headfirst into the ocean, head over heels, his mind flickering between a daze and soberness, his entire body was ice-cold.

In his fiery impatience, he raged and shouted, “Ah ah ha…grr grr…ah ah…grr…”

Ding.

With an explosion, blue lightning burst out, causing a gigantic jet of water to rise.

The water scattered, and Dian Wan, his muscles flexing and lightning curing around him, stood on the ocean once again.

His entire body’s battle qi was active; he didn’t stint to spend any of it as the silver level ghost was squeezed out of it.

The silver level ghost became more transparent and was just about to throw itself at Dian Wan, however, the latter knew he had eaten a loss and prioritized escape, instantly putting distance between the ghost.

The ghost chased right after Dian Wan.

Cang Xu controlled the ghost while feeling a trace of regret.

“If I had known a battle would occur, I would have sold off a silver level ghost at the undead gathering.”

He had a very burden on him now, and if he couldn’t control the ghost how he wished, it would be difficult to display its full combat potential.

In Cang Xu’s original plan, he really wanted to sell a silver level ghost during the undead gathering.

But the plan couldn’t keep up with change, and Cang Xu had easily obtained the Burden Alleviation and Karma Reduction spells.

With these two spells, he had changed his plan and kept a silver level ghost.

No one could have foreseen pirates attacking as he left the island.

Dian Wan had left his fleet in the dust as he charged at the Iron Lump. Meanwhile, the Lightning Pirates’ fleet was speeding up because they had extricated themselves from the skeleton archers.

“Overtake the captain!”

“He charged out by himself again.”

“Chase after him, he needs our assistance!!”

The pirates shouted, their morale high.

But a moment later, a large amount of ghosts came from the seafloor, penetrating through the bottom, the hulls, and stormed inside the pirate vessels.

The pirates were ambushed and sank into great chaos.

“Gho, ghosts!”

“Damn it, blades can’t cut them.”

“Eeeahh…”

For a moment, cries of alarm lingered on.

The one attacking was the ghost magician Qi Ling.

She had an army of water ghosts under her control.

Not limited to just that, she also began to use magic from her seafloor hiding place.

Necromancy spell – Middle Level Soul Sending.

A silver level ghost quietly made its way into the body of a helmsman.

Even though the helmsman was an iron level, he was immediately eroded, his eyes went wide, and his face was filled with fear, yet his mouth was silent, he could only look on helplessly as his body was taken over by the ghost.

Qi Ling coldly smiled on the sea floor.

A moment later, the ghost under her remote control forced the helmsman to turn the rudder fully to the left.

Another pirate ship shouted warnings while taking evasive action.

But it was no use.

The two had been rather close to each other, and every pirate ship had ghosts ambushing and slaughtering those aboard.

It was very difficult for the pirate ships to right their course in a timely manner.

Ding!

The two pirate ships collided.

A moment later, wood shavings flew as many pirates were tossed from the decks and masts.

The collision had left the two ships in a horrific state, their hulls had massive holes in them and lots of seawater was pouring into them!

Zi Di on the Deep Sea Monster Fish had been watching these two ships crash into each other.

“Amazing!” Zi Di couldn’t help but praise from the bottom of her heart.

Zi Di also knew the Soul Send spell.

She had once planned to use the Soul Send spell on the big guy’s body to help him complete his first battle qi circulation.

But Zi Di’s Soul Send spell was only the basic version. The magic allowed the caster to possess another lifeform or an object with their soul. The stronger the soul, despite being used on a corpse or a white skeleton, the formed zombie or skeleton soldier would have exceptional intellect.

Qi Ling’s Soul Send spell was a level higher than that.

She could use Soul Send through her ghost troops, and by controlling the ghost, and the ghost controlling the target, she formed a control chain.

Doing this has many benefits.

The caster putting their soul into the target had a large element of risk to it. With a layer of separation, when the soul within one’s body was resolved, the necromancer would remain safe, for losing one ghost was worth it to secure their own safety.

In addition, the caster controlled many ghosts, and could take it a step further, they could control many targets simultaneously, letting them control the battlefield.

The magician Qi Ling only made one control chain, and that reduced two of the Lightning Pirates’ ships to scrap wood and cause the pirates aboard to cry for their parents as they jumped into the water to escape. One could see how powerful this magic was from this.

This was also the power of a magician.

This group could leverage stronger military power and had abundant methods available.

Therefore, in any battle, killing enemy magicians was a primary mission.

Qi Ling’s water ghost army’s attack had thrown the Lightning Pirates into disarray.

Luckily the first mate was still in command.

Their command structure hadn’t broken down yet and was still operating.

The Iron Lump was like an old ox as it puffed hard to sail ahead.

This was its highest speed.

Due to its strong defense and heavy hull materials, Iron Lump’s speed compared to its foes was no surprise.

On the Iron Lump’s aft, Cang Xu was watching the Lighting Pirates being devastated and couldn’t help but hope, “This is the true strength of necromancers!”

There were several hundred water ghosts, with at least two silver level ghosts among them.

The necromancer Qi Ling had been summoned back by Jian Gui and had served as his testing assistant for many years before being set free, yet she maintained a very close relationship with Jian Gui.

Cang Xu was able to obtain an advanced soul aspect inheritance from Jian Gui the first time they met. It can be surmised that Qi Ling had gotten theirs far earlier.

“The Burden Alleviation spell and Karma Reduction spell are so significant.”

“With these two supplementary spells, a necromancer can be a one man army.”

Cang Xu had unending praise for them.

On the other side Qing Xin was using magic.

Wind path magic – Wind Signal spell.

Qing Xin transmitted to Dian Wan, “Dian Wan, your troops are being swiftly decimated, are you not going to save them?”

Dian Wan knew full well the devastation laid upon his pirate group, and when he heard Qing Xin’s words, he couldn’t help but glance behind him.

But a moment later, he still bit his teeth and continued to pursue the Iron Lump!

Dian Wan’s choice made Qing Xin grow ever graver.

Cang Xu calmly ordered, “Activate the sail.”

Qing Xin’s look instantly changed.

“I’ll overtake you! I’ll repay the damage you’ve done to us tenfold!” Dian Wan screamed in his thoughts.

The distance between both had closed to its limit when Dian Wan jumped with all he had!

Buzz, hmm, buzz…

A great amount of magic power flowed from the magic power reservoir into the Feather Snake Sail.

The magic power passing through the temporary connecting channel produced a resounding buzzing.

This made Qing Xin look onwards with worry, she was very anxious that the temporary channel wouldn’t be able to bear the flow and would collapse at this crucial moment.

Luckily, the materials Cang Xu had used were exceptionally sturdy and Qing Xin’s worries weren’t realized for the time being.

Chapter 457: Weeping Pine Naval Battle (4)

When great amounts of magic power flowed into the Feather Snake Sail, it resembled an out of practice expert encountering an army of tigers and wolves that suddenly erupted with a dark green light.

The light scattered, causing the while cloth over it to be torn to shreds, completely exposing the gold level sail to full view.

“Ah, the Feather Snake Sail!” Dian Wan was startled.

It wasn’t that he didn’t know Cang Xu had the Feather Snake Sail, after all he had been at the auction too.

But Cang Xu’s conduct was very treacherous.

He had camouflaged the Feather Snake Sail and didn’t rush to activate it; thus, its gold level aura never appeared.

This caused Dian Wan to have an error of judgement.

Dain Wan had already jumped into the air and used a defensive battle skill, he was about to crash into the Iron Lump’s deck.

But a moment later, the Iron Lump accelerated!

The old ox suddenly turned into a rabbit.

The Iron Lump abruptly sped forth.

Dian Wan couldn’t fly and wasn’t able to use his abilities in the air, he had no choice but to drop back down as he looked onwards helplessly as his target escaped.

“Damn it, don’t you escape!” He unconsciously extended an arm that wanted to grab it.

Then, the waves stirred up by the Iron Lump submerged him.

The pitiful Dian Wan, he had been played by Cang Xu again.

The Iron Lump’s fleeing figure attracted the eyes of many.

“The newcomer is unexpectedly savvy!” Xiao Wang Zi laughed out his praise.

He had been worried a moment ago, after all, a silver level fighter closing the distance with a magician was very dangerous.

But a moment later, the Iron Lump’s surprising burst of speed allowed Cang Xu and the others to successfully avert disaster.

Ku Feng standing next to Xiao Wang Zi suddenly realized, “The Feather Snake Sail, they actually managed to attach it to the Iron Lump.”

Neither of them suspected Cang Xu’s motives.

Because Cang Xu had warned them beforehand and didn’t request for help actively.

If he had done so and deliberately placed himself in danger to compel the other necromancers into helping him, then that would undoubtedly draw attention to Cang Xu’s malice.

“No need to worry about the newcomer for now.”

“Hehehe, it is our turn to show off.”

“Generalissimo, we charge!”

On the skeleton magician Xiao Wang Zi’s order, his three headed undead sea serpent mount obeyed and suddenly rushed out of the pine forest.

Many pine trees were crushed down, and countless broken branches flew into the air, its impetus was astonishing.

But a moment later, a large hand picked up Xiao Wang Zi’s slim skull and easily lifted him up.

Ku Feng picked up Xiao Wang Zi, then jumped up over the three headed undead sea serpent, then landed back down.

“Huh, hey?” Xiao Wang Zi looked up at Ku Feng, his feet not touching the ground. His four limbs flailed about, “Let me go at once, I want to go fight and kill those damned pirates!”

Xiao Wang Zi detested pirates greatly.

In the past, he had frequently attacked pirates to protect his kingdom’s merchant ships.

Every time his parents went traveling, he had gone ahead of them to wantonly mop up pirates to prevent them from damaging his meeting with his parents.

Ku Feng was indifferent, and his tone was calm, “Lord Jian Gui sent me here to protect you.”

“Don’t forget, you are a magician.”

“Charging into enemy lines isn’t your role.”

“Were the many lessons you got about this not enough?”

Xiao Wang Zi’s big head immediately bobbed up and down, and his legs and feet no longer waved around as he mocked, “Alas, isn’t Lord Jian Gui here? Normally, I wouldn’t dare to do this.”

“Stop being pesky and hide with me.” Ku Feng carried Xiao Wang Zi into the depths of the forest.

Xiao Wang Zi was forced to then order, “Generalissimo, you can charge by yourself, make an extra effort!”

The undead three headed sea serpent continued to charge, it jumped into the water and quickly swam towards the Lightning Pirates.

When the Lightning Pirates’ first mate saw this, he took a cold breath, “Why is there also a silver level undead magic pet?”

He sensed things were getting worse.

Because during the course of this battle, the other side had exposed strength far beyond prior reports.

Their information had described in detail Cang Xu’s strength on Two Eyes Island.

But now, there were skeleton archers, water ghost troops, several silver level ghosts, and also a silver level undead three headed serpent.

“We cannot let that serpent approach!” The first mate felt a trickle of cold sweat as he rushed to give the order to open fire to stop Xiao Wang Zi’s “generalissimo”.

There were differences between silver levels.

Such as this enormous undead magic beast, it was the greatest threat to the pirates’ ships.

Soon after, the Lightning Pirates opened fire.

But the artillery was irregular, the reason was that most of the crew were being entangled by water ghosts and already had their hands full.

The electric net artillery shells the first mate had placed his expectations upon were never fired.

As it turned out, the gunners there were gravely impeded.

Magician Qi Ling had been watching for an opportunity and had focused on controlling a silver level ghost to penetrate deeply into the Electric Net Fisher to wreak havoc, coordinating with the three headed serpent’s advance.

The first mate clenched his teeth as he brazenly ordered, “Smash into it!”

When the pirates around him heard this order, their eyes widened with incredulousness.

But the first mate sternly roared, “You aren’t going!”

There was something that made the Lightning Pirates a bit different from other pirate groups - the first mate’s reputation was greater than its commander.

The pirates’ vicious nature was also stimulated.

“Fuck his mother!”

“Crash into it, run it down.”

“That three headed serpent is already dead!”

“Then let’s run it down, smash it to bits!!”

The pirates yelled as the flagship Electric Net Fisher quickly broke away from the fleet and took the lead in charging at the undead three headed serpent.

When Xiao Wang Zi saw this, he rushed to control the three headed serpent to dive down.

The Electric Net Fisher hit nothing.

The first mate had foreseen this and had ordered in advance, “Fire the electric net shells around us!”

Ding, ding.

Due to Qi Ling’s interference, only two shells were fired, forming two electric nets.

Xiao Wang Zi giggled, “Having a teammate working with you feels wonderful!”

He ordered his generalissimo to float up and easily avoid the two electric nets.

The generalissimo extended its three long serpent heads and easily pulled itself onto the deck of the Electric Net Fisher, with its limbs and tail moving to and fro.

Because of the three headed serpent’s weight, the entirety of the Electric Net Fisher began to tilt.

The first mate had no choice but to take out his cutlass and charge first.

With Xiao Wang Zi and Qi Ling, two veteran silver level necromancers, working together, the entirety of the Lightning Pirates was trending towards total defeat.

Seeing the situation, Dian Wan finally turned around and gave up pursuing the fleeing Iron Lump, returning back to his fleet to assist it.

While Dian Wan rushed back to his flagship, another pirate group was heading over to support them.

It was Yang Cong Tou’s Stench Pirates!

Dian Wan yelled at Yang Cong Tou, who was standing on his ship’s bow, “Quickly, let’s work together to get rid of that three headed undead serpent!”

On Two Eyes Island, these two groups had wanted to work together to capture Hun Tong’s city government building.

Chapter 458: Weeping Pine Naval Battle (5)

On the ship’s bow, Yang Cong Tou tightened his brows and yelled at Dian Wan with a gloomy face, “You still don’t know that we are not just facing a single necromancer?”

The Lightning Pirates didn’t detect anything, but Yang Cong Tou was a magician and had better investigative abilities than fighters like Dian Wan.

“I surmised that long ago!” The Lightning Pirates’ first mate nodded, “The other side’s military power exceeds the reports by too much.”

“There should be a second necromancer attacking! When they attended the auction, there were two of them.”

After hearing what Yang Coing Tou and his first mate said, Dian Wan impatiently replied, “Then we have to work more together!”

Yang Cong Tou coldly snorted and didn’t refute the first mate’s conjecture.

He had checked the depths of the seafloor and found the aura of a necromancer casting, but he hadn’t detected Xiao Wang Zi or Ku Feng.

Let alone Jian Gui.

Therefore, despite being able to detect something more than Dian Wan, he was still completely in the dark and didn’t know what the enemy’s actual military power was.

This was the proper strategy for a magician.

As long as they remained hidden and didn’t reveal themselves, they could cause the enemy to misjudge things and obtain a level of initiative.

If Xiao Wang Zi came over with the three headed undead sea serpent, then the pirates could make out a portion of the truth and become more vigilant.

Despite not discovering the truth yet, Yang Cong Tou still felt under pressure. Of the revealed military power, the pirates were still on top.

Yang Cong Tou really valued his gut feelings.

Because this feeling had helped him get through many dangers.

As an experienced and knowledgeable magician, he knew this feeling was called “spiritual awareness”.

Often those that steeled themselves at the edge of life and death would gradually create spiritual awareness, it was a type of spiritual power!

“Things feel wrong.”

“Be more cautious, saving Dian Wan and his people is essential.” Yang Cong Tou said to himself.

Truth be told, he didn’t have a good opinion of Dian Wan’s group.

Dian Wan had looted the Sun Halo before Yang Cong Tou could, something Dian Wan mocked Yang Cong Tou for.

Yang Cong Tou had joined the auction to purchase the Sun Halo, but Zi Di and Cang Xu had bought him out.

He had agreed to join Lady Sea Snake’s alliance because he was both afraid of her and that his main goal here was to seize the Sun Halo.

The Sun Halo was very significant to Yang Cong Tou’s cultivation!

“If it weren’t for Dian Wan being a step ahead of me, would it be necessary for me to be dragged all the way here?”

Thinking of that, Yang Cong Tou became aggrieved.

He sneered at Dian Wan, “Hehe, work together? Don’t be so flowery.”

“Dian Wan, you need my help now!”

Dian Wan’s look immediately sank, and the pirates by him also began to swear.

“I can help you, but what will it cost?” Yang Cong Tou shamelessly seized the opportunity to extort him.

The pirate alliance was not a united group, that it didn’t hit another while they were down already gave Lady Sea Snake face.

Taking the chance to extort another was wholly within common procedure.

The Iron Lump.

Cang Xu looked at the Lighting Pirates and the Stench Pirates converging in the distance, and when the latter was aiding the former, it immediately caused his brows to purse.

He instantly acted, unexpectedly stopping the Feather Snake Sail.

The heavy Iron Lump’s speed turned from a fleeing hare back into an old ox’s pace.

Qing Xin immediately turned around, giving Cang Xu an incredulous look, as if a giant question mark was appearing above her head.

“We can leave this battlefield! This is no place to stay for long.”

“Are you thinking of going back to help?”

“That’s unnecessary. They just communicated that we should leave without delay. With our strength, we’ll just be a burden.”

Cang Xu shook his head, “No, wait a moment, take a look at how the situation is developing.”

Qing Xin didn’t know the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was around.

Seeing Cang Xu do this, Qing Xin’s misunderstanding deepened, “Are you waiting for Jian Gui to act?”

“Indeed, the strongest of these pirates are only silver level, the necromancers have a clear advantage.”

“But have you considered that since the enemy’s strongest is a few silvers, why hasn’t Jian Gui acted yet? This proves that he doesn’t want to act.”

“You are all necromancers, but you all have a different capacity. If a gold level necromancer appears, that will provoke far more trouble that a silver level, Jian Gui probably doesn’t want to stir up such trouble.”

But at that moment, a communication device transmitted Zi Di’s voice, “Older sister Qing Xin, don’t worry, you still have my support.”

The Deep Sea Monster Fish was far faster than conventional ships, and had arrived already, but didn’t enter battle.

At the necessary time, Zi Di would act.

Qing Xin’s eyes flashed, “Of course I trust you. So, you were actually nearby.”

“So that’s how it is.”

“You wanted to express your goodwill to these necromancers by helping?”

“You must be careful! Those pirates are not easy to push around. That Jian Gui seems to speak well, but since Jian Gui is a direct inheritor of the undead faction, he won’t be easy to deceive.”

The Stinky Tofu.

The lookout on the mast shouted, “Commander, there is something!”

“The Iron Lump has slowed down to its prior speed.”

Yang Cong Tou was in the middle of extorting the Lighting Pirates when he heard this and turned to look into the distance with shock.

“Huh?” He saw the situation, it was just as the lookout had said, the Iron Lump had indeed slowed down to its previous speed.

Yang Cong Tou’s heart moved, “Could it be that they weren’t able to fully install the gold level Feather Snake Sail, that they were able to only use it once when forced by Dian Wan?”

“But on the other hand, the Feather Snake Sail and the Iron Lump are not compatible, it will be very difficult to put them together.”

“One needed at least an outstanding alchemy designer to plan out a sensible magic power circulation flow.”

On the other side, the Lightning Pirates’ first mate also felt this and quickly shouted, “Commander Yang Cong Tou, we agree to your terms…”

Before he could finish, Yang Cong Tou interrupted him.

He burst into laughter, “I gave you a chance, but you thought it was too expensive!”

“It is too late to agree now.”

“Set off! Seize the Iron Lump!!”

The Stench Pirates loudly answered Yang Cong Tou’s command with great joy.

The pirates were discontented with going to help another pirate group.

Now, with wealth right in front of their eyes, who would want to help the Lightning Pirates? Would they really help them and divide up the treasure with them?

Simply ridiculous!

The Stinky Tofu was the first to sail past the Electric Net Fisher, aimed at the Iron Lump in fierce pursuit.

Tightly following it were the ordinary pirate ships.

Before leaving, Yang Cong Tou taunted Dian Wan, “Dian Wan, have fun with your new friends. We will go get rich!”

Dian Wan angrily rained curses back.

This made Yang Con Tou even happier, and he raised his head in raucous laughter.

Yang Cong Tou led the Stench Pirates in a constant pursuit, quickly closing in on the Iron Lump.

Seeing that they had reached a certain distance from them, Cang Xu once again activated the Feather Snake Sail.

The Iron Lump churned water as it accelerated and began to put distance between the two groups.

Yang Cong Tou’s eyes glared, and he realized things were far from good, “Not good, it’s a trap!”

But what would happen if he turned around to help the Lightning Pirates?

Yang Cong Tou didn’t want to lose face too.

“Chase them, keep chasing them!” Yang Cong Tou sternly shouted.

The Stench Pirates were in hot pursuit, but speed wasn’t their strength, and they could only look helplessly as the distance between them and their quarry continuously grew.

However, just as Yang Cong Tou and his crew gradually despaired, Cang Xu quietly acted again and turned off the Feather Snake Sail.

Seeing the Iron Lump slow down again, Yang Cong Tou stared blankly, “Eh?”

However, when he looked at the Iron Lump’s deck, he saw thick smoke rising.

Yang Cong Tou rejoiced as he surmised, “Sure enough! They were forced to do that, the Feather Snake Sail is very difficult to link to the Iron Lump, their connections have problems.”

On the Iron Lump’s deck, Qing Xin gazed at the thick smoke on the deck, quite amazed, “I didn’t expect that this design included an automatic smoke discharge function?”

Qing Xin looked at Cang Xu, “Is this your design?”

Cang Xu shook his head, “Of course not. However, it was my idea.”

The channel between the gold sail and the Iron Lump’s magic power reservoir wasn’t damaged. Its design had come from the tower spirit.

This kind of design was made through the tower spirit looking through a large amount of blueprints and amending one of them.

The thick smoke once again deceived Yang Cong Tou, and the entirety of the Stench Pirates were once more inspired to pursue the Iron Lump.
