When the distance closed between the two sides again, Cang Xu activated the Feather Snake Sail once more..

The Iron Lump sped up again.

Yang Cong Tou, “Eh?”

The Iron Lump slowed down.

The Stench Pirates stirred back up.

The Iron Lump sped up again.

Yang Cong Tou didn’t grunt again, instead he deeply pursed his brows, his face filled with fury, “A trick! This necromancer is really treacherous!!”

But at this distance, he was already too far from the Lightning Pirates and it would be too difficult for him to turn around and help the latter.

Yang Cong Tou simply stopped paying attention to Dian Wan’s group and maintained his prior pursuit order without alteration.

The Iron Lump had successfully hung up a pirate group.

Cang Xu coughed, then he used an alchemy device to notify Zi Di, “Everything is under control.”

“But Jian Gui still hasn’t acted yet.”

“This person is very important; he has many good things on him.”

“The dependable plan is to express goodwill to him.”

“But if circumstances allow, we will attack him, the spoils of war might be massive!”

Whether it was the soul crystal creation method or how to use memory crystals, both were very beneficial to the survivors.

When Qing Xin heard this dialogue, her eyes couldn’t help but widen with shock.

“You want to attack Jian Gui?! He is a gold level…”

Zi Di transmitted a laugh, “Older sister Qing Xin, don’t forget, the pirates will serve as our cannon fodder.”

Telling Qing Xin this ahead of time was so she could accept it was happening.

Qing Xin’s brows creased greatly, “Little Zi Di, how much power is under your control? You want to plot against a gold level necromancer, that is too risky!”

Zi Di didn’t directly reply, instead she said, “We are also acting according to circumstances.”

Cang Xu added, “I will now send you the precise coordinates of the cave to you now. If the necromancers show signs of teleporting, you can discern whether or not to bombard the cave and destroy the Eulogy Tombstone!”

Qing Xin took a cold breath.

Upon hearing Zi Di’s and Cang Xu’s audacious scheme, she grew far more tense.

Cang Xu consoled her, “Relax a bit.”

“The current situation is more favorable for us.”

“Both sides are either criminals or disciples of evil, this is the right thing to do, this is exercising justice.”

Qing Xin’s mouth twitched, she wanted to ridicule that with: did you forget that you are also a necromancer?

While Cang Xu was plotting against both sides, the Applaud Pirates had finally circled around the island and were now in the field of view of Cang Xu and the others.

“The Iron Lump is still here!” Bei Xiang joyously shouted when he saw the Iron Lump running away.

While sailing around the island, he had been very worried that the Lightning Pirates had already captured the Iron Lump and now held all of its people and treasures.

He didn’t expect that the Iron Lump would be in a very different situation to the one he had envisioned.

The group he believed to be his biggest competitor, the Lightning Pirates, were unexpectedly in a terrible situation, they were entangled in grave danger with water ghosts and an undead three headed serpent.

The Stench Pirates were the closest to the Iron Lump.

“That is the gold level Feather Snake Sail!” After Bei Xiang saw the state of the Iron Lump, he immediately realized the crucial factor that had brought about the current situation.

“My flagship too will find it hard to overtake the Iron Lump’s speed.”

“It is worthy of being a gold level sail, it could actually elevate the Iron Lump’s speed by so much!”

Bei Xiang sighed to himself.

After a bit of reflection, he ordered, “After closing distance with the target, shell it!”

The Applaud Pirates’ flagship was the Love Triangle.

It was an iron level magic ship and was a voyage type ship, its speed was pretty good and exceeded that of the Iron Lump’s by a bit.

But Bei Xiang didn’t want to get close to the Iron Lump and do a boarding action.

The reason was simple, he was only iron level, not silver.

If he was a silver level fighter, he would have done the same as Dian Wan, charging at the Iron Lump directly was the best tactic!

Bang, bang, bang.

The Love Triangle came from the northeast, and after closing the distance, began to bombard in succession.

Everyone on the Iron Lump found it difficult to bear.

To ensure safety, the Iron Lump was forced to take evasive maneuvers.

The ship didn’t have a qualified helmsman!

The Iron Lump had a bad showing against the bombardment, this made Yang Cong Tou behind it both worried and delighted.

He was delighted that since the Iron Lump was weak to this, if the Stench Pirates joined in, it would make the latter’s situation worse and cause their speed to fall further.

He was worried that such a bombardment would damage many valuable things.

“If only the Sun Halo was in his storage device…” Yang Cong Tou sighed, then followed that with an order.

The Stench Pirates would also start shelling.

The Iron Lump’s situation grew more difficult.

This ship’s entire gunnery was filled with last moment substitutes, dwarfs and humans were passively standing around, studying how to operate the cannons.

While this situation was playing out, at the edge of the battlefield, a new power appeared.

The White Bandage was in the lead, serving as the flagship.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ main force had entered the battlefield, officially joining the Weeping Pine Naval Battle!

Chapter 459: Weeping Pine Naval Battle (6)

The White Bandage.

The dragonman youngster was standing on the pole inclining from the ship’s bow, his strong and tenacious red dragon scales welcoming the sea breeze, revealing his mass and vigor.

He raised a telescope and surveyed the Iron Lump.

Zong Ge was standing on top of the aft house.

Sailors were bustling across the ship, two battle squads were hidden on standby, with no one knowing where they were.

Numerous cannons had already poked their barrels out; a number of dark green cannon mouths looked like the sharp toothed maw of a gigantic beast.

Unlike the two other sides, the last participant of this battlefield, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, had entered in peak condition.

But the dragonman youngster was solemn, because he knew that they only looked the strongest, it wasn’t truly the case.

The gold level necromancer Jian Gui hadn’t acted yet, and the coalition of three pirate groups was also a formidable opponent.

“Move, it’s the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps!” On Weeping Pine Island, Ku Feng saw the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ flag, and once again carried Xiao Wang Zi by the nape and retreated deeper into the pine forest.

“I once associated with them for a time.”

“This mercenary corps’ backer is very strong; they are rumored to be arms smugglers.”

“Their commander and auxiliary commander are not ordinary silver levels. The auxiliary commander Shi Qi already has a gold luster to his silver battle qi and Commander Long Fu is even stronger than him.”

“During the battle for Two Eyes Island, no one of the same level could withstand him. He was even able to fight Er Gua Zi directly and was only a bit weaker than him.”

Ku Feng explained this to Xiao Wang Zi while also transmitting it through a communication device to inform the other necromancers, including the newcomer, the one time participant of the undead gathering, Cang Xu.

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps…they still came!” Yang Cong Tou’s brows deeply wrinkled.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was undoubtably a powerful enemy.

Dian Wan was even more anxious.

Because according to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ course, they would be the first to meet them.

It wasn’t that Dian Wan didn’t want to withdraw and get away from the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, rather it was because they were entangled with the undead three headed sea serpent and the water ghost army and unable to free themselves.

“Fire the cannons at me, fire the cannons at me!” Dian Wan clenched his teeth, roaring with firm resolve.

He jumped into the ocean and decided to face his own cannons.

The first mate followed up by yelling, “Do as the commander says!”

Bang, bang!

Two cannons fired, shooting two electric net shells that detonated above Dian Wan, one on top of the other.

The two electric nets that exploded out directly fell on Dian Wan’s body.

Dian Wan summoned all of his battle qi to withstand the two layers of electric nets.

Dian Wan’s skin quickly burned black and emitted the cooked smell of roasted meat.

Dian Wan shuddered fiercely all over, his head fell to his waist, he kept himself from dying and fainting.

He managed to endure the most difficult phase.

After slowly breathing out, he immediately used his battle qi to absorb all of the electrical currents into his body.

His cultivation was the Electrocution battle qi technique, and this battle qi technique’s philosophy was cultivation through pain. During normal cultivation, he would touch an electric current constantly and be shocked by it at his own accord, using the electric current traveling through his muscles and internals to stimulate his body and produce battle qi.

Normal cultivation was very painful, and at this time, Dian Wan used a secret technique, but the theory came from the same place.

It was all based on electric current stimulation, through this massive suffering, one could attain some cultivation goal.

Two electric nets was Dian Wan’s limit.

The pursuit of the Iron Lump, along with fighting the undead three headed sea serpent and the water ghost army had depleted his battle qi reserves to less than 30%.

But through this electric shock, his potential was stimulated to the utmost and his battle qi quickly recovered to 85%. At the same time, his offense, defense, and speed were also amplified immensely.

“It really hurts!” Dian Wan slowly raised his head and extended arms that were now twice their size across his chest.

Then he looked up, and with a shake of his arms, tore the two electric nets sparking weakly to shreds.

Dian Wan’s chest was exposed, his entire being had swelled, his muscles looked like marble lumps, and his full head of sharp blue hair stood up perfectly straight like steel needles, the space between each hair tip sparked with electricity.

Dian Wan’s eyes were pitch black, with only his azure pupils shooting out glaring electric light.

Dian Wan at this moment had reached the summit of his military power!

Bang.

He took a step on the ocean surface, then suddenly disappeared.

Dian Wan had become a bolt of lightning that ferociously slammed into the undead three headed serpent’s abdomen.

The undead three headed serpent looked like a locomotive being knocked off its tracks as its entire serpent body was thrown off the Electric Net Fisher.

The three snake heads then reacted, facing towards their own abdomen as they opened their mouths to unleash its formidable racial magic.

Dian Wan roared and used a battle skill.

After both of them collided, a gigantic explosion occurred.

The undead three headed serpent was blown into the ocean, with a gigantic hole in its abdomen.

Dian Wan panted from the top of his flagship’s aft house, watching as the undead three headed serpent fell into the water, his complexion pale and his swollen body shrinking somewhat.

“Generalissimo!” Xiao Wang Zi lamented from his hiding place on the island.

He could sense the undead three headed serpent’s injury was big.

Of course, it could still fight.

But Xiao Wang Zi didn’t want to risk his generalissimo anymore.

Xiao Wang Zi used his spirit connection to order the undead three headed serpent to retreat immediately.

The latter had no retorts, it didn’t have a living being’s battle intent, it was an ice-cold machine that swam back to Weeping Pine Island after receiving the order.

Dian Wan then fought the water ghost army, causing great damage to the latter.

His battle qi mixed with electricity, increasing its destructive power against ghosts greatly.

In response to this, magician Qi Ling also commanded her water ghost army to retreat.

The water ghosts were nimble and conventional physical barriers were nothing to them, so they quickly vanished completely, as if they were never there.

But the Lightning Pirates’ sinking ships and dead pirates everywhere proved their horror.

“We won?”

“Finally, we beat back those wretched undead!”

“Boo hoo hoo, I thought my death was for certain.”

“It was all thanks to our captain, Captain Dian Wan’s power!!”

Dian Wan strived to ease his aura, then he hastily ordered, “We are leaving now, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is coming!”

The pirates’ cheers came to a sudden stop.

They really were in a miserable situation.

After two veteran silver level necromancers had devastated and bullied them, they would now be greeting the formidable Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

The pirates were all nervous, they weren’t able to breathe during these successive operations.

The first mate stood next to Dian Wan’s side, looking at the latter with concern.

The latter’s fist was faintly trembling, but he was still standing straight like a rifle!

He gazed at the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps with rapt attention; his heart filled with bitterness.

It was too late to do anything.

Dian Wan didn’t want to fight, the Lightning Pirates had already lost enough people.

But setting sail now, their speed wouldn’t be faster than the mercenary corps’ fleet.

“Prepare for battle!” Dian Wan then ordered, tenaciously looking at the approaching enemy.

But the first mate’s opinion was different from his, “Commander, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps might not be coming for us.”

At almost the same time, the dragonman youngster ordered, “Go around, avoid the Lightning Pirates.”

A moment later, the flagship, the White Bandage, slightly changed its route and so did the ships following behind it.

They were like a sharp blade that cut at an angle that was some distance from the Lightning Pirates.

Next to Dian Wan, the first mate’s eyes flashed sharply, “Our prior investigation said that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps have the dwarf Men Shi. It seems they absorbed this silver level dwarf into their ranks.”

“The Iron Lump has many dwarf prisoners, all Men Shi’s clansmen.”

“Therefore, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ most important goal is not us pirates, but the Iron Lump.”

“Commander Long Fu wants to rescue those dwarfs!”

Dian Wan had a realization, “So that’s how it is.”

But just as he let out a breath, he saw the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ships open up with sudden puffs of white smoke.

Both Dian Wan’s and the first mate’s eyes contracted fiercely, roaring one after the other, “A bombardment, take evasion action now!”

Although the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had intentionally gone around the Lightning Pirates, they didn’t let them off completely.

The Lightning Pirates’ ships were all bunched up while the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ships formed a line, perfect for a broadside against the Lightning Pirates.

Under normal circumstances, ships had most of their cannons arranged on the sides of their hulls, thus these sides had the strongest firepower, while the bow and the aft had the weakest.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps didn’t lack cannons.

They used to, but after the battle of Two Eyes Island, they had recuperated and were able to make a deal with Hun Tong and were backed full with ordinary cannons.

The densely packed cannons seemed to create a rain of cannonballs that indifferently and mercilessly overshadowed the Lighting Pirates.

Bang, bang, bang…

The continuous explosions added insult to injury to the Lighting Pirates, instantly increasing their casualties dramatically.

Dian Wan howled.

The first mate held his head, burying himself behind the cover in the back, doing his best to curl up himself.

Suddenly, an artillery shell struck near them.

It was a silver level artillery shell!

The artillery shell exploded, knocking the first made unconscious, blowing a massive hole into the Electric Net Fisher, and flinging Dian Wan three meters away.

The shooter was Chi Lai.

Although he saw Dian Wan flying away, he quickly stood up and sighed regretfully.

In front of him was an ordinary cannon, it had blown up and was utterly destroyed, unable to stand another of his shots.

“If this was the gold level cannon, then the entire Electric Net Fisher would be gone!” Chi Lai began to reminisce.

During the battle for Two Eyes Island, he fired the gold level cannon once.

That profound and invigorating feeling was something he found hard to forget.

“Captain Dian Wan, how are you?” The troubled pirates asked.

“It’s nothing!” Dian Wan stood back up, but his back was filled with blood.

“Cough, cough, cough.” He suddenly coughed intensely, his complexion growing even whiter.

But he knew he couldn’t fall; he had to grind his teeth and persevere.

At that moment, Lady Sea Snake’s voice came from a communication device, “Captain Dian Wan, please have your group impede the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps as much as possible!”

Dian Wan blankly stared, then cursed without restraint, “Fuck your mother with your impediment!”

The Lighting Pirates had suffered disastrous damage, and Dian Wan soon ordered them to all retreat.

The Lightning Pirates were the first to retreat from the battlefield.

Chapter 460: Weeping Pine Naval Battle (7)

The Lightning Pirates left.

Once Dian Wan made a decision, the entire pirate group would follow it instantly and to the letter.

Dian Wan was a veteran pirate commander, and was swift and decisive, and the entire pirate group was also made almost completely of veterans too.

During the battle for Two Eyes Island, they had lost many ships. In this battle, they had seven ships, and they retreated with only three (including this flagship), an absolute blow to their strength.

“The Lightning Pirates are in a dire state, and the Electric Net Fisher is an iron level magic ship. It’s a bit of a pity that we are letting them get away.” Zong Ge secretly communicated to the dragonman youngster, “I can lead a few ships to deal with them. Dian Wan looks powerful, but in actuality, he is at his tail end, I determine that he is probably bluffing.”

The dragonman youngster muttered to himself for a bit, then refused, “Let them go, the battlefield still has the Applaud Pirates and the Stench Pirates.”

“Now is not the time to divide our forces.”

“The most crucial person is Jian Gui!”

“You must know, with our current course, it will likely induce him into attacking the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps!”

Zong Ge nodded, and didn’t say anything else.

The dragonman youngster’s misgivings were also his misgivings too.

That was the crisis.

In danger there was opportunity.

It all depended on what choices they made.

Sensing the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was still firmly locked onto the Iron Lump, Men Shi secretly let out a breath.

He also saw how weak the Lightning Pirates were.

He too was worried that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would make a detour to deal with them, seeking their iron level magic ship.

“Commander Long Fu gave up that temptation.”

“He could have gone to attack it, after all, having an iron level flagship would greatly improve the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps immensely.”

“But he didn’t do that, he is pursuing the Iron Lump wholeheartedly.”

“He is doing that to save my clansmen!”

Men Shi’s heart was incessantly moved.

The Iron Lump.

Qing Xin was nervous, “I can see Men Shi on the White Bandage. We are the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ target!”

Cang Xu nodded, feigning confusion, “So that’s how it is.”

Qing Xin’s brows wrinkled, and she softly said to Cang Xu, “If that is the case, then perhaps we can make use of those dwarfs. Threatening the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps with them can perhaps be of some use to us.”

Cang Xu pretended to ponder, then refused, “Long Fu is not that kind of man. Moreover, we have already set the dwarfs and humans free, and we made a promise to them, holding them hostage now will likely trigger internal disorder! That is very detrimental for us.”

Qing Xin sighed, “The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps are too much of a threat! During the battle for Two Eyes Island, we fought against them. They won’t let us go. By using the dwarfs as hostages, we can restrict them and force them into refraining from attacking in fear of harming the innocent.”

Cang Xu would never accept this suggestion, as he was part of the dragonman youngster’s group, he was simply putting on a play right now.

Cang Xu shook his head, “I am not a disciple of evil, Magician Qing Xin. I believe that it would be for the best if we didn’t provoke the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps too much. But the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps can be exploited! We can change our course, so the Applaud Pirates and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps get close to each other!”

Qing Xin raised a brow, “Good plan. Since the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps wants to help Men Shi save his fellow dwarfs, they won’t shell the Iron Lump while also simultaneously abhorring those pirates.”

The Iron Lump immediately altered its course.

They didn’t have a qualified helmsman, and could only steer by leaning to the side, altering its course bit by bit, swerving in the battlefield.

At this moment, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps were coming in from the east, they had already steered around the Lightning Pirates and were south of Weeping Pine Island.

The Iron Lump and the Applaud Pirates were southwest of the island.

The Applaud Pirates were northeast of the Iron Lump. To the Iron Lump’s southwest, there was nothing.

After Cang Xu gave the order, the Iron Lump easily sailed southwest. This course also had a gigantic advantage, it would keep the Applaud Pirates points ahead, preventing them from using their strongest firepower.

The Love Triangle.

The new pirate commander, frogman Bei Xiang, was beating the side of the ship with his fist.

“Abominable!” He immediately saw how the Iron Lump was moving.

The Iron Lump wasn’t fleeing westward, rather it had changed course to the southwest.

Bei Xiang was experienced in naval battles, and also knew Men Shi had joined the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, he instantly recognized Cang Xu’s stratagem.

It wasn’t that he couldn’t overtake the Iron Lump with speed, he just didn’t do so.

Because based on his information, Cang Xu and Qing Xin were silver level magicians.

The Applaud Pirates lacked a silver level, and their only sole silver level, their old commander Hu Li, had been sentenced to death by a pirate trial.

The Applaud Pirates’ systemic problem had given them layers of heavy qualms; they didn’t dare to launch an all out attack and were forced to harass the other side with cannons.

But the cannons on the bow were nothing to write home about.

This kind of bombardment against the Iron Lump was no significant threat, and didn’t affect the latter’s speed.

As Bei Xiang was at his wits’ end, Lady Sea Snake’s voice came through from a communication device.

Lady Sea Snake told Bei Xiang this: she was approaching the target; she needed Bei Xiang to attract the target’s attention and wait for her to make her move!

Bei Xiang expressed that it would be difficult.

Lady Sea Snake coldly snorted, and didn’t mince words, “Use your battle squad, accelerate the flagship. The other side only has one ship, and it is just a bronze level one, what are you afraid of?”

Bei Xiang’s expression immediately went gloomy.

The means couldn’t be misused; it should be used last and be treated as an emergency retreat method.

Many times, the Applaud Pirates had used this method to escape alive.

Bei Xiang was not at all surprised that Lady Sea Snake knew this information.

This method has been used many times and has been completely exposed.

After a moment’s hesitation, Bei Xiang resolutely transmitted the order.

He was only an iron level, and he becoming the Applaud Pirates’ commander was because Lady Sea Snake was vigorously supporting him. To stabilize his position, he had to win this first battle, otherwise he’d be in big trouble.

The Applause battle squad quickly assembled on deck, under the main sail.

Battle skill – Applauding Love!

The frogmen all used the same battle skill, with one after the other lifting their frog palms and clapping them together.

The battle skill’s might smoothly added on each other, forming sound waves – clap, clap, clap.

The sound waves pounded against the main sail.

The main sail suddenly swelled, forming a full sail, accelerating the entire Love Triangle!

The Love Triangle had been matching its fleet’s speed, but now with its speed shooting up, it instantly broke from it like a launched arrow and charged towards the Iron Lump.

Cang Xu knew about the Lovely Applause battle skill too and coldly snorted, “The Applaud Pirates are using their best method and think that our destruction is certain.”

The Iron Lump found it difficult to deal with the tumultuous Love Triangle by itself.

Qing Xin said, “Hu Li hasn’t appeared, quite strange. According to intelligence, he is not an assassin type fighter, it is possible that internal problems have appeared in the Applaud Pirates.”

If Hu Li was present, he would have stormed the ship by himself by now.

To some degree, Hu Li was a greater threat than Dian Wan.

Because Dian Wan could only walk across the water, and he had to run across the ocean to get close to the Iron Lump. While Hu Li could use his battle squad and special battle skills to jump through the air.

From this point of view, one could regard Hu Li as a silver level with a flying skill!

In the battle for Two Eyes Island, Hu Li displayed his battle talent.

He was indeed powerful and had suppressed both Qing Xin and Men Shi for a long time. If the dragonman youngster hadn’t helped them in time, Hu Li would have been completely victorious.

However, from the beginning of the Weeping Pine Naval Battle till now, Hu Li had never appeared.

Despite not knowing about the pirate trial, Cang Xu and Qing Xin could guess that something happened to Hu Li.

Since Hu Li had an accident, Cang Xu and Qing Xin had methods to deal with the Love Triangle.

After the two looked at each other, both flew into the air and decided to attack the Love Triangle first!

Cang Xu and Qing Xin both appeared to be silver level.

Seeing two silver level magicians attacking, Bei Xiang’s heart suddenly popped into his throat.

This was exactly what he had worried about.

But Bei Xiang didn’t retreat.

His situation compelled him; he had to gamble it all.

He was gambling on Lady Sea Snake, who had not appeared yet.

Lady Sea Snake didn’t disappoint Bei Xiang.

Somewhere in the ocean, she took out a scroll.

A moment later, she opened the scroll and poured magic power into it.

Wind path magic scroll – Windless Domain!

The magic took effect immediately, and from its center, its range stretched for several hundred meters where there wasn’t a trace of wind.

The Iron Lump’s sails went limp, and its speed suddenly dropped to rock bottom.

The Love Triangle’s speed also decreased suddenly, but due to the Lovely Applause battle skill, its main sail could still provide motive force.

After the magic scroll took effect, it turned to flowing ash that sprinkled through Lady Sea Snake’s tender fingers and diffused into the seawater.

Cang Xu and Qing Xin were shocked.

When Lady Sea Snake used the scroll, her camouflage failed, and her magic aura was very distinct.

Qing Xin cried out in alarm as she fell from the sky.

The Windless Domain had affected her magic and caused her flying skill to fail.

But Cang Xu didn’t save her, instead he was fully alert and allowed Qing Xin to fall into the ocean.

Then a moment later, Lady Sea Snake took out another scroll.

The scroll was torn apart, shooting out a rapid succession of water arrows.

The water arrows were aimed at Qing Xin.

Cang Xu’s eyes flashed, he took out a scroll and used it to put defensive magic onto Qing Xin.

Qing Xin had plentiful flying experience, and while falling, she immediately adjusted her body posture to maintain her balance as much as possible.

Seeing that the ocean was growing bigger in her field of view, she took out a flying scroll and tore it.

She stopped falling, regaining the ability to fly.

Then, Cang Xu’s defensive magic covered her.

At nearly the same time, three water arrows hit her in a row, flinging her into the air.

The magic protecting Qing Xin endured the three water arrows, turning transparent afterwards.

Chapter 461: Weeping Pine Naval Battle (8)

Qing Xin adjusted her posture, then immediately flew back up and zig zagged through the air in evasive flight.

Cang Xu did the same, flying through the air in an irregular fashion.

Both were sweating with cold fear.

They had just been ambushed by Lady Sea Snake’s magic, fortunately they responded correctly and had adequate magic scrolls, otherwise they would be far greater trouble!

Lady Sea Snake saw that her ambush was ineffective and couldn’t help but sigh to herself with deep regret.

She quickly adjusted her mentality and took out a piece of snake skin, then took out a glass full of blue liquid.

She began to chant, because she could speak normally in seawater.

As she chanted, a tenth of the blue liquid bubbled out and flowed into the snake skin.

The snake skin blossomed with a faint blue light.

Lady Sea Snake raised her left hand and threw the snake skin into the air.

The snake skin suddenly exploded to bits and turned into a serpentine stream of water that shot towards Cang Xu and Qing Xin.

Water path magic – Serpentine Water Arrow!

The water arrow ripped through the sky with great speed, and in a few breaths, it neared Qing Xin.

It was being controlled by Lady Sea Snake’s spirit and could lock onto things.

Despite Qing Xin dodging, she was still hit.

This time, the defensive magic collapsed and after slowing a bit, the serpentine water arrow penetrated Qing Xin’s left shoulder blade.

Qing Xin grunted as her left shoulder turned scarlet.

When Lady Sea Snake saw the serpentine water arrow work, she took out another snake skin and began to chant again.

Cang Xu appeared callous as he unleashed his spirit and controlled ghosts to find her.

But when the ghosts got near her, they instantly triggered many magic traps, some that blew up, and others that hindered the ghosts.

Lady Sea Snake seemed to have built a tight perimeter around her.

Cang Xu’s heart sank, and he knew within a short period, he would find it difficult to break through the perimeter and threaten Lady Sea Snake.

I don’t really know how this person managed to hide and covertly deploy so many things!” So far, Cang Xu still didn’t know it was Lady Sea Snake, only that there was some water path magician in the seawater somewhere.

It was this magician that was attacking them!

Since he couldn’t prevent the enemy from casting, Cang Xu and Qing Xin both used scrolls as if by prior agreement to give themselves a layer of magical protection.

But choosing to go on the defensive meant they were going passive.

Lady Sea Snake was in a one versus two, but she held a large initial advantage, allowing her to focus on attacking Cang Xu and Qing Xin, firmly pushing them into a disadvantageous position.

The second serpentine water arrow was still aimed at Qing Xin.

Qing Xin couldn’t dodge it, so she didn’t try.

If she had wind path flying magic, she could fly much faster and possibly evade it.

Although this ordinary flying spell worked in the Windless Domain spell, its flying speed was nothing to write home about.

The serpentine water arrow had remarkable might, and after being reduced to a tenth of its size upon drilling through and breaking the protection magic, it still bit into Qing Xin’s abdomen like a poisonous viper.

Qing Xin deceivably took out an artifact.

It was a square piece of white cloth, and it looked like plain cotton. Two long protrusions stuck out from both sides that looked like two unfurled wings.

The white cloth emitted a silver level aura.

Qing Xin put it on her left shoulder wound.

The strip of white cloth instantly took effect, producing a pure light that flowed with an exquisite pattern.

It was the Life Saving Pad!

After three breaths of the Life Saving Pad taking effect, Qing Xin’s left shoulder was restored to how it was before.

She also tore it down to stick it on her abdomen.

Soon after, her flesh was completely healed.

Lady Sea Snake clicked her tongue, feeling regret again. She had the better offense, and the other side lacked defense. Her great results had been undone by Qing Xin taking out the Life Saving Pad, its formidable healing making up for her shortcomings in defense.

Lady Sea Snake’s heart moved, she instantly changed her methods and cast an acid spell.

Spell fusion!

Acid magic and the Serpentine Water Arrow spell fused together, forming a serpentine acid arrow.

By doing this, the magic came with corrosion effects and would counteract Qing Xin’s dominance in healing.

Lady Sea Snake had obviously come prepared and had deep casting abilities.

Cang Xu and Qing Xin had been firmly suppressed by her, and their situation was getting worse all the time.

They had planned to deal with Bei Xiang and stop the Love Triangle’s approach, but now they had their hands full and weren’t able to look after the Iron Lump.

Cang Xu strived to persevere.

He was only an iron level and lacked combat strength, and if Lady Sea Snake attacked him, he would be in an even worse situation than Qing Xin.

However, Lady Sea Snake had been deceived by him.

To her, it seemed that it would be better to deal with the conventional wind path magician before tackling a necromancer of the same level.

“I should have sold off a silver level ghost.” Cang Xu felt very remorseful, his soul was heavily burdened, causing his spirit power to be at a low point, causing his casting speed and success rate to be low.

But it was too late now.

Getting rid of a silver level ghost was not as simple as throwing it away. Cang Xu needed to undo his spiritual connection and needed at least half an hour and couldn’t be disturbed during the process.

Then at this moment, Cang Xu’s communication device rang.

After Cang Xu connected, Jian Gui transmitted, “Return to Weeping Pine Island.”

Cang Xu’s mind shook fiercely!

Cang Xu glanced at the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ fleet and noticed that they were already here.

He used the communication device to respond to Jian Gui, “Understood, Lord Jian Gui!”

Cang Xu called out to Qing Xin and the two quickly flew towards Weeping Pine Island.

Lady Sea Snake’s eyebrows deeply creased.

Of course, she wouldn’t sit there and stare blankly as Cang Xu and Qing Xin flew off, thus, she was forced to leave her tight defensive line and pursue them.

However, she still hid in the seawater, so people could only sense her aura and approximate position.

Cang Xu and Qing Xin originally thought of protecting the Iron Lump, but Lady Sea Snake had forced them to flee to Weeping Pine Island.

Qing Xin was nervous and fretful, while Cang Xu was pleasantly surprised.

Jian Gui had decided to contact him and tell him to fly back to Weeping Pine Island, his willingness to help him was clear.

Cang Xu had been anxious that perhaps Jian Gui wanted to attack the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ fleet.

“That water path magician is in pursuit, very good!”

“In light of the developing situation, I only need to serve as bait and lure them to Weeping Pine Island, then have Jian Gui deal with them.”

“If neither side wins, then we’ll be the one who gets everything, that would be the best outcome.”

Cang Xu was only thinking about it.

The water path magician that ambushed them was only silver level, and to him, it should be the pirate alliance’s secret weapon.

How could the silver level water path magician be the gold level Jian Gui’s match?

Cang Xu fleeing took away Lady Sea Snake, but not the Love Triangle.

The Love Triangle didn’t lack motive power and easily approached, then anchored next to the Iron Lump.

The humans and dwarfs aboard the Iron Lump were panic-stricken, they fired off some cannons inaccurately, provoking laughter from the pirates on the Love Triangle.

At this moment, Bei Xiang and the others heard the dragonman youngster shout, “Applaud Pirates!”

“I’ll give you one chance to run away.”

“The one you want to loot has my comrades on it; I won’t allow you to touch them!”

“If you run away now, then I Long Fu will use my reputation to guarantee that I will let you leave this naval battle alive!”

The dragonman youngster had used a sound amplification device to warn the Love Triangle.

Bei Xiang and the others all became discolored.

“Captain, what should we do?” The frogmen pirates were universally terrified.

Beig Xiang was both angry and hateful, “Rob them! Are you telling me you’re afraid?”

He couldn’t cower now.

He had just become commander, and if he shrank back now, it would damage his reputation too much. How could his underlings look at him as their new commander?

Bei Xiang knew he had to stabilize his position as commander, he couldn’t look cowardly, be timid of battle, or have other such things associated with his reputation.

After all, the Love Triangle was much closer to the Iron Lump than the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ fleet.

They could quickly come next to the Iron Lump, then with the frogmen’s strong jumping ability, many frogmen didn’t need to set up embarkment planks and could just jump onto the Iron Lump and start fighting.

The Iron Lump’s humans and dwarfs were already on full alert.

Both sides began to fight each other fiercely.

Because Cang Xu and Qing Xin had released them earlier, and had mobilized them, the humans and dwarfs had high morale.

Also, the main force and defense on deck was not them, rather it was the swordsmen guards Cang Xu had intentionally left behind.

These alchemy puppets were scattered across the deck and became the cornerstone of the defensive lines.

With these alchemy puppets’ support, they locked the frogmen pirates in battle.

This battle continued for a few minutes.

The humans and dwarfs lost the first deck level, for in the end, they were no match for the frogmen’s offensive and were forced to retreat to and defend the middle deck level.

Bang.

With an explosion, the dragonman youngster jumped into the sky, then with a brazen drop, directly landed on the Love Triangle!

While Zong Ge and Men Shi led the Quick Knife battle squad and the other mercenaries onto the Iron Lump.

This was a wise plan.

Because the dragonman youngster used explosives in his attacks and was liable to easily cause accidental injuries to teammates. His most suitable move was to be a one man army that could rampage through the enemy’s base.

Battle skill – Surge.

Battle skill – Demolition Fist.

The dragonman youngster looked like a crimson grim reaper with every explosion, causing frogmen flesh fly apart and the Love Triangle to erupt with groans.

Bei Xiang had no choice but to bite his teeth and stand against the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster threw him into the air with two consecutive fists.

“Where is Hu Li? Get him for me!” The dragonman youngster roared with astonishing grandeur.

On the Iron Lump.

Zong Ge wielded his two handed broadsword, but he didn’t charge ahead, instead he stood next to the Quick Knife battle squad to protect them.

“Throw!” Zong Ge loudly ordered.

Leader Kuai Tui and the dogmen all threw their swords.

The swords flew through the air in an arc, cutting a number of frogmen heads off then flying back to their master’s hand.

The bronze level weapons – the ring swords.

Battle skill – Throwing Knife.

With the two of them paired together, they had remarkable power!

There used to be frogmen pirates across the Iron Lump.

With one volley of the Throwing Knife battle skill, a wide expanse had been cleared, with only the frogmen in the corner barely able to avoid disaster.

Chapter 462: Weeping Pine Naval Battle (9)

The frogmen pirates were utterly shocked.

After reacting, they jumped off the Iron Lump, one after the other.

The dwarf Men Shi, holding his two-handed war hammer, moved with his short legs along the ship’s ladder, killing his way in.

The ship’s ladder was jam packed with frogmen pirates and their heads exploded upon being struck by Men Shi’s hammer, leaving a ghastly scene behind.

Men Shi was a silver level, and as he charged down, not a single frogman could stop him.

When the dwarfs and humans inside the ship heard Men Shi roar, they were overjoyed at this good news and counterattacked.

Under the pincer attack, the remaining frogmen pirates were a wrapped dumpling, and with nowhere to run, they were all slain.

When the dwarfs saw Men Shi, they surrounded him and shouted, “You’ve finally come to save us!”

Men Shi’s eyes were red, “So sorry that I gave you over to that necromancer.”

“Never mind that, you didn’t know the truth either.”

“You’ve already driven away the necromancer, right?”

“Speaking of which, that necromancer wasn’t that bad, we were treated better on this ship than we were as slaves.”

The dwarfs consoled Men Shi one by one.

Men Shi took a deep breath, “Everyone, I have joined the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps! Listen to me, it wasn’t a transaction. I wanted to join them.”

The dwarfs’ voices suddenly cut off, then they began to clamor again.

“Men Shi, you didn’t need to do so much for us!”

“You sold yourself off to save us? That is out of the question, we are free, you also need to be free!”

“Speaking of which, Commander Long Fu is pretty good, isn’t working for him pretty good too?”

The humans stood in a corner, awkwardly watching the joyous hubbub of dwarfs.

Since Qing Xin had exposed her identity and her strength, she had become the leader of these humans. But now that their leader had followed the fleeing necromancer, how that affected them was somewhat unclear.

The dwarfs conversed for a bit, then escorted Men Shi back up to the top deck.

All of the alchemy puppets had fallen down.

Some had been knocked down by frogmen pirates, others had been surreptitiously controlled by Zi Di who caused them to fall into the water.

“Where is Commander Long Fu? We must talk to him!”

“That’s right, he must explain clearly what is going on with Men Shi.”

Men Shi shouted, “I speak to you my people, I really joined them honestly. You must know when to stop, please don’t embarrass Lord Long Fu.”

The dragonman youngster was embroiled in combat right now, he was wreaking havoc across the Love Triangle, leaving a complete mess wherever he went, causing endless grievances for the frogmen pirates.

The dwarfs witnessed the dragonman youngster’s dashing figure in battle, and some began to swallow their saliva.

“So bold and powerful!”

“So much stronger than Men Shi!”

“Be a bit polite during negotiations, he is indeed our life saving benefactor.”

“Ok, we are absolutely not scared, after all he saved our lives.”

The dwarfs softly said to each other.

After being tossed into the ocean by the dragonman youngster, Bei Xiang hadn’t floated back up, let alone climbed back onto the ship to keep on fighting.

“He is too strong, not even Hu Li is his opponent, do I want to throw away my life?”

“Damn you Long Fu, damn you Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps!”

“Damn you Lady Sea Snake, you are simply unreliable!!”

Bei Xiang deeply felt hatred and powerlessness as he dived deep and headed towards the main fleet.

He had no choice but to abandon his flagship.

Meanwhile on the flagship, the frogmen no longer had a leader, nor any transcendent on par with the dragonman youngster, their morale quickly fell to rock bottom as they died and fled.

“Good, the Love Triangle is ours.” The dragonman youngster was covered in frogman blood as he walked to the center of the first deck level.

The ship had already been purged.

The flagship had not a single living frogman on it.

The dragonman youngster could confirm this!

Because Zi Di on the Deep Sea Monster Fish helped the dragonman youngster investigate this.

The Love Triangle was iron level, and with it, the White Bandage was reduced to an auxiliary warship.

This was the impact of the strong.

The ability to defeat many with one when put to good use could provide much more value on the battlefield.

In transcendent battles, the most important factor for victory was the strength of the individual transcendent!

The Applaud Pirates could be said to have destroyed their own walls; if Hu Li was still alive, he could have obstructed the dragonman youngster for a time and given the flagship time and space to escape, it wouldn’t have fallen into enemy hands so suddenly.

When the dragonman youngster stepped on the Iron Lump’s deck, he was completely soaked in blood and the aura of death was steaming off him, and his red dragon scales still had some dregs of frogmen flesh and bones on it.

This had all been done by Demolition Fist, which had blown frogmen pirates to bits.

The dwarfs took a cold breath; they forced out a smile and greeted the dragonman youngster as he boarded the Iron Lump.

“My comrades, we meet each other again!” The dragonman youngster looked down at the dwarfs, showing a giant smile that conveyed his clear goodwill.

A dwarf was suddenly pushed forward by his peers, and he staggered in front of the dragonman youngster.

He looked up at the dragonman youngster.

The scorching sun was above the dragonman youngster, and his shadow covered the dwarf.

The dwarf suddenly felt a mountain range worth of pressure.

The dragonman youngster was somewhat puzzled, “What did you want to say to me?”

The dwarf shook his head, stammering, “No no no, oh, right right right, it’s about, it’s about Men Shi. We heard he joined your Lordship Long Fu’s mercenary corps. I wanted to ask if he did so at his own volition?”

The dragonman youngster burst into laughter, “Of course, I never forced him to do so.”

After saying that, the dragonman youngster looked at Men Shi in the crowd of dwarfs, “Men Shi, what say you?”

Men Shi frustratedly threw up his hands, “I have already said that, Lord Commander, but my clansmen didn’t want to believe it.”

The dragonman youngster raised his head and laughed again, “Your camaraderie moves me. Friends, don’t be so ill at ease, don’t be so nervous, we once fought together, we are comrades-in-arms!”

“I never forced restrictions on Men Shi’s freedom; it was he who wanted to join my corps.”

“I saw his character and abilities, so of course I warmly welcomed him in.”

“But…” Upon saying that, the dragonman youngster looked at Men Shi, his smile vanishing and his tone became serious, “I cannot rule out the possibility that Men Shi joined my corps to save you all.”

“Oh, Men Shi, I can now give you a new choice.”

“I’ll let you choose again; you can either join or leave the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.”

“Be at ease, your clansmen and you are free, I can accept a reward for saving you all.”

“Perhaps, you and your people living together will make you all happier.”

The dwarfs looked back at Men Shi one after the other.

Men Shi’s red eyes were now flowing with hot tears.

He suddenly kneeled on the deck, then growled to the dragonman youngster, “Lord, Lord Long Fu!”

“Please let me join the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, let me follow you closely, let me reciprocate your kindness!!”

“If I cannot repay your kindness, the latter half of my life will sink into pain and self-condemnation!”

“My clansmen, will you prevent me from paying my debt of gratitude?!”

The dwarfs went silent; they could all make out Men Shi’s true feelings.

But the dragonman youngster still shook his head, his expression becoming graver, “Men Shi, I have taken your every word to heart, I acknowledge and understand your intentions, and also wish to accept your reciprocation.”

“However, there are many ways of repaying a debt of gratitude, you don’t need to lose your liberty and follow me. You must know that my life is accompanied and followed by battle and blood, it is full of crisis.

Men Shi took a deep breath and hung his head towards the dragonman youngster, “Lord Long Fu, I will follow you, not just to repay your kindness.”

“Rather it was your heroic sentiments that touched me.”

“It is my honor to follow a transcendent like yourself!”

“Please let me be by your side, let me dedicate my worth as a fighter.”

After that was said, the dragonman youngster no longer hesitated, and he smiled once again.

He took a big step forward, splitting the crowd of dwarfs before him, and came to Men Shi.

He stooped down and lent an arm to Men Shi.

He clapped Men Shi’s shoulder fully and shouted to him, “Excellent, Men Shi! Since that is your way of thinking, then like before, I welcome you in!”

Then the dragonman youngster looked at the dwarfs around him, “So sorry, my comrades that I snatched away your clanmate Men Shi, he is now one of my people!”

The dwarfs laughed heartily, the atmosphere was no longer so tense.

They had seen that the dragonman youngster and Men Shi had a genuine friendship together.

Some dwarfs congratulated Men Shi, while others decided to bellow that they wanted to follow Men Shi’s decision and also join the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps too.

To the dwarfs those that wanted to join, the dragonman youngster naturally refused nobody, admitting them all.

Men Shi couldn’t help but sigh, he intended to stop them, not wishing to see his people brave danger, but like how his clansmen couldn’t stop him, he could only look on helplessly as he saw his clansmen join one after the other.

“These people are returned back to your care, Men Shi.” The dragonman youngster clapped Men Shi’s shoulder.

“Understood, Lord Commander!” Men Shi nodded, accepting the order at once.

And so, the dragonman youngster had completely subdued Men Shi and also gained a group of dwarfs too.

As this brief interlude was playing out, Cang Xu and Qing Xin arrived at Weeping Pine Island.

“Newcomer, quickly fly towards me.” Cang Xu’s communication device transmitted Xiao Wang Zi’s voice.

Cang Xu and Qing Xin glanced at each other, readily following good advice.

Densely packed skeleton archers came from the pine forest and began shooting their bone arrows.

Lady Sea Snake did not care about them at first, but soon after, she became grave.

Because mixed in with the skeleton archers were also a few skeleton magicians.

These skeleton magicians could only use one spell, but those spells were still magic and were more threatening than the bone arrows.

Lady Sea Snake was forced to activate a magic protection shield while simultaneously using another spell to destroy the skeletons in the forest.

The skeleton archers suffered disastrous casualties.

The skeleton magicians were shown more of Lady Sea Snake’s consideration and were priority targets for her Water Arrow spell.

Very quickly, the skeletons were nearly wiped out.

Xiao Wang Zi also felt a trace of distress.

All of his skeleton magicians were wiped out; they were so much rarer than skeleton archers!

Chapter 463: Weeping Pine Naval Battle (10)

Lady Sea Snake wasn’t looking too good.

She knew this kind of damage was not worthwhile, because she had to use her valuable magic power to destroy the many skeleton soldiers.

Her magic power was finite, and it was very easy to get more skeleton archers.

To maintain magic power reserves, Lady Sea Snake had no choice but to drink a magic power potion.

Lady Sea Snake continued to pursue.

This time, she was obstructed by magician Qi Ling.

The latter’s water ghost army appeared again and circled above Lady Sea Snake, launching their attacks.

Lady Sea Snake was unwilling to engage with them, choosing to fly up higher into the air while simultaneously using a spell to destroy many water ghosts.

The water ghosts were seriously reduced, and magician Qi Ling quickly had them retreat.

Lady Sea Snake was hovering in the air, overlooking the big Weeping Pine Island, and although she couldn’t see Cang Xu, her spirit had locked onto him.

Lady Sea Snake sneered and murmured, “You’re still thinking of hiding somewhere? Today, you will fall into my hands, don’t hold any illusions about it!”

After saying that, she first used an alchemy communication device to contact Yang Cong Tou and Bei Xiang, then ordered that they all make landfall on Weeping Pine Island to pursue and blockade Cang Xu and Qing Xin.

Soon after, she gently whistled and connected her spirit to her magic pet.

Whoosh.

With a gigantic wave, an enormous silhouette of a snake broke from the sea.

It was a monstrous snake that had a singular long swath of red hair from its neck down, it had wide snake scales, and the scales had a eye like pattern on them.

What amazed people the most was that its life aura was gold level!

It was a gold level sea snake.

Although Lady Sea Snake was only silver level, she had a gold level sea snake familiar.

That was also where her pirate nickname had come from.

The gold level sea snake held up its head and threw itself onto Weeping Pine Island.

It used its racial magic, and before Lady Sea Snake acted, it had completely memorized Cang Xu’s aura and locked onto him with its spirit. Then Lady Sea Snake helped conceal its aura from leaking as it used its racial magic.

After the gold sea snake cast successfully, its master, Lady Sea Snake, used her spiritual connection to ascertain Cang Xu’s position.

The sea snake went ashore and scuttled into the lush pine forest.

The tallest pine trees were only a head taller than the sea snake.

The sea snake only came into full view when it came ashore. Xiao Wang Zi couldn’t help but take in a cold breath, “What a long snake, it must be at least 15 meters! So much stronger than my generalissimo.”

Xiao Wang Zi suddenly felt that his own three headed undead sea serpent was not so appealing.

“Move now!” Ku Feng grabbed his neck and quickly retreated again.

“Generalissimo, you’re too loud, go another way.” Xiao Wang Zi was cautious, after all Lady Sea Snake’s magic pet was gold level.

Magician Qi Ling had withdrawn earlier and had been silent for a long while.

Cang Xu and Qing Xin rapidly traveled through the forest, pine trees swiftly streaking across their vision.

The two magicians were flying close together.

But they were in different situations.

Qing Xin had abundant flying skills and experience, she didn’t touch the ground, and her body nimbly passed through the trees like a bluebird.

Cang Xu’s flying skill was obviously inferior to Qing Xin’s. Therefore, his flying skills couldn’t pass through the many obstacles precariously perched throughout the forest.

As more time passed, he had to use his hands and feet to step across tree trunks and break through branches to get through.

But his speed was about the same as Qing Xin’s.

Because he had transformed his body into a living dead person, although Cang Xu didn’t have much physical strength, he had great endurance; furthermore, he had remained calm, and every bit of strength was used meticulously.

The two were already going at top speed, despite this, the powerful enemy behind them was getting closer.

The gold level sea snake was enormous, and although it was on land, it was still very fast.

“Come this way!” As the distance between the two grew ever closer, and with Lady Sea Snake watching from above, Cang Xu’s communication device transmitted Jian Gui’s voice again.

Jian Gui guided Cang Xu and Qing Xin towards a new escape route.

Soon after, the two arrived at a small, sloping hill.

“You go left, I go right!” Cang Xu pushed against the gale to say to Qing Xin.

Qing Xin nodded.

(possible typo on zhenren)

Thus, Cang Xu went right, and Qing Xin went left, temporarily separating.

The gold level sea snake didn’t hesitate to follow Cang Xu tightly.

But as it wound around the hill, it was ambushed.

Two undead horsemen, saddled in skeleton warhorses and hands holding bone lances had been waiting for a long time, their aura restrained.

Then, they suddenly galloped and crashed into the sea snake.

The two undead horsemen were immediately smashed off their mounts, but the sea snake’s charge lost momentum as a result, and it watched helplessly as Cang Xu’s back quickly faded into the distance.

Under Jian Gui’s guidance, the two undead horsemen swiftly climbed back onto their warhorses and attacked again.

The sea snake was forced to deal with them, and filled with anger, it unleashed a huge offensive and began to win heavily.

Although the two ambushing undead horsemen were silver level, they didn’t have Ku Feng’s intelligence.

They moved stiffly and could only fight rigidly. This immediately gave the gold sea snake the overwhelming advantage.

Soon after, the two undead horsemen, their steeds, and their lances all had distinct cracks, they would soon fracture and break.

When Jian Gui saw this, he deployed three more undead horsemen as reinforcements.

One silver level, two iron levels.

With these new reinforcements, the sea snake was once more besieged.

When Lady Sea Snake flying above saw this from her vantage point, she couldn’t help but narrow her eyes.

Five undead horsemen strived to encircle the gold level sea snake, causing the latter to throw its body about, throwing dirt and rocks into the air, and swiftly devastating the nearby pine trees.

Within a half-minute, the iron level undead horsemen were all slain, turned to two piles of crushed bone fragments.

The three remaining silver level horsemen were in imminent danger.

Jian Gui promptly withdrew them, these silver level horsemen were valuable treasures he as a gold level necromancer was unwilling to easily lose.

Without these hindrances, the gold sea snake continued to chase down Cang Xu.

It firmly remembered Cang Xu’s aura, and as long as they were within a kilometer of each other, the sea snake could easily find Cang Xu.

It was no simple sense of smell, rather it was a type of racial magic.

It was worth mentioning that Cang Xu had Deceptive Disguise and the Anti-investigation and Prophecy spells on him.

But at this time, one could see the systemic problem with external casting. If one wasn’t the one to use a spell, the actual results would be of lesser value.

Only when Cang Xu used Deceptive Disguise again to change his current fake aura, would the sea snake lose track of him.

As for the Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell, it was mainly used to supplement the use of Deceptive Disguise.

The actual result of this was that when other people investigated the survivors’ fake identities, they wouldn’t sense anything peculiar, even if they took it a step further with prophecy, they would verify it as “true”.

If it was purely just the Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell, one would sense their investigation and prophecies fail, which was bound to trigger alerts and suspicion, and thus even more investigations.

Lady Sea Snake high in the air, followed Cang Xu through the spiritual connection to her sea snake, never losing her target.

A moment later, ghostly figures suddenly appeared high in the air, quite close to her!

Five silver level ghosts threw themselves at Lady Sea Snake.

Lady Sea Snake went stiff, then turned her body into a blue liquid that fell and dispersed from the air.

Jian Gui couldn’t help but express his surprise.

The Lady Sea Snake in the air was actually a false water path mirage!

“When did she disguise herself?” Jian Gui’s brows lightly creased as he pondered.

Jian Gui inspected his memory, not noticing any flaws.

“It is very possible that before she approached Weeping Pine Island that she hid herself somewhere secretly.”

“Interesting.”

Jian Gui was somewhat flabbergasted for a moment, then calmed back down.

Lady Sea Snake was a magician, and this kind of tactic was very normal, or in other words, this was the difference between a smart magician and an impulsive fighter or a faith blinded priest!

Since Lady Sea Snake was hiding somewhere, Jian Gui didn’t look into it. It was too troublesome, and not only would it cost time and magic power, when he was using detection magic, he might expose himself first.

Therefore, that was why Jian Gui had hidden ghosts in the air and deployed undead horsemen to besiege the gold sea snake.

Lady Sea Snake had already seen her mirage die tragically and would never show her true self.

She also couldn’t ignore her magic pet’s predicament and immediately used a communication device to give an order to the pirates, “Pursue the target, I have already pinned down their main force.”

Lady Sea Snake had summoned three pirate groups, and currently, the Lightning Pirates had withdrawn, leaving the Applaud Pirates and the Stench Pirates.

Bei Xiang received Lady Sea Snake’s message and was very hesitant, “I am only an iron level, my pirate group doesn’t have a silver level, how can I hunt down two silver level magicians?”

The Love Triangle had already been seized by the dragonman youngster, and after facing the dragonman youngster, Bei Xiang was terrified.

He had converged back with the Applaud Pirates’ main force, and boarded an auxiliary warship, an awkward situation.

Lady Sea Snake coldly snorted, shedding all pretenses of cordiality for tough words, “Bei Xiang, you will obey! You will gather your entire force and storm Weeping Pine Island! Otherwise, I will kill you.”

“In fact, even if I don’t kill you, do you really think you’ll have a good ending to this?”

“If this battle is lost, how will your crew look upon you, will you too be brought to a pirate trial?”

When Bei Xiang heard this, he was both scared and furious; his back also chilled.

He relied on Lady Sea Snake’s help, plotted against Hu Li, conducted a pirate trial, all to take the position of commander.

If he lost this battle, then it would likely be his last battle.

Never underestimate the savageness of pirates.

They not only treated their prey and enemies savagely, but also their own.

After waiting for Bei Xiang’s response for a long while, Lady Sea Snake added, “Don’t have any fantasies.”

“Even if your crew is willing to let you off, and give you another chance, will I?”

“If I can help you, then I can naturally help another.”

“Standing from your point of view, you might as well take advantage of your remaining authority, your crew won’t dare to revolt against you on the battlefield, and will be quite obedient for the time being, let them throw away their lives!”

“If this battle is won, then everyone will be delighted, you will be regarded by your underlings as one who dares to fight and dares to be unyielding to risk. But if you lose, do you want more or less crew that’ll make things deliberately difficult for you?”

Chapter 464: Weeping Pine Naval Battle (11)

“Vicious woman…” Bei Xiang was dismal.

Lady Sea Snake had stated her plan; she wanted to use the Applaud Pirates’ frogmen as cannon fodder!

But Lady Sea Snake would destroy Bei Xiang, and after thinking about it, he had no choice but to submit.

When meeting with Yang Cong Tou, Lady Sea Snake changed her tone to a more moderate one, “Commander Yang Cong Tou, it is now the best opportunity to attack!”

“You want the Sun Halo; it must be on the target.”

“Will you give up, are you willing to?”

“The other side’s main force has been probed by my magic pet, and the target fleeing reveals they are weak.”

“With a bit more effort now, we can win!”

Yang Cong Tou hesitated.

On the one hand, he feared the undead troops that poured in like flies, on the other hand he really was unwilling to give up on the Sun Halo.

He wanted to attack the Iron Lump.

But the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was a step ahead and had done so already.

Yang Cong Tou had seen Long Fu and Shi Qi fight and didn’t want to run into such strong individuals.

Lady Sea Snake acutely sensed Yang Cong Tou’s hesitation, she increased her offer at once, promising that if they won this battle, she would pay Yang Cong Tou a handsome reward and also give Yang Cong Tou a chance to join the Fire Beard Pirates.

When Yang Cong Tou heard what Lady Sea Snake said, he led his underlings onto shore behind the frogmen.

Of course, he also left something behind.

He arranged for his flagship Stinky Tofu to lie at anchor and not come close to shore, so that if something unexpected happened, he could quickly climb aboard and escape the battlefield.

The White Bandage.

“Commander, the Iron Lump can sail, what will we do next?” Men Shi followed behind the dragonman youngster, his fighting spirit high.

His clansmen had been rescued, and his heart’s burdens had been relinquished, now his heart was set on dedicating his strength to the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster saw Cang Xu go to the island, the gold sea snake fighting fiercely, as well as the pirates moving in too, and muttered to himself, “We will retreat first.”

Although he was very worried about Cang Xu, Lady Sea Snake was hiding somewhere, and although Jian Gui had acted, her true body hadn’t been revealed.

Nothing had reached the final juncture just yet.

Therefore, the dragonman youngster ordered that they withdraw so both sides could fight without worry.

But the survivors still had some control over the battlefield.

Because Zi Di was sailing the Deep Sea Monster Fish, still hidden on the seafloor and could act at any time.

Of course, if they could avoid using the Deep Sea Monster Fish, they would.

If it was exposed, the survivors would have to deal with many follow-up troubles.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ fleet slowly withdrew from the battlefield.

Almost all of the pirates had come onto Weeping Pine Island.

They moved around the gold sea snake and the undead and charged towards where Cang Xu and Qing Xin were.

Qing Xin seemed to sink like water, “We must have a magic mark on us for them to find where were are hiding.”

“Their target should be me.” Cang Xu thought about the time they had gone around that hill, how the gold sea snake chased after him and not Qing Xin.

This action had revealed many things.

Qing Xin deeply sighed, “The Iron Lump has fallen into the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ hands, some luck in misfortune.”

Just as she said that they heard a sharp warbling coming from above them.

Cang Xu and Qing Xin unconsciously looked up and followed the noise, seeing an artillery shell shooting from some distant place.

The metal artillery shell had steel wings on both sides, and it was the steel wings vibrating in the air that produced that warbling noise.

After it cut through the sky in a full parabola, it landed onto Weeping Pine Island.

A second later.

Bang!

With a loud sound, the earth and mountains shook.

A six meter tall white mushroom cloud slowly rose.

Mountainous rocks splashed.

“Not good, that place is?!” Qing Xin and Cang Xu both changed color at the same time; they no longer cared about escaping as they quickly rose into the air to survey the impact zone.

What they saw made both of them ashen.

The artillery shell had bombarded the hidden cave where they had gathered.

“A gold level artillery shell! Why did it so precisely hit the meeting cave?” Cang Xu’s eyes coldly flashed, he felt things were going south and that the sudden and startling change to the war situation had broken it away from Cang Xu’s control.

From where the artillery shell shot from, a fleet gradually appeared.

The lead flagship was a tower ship that was being pulled by two gigantic sea snakes.

Around the three story high ship was a metal cannon that twisted like a python.

The metal cannon wound around the center of the ship at least three times, and the cannon mouth at the peak created the bloody wide mouth of a python.

“That is Lady Sea Snake’s fleet!” Qing Xin’s voice was ice-cold.

Lady Sea Snake was one of the Fire Beard Pirates’ captains, thus, she naturally had her own flagship and fleet.

“Our support is finally here! Accelerate the pace, overtake the enemy, advance and attack!” Yang Cang Tou precisely grabbed hold of this chance to encourage morale.

Bei Xiang also let out a breath, and the frogmen’s morale climbed back up.

In the Deep Sea Monster Fish, Zi Di was closely following these pirate reinforcements, and was feeding this information to the dragonman youngster and the others, “Lady Sea Snake’s fleet has 9 ships, and the flagship is an iron level flower snake tower ship. The ship has one modified curved anti-aerial gun. The shell it just shot was a Baby Bird Mushroom Cloud shell; its explosion power is extremely high!”

“What should we do now?” Qing Xin looked at Cang Xu, somewhat helplessly.

To her, the only chance to escape was the Eulogy Tombstone. But that teleportation device couldn’t have survived that sort of explosion.

Cang Xu pondered, then picked up the communication device and was about to ask the other necromancers for help.

At that time, Xiao Wang Zi transmitted, “Never mind that, newcomer. Weeping Pine Island doesn’t just have one Eulogy Tombstone, there is another.”

“Hehe, I also built the second Eulogy Tombstone just for emergencies such as this.”

“I will give you directions and join you at the tombstone.”

Qing Xin and Cang Xu were both shaken.

But at that time, another ear-piercing warble screeched through the sky.

The second Baby Bird Mushroom Cloud shell shot into the distance and hit a small hill on the northwest corner of Weeping Pine Island.

Bang,

Another intense explosion occurred.

But because it was further from Cang Xu and Qing Xin, they didn’t feel the ground vibrating.

The two saw another white mushroom cloud rising from the hill, then soon after, the winds the explosion created swept across the pine forest.

Xiao Wang Zi’s voice in the communication device paused, then was suddenly jammed in the wake of the shell.

Cang Xu thought to himself, feeling things were anything but reassuring, then asked, “I say, the second tombstone you mentioned, was it on that hill?”

Xiao Wang Zi was incredulous, “Yes…yes. But how is that possible?!”

Yes, both shells had hit the Eulogy Tombstones with unparalleled accuracy.

What did this mean?

Qing Xin’s scalp tingled, and she growled, “A traitor!”

Xiao Wang Zi’s yelling also came through, “Newcomer, you twisted our trust in you! I didn’t expect you to be a traitor.”

Cang Xu shook his head, denying it, “It isn’t me, I didn’t know where the second tombstone was, how could I be the traitor?”

Just as he said that Qing Xin loudly warned, “Be careful, the enemy is attacking!”

A large group of water ghosts quietly appeared.

“Damn it!” Cang Xu rushed to battle while simultaneously saying to Xiao Wang Zi, “Water ghosts! Magician Qi Ling is attacking us!”

Xiao Wang Zi was dumbfounded.

Behind him, Ku Feng was grave, “I am not the traitor, nor are you. Qi Ling is the most suspicious!”

Xiao Wang Zi unconsciously refuted, “No, perhaps the newcomer is deceiving us.”

Compared to a newcomer like Cang Xu, Xiao Wang Zi naturally trusted Qi Ling more.

But Ku Feng shook his head, “The newcomer is more trustworthy! Because his situation is very easy to verify. If he needed to lie, he wouldn’t sprinkle lies like this.”

Xiao Wang Zi was stunned, but soon after, he used his communication device to contact Qi Ling and question her.

Magician Qi Ling was silent at first, then began to talk, “So sorry everyone. I really couldn’t help it. My people were taken hostage, if I didn’t obey, they would have all been massacred by pirates.”

Magician Qi Ling’s tone was full of remorse, she actually admitted she was the traitor on the spot.

Xiao Wang Zi angrily tore open with curses, and Qi Ling didn’t answer back, keeping her silence after giving her words.

Ku Feng deeply sighed, “Don’t curse her, since she made her choice, we should look after ourselves now.”

Xiao Wang Zi’s fury was difficult to quell, “Why did she do this? She was summoned by Lord Jian Gui. It was Lord Jian Gui who gave her a second life, who gave her freedom! How could she do this? She actually betrayed Lord Jian Gui, betrayed us!”

Upon saying that, Xiao Wang Zi recalled something, then rushed to contact Jian Gui with the communication device.

Jian Gui’s voice soon came through, “I heard what Qi Ling said.”

“I understand her, this is something she can do.”

“Xiao Wang Zi, remember that from now on, Qi Ling is our enemy.”

“Furthermore, you guys take care and think of a way to get out.”

“Don’t look for me!”

“Where I am…is far more dangerous.”

Jian Gui was hiding in a cave.

After saying that, he turned off the communication device.

The cave was dark, and Jian Gui’s aura was also dwindling to nothing.

The surrounding detection methods around him hadn’t triggered, but Jian Gui was feeling ever worse.

When his attack against Lady Sea Snake failed, attacking a mere water path clone, he had a premonition.

Now with two gold level artillery shells precisely destroying Weeping Pine Island’s Eulogy Tombstones and Magician Qi Ling simultaneously revealed to be a traitor, it proved that the other side was far more prepared than previously envisioned.

“Information leaking that undead gather here repeatedly is not at all strange.”

“Since the other side has forced Qi Ling to turn traitor, I will inevitably become a target.”

“It’s just that for now, there isn’t a method able to find me yet.”

“Has it not been used yet, or has it already…been used?”

Then abruptly, Jian Gui’s eyes shot out an ominous glint.

Necromancy spell – Soul Scream!

His body floated up and he raised his head to screech.

A tempestuous soul fluctuation, with him as its center, quickly spread out in all directions.

Shadows undulated, and someone was forced to reveal themselves, their brief outline revealing a sorry figure.

Chapter 465: Weeping Pine Naval Battle (12)

“Tsk, just a bit more.” The revealed person was not tall, their stature was slim, they were wearing a night gown, and their face was also covered with a layer of black cloth.

When Jian Gui saw him, his heart suddenly trembled.

The mysterious enemy he had forced out, with some unknown method, had managed to sneak so close to him without him noticing anything!

Perhaps due to his strong promotion or just being safe, he had indiscriminately used an offensive spell to force the other person to appear.

The gold level fighter’s aura also caused Jian Gui great fear.

Without any hesitation, Jian Gui crashed into the top of the cave.

Necromancy – Soul Flying spell!

With this magic, Jian Gui, along with his equipment, turned into ghosts and drilled their way through the cave.

After leaving the cave, Jian Gui didn’t stop and directly flew into the air.

This was a good choice.

Although gold level fighters might have flying skills, it was comparatively rare. Not every gold fighter had a flying skill.

Lady Sea Snake was one on the battlefield that could also threaten Jian Gui in the air.

The magician was only silver level and wasn’t that threatening.

Jian Gui quickly soared to the sky and quickly climbed to 9 meters from the ground.

There were no traces of the mysterious black gowned man he had forced out.

“What’s going on?” Jian Gui grew increasingly vigilant.

A moment later, the space behind Jian Gui fluctuated as the black clothed man instantly appeared!

The black clothed man held a dagger that was ruthlessly jabbing towards Jian Gui’s lower back.

Jian Gui was caught unprepared, and his ghostly transformed body was damaged by the dagger.

He didn’t turn around, or even turn his head, instead he swung his arms backwards and tore apart a necromancy scroll.

An ash-colored soul wave in the shape of a cone exploded from the scroll.

But the mysterious black clothed man vanished again.

Jian Gui flew to where he had come from, healing while using defensive magic.

The black clothed man appeared again, once again behind him. His dagger was gold level and immediately shredded Jian Gui’s defensive magic and wounded him once more.

Jian Gui was attacked two more times, completely losing, and had no power to retaliate for a moment.

“The Shadow Plane!” Zi Di on the Deep Sea Monster Fish was watching the two gold levels fight.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish had a special module, the Space Lock (Base).

Once, the survivors, in Snake Rat Island’s underground caves, had used it to look for a teleportation formation and had spent a vast sum building it.

With its help, Zi Di could see clearly.

Every time the mysterious black clothed man appeared, it would trigger a spatial fluctuation. Every time his silhouette appeared, the spatial fluctuation would expose the vague image and aura of the Shadow Plane.

Zi Di immediately contacted Cang Xu, “Jian Gui is battling a gold level fighter, he is in grave danger.”

“I can see clearly that this gold level fighter is capable of traveling through the Shadow Plane, able to blink shift.”

“It’s up to you on what you’re going to do.”

When Cang Xu got this information, he and Qing Xin were watching the water ghosts besieging them slowly retreat.

“Qi Ling decided to withdraw?” Qing Xin was flabbergasted.

Soon after, Cang Xu and Qing Xin were safe again.

Cang Xu sighed, “Qi Ling didn’t have killing intent. Her attacking us and then conceding that she was the traitor could actually be regarded as a warning.”

“In fact, if she waited a bit longer, we and Xiao Wang Zi could have reached the second tombstone. Then, when the Baby Bird Mushroom Cloud shell was fired, we would have likely died from it.”

“The pirates threatened her with her clansmen, this ghost magician clearly knows who the real enemy is.”

Qing Xin’s eyebrow deeply wrinkled, “Even if Qi Ling didn’t deal with us, we are still in a terrible situation.”

“The pirates are already on the island; their target is us.”

“Jian Gui over there cannot be counted on, what should we do?”

Cang Xu took a deep breath and tried to calm down.

He tried to identify his current location and found he wasn’t that far from Jian Gui.

Cang Xu thought deeply for a bit, then decided to assist Jian Gui.

He wanted both sides to suffer; that would be the most advantageous outcome for the survivors.

But now, Jian Gui was absolutely losing, and if it continued, the black clothed fighter might kill him.

The survivors didn’t want this.

Of course, Cang Xu gave another pretext to Qing Xin, “The key person in this battle is Jian Gui. We must help him as much as possible, only with his strength can we repel the pirates. Otherwise, we won’t be able to escape this place by ourselves.”

“Follow me!” Cang Xu led Qing Xin, and after flying for a bit, they reached the top of the tallest pine tree.

Standing there, he could see Jian Gui in the air.

Jian Gui was flying erratically while the black clothed fighter’s figure was flashing constantly around Jian Gui.

Every time he appeared, he would injure Jian Gui.

Jian Gui had turned into a ghost, he hadn’t been fatally wounded and had plentiful reserves, and was able to heal in a timely fashion, allowing him to persist until now.

“Can we really help?” Qing Xin shook her head, looking pale, “That is too risky.”

Cang Xu replied, “We don’t need to forge ahead, we can stay here and give Jian Gui space, so if Lady Sea Snake or Qi Ling appear, we can stop them.”

“In addition…” As Cang Xu said that he used a communication device to contact Jian Gui, “Lord Jian Gui! That other person is using the Shadow Plane to be elusive. Do you have a Dimensional Anchor magic scroll?”

“So that’s what’s happening!” After Jian Gui was told, his eyes flashed.

However, he didn’t have a Dimensional Anchor magic scroll and was unable to seal off local space.

“That doesn’t matter, I have another way!” Jian Gui flew towards the coastline.
