The mysterious black clothed person chased after him, and although they didn’t know what Jian Gui was doing, they still used everything they had to stop him.

By the time Jian Gui reached the sea surface, his injuries had at least doubled.

He reached a hand under the water and called, “Come, my warship!”

A moment later, the ocean surface bubbled up and waves rapidly broke into a whirlpool.

A dozen seconds later, the whirlpool expanded to a radius of a hundred meters.

From the whirlpool came a derelict ship.

This ship emitted strong negative energy, its verdigris hull was utterly decayed, and its sails were ragged.

A great number of undead lifeforms were dithering across the ship.

It was an astonishing silver level ghost ship!

Jian Gui landed on the ghost ship, with the mysterious black clothed person right behind him.

A moment later, Jian Gui’s entire being merged into the ghost ship and completely vanished.

The mysterious black clothed man blankly stared, he sensed that Jian Gui had become the ghost ship under his feet!

Necromancy spell – Ghost World Traversal.

A moment later, a large quantity of strange fog appeared around the ghost ship.

The black clothed person’s face changed, because from the fog, he sensed the aura of the Ghost World!

The fog quickly concentrated into a pitch-dark color.

The ghost ship under the black clothed person seemed to gradually merge with the fog as it faded from reality into nothing, vanishing from where it stood.

“Cang Xu (Xu Jia), many thanks for your warning.” Jian Gui suddenly transmitted.

But this time, he wasn’t using a communication device.

Cang Xu and Qing Xin quickly turned around to look at the source of the voice, then saw the ghost ship sailing across a river through the baseless and thick fog.

The ghost ship continued to transmit Jian Gui’s voice, “Don’t resist, I will pull you onto the ship. The other side has come prepared; you cannot stay here.”

After that was said, Cang Xu and Qing Xin felt a powerful force sucking them in.

After a moment’s hesitation, Cang Xu didn’t resist it and allowed Jian Gui to pull him onto the ghost ship’s deck.

Qing Xin immediately did the same.

After the two boarded, Jian Gui pulled in Xiao Wang Zi and Ku Feng too.

Jian Gui had clearly informed them ahead of time and they were more prepared than Cang Xu and Qing Xin for this.

Xiao Wang Zi once again sat on the back of his “generalissimo”.

Most of the skeletons he had recovered also stood back up too.

After picking them up, Jian Gui didn’t stay any longer and sailed the ghost ship away from Weeping Pine Island.

The heavy curtain of ending fell over the Weeping Pine Naval Battle, which involved the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, the pirate alliance, and the necromancers, when Jian Gui decided to retreat.

“Lord Wei Ying, the target?” Lady Sea Snake used a communication device to contact the mysterious black clothed person.

The black clothed person, Wei Ying, was indifferent, “Nothing could be done, the other side used the Ghost World Traversal spell, using the Ghost World to escape. If I used the Shadow Plane to break into the Ghost World, I would be entering Jian Gui’s trap.”

“The ghost ship can drift through the Underworld River, but I am a living person, if I go into the Underworld River, I will die!”

Lady Sea Snake looked embarrassed, “But if that happened, then we lost our target, and this operation has failed!”

Wei Ying slowly shook his head, “Letting them escape really is a failure. But not a complete one, my battle skill will continue for at least a week. I don’t believe Jian Gui can maintain Ghost World Traversal forever, as long as he anchors to the Main World, he will leave behind traces that we can track down.”

Elsewhere, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

“What? You lost contact with Cang Xu?” The dragonman youngster was stunned.

Zi Di helplessly replied, “He was saved by Jian Gui, and the entire ghost ship entered the Ghost World and has completely vanished.”

The dragonman youngster’s heart filled with worry.

Zi Di then said, “Cang Xu left behind some stuff on the Iron Lump, Lord Commander, did you find them?”

The dragonman youngster nodded, “His gains from the undead gathering, some of it was stored in a ship cabin, along with his secret letter.”

“I have already read the letter.”

“In the letter, Can Xu listed a few possible situations. The current one is accounted for.”

“According to the letter, if mutual destruction is not achieved, Cang Xu will help Jian Gui. In that case, he will hide by Jian Gui’s side for a time, strive to gain his trust, and seek to obtain more information.”

“Afterwards, he will look for a chance to move alone, separate himself from Jian Gui, and rejoin us once again.”

Cang Xu proved himself a valued confidant of the dragonman youngster. He had planned many things in advance.

After all, many plans were things he couldn’t communicate to another in front of Qing Xin.

Chapter 466: Harlot

Weeping Pine Island.

“A ghost ship!” Fear coldly gripped Bei Xiang’s heart.

He had led the frogmen pirates into the pine forest to pursue Cang Xu and Qing Xin.

The dozen frogmen by his side also witnessed how the ghost ship appeared, and how Cang Xu and Qing Xin boarded it.

“Captain Bei Xiang, what do we do now?” An underling asked.

After completely losing their target, the frogmen pirates were somewhat at a loss.

“What more can we do? Return to the ships!” Bei Xiang unconsciously answered, he didn’t want to stay on this ghostly island for a moment longer.

Another underling yelled, “Right, return to the ships. We lost our flagship; we have to take it back!”

“Yes, yes, the Love Triangle cannot be lost!”

“It is our only magic ship.”

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is too strong; we will just be throwing away our lives.”

“The Applaud battle squad has also been annihilated, that Long Fu is too vicious!”

The pirates began to argue and quarrel with each other.

Some wanted to take back their flagship, while others were cowering, but they didn’t say that, only stating how powerful the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was.

“You’re all thinking nonsense!” Bei Xiang couldn’t help but roll his eyes back.

He was terrified of the dragonman youngster, and when recalling his fight with the latter, he lost all will to fight.

Furthermore, the frogmen on the Love Triangle had been the most elite in the Applaud Pirates.

They were already dead!

The Applaud Pirates had already been crippled.

Wanting to take back one’s flagship like this was no difference from tossing their lives away.

“Stop quarreling and listen to our new captain.”

“Boss, what did you say to do?”

“Right, the flagship was stolen from your hands, you are responsible for taking it back!”

A portion of the frogmen pirates began to deride Bei Xiang.

Bei Xiang was tense, and sensed the undercurrents, these frogmen were discontented and began to use this method to rise in revolt against him.

On face of it, Bei Xiang remained calm and collected, “Wait until I promote to the silver level, I will bring you along to retake our flagship and wipe away the humiliation of today!”

“Right now, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps has already run away, and our ordinary ships cannot chase them down.”

The frogmen’s expressions changed.

Bei Xiang reminded them that he was a peak iron level, and with his excellent bloodline, he would definitely become a silver level in the future.

Some of the frogmen pirates secretly revolting against Bei Xiang realized this, and they began to waver.

But other frogmen didn’t give up, “We aren’t alone, we still have our allies.”

“As long as Boss Bei Xiang asks, Lady Sea Snake will surely help us again!”

Bei Xiang’s speech was deeply noted in the frogmen pirates’ hearts, he didn’t refute them, rather he insincerely promised, “Wait a minute, I will speak with Noble Lady Sea Snake and see what she thinks of that.”

“But before that, everyone, we cannot let this good chance for loot slip by.”

The frogmen stared at him.

Bei Xiang raised his voice and lifted his arms, “We are victorious!”

“Our enemy has fled.”

“All of Weeping Pine Island is ours.”

“I feel the necromancer stayed here for a while, there must be arrangements here, perhaps we can find a magician’s treasures.

The frogmen looked at each other in dismay.

Some frogmen disdained him on the sly, Bei Xiang’s face was too thick, he Applaud Pirates had suffered disastrous damage, yet he declared himself the winner.

However, Bei Xiang’s point really did make sense.

After all, Jian Gui, Cang Xu,, Xiao Wang Zi, and the others did indeed flee.

“Right, Boss is right, why didn’t I think of that?”

“Those necromancers have run off, why were they gathered here at this place?”

“That’s right, this must be their stronghold!”

“Can’t say for sure, but there might be a magician tower.”

“Move, gather now, we must snatch these things before the Stench Pirates!!”

The frogmen grew excited, and they shouted one after another restlessly.

Bei Xiang let out a big gulp of air.

He had no evidence of magician treasures, it was pure speculation, but it was fine as long as it shifted the frogmen pirates’ attention, even if only temporarily, he could consider it a great success!

Of course, he didn’t forget to contact Lady Sea Snake and ask her to help the Applaud Pirates take back their flagship.

As Bei Xiang predicted, Lady Sea Snake refused.

Lady Sea Snake just hung up on Bei Xiang when Yang Ccong Tou contacted her.

“Lady Commander Sea Snake, our target has escaped. You will give me an explanation!” Yang Cong Tou was in a bad mood.

His main purpose in coming was to seize the Sun Halo.

This alchemy component was very important to him.

But Cang Xu had escaped, and his purpose in coming was fruitless.

Lady Sea Snake coldly snorted and instead asked, “What do you want explained to you?”

Yang Cong Tou replied, “At least honor the promise you made to me before.”

Beforehand, to motivate the Stench Pirates, Lady Sea Snake had offered a high reward and gave the Stench Pirates a chance to join the Fire Beard Pirates.

Lady Sea Snake’s tone was ice-cold, “Commander Yang Cong Tou, you didn’t capture the target, don’t think about the money, there is no such charity in this world.”

“As for nominating you to join the Fire Beard Pirates, I will go do that, but truth be told, your pirate group still has some ways to go before it is strong enough to join us.”

When Yang Cong Tou heard this answer, he couldn’t help but clutch his communication device with an aching heart, “This harlot wants to renege on my debt!”

Lady Sea Snake was very insincere, she said she would, but who could verify that after this, she would really nominate his group?

Yang Cong Tou took a deep breath, then proposed, “Although the target is gone, the Iron Lump is still here. We can work together to take on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps!”

Cang Xu had withdrawn on the ghost ship, but Yang Cong Tou was still unwilling to give up.

Because the Sun Halo was massive and might have been left on the ship.

Lady Sea Snake smiled, “Just now, the Applaud Pirates also want to attack the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, asked me for help, I turned them down.”

“You two can work together instead to deal with this matter. I won’t be joining you.”

Yang Cong Tou coldly snorted, feeling it beneath his dignity, “Hu Li is dead, and the Applaud Pirates’ commander is a trifling iron level, what are they capable of?”

Lady Sea Snake laughed, “Cannon fodder has value too.”

Yang Cong Tou tried to persuade her, even offering a high price, but Lady Sea Snake’s refusal was very firm.

Finally, Yang Cong Tou angrily threw the communication device to the ground and cursed wantonly, “This damned bitch, she only wants to exploit me!”

“If I agree to work with you again next time, I will be a dog!!”

Lady Sea Snake had said she wanted to deal with Cang Xu, but she actually had a deeper purpose.

The sudden appearance of the black clothed person proved that Lady Sea Snake understood necromancers and had made arrangements for them.

Jian Gui had been caught in critical circumstances, and only after receiving Cang Xu’s warning was he able to break away from the black clothed person.

After getting through the Weeping Pine Naval Battle, Yang Cong Tou finally saw and realized that he and the other pirate groups had been mere tools used by Lady Sea Snake, greatly diminishing his opinion of her.

Lady Sea Snake didn’t care what Yang Cong Tou thought. After confirming she was safe, her true body flew out of the ocean and slowly landed on her flagship.

Soon after, the ghost magician Qi Ling also emerged in front of Lady Sea Snake.

Lady Sea Snake narrowed her eyes and coldly snorted, “Qi Ling, have you figured out a good explanation for me? You obviously had strength, yet you deliberately let the target go.”

Magician Qi Ling shook her head, “I have accomplished what our agreement stated.”

Lady Sea Snake sneered, “It seems that you don’t plan on saving your clan. Your clansmen are all within our grasp,”

Qi Ling’s face quivered, “It is because they are still in your hands that I let the target go. Now, do you know where they are? Hehe, I do!”

“Oh?” Lady Sea Snake’s expression minutely changed, and her eyes coldly flashed. She couldn’t help but extend her tongue and lick her scarlet lips, sizing up magician Qi Ling with full interest, “You do know.”

Qi Ling gave her conditions, “Let my people go, then I will tell you what you most want to know!”

Lady Sea Snake shook her head, “I cannot release them all, how will I know if your information is true or false? However, I can release 10 fishmen to indicate my good faith.”

“Just 10?” Qi Ling shook her head, her attitude firm, “That’s too few.”

Shortly after, the two magicians began to haggle over how many hostages would be released for the information.

After wasting a lot of time, the price of this transaction was finalized.

Qi Ling stated the position of an uninhabited island.

Lady Sea Snake immediately ordered that they would be sailing there!

Chapter 467: Sailing the Ghost World

Lady Sea Snake’s fleet officially departed.

After Lady Sea Snake had greatly exploited the Applaud, Stench, and Lightning Pirates, she had pitilessly tossed them to the back of her mind.

When she went to the highest level of her ship, and returned to her captain’s room alone, Wei Ying flashed to her.

“You’re letting that fishman ghost off?” Wei Ying questioned.

Lady Sea Snake giggled, “Lord Wei Ying, I am not a gold level fighter like you. I am only a silver level magician, having a necromancer of the same level as my slave is a treasure I must treat well.”

“Qi Ling, that necromancer, is really interesting. She has become an undead, yet she is unable to let go of her former clansmen.”

“With such great leverage in my hands, she won’t be able to turn things around.”

“She still has resistance in her heart, so I’ll play with her first and give her a bit of hope.”

“By the time she genuinely despairs, she’ll be willing to acknowledge allegiance to me.”

Wei Ying was silent for a moment, then nodded slightly, “She is your catch, and also your responsibility. Anyways, the coordinates she gave are real. According to our information, that place certainly is one of Jian Gui’s long-time bases.”

Lady Sea Snake cackled, “I also think that information is true.”

Lady Sea Snake unfolded a sea map and quickly found where the barren island was, “Lord Wei Ying, come look. This barren island is quite close to Weeping Pine Island and is part of the Eulogy Tombstone teleportation network.”

“I can surmise that Jian Gui used the Eulogy Tombstone hidden here to teleport to Weeping Pine Island.”

“Now the question is how many defenses Jian Gui has on this island? If we rush over, we won’t have any cannon fodder to use.”

Wei Ying shook his head, “This mission is very important, we have to capture the target alive! If this mission fails, you and I won’t have a good end.”

“What I’m more worried about is that Jian Gui is purposely avoiding this place. Qi Ling was exposed as a traitor, and this barren island is not secure. If it was me, I wouldn’t stay there.”

Lady Sea Snake muttered to herself, “I still believe that Jian Gui might make a stop here.”

“Lord Wei Ying, it seems your understanding of the Underworld River isn’t deep.”

“That river is very dangerous, and a mere silver level ghost ship cannot sail it consecutively.”

“When Jian Gui wants to leave the Ghost World and return to the Main World, he needs spatial coordinates. Every Eulogy Tombstone location is a stable spatial coordinate. If they are attacked by a powerful enemy in the Underworld River, they must spatial shift immediately. Under those circumstances, the closest spatial coordinate is that barren island.”

“I believe that it is both possible, and very likely.”

Wei Ying nodded, approving of Lady Sea Snake’s inference, “Then we’ll travel there.”

Lady Sea Snake’s fleet sailed off, leaving Yang Cong Tou’s and Bei Xiang’s respective pirate groups at odds and vigilant of each other.

They didn’t attack the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, mainly because they weren’t strong enough.

But the dragonman youngster didn’t care about all that.

He asked Zi Di again, “Can you really not find Cang Xu?”

Zi Di sighed, “I am powerless to do anything, the Deep Sea Monster Fish’s detection methods cannot find the ghost ship. Especially when the ghost ship is still in the Ghost World.”

“In fact, interplanar detection is always challenging.”

The dragonman youngster sighed, and after confirming it again, he was forced to drop the matter.

Zong Ge raised his own proposal, “If we decide to track him down, perhaps we can follow Lady Sea Snake’s fleet. Look at how they set out immediately, plus Qi Ling has boarded their ship, its very possible they have some crucial clues.”

Zong Ge had many ideas.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps couldn’t follow the ghost ship directly, but they could hang behind Lady Sea Snake’s fleet.

Although normally, Zong Ge and Cang Xu didn’t get along, Zong Ge was still trying his utmost for Cang Xu now.

This was the battle friendship that was fostered on Mystifying Monster Island.

“Following Lady Sea Snake…” The dragonman youngster hesitated.

Although he was worried about Cang Xu, as leader, he had to shoulder many other responsibilities.

Lady Sea Snake didn’t attack us, great fortune indeed.”

“If we follow behind them now, that might provoke that black clothed fighter into attacking us.”

“They are a gold level, and with the help of the Shadow Plane, they can blink attack, too great a threat to us.”

“We don’t have a gold level, and outside of me being able to hold him back, we don’t have anyone else who can.”

“Hmm…perhaps if Zong Ge wears the Golden Silk Chainmail, he can also vie with him for a time.”

“But anyone else will be in grave danger.”

The dragonman youngster contemplated.

Zong Ge didn’t say anything else, that proposal of his naturally had many clear systemic problems.

Following Lady Sea Snake’s fleet was very risky.

But sometimes, troops marched forward understanding the extreme risks, and that sacrifice was very likely, yet still advanced ahead.

Zong Ge never feared sacrifice.

Zi Di spoke at this time, reminding, “For now, Cang Xu is still safe. We have already saved the dwarfs and humans, and don’t have sufficient grounds to pursue a gold level enemy. The risks are too high, the profits are unclear, and this will stir up suspicions. We are just one mercenary corps, and at worst munitions smugglers.”

Zong Ge also said, “The gold level black clothed fighter who appeared was obviously prepared for Jian Gui. It was a meticulous attack plan; we just mistakenly stumbled into it.”

“Following Lady Sea Snake is rather risky, and it just occurred to me that we actually have other clues.”

This reminder also caused the dragonman youngster to reply, “Yes, we still have other clues.”

In Cang Xu’s secret letter, heh had given them information on the entire undead gathering.

The dragonman youngster spoke to himself, “Cang Xu, Xiao Wang Zi, and the others boarded the ghost ship. Every year, Xiao Wang Zi meets with his parents, said meeting is not far off.”

“The ghost magician Qi Ling said something during the meeting. It had to do with the White Hair Ocean Trench, and the matter about the seabed beast groups migrating in. This might imply that the fishman Qi Ling protects lives near White Hair Ocean Trench. Even if it isn’t, their clan might have migrated and participated in the hunting of those beast groups.”

Zi Di agreed, “That’s right, there are still many clues. We don’t need to shadow Lady Sea Snake.”

“Even if we can rescue Cang Xu, what are the benefits of doing so?”

“Jian Gui and the other necromancers have shown that they aren’t fiendish or the type that kills people like flies.”

“We should do our own thing, do our best to strengthen ourselves so that if Cang Xu gets into danger in the future, we can provide him with more support.”

“Don’t forget, our time is very scarce. It has already been two months since Mystifying Monster Island has been destroyed.”

The dragonman youngster nodded, and all thoughts of following Lady Sea Snake were dispelled.

This voyage to Weeping Pine Island went smoothly at first, but in the later stages, unexpected things appeared.

The survivors had accomplished most of their plans, the dwarfs had been saved, they had captured the Iron Lump, and they white washed lots of the treasures they picked up from Two Eyes Island’s seafloor.

But Cang Xu was gone.

There were discrepancies between the plans and reality.

With deep frustration and worry about Cang Xu in mind, the dragonman youngster ordered the fleet to sail to Ice Sculpture Island.

The Ghost World.

Dark clouds covered the sky densely.

The earth was a sickly white, like a dead man’s bones.

The ghost ship quietly sailed through the Underworld River.

Cang Xu stood on deck, on the sides of the ship, watching everything around him with a novel expression.

“This is what the Ghost World looks like?”

“Such dense negative energy!”

Qing Xin was standing next to him, her face green and deep black bags under her eyes.

She was using magic power to protect herself from the negative energy everywhere. As a living being, she was incompatible with the Ghost World.

In fact, Cang Xu was also feeling the burden.

The negative energy was constantly eroding his body, and even though he had transformed his corporeal body, to maintain his humanity, he was half alive and half dead, part of his body was still alive.

By contrast in the distance, Ku Feng and Xiao Wang Zi felt incomparable pleasure, like a fishman returning to the ocean.

Xiao Wang Zi reclined on his pet three headed undead sea serpent’s back, treating his generalissimo as a couch. He lazily said to Cang Xu, “Not all of the Ghost World has such dense negative energy.”

“We are now in the Underworld River, which could be said to be a river of negative energy.”

When Cang Xu heard this, he looked down at the Underworld River under the ship.

The Underworld River was very wide, and Cang Xu couldn’t see either shore.

Fog floated across the surface of the Underworld River, occasionally dense, and occasionally thin.

The water of the Underworld River was light gray.

Cang Xu gazed at it with rapt attention, noticing to his astonishment that as he kept gazing at it, it grew clearer and more transparent.

“Not good!” Xiao Wang Zi shouted loudly, “Save him now!”

Chapter 468: Snow Elf Patrol Inspection

Ku Feng acted almost as fast as Xiao Wang Zi called for help.

He grabbed Cang Xu by the neck and pulled him back.

Cang Xu was roused.

He was horrified to discover that he was unwittingly lying across the side of the ship, stretching out his upper body, and was about to fall into the Underworld River.

“Newcomer, you went so far as to dare gaze into the Underworld River, you really don’t know what fear is.” Xiao Wang Zi slapped his palm across his ribs with a gasp of surprise.

“Don’t gaze into the Underworld River, it will absorb your soul.” Ku Feng explained.

“Many thanks.” Cang Xu shivered with post-traumatic stress.

Compared to veteran undead like Xiao Wang Zi and Ku Feng, his knowledge appeared superficial.

In fact, conventional books had no accounts about the taboos of the Underworld River.

Any knowledge pertaining to the undead was strictly controlled by major powers. Furthermore, in the wake of growing numbers of necromancers, this control grew ever stronger.

Xiao Wang Zi sighed, “It’s a pity that the gold level black clothed person didn’t chase us. Since he used the Shadow Plane to move, he should have been able to use it to intrude into the Ghost World.

“If he came here, without the ghost ship to carry him, he would have definitely been absorbed by the Underworld River.

The Underworld River was very treacherous, and if Qing Xin wasn’t on the ghost ship and was alone, she wouldn’t have enough time to react before the Underworld River absorbed her.

Cang Xu calmed down, then looked at the Underworld River’s surface, but this time he only looked at it from the corner of his eyes and constantly shifted his line of sight.

He let out a sincere sigh, “The Underworld River, how mysterious and intriguing. When my soul was being sucked in, I felt infinite peace and seemed to be an exhausted traveler finally returning to a warm home.”

“In the Underworld River I also saw a shadow, I don’t know what it was.”

When Ku Feng heard that, he immediately grew discolored.

Xiao Wang Zi cried out in alarm, “What newcomer, what did you just see?”

Cang Xu suddenly grew tense, “I didn’t see it clearly, it looked like a vague silhouette of a human.”

A moment later, there was a wail, and a ghost flew out of the Underworld River and blocked the path in front of the ghost ship.

A gold level water ghost!

Cang Xu’s heart shook, then relaxed slightly, “Not bad, its only gold level.”

But Xiao Wang Zi and Ku Feng were panic-stricken.

Xiao Wang Zi was so anxious that his voice cracked, “What nonsense! Gold levels in the Underworld River can only survive in groups.”

Just as he said that there was more ghostly wailing.

Ten gold level water ghosts floated up and barred the ghost ship’s way.

Cang Xu and Qing Xin were overwhelmed with horror.

“Lord Jian Gui, save us, save us!” Xiao Wang Zi called.

As a matter of fact, Jian Gui didn’t need Xiao Wang Zi’s yelling, he had been alarmed by the situation already.

“Everyone into the ship cabin, I need to return to the Main World!” Jian Gui shouted.

Cang Xu and the others rushed into the ship’s cabin.

Fortunately, the water ghost group had no intelligence, they had been utterly eroded by negative energy and their original memories had been eroded to nothing.

The water ghosts only detested living beings.

They vaguely sensed the smell of a living being from the ghost ship, but then felt perplexed.

As they circled around the ghost ship, space fiercely fluctuated, and the ghost ship seemed to dissipate like the fog.

“Returned!” When Cang Xu and the others returned to the deck, they saw a blue sky and white clouds, along with a barren island not too far in the distance.

“Where are we?” Qing Xin asked.

Cang Xu took out a palm compass and began to orient himself.

Jian Gui transmitted, “This is one of my bases, it has some resources in storage. Of course, most importantly, it has a Eulogy Tombstone, and by relying on its spatial coordinate, I was able to quickly return to the Main World.”

“Wait a moment.” Cang Xu felt something was fishy, he looked at the eastern sun, “When we left Weeping Pine Island, it was afternoon.”

Xiao Wang Zi giggled, “Newcomer, you are so stupid.”

“You think that sailing through the Underworld River only affects space?”

“Time flows differently in the two planes, or to be more precise, it has been four days since Weeping Pine Island.”

Cang Xu’s eyes couldn’t help but widen, “So that’s how it is!”

Jian Gui transmitted, “I must heal and rest here for a bit. It is inadvisable to stay here for long since Qi Ling is a traitor, this place is no longer safe.”

When Qi Ling was mentioned, Xiao Wang Zi coldly snorted, and his anger quickly flared.

He was just about to curse when a fleet came from the other side of the island.

Everyone on the ghost ship changed color simultaneously.

“Not good, its Lady Sea Snake’s Petal Snake Pirates!”

Meanwhile, as the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps entered Ice Sculpture Island waters, they all had the same feeling.

The air temperature was dropping.

The closer they got to Ice Sculpture Island, the colder it got.

While the necromancers encountered Lady Sea Snake’s fleet, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps also had a fleet appearing before it.

“This is Ice Sculpture waters, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s seas, we are the kingdom’s 17th patrol fleet, if you want to land on Ice Sculpture Island, please stop your ships and accept inspection and registration.” The patrol captain was a silver level fighter who spoke through a sound amplification device to convey their message.

“Two silver levels, three bronze level magic ships.” The dragonman youngster gazed into the distance, immediately sensing the other side’s auras.

Bai Ya stood on the side of a ship, today it was his turn to wash the deck. When he heard the patrol fleet’s declaration, he couldn’t help but exclaim in surprise, “Heavens, they are only patrol fleet 17, that means there are at least 16 other fleets of similar scale? The Ice Sculpture Kingdom is so strong!”

The mercenaries near him immediately ridicule that, “Youngster, you have little experience. Ice Sculpture Island cannot be compared to Snake Rat Island or Two Eyes Island.”

“Its territory is vast, with more than a dozen cities and no less than 8 large harbors.”

“The patrol fleets are not the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s main maritime power, the kingdom has three main fleets, which includes the royal fleet’s saint level flagship!”

Bai Ya was dumbstruck by this.

Sufficient maritime military power gave Ice Sculpture Island safe waters.

In fact, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had encountered a patrol fleet a long way from the island.

In light of this distance, it could be assessed that the patrol fleet’s routes were at least a hundredfold larger than Two Eyes Island’s patrol fleet’s!

The dragonman youngster ordered the ships to stop and accepted an inspection.

It was quite hard to stop ships in the sea, sometimes ship anchors couldn’t hit the bottom.

Anyways, the patrol fleet didn’t need the ships to stop, they dispatched an inspection team composed of all cultivators that could jump the five to six hundred meters distance to them.

Snow elves!

Most of the mercenaries widened their eyes, amazed to take measure of these people.

To be more precise, these were not humans, they were elves.

Elves had many classifications, there were wood elves, dark elves (Drow), sea elves, and more, and one of them were snow elves.

Snow elves had the characteristics of elven kind, regardless of gender, they were equally pretty, and their statures were tall and thin.

They had the distinctive long ears of elves, along with white hair and eyebrows, a bloodline trait that distinguished them from other elves.

The entire snow elf inspection team wore leather armor and had cutlasses strapped to their waists.

They were equipped with special alchemy equipment that were used to scan for hidden things.

Zong Ge calmly observed them, noticing that in a small team of 7, there was at least one magician.

The team had a magician to fighter ratio of 1:6.

This ratio was astonishing!

This was also the advantage of elven bloodlines – their magical gifts were very strong and far exceeded that of humans.

Two inspection teams examined the Love Triangle together.

This was their main inspection target.

“Stay away, humans.” The snow elves haughtily held their long handle alchemy items as they scanned back and forth.

At the front end of the item was an iron circle that emitted a blue light.

The inspection team climbed down the ladder to a lower floor, then stopped moving in front of a cabin door.

Their long handle iron circles produced a red light.

“What’s inside?” A snow elf investigator asked with a tough attitude, “Please open it, so we can continue inspecting.”

The dragonman youngster ordered that the cabin door be opened.

A cabin filled with weapons and armor was revealed to the snow elves.

The snow elves looked at the walls filled with alchemy crossbows and firearms, their eyelids jumping.

These long range weapons came from War Merchant’s warehouse and were of superior quality.

The snow elf investigators had seldom seen such a scene.

“This is the armory, under normal circumstances, this equipment is stored here. Only during wartime are they given out.” Lan Zao, who was accompanying them, presented.

The snow elf investigator nodded, “A good system.”

“Hey, partner, look what I found!” Another snow elf investigator couldn’t help but whistle.

The long handle iron circle in his hand was also glowing red.

He was in front of a cannon.

The gold level cannon.

Chi Lai was nearby, watching the cannon, and he coldly replied to the snow elf investigator, “It is my treasure, and as you can see, it needs repairs.”

The snow elf investigator nodded, then took out paper and pen to record it while saying, “My country has many outstanding alchemy masters, the Ice Alchemy Workshop is even one of the best in the world. You will be able to repair it. Of course, if you have enough money to repair it.”

Chi Lai nodded back and didn’t reply.

The snow elf inspector on the Iron Lump was taking measure of the Feather Snake Sail with a reprimanding tone, “I find it hard to imagine that you actually installed such a beautiful sail on such a stupid thing. Heavens, this is just a temporary magic power flow channel, don’t tell me you didn’t know this kind of magic power transference is not stable? Using the Feather Snake Sail like this will reduce its usage life, this simply is a waste of resources!”

The inspection team took quite a while, ascertaining what was what to the bottom of the ship.

Zong Ge had an iron expression.

As a general, he had investigated his troops thoroughly, and this made him extremely resentful and even humiliated.

The inspection team reported all kinds of information to the patrol fleet captain.

“Three magic ships, the iron level Love Triangle, the bronze level White Bandage and Iron Lump.”

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps…Commander Long Fu is silver level; Auxiliary Commander Shi Qi is also silver level. In addition to that, there are other silver level fighters.”

“A gold level cannon, and a Feather Snake Sail of the same level…”

“As a small sized mercenary corps, they actually have gold level equipment! Along with many silver levels. It could even be said they have a deep foundation and are a group of competent people.”

“But…” When the patrol captain saw it, he exposed a weird expression, “What’s going on with their ranks? They have all kinds of races.”

“Dwarfs, water pigmen, dogmen, humans, a half giant, a dragonman…do they want to open a circus?”

“What a mess!”

The patrol captain was worried about the dragonman youngster. This was a mixed group composed of many races and many religions, how could such a group continue to exist?

If only he knew that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps also had a half beastman, a goblin, and even a necromancer, who knows what he would think about that.

Chapter 469: Cang Xu’s Despair

The Petal Snake Pirates.

Flagship – Flower Snake Tower.

“Reload!” A gunner shouted.

The six sailors assisting them used their entire bodies to lift a gigantic artillery shell together.

The artillery shell was overall cylindrical, its front end was a hemisphere, and it gave off a golden light.

The long cylindrical artillery shell was a Baby Bird Mushroom Cloud shell.

This shell emitted a gold level aura and was obviously not made of ordinary materials.

The artillery shell was pushed into a cannon by the sailors.

This peculiar cannon was known as an Arched Anti-air Cannon, it was ten meters long, and its cannon barrel curved several times around the ship’s central tower until the cannon mouth sat on the tower’s roof.

The Arched Anti-air Cannon had most certainly been remade by alchemy, its outward appearance and the Flower Snake Tower were in agreement, with the cannon’s mouth being a large, open snake mouth.

The surface of the entire serpentine cannon was covered in thousands of shields that were layered upon each other, completely covering the cannon to protect it.

“Close the cannon breech, activate the Arched Anti-air Cannon.” The main gunner sat in the central ship tower, controlling the alchemy system; this cabin was dedicated to shooting the cannon.

A large amount of magic power poured in from the Flower Snake Tower’s magic power reservoir and into the Arched Anti-air Cannon.

The anti-air cannon began to faintly shudder while simultaneously producing a snake hiss.

Two auxiliary gunners put both hands on the snake tail, mixing their battle qi into it to control the Baby Bird Mushroom Cloud shell.

The gold level artillery shell slowly activated, and from the snake tail, it began to accelerate through the tight spiral rifling of the cannon barrel.

As it circled through the cannon’s serpentine barrel once, the main gunner heard the alchemy system’s monotonous voice, “Level one acceleration complete.”

Then when the artillery shell circled around a second time, the alchemy system stated, “Level two acceleration complete.”

Soon after.

“Level three acceleration complete.”

The main gunner growled, “Fire!”

After the third level of acceleration, the gold artillery shell had reached an astonishing speed, it became a shadow that shot out from the cannon and within two seconds, it swiftly ascended into the air to an altitude of 1000 meters.

After the Baby Bird Mushroom Cloud shell reached maximum altitude, it began to fall.

At this time, its steel wings ejected from its two sides.

The steel wings vibrated through the air, producing a warbling rumble.

Whizz!

As it took aim at the ghost ship, the shell swooped down as its warbling lengthened, causing hearts to tense and hair to prick up of those who heard it.

On the ghost ship, everyone abruptly became discolored.

Ku Feng growled, “Not good, we must intercept it!”

Jian Gui’s ghost ship was only silver level, while Lady Sea Snake’s Arched Anti-air Cannon and Baby Bird Mushroom Cloud shell were both gold level.

If it hit the ghost ship, the consequences would be too horrible to contemplate!

Before Ku Feng shouted his warning, the three magicians were already moving,

Qing Xin produced a gigantic wind blade.

Xiao Wang Zi suddenly pulled off his left index finder and threw it.

The index finder turned into a lance as it flew through the air.

While Cang Xu took out the deteriorated magic book.

It was the water path magic book he and Zi Di had bought from Two Eyes Island’s auction.

The magic book only had three pages, but they were all gold level magic spells.

Cang Xu’s soul burden was very heavy, thus, his casting speed and success rate were very low, so during this emergency, he could only use this trump card.

One of the magic book’s pages disintegrated to ash and drifted into the wind.

A water arrow appeared from nothing and launched towards the Baby Bird Mushroom Cloud shell.

Qing Xin’s massive wind blade was the fastest and was the first to crash into the gold artillery shell. It turned to green light and only slowed down the artillery shell somewhat, nothing else.

Xiao Wang Zi’s bone spear had exceptional quality, it was an adequate silver level. It was not a spell, but rather a magic item that was shrunk normally, disguised as his finger.

But the bone spear didn’t hit the Baby Bird Mushroom Cloud shell.

In a crucial split second, the Baby Bird Mushroom Cloud shell unexpectedly ascended a bit to avoid the bone spear!

Of course, the gold level shell was not only powerful, but it also was excellently designed.

Since the Baby Bird Mushroom Cloud shell was designed to be a high altitude shell and its trajectory was a huge parabola, it gave the enemy a lot of time to intercept it.

With this taken into consideration, the alchemist master who manufactured this artillery shell had installed some meticulous things inside it to give it the capacity to evade.

“It avoided it?” Xiao Wang Zi was astonished and rushed to use his spiritual control to bring the spear back.

But how could the bone spear catch up with the gold artillery shell?

Xiao Wang Zi was forced to watch as the artillery shell quickly outpaced the bone spear.

Cang Xu used the magic book, and according to convention, his water arrow should have formed first.

But his mental state was quite terrible and in actual combat, he was the last to move.

“It’s all on me.” When Cang Xu saw Qing Xin and Xiao Wang Zi fail, he took in a nervous breath and used all of his spirit power.

He willed the water arrow to smash into the gold artillery shell

The Baby Bird Mushroom Cloud shell’s steel wings slightly turned, it wanted to use the same trick to avoid the Water Arrow spell.

However, the Water Arrow spell was being controlled by Cang Xu’s spirit and Cang Xu had been prepared for this and immediately changed the Water Arrow spell’s flight direction.

Ding.

The Water Arrow spell struck the gold artillery shell.

The gold artillery shell screeched to a halt, and the hemisphere warhead was smashed vertically down into the ocean.

It ended up in the seawater.

Boom!

With a large explosion, the shell blew up in the ocean.

The fierce explosion lifted a jet of water up to ten meters tall, which crashed back down into gigantic white waves that swept from the explosion.

The ghost ship rode the waves.

On the deck, the necromancers and the rest were in indeterminate panic and lingering fear.

Xiao Wang Zi patted his chest, “How dangerous, good think it was intercepted.”

The bone spear he had thrown out had flown back, and Xiao Wang Zi grabbed it.

Xiao Wang Zi then shouted, “Save me, save me Lord Jian Gui!”

Like before in the Underworld River, he cried for help just the same now.

Cang Xu couldn’t help but raise his eyebrows and think, why was Xiao Wang Zi’s call for help so practiced?

A moment later, Jian Gui weakly replied, “A gold water ghost came back with us, I am in the process of suppressing it.”

“For a short time, we cannot go back to the Ghost World.”

“The magic power reservoir has already reached critical levels.”

“Next, I will use the Ghost Fog spell, you will all hide in the ship cabin and do your best to fight for time.”

If Jian Gui used Ghost World Traversal again, the ghost ship would return to the Underworld River again.

They would once again face the water ghost group and were sure to face disaster.

Even if they bet on the ghost group dispersing before then, the ghost ship couldn’t return to the Ghost World.

Because planar teleportation used up great amounts of magic power, and traveling the Underworld River also consumed lots of magic power too.

The ghost ship had inadequate magic power.

If it went back to the Ghost World again, it wouldn’t have enough magic power to return to the Main World.

They would meet their end in the Underworld River, for after barely traveling a bit, their magic power would be utterly exhausted, and the ghost ship would be eroded completely by negative energy, until finally, it was assimilated into the ecology of the Underworld River.

The Flower Snake Tower.

When Lady Sea Snake saw that her first artillery shell was stopped, her face flickered and she ordered, “Keep shooting.”

The other side using gold level magic to intercept the Baby Bird Mushroom Cloud shell was sensible.

The advantages of the strong were reflected here.

If a gold level magician used magic to intercept the artillery shell, they would have a high success rate of doing so. Magicians only expended magic and spirit power.

After a time, they could autonomously recover it.

The cost was so low!

By contrast, when factoring in the artillery shell’s price, along with the anti-air cannon’s maintenance and magic power cost, Lady Sea Snake had paid a few hundred gold coins to fire that shell.

But Lady Sea Snake remained unmoved and still ordered the crew to keep firing.

Because even though the first was intercepted, it didn’t mean the second or third would be.

Even if only one worked, she would be content.

This was tantamount to her spending money to deplete the other side’s magic power.

Magic power needed time to recover, or recovery medicine, both would consume the enemy’s war potential.

Soon after, a second Baby Bird Mushroom Cloud shell was loaded in.

But as the anti-air cannon was being activated, a thick fog rose around the ghost ship.

The thick fog quickly expanded, completely covering the ghost ship.

The main gunner reported, “Commander, we lost visual.”

Lady Sea Snake coldly snorted, “This is the Ghost Fog spell, the necromancy school’s advantage still exists, and conventional detection methods won’t work. Forget about it, stop firing.”

After Lady Sea Snake said that she couldn’t help but laugh.

She turned around to look at Wei Ying.

The gold fighter’s arms were crossed, and he was leaning in a corner. The captain’s room was obviously well lit, but he was surrounded by dense shadows.

Lady Sea Snake said to him, “It seems our luck is pretty good. The ghost ship was obviously forced to come here, the Underworld River’s dangers are hard to estimate.”

“They are now stalling for time; grab hold of this chance to accomplish our mission.”

“What are we waiting for?” As Wei Ying spoke, the shadows around his body quickly vanished.

Cang Xu hid in a ship cabin.

But this ship cabin didn’t give him any sense of security.

He sensed that the ghost ship was sailing far away from where it had once been.

But not far enough.

The Ghost Fog spell had a finite range, and the other side had many men.

Given enough time, they would be found.

Cang Xu most dreaded the mysterious black clothed fighter, followed by Lady Sea Snake’s gold level magic pet.

If any came calling, the necromancers would be in trouble.

“If battle starts, I will be most in danger.” Cang Xu was only an iron level in actuality.

“Battle strength, I need battle strength!” Cang Xu’s mouth and tongue were dry as his heart filled with a strong longing for greater battle strength.

Every increase of strength would increase his chances of survival.

But how to increase it?

Faced with this difficult problem, Cang Xu sank into rapid pondering.

Despite obtaining many undead inheritances, these all new magics needed time to familiarize with and to develop, so he could cast them successfully.

Risking casting them now would likely lead to them failing and him suffering the terrifying backlash.

“Calm down.”

“I cannot take too many risks, that would be almost equal to committing suicide.”

Cang Xu pondered for a moment, unable to find a solution, causing the trace of despair in his mind to turn into a flood.

Chapter 470: Ghostly Transformation

Cang Xu was only an iron level, and he was facing gold level enemies.

The gap between their life levels were too high, and it was too difficult for him to resist them.

The formidable spiritual pressure caused Cang Xu to inevitably sink into self-doubt, “To scheme for the benefits Jian Gui had on him, I chose to take a risk, was that the wrong choice?”

“Should I be more cautious and not act so radically?”

Cang Xu felt regret.

But soon after, he shook his head and strived to get rid of this defeatist mindset.

He understood that at this crucial juncture, any defeatist feelings would lessen his chances of survival.

In fact, Cang Xu never thought that he’d be in danger.

In addition, at the time, he had no choice but to board the ghost ship.

If he hadn’t, he would have been chased down by pirates. When Jian Gui and the ghost ship vanished, he would be left behind and turn into the sole target, likely being killed by the mysterious black clothed man in passing.

Calm.

Deep breaths.

Cang Xu forced himself to calm down.

Suddenly, he had a flash of inspiration, “The chances of getting myself out of trouble with these gold level enemies is next to nothing. What I really need to do is learn from Xiao Wang Zi!”

He coughed a bit, then started talking, “Lord Jian Gui, help me, I need your help!”

Although Jian Gui was not in front of Cang Xu, the entire ghost ship was him. According to Cang Xu’s conjecture, his every movement and word could be sensed swiftly by Jian Gui.

Were that not so, Jian Gui wouldn’t have been able to hear Xiao Wang Zi’s call for help and quickly act on it.

As expected, Jian Gui soon answered Cang Xu, “You need me to help you? Xu Jia.”

Xu Jia was the pseudonym Cang Xu had given in the undead gathering.

Cang Xu candidly recounted his predicament, “You know me, Lord Jian Gui. I just received an advanced soul aspect inheritance, and have three silver level ghosts, my spiritual burden is enormous. I need to use the Burden Alleviation and Karma Reduction spells as soon as possible, but those two spells need a lot of time and practice before I can grasp them. Did you say you had some pertinent magic scrolls? I can purchase them…”

Before Cang Xu could finish, the space in front of him fluctuated.

The fluctuation quickly faded.

A magic book appeared from nowhere.

“This is my secondary magic book, you can borrow it.” Said Jian Gui.

Cang Xu was overjoyed at the unexpectedly good news, he only wanted some magic scrolls and never expected to get an entire magic tome.

“So, the price…” Cang Xu asked again.

Jian Gui interrupted him again, “You can use it first, don’t worry, I won’t extort you!”

Jian Gui’s bearing was just as always, and didn’t take the opportunity to ask for a high price.

He could have done so, because Cang Xu at this moment was mentally prepared to bear a high price.

But he didn’t do that.

The magic tome was a conventional type of magic item, similar to magic staves, wands, crystal balls, and more.

But magic books were different from other casting items.

It could be regarded as a collection of an indeterminate amount of magic scrolls.

But magic scrolls would be destroyed upon use, while magic books possessed the ability to be recharged, able to be used a number of times.

The manufacturing cost of magic books differed, it was based on their magical contents, the amount of pages, materials, and so on, leading to the cost range to be quite wide.

With regards to necromancers, their magic tomes were very difficult to buy externally and were normally made by themselves.

Jian Gui was worthy of being a direct descendent of the undead, the book he lent to Cang Xu was merely his secondary book. This indicated that he had at least two magic tomes, the first undoubtedly more exceptional than the second.

Cang Xu opened up the secondary magic tome.

The secondary book’s first spell was the Burden Alleviation spell.

After that there was the same spell four more times.

Then on the fifth page was the Karma Reduction spell.

The pages dedicated to those two spells made up one tenth of the magic tome, this made clear how important they were to a necromancer’s cultivation.

Without any hesitation, Cang Xu immediately poured in some magic power and formed a spiritual link.

Not much magic power was consumed; it was more used as the trigger.

The magic book blossomed with ashen light that enveloped Cang Xu’s entire body.

Afterwards, the spell’s power seeped into Cang Xu’s soul.

First was the Burden Alleviation spell, then the Karma Reduction spell.

Ah, what a wonderful feeling!

Cang Xu groaned a bit.

The enormous spiritual pressure on him was like a sick person carrying a mountain reaching the limits of their stamina as they walked along a precipice, the slightest mistake able to destroy his life and soul.

Now, the heavy weight on him dissipated for the most part, his entire being relaxed, and his illness had been alleviated greatly.

Cang Xu had used ten of the magic book’s pages.

Five Burden Alleviation spells and five Karma Reduction spells.

The former reduced his soul burden greatly and the latter eased the negative energy that eroded Cang Xu’s heart and soul.

After those ten spells, Cang Xu released three silver level ghosts.

He no longer felt dizzy or depressed.

So unstressed.

It seemed as if had another silver level ghost, he’d still be capable of controlling it.

Unfortunately, they couldn’t be used further.

Cang Xu noticed that after using the ten pages, the pages now appeared to be gray, they need to be replenished with magic power, materials, and other things. They need to be “charged” to be used again.

Of course, forcefully using them wasn’t out of the question.

But doing so would squeeze the magic book and turn it into a disposable item. In the future, even if it was “recharged”, it wouldn’t be usable again.

This was not Cang Xu’s magic book, and Cang Xu didn’t intend on killing the goose that lays golden eggs.

He continued to thumb through the magic tome.

Intermediate level Water Ghost Refinement spell.

This was the content of page 11.

Generally speaking, low level magic was bronze and iron level. Intermediate level was silver and gold level. High level was saint and legend level.

Cang Xu immediately retrieved knowledge from the advanced soul aspect inheritance he had just obtained.

“Intermediate level Water Ghost Refinement spell, it can turn a ghost into a silver level or a gold level water ghost. But the main material needed must be an intermediate level ghost.”

“Furthermore, the ghost lifeform should have abundant water attributes. The more water attributes the ghost bloodline has, the higher the casting success rate of transforming it into a water ghost.”

“The undead bloodlines, most are from the remnants of what they once were alive, therefore, their living bloodlines usually determined the ghost’s transformation path.”

After looking this up, Cang Xu had an understanding.

“Undead lifeforms have their foundation in life and were made from them.”

“Undead lifeform creation, regardless of whether it is soul, corpse, or bone aspect, must all be in line with the being’s aptitude.”

“One cannot change them as they wish.”

Cang Xu was somewhat restless.

He had three silver level ghosts on hand; they were all intermediate level and suitable for this magic.

But there was one crucial problem.

“Do any of my three silver level ghosts contain water attributes?”

“If they don’t contain a water nature, or have a minimal amount of it, then the refinement spell will likely fail, and even if it succeeds, the transformed water ghost’s battle strength will be quite low.”

“Because this path does not suit its development, and it cannot display its potential from life!”

This problem stumped Cang Xu.

Because this involved bloodline testing, and of the survivors, only Zi Di could barely do it.

It was impossible for Cang Xu to do that under the current circumstances.

“Perhaps the magic tome has a testing magic?” Cang Xu’s heart quivered and he began to flip through the book.

Ghost Horse Refinement spell.

This was a horse ghost type of transformation magic; after being refined, the ghost horse could become a necromancer’s mount.

Ghost Elf Refinement spell.

The higher the charisma of the undead life form, the greater the chances of success and the stronger it would be.

Hungry Ghost Refinement spell.

Living entities with the Gluttony, Starvation, or similar bloodlines can be refined by this spell after being turned into a ghost.

Frost Ghost Refinement spell, Death Substitute Refinement spell, Shield Bearer Ghost Refinement spell, Poltergeist Refinement spell…

There were many pages of ghostly transformation spells.

The latter part of the magic tome dealt with soul aspect attack, defense, teleportation, and other such magics.

“Nothing that can detect a ghost’s aptitude…” After rummaging through the magic book, Cang Xu was somewhat disappointed.

He quickly settled his mind and calmly reflected, “Wrong, the magic tome might not have it, but that doesn’t mean Jian Gui doesn’t have it!”

“Since he has the ghostly transformation spells, then that proves that this field has been studied deeply, it is impossible for there to be no testing magic.”

“Beforehand, Jian Gui saw through me and knew that I had transformed my body somewhat.”

“Furthermore, he also refused my request for a deeper level necromancy inheritance, the reason was my aptitude wasn’t good enough!”

“That proves that he has a method of gauging aptitude!”

When he thought of that, Cang Xu shouted again for Jian Gui’s help.

Jian Gui admitted that he did have this supplementary magic that could determine an undead life form’s aptitude and potential to a degree. However, he couldn’t give it to Cang Xu. Because even a magic scroll could give Cang Xu a glimpse at a few of the magic’s secrets, thereby obtaining crucial enlightenment of its properties.

This was one of the high level core inheritances and needed to be treated very prudently.

Jian Gui wanted to help Cang Xu, Xiao Wang Zi, and the others, but his help was not endless, Jian Gui had a firm bottom line.

Cang Xu sighed, because the feeling of frustration and despair over his unqualified aptitude surfaced in his mind again.

Therefore, he asked Jian Gui to help him test his ghosts’ aptitudes.

But Jian Gui still declined, “So sorry, currently we need to stay hidden in the Ghost Fog and stall for time. Xu Jia, this magic takes a long time to cast and will draw in a large amount of my spirit, it cannot be concealed and will expose us.”

“Perhaps, you can wait until after this crisis, then I will naturally help you.”

This path was also blocked.

Cang Xu shook his head internally as Jian Gui overruled his final suggestion.

He never entrusted his survival to luck, nor would he be unduly conservative, which would lead to his chances of survival declining.

“These three silver level ghosts must be transformed!”

“Since aptitude can’t be tested, then there’s no harm in switching my angle.”

“Which ghost do I need the most?”

Cang Xu’s eyes twinkled with calmness and wisdom.

Chapter 471: Cang Xu Dies

Cang Xu once more opened Jian Gui’s magic tome.

The first part of the ghostly transformation section was the Water Ghost Refinement spell.

In this oceanic environment, refining water ghosts was undoubtedly the most practical choice. From how the book’s contents were arranged, one could glimpse the author’s, Jian Gui’s, pondering.

“Is it worth refining a water ghost?” Cang Xu calmly analyzed.

He thought of Qi Ling’s water ghosts.

The water ghosts had dealt enormous casualties to the Lightning Pirates.

Especially when Qi Ling used an intermediate level Soul Send spell with her silver level water ghosts, allowing her to control key members of the pirates.

Cang Xu personally experienced how strong those water ghosts were.

“But it doesn’t suit me right now.”

The reason was very simple; water ghosts were partial towards attacking.

They were not individually strong, they needed numbers.

Provided they were a group and fighting at sea, their combat effectiveness was usually decent.

Magician Qi Ling’s group magic and utilization of the Soul Sending spell really was sharp.

But now, Cang Xu was facing a gold level enemy. There was a two life level difference between him and them, Qi Ling’s group magic would be ineffective.

Cang Xu constantly thumbed through the magic tome’s pages.

Suddenly, he froze.

Dragon Spirit Refinement spell!

Gigantic dragons were one of the strongest lifeforms in the world, and dragon spirits were absolutely one of the strongest ghosts too.

Cang Xu couldn’t move his eyes away.

“If I had a dragon spirit, even only a silver level one, it could temporarily hold back the mysterious black clothed person. Even an iron level one, could last a few seconds.”

A few seconds was a fundamental change!

Because Cang Xu was on the ghost ship, and a few seconds was enough for Jian Gui to react and lend a helping hand.

However, Cang Xu lacked the most important material to refine a dragon spirit.

He needed a dragon soul, the purer the better, while wyverns were second level quality.

“Lord Jian Gui, do you have a dragon soul on you? I can buy it at a high price!” Cang Xu asked again.

But Jian Gui didn’t have one, “Pure dragon souls are very rare, I don’t have such a good thing.”

Cang Xu thumbed through the tome once more.

“Ghost Horse Refinement spell?”

He quickly shook his head.

Even if he could refine a ghost horse to be his mount, his speed could never compare to the mysterious black clothed figure’s blink shift.

“Shield bearer ghost?”

This was a type of protection ghost.

Cang Xu had seen how they were used from the advanced soul aspect inheritance he had received.

One of them was after a necromancer turned themselves into a ghost, they could entrust themselves to a shield bearer ghost.

When that happened, then a ghost would be in a ghost.

Cang Xu could get the shield bearer ghost’s defenses.

“A silver level shield bearer ghost, almost no offensive use, all of its potential is spent on defense.”

“If I use it this way, then against the mysterious black clothed person, I can struggle at death’s door for a second more.”

Cang Xu’s brows wrinkled.

Because he knew this option was barely passable.

The mysterious black clothed person not only had a gold level life level, but also three pieces of high end gold level equipment.

When his powerful dagger stabbed Jian Gui, the latter of whom was a direct inheritor of the undead, it put him into peril and seriously injured him.

Even with the shield bearer ghost, it would only be a difference of two to three blade strikes to Cang Xu at most.

“The Death Substitute Refinement spell?”

“That could do!”

When Cang Xu saw it, his brows finally smoothed out, and his mind shook.

He instantly envisaged a few good tactics of playing dead and fleeing for his life.

Looking again at the content after it, Cang Xu returned back to the “Death Substitute Refinement spell” part of the magic tome.

“This is the one!”

Cang Xu Made his decision.

He immediately used the magic tome on one of his silver level ghosts.

Ding.

A few seconds later, the ghost looked like a tiny firework that exploded, then died.

Cang Xu took a deep breath.

His cast failed!

This was within Cang Xu’ expectations.

The silver level ghost being used as a material probably didn’t have the pertinent bloodline; therefore, the refinement spell failed and killed the ghost.

Luckily, Cang Xu wasn’t affected that much.

Because he was casting with the magic tome, not with himself.

This was the benefit of a casting tool!

Without any hesitation, Cang Xu immediately used it on a second ghost.

Ding.

The second silver level ghost was also blown up.

Another failure!

Cang Xu felt distressed.

Silver level ghosts were something that were hard to obtain.

“If Zi Di were here, it would be better.”

“No, her medical tests still have a way to go before they can be used on undead lifeforms.

“Only one silver level ghost left”

Cang Xu stopped trying to refine a Death Substitute ghost.

The reason was that the pages with the Death Substitute Refinement spell had gone gray after those two casts, it couldn’t be used a third time and needed to be “recharged” again.

“What to transform the final ghost into?”

Cang Xu quickly pondered, rationally altering his strategy, and became conservative.

He decided to refine a shield bearer ghost.

On the third try, he finally succeeded!

After transforming the silver level ghost, it looked like an old turtle-man, its body was stooped, and its back and even its buttocks were covered with an enormous shield. The elliptic shield was very similar to a turtle shell.

“Good, next is to use the intermediate Soul Sending spell!”

Cang Xu used the magic tome again.

But a moment later, a shadow suddenly appeared!

“Found you!” The mysterious black clothed man sneered as he abruptly appeared behind Cang Xu, his dagger stabbing towards him.

By the time Cang Xu reacted, his heart was already stabbed by the dagger.

“Eh? You’re not him.” The mysterious black clothed man stared at him, then shadow covered his body, and he vanished once again.

Plop.

Cang Xu’s body fell onto the floor.

His eyes opened wide, and his last thought facing death’s door was the mysterious black clothed man thought he was Jian Gui, this showed that Jian Gui had used him as bait!

“It’s no wonder I was given his secondary magic tome!” Cang Xu’s life aura quickly dissipated into nothing.

His body had been transformed by his hands into a living dead person. But he was a bottom iron level undead lifeform, and he would certainly die from an all out assassination from the black clothed man!

Cang Xu was no exception.

He died.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish.

The survivors had gathered in the alchemy cabin, even the big guy was crammed in the hallway outside the cabin.

The alchemy formation slowly ceased, and the dragonman youngster walked out.

“Next.” Zi Di controlled the formation.

Zong Ge grunted his compliance and stepped into the formation.

Buzz.

Soon after, the formation activated again and replenished Zong Ge’s Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had gotten through Ice Sculpture Island’s first inspection and hadn’t reached their destination yet.

Taking advantage of this gap of time, the survivors camouflaged themselves and quietly returned to the Deep Sea Monster Fish to fill in the gaps in their Deceptive Disguise and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spells.

These two magics were wonderful, but were not constant, they needed to be replenished every so often.

The dragonman youngster watched the formation operate, his mind worried about Cang Xu, “He hasn’t replenished these magics, if it is exhausted near Jian Gui and the others, then it will be very troublesome. I hope he can return soon.”

Chapter 472: Shadow Monk

The Deep Sea Monster Fish

The Anti-investigation and Prophecy and Deceptive Disguise spells had just finished being replenished.

The survivors began to swim up to the surface one after the other.

The dragonman youngster was the last to depart.

Zi Di opened the captain room’s door and met with the dragonman youngster alone.

“Lord Commander, everyone’s Deceptive Disguise and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spells have been replenished, this is the subsequent financial report.” Zi Di handed the dragonman youngster a list.

The dragonman youngster quickly skimmed through it.

Converted into gold coins, it cost 180 gold coins.

Compared to the first time, it cost an additional 15 gold coins.

This was mainly due to the Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell.

The Deceptive Disguise spell’s cost couldn’t be calculated, because the main thing it used was the god level material – Pearl Bubbles.

“Not bad.” The dragonman youngster was still, “We have a few hundred thousand gold right now, and in the short term, replenishing these two spells isn’t an issue.”

Zi Di immediately shook her head, “Lord Commander, you’re thinking about it wrong.”

“The more of the Pearl Bubbles we use, the less there are, they cannot be replaced with normal materials.”

“Although we have a lot of capital, we need to spend money in a lot of areas!”

“For example, when we reach Ice Sculpture Island, we need to purchase a silver level magic ship, that will cost a huge sum.”

“Another example, your Lordship’s and Zong Ge’s cultivation also need constant investment.”

“This time, the main reason why the Anti-investigation and Prophecy cost 15 more gold coins is because you two have grown stronger.”

The stronger the survivors got overall, the more it would cost to conceal themselves.

This was obvious. To cover up an ant, one only needed a finger. But to cover up a tree leaf, one needed at least an open palm.

“Your bloodlines are the most exceptional, therefore in the future, the cost of the Anti-prophecy and Investigation spell will continue to rise.”

When the dragonman youngster heard this, he shook his head, “No you are overlooking Xiao Guai.”

The big guy Xiao Guai’s bloodline had been tested to be at least legend level.

Zi Di nodded, “I forgot about that.”

The dragonman youngster smiled, “Understandable, after all with Xiao Guai’s lowly strength and us hiding him away on the ship most of the time on the ship’s bow house, and not showing himself rashly, his sense of presence has always been small.”

“But I believe that as long as our guidance is fruitful and he is able to cross over the most crucial first step, he will be able to truly cultivate and be able to show his extraordinary svelte.”

The dragonman youngster had high expectations for the big guy’s future.

“That is the reason why.” Z Di plainly looked at the young man, “Commander, you must be prudent and cannot be negligent.”

“Don’t be fooled by our current funds and become lax.”

“Our finances have a massive systemic problem.”

“We will be spending ever more in the future, if we don’t operate properly, it might become a vicious circle, and we’ll be unable to make ends meet.”

“At the moment, we need to set up a steady flow of income. Perhaps, we can try to sell our medicine on Ice Sculpture Island.”

“In addition to that, looking for a suitable location to covertly build an alchemy factory to produce munitions will also work for us.”

The dragonman youngster nodded, he looked deeply at Zi Di, “My ability in this aspect cannot in a million years compare to yours.”

“Luckily we have you, I believe that our financial situation will be very healthy.”

As he said that, the dragonman youngster stretched out his palm to Zi Di.

Zi Di softly placed her palm above his, a minute gap between their palms.

She was a ghost, and if they didn’t maintain distance, she would go through the dragonman youngster’s palm.

To be regarded as this important by her beloved, Zi Di’s mouth could help but rise as her heart filled with happiness and she softly said, “Don’t worry, I will manage our assets well.”

…

In a ghostly mist covering the entire sky, specters were howling on the ghost ship.

A fierce battle was taking place.

A large number of ghosts were crazily shuttling through the crushed hull; they had a single enemy, the black clothed fighter who could use the Shadow Plane.

The black clothed man had boarded the ghost ship in the boundless ghost fog and did an assassination when he first appeared.

Cang Xu had died by his hand.

But to him, it had been a failure.

Because his target was his equal, Jian Gui!

“That life aura misled me. Jian Gui’s magic tome? Although a small trick, it…”

The black clothed man’s brows deeply wrinkled as he constantly pondered.

“Why am I still not able to detect Jian Gui’s location inside the ghost ship?”

“He has obviously been marked by my battle skill.”

Wei Ying was very puzzled.

At this moment, three ghosts found him in the corner and threw themselves at him simultaneously.

A heavy shadow suddenly spurted up next to Wei Ying, and like ink, it flowed around him, causing him to vanish instantly from where he stood.

Appearing once again, he found himself in another cabin.

Although three ghosts that attacked him were not his match, Wei Ying didn’t want to fight them.

“This ship has too many ghosts, if I waste battle qi to destroy those insignificant things, I will fall into the other side’s tactics.”

“The most crucial thing is to find Jian Gui’s real body!”

Wei Ying was strong, and in an open contest, he could suppress Jian Gui.

But now, Jian Gui had “disappeared”.

The only thing Wei Ying was certain about was that “Jian Gui was somewhere on this ghost ship”.

Thump.

At that moment, there was a loud sound.

The entire ghost ship’s hull suddenly tilted and nearly capsized!

The main culprit was Lady Sea Snake’s magic pet; the gold level sea snake had used its gigantic and sturdy head to ruthlessly smash the ghost ship’s bottom.

“Support has finally arrived.” Wei Ying was unperturbed.

He had found the ghost ship first, and had informed Lady Sea Snake soon after, her gold level sea snake coming in to provide assistance came at no surprise to him.

The sea snake had made matters even worse for the necromancers.

“Generalissimo, attack! You must entangle the magic snake on the seafloor; don’t let it destroy the ghost ship!” Xiao Wang Zi wobbled about as he gave orders in a flurry.

Although the ghost ship was silver level, it had the undead school’s advantages and was able to withstand the gold level sea snake fairly easily.

But now, most of the ship’s ghosts were occupied with dealing with Wei Ying and were barely able to resist the gold level sea snake.

The undead three headed sea serpent plopped into the water and began to fight tooth and nail against the gold magic snake.

Its three snake heads attacked from different directions, biting at the gold magic ship’s body.

The gold magic snake yelped and wanted to turn around to deal with the undead sea serpent, but then it received Lady Sea Snake’s order.

The gold sea snake vigorously used all of its strength to pull itself onto the ghost ship and wrapped its immense snake body around it, beginning to strangle it!

The undead three headed sea serpent sprayed negative energy and attacked the gold magic beast with all it had, but good things didn’t last forever as Lady Sea Snake quickly shot it with magic.

The undead three headed sea serpent was turned sluggish and stiff by water path magic, and its offensive was greatly affected.

The ghost ship couldn’t bear the heavy load and echoed with groans.

Its hull had been inherently corroded by negative energy to utter decay, and now with a gigantic snake strangling it, large stretches of ship planks were splintering, and massive amounts of cracks were forming on both sides of the ship.

When Jian Gui saw this, he had no choice but to transfer ghosts to attack the gold sea snake.

The unending ghosts of tremendous scale enveloped the battlefield in a cold eeriness.

The gold sea snake had many ghosts flooding into its body, suffering intense negative energy erosion, its memory quickly became chaotic and its mind delirious.

The gigantic sea snake gradually went limp, winning the ghost ship valuable time to take a breather.

“Ku Feng, please engage the black clothed fighter for a time.” Jian Gui’s voice transmitted to Ku Feng’s ears.

Ku Feng immediately obeyed, abandoning Xiao Wang Zi and rushing into the ship cabin, towards Wei Ying.

As a silver level with strong blade techniques, he would be able to resist Wei Ying for a short time.

Very quickly, Wei Ying sensed Ku Feng’s movement.

He sneered, and instead of dealing with Ku Feng, he blink shifted again, this time behind Xiao Wang Zi!

Xiao Wang Zi turned pale with fright, he didn’t have enough time to run away before Wei Ying stabbed him.

Then at the moment Wei Ying had succeeded in that, Jian Gui appeared, and with lightning speed, he jumped into Wei Ying’s body!

Wei Ying’s figure instantly collapsed, turning into a dense shadow.

As it turns out, the one Jian Gui attacked was actually his disguised clone.

“So, you finally appeared!” Wei Ying’s real body appeared behind Jian Gui and used a battle skill on Jian Gui’s back.

Jian Gui wailed in anguish, and his ghost body was heavily damaged.

He used Xiao Wang Zi as bait, but in the end, Wei Ying had beaten him at his own game.

Jian Gui had been injured to start with, and with these wounds added on, he was nearing the edge of death!

He hurried to use a movement trump card and vanished.

But this time, Wei Ying saw it and made out clues.

The gold level transcendent slightly changed color, “So that’s how it is!”

“Jian Gui turned into a ghost and merged into the ghost ship.”

“He became the ghost ship, in other words, the ghost ship is him.”

“It’s no wonder the mark I left him only appeared on the ghost ship.”

“From a certain point of view, he has become this ghost ship’s ship spirit.”

Realizing the truth, Wei Ying couldn’t help but expose a smile of triumph.

No matter how Jian Gui was hiding, it was no use. It was merely struggling at death’s door and delaying the time before defeat and death.

Even if Wei Ying couldn’t find him, so long as he dealt with the ghost ship and destroyed it, he could force Jian Gui’s main body to separate from it and appear once again.

Of course, Jian Gui also knew that he had exposed the truth.

Therefore, after merging with the ghost ship, he began to cast with all he had.

Necromancy spell – Ghost World Traversal!

Space fiercely fluctuated, and the ghost ship separated itself from the Main Plane once again, appearing above the Underworld River.

The gold sea snake howled in agony.

It had been wrapped around the ghost ship and landed in the Underworld River.

In a split second, it was soaked in the Underworld River’s water, the severe corrosion turned its flesh into nothing, leaving only some gloomy white snake bones behind.

Wei Ying crossed his arms and sneered, despite being able to blink shift away, he didn’t choose to do so, instead he followed the ship into the Ghost World.

But this time, he was inside the ghost ship and hadn’t fallen into the Underworld River.

“The same trick, do you think it’ll work again, Jian Gui?” Wei Ying ridiculed with a smile.

Jian Gui weakly replied, “If you kill me or sink this ghostship, then you will die too!”

Jian Gui was at the end of his rope and was forced to use mutual destruction to coerce the enemy.

However, Wei Ying raised his head and burst into laughter, “Jian Gui, why don’t you take a look at this?”

He took out a sacred emblem and put it on his chest.

Jian Gui growled, “The Gloom God’s sacred tool – the Shadow Monk Sacred Emblem?”

“You are a pure human, yet you believe in an elven god?”

Wei Ying was solemn, “I believe only in the Gloom God, the master of the Shadow Plane!”

“Without my God’s bequeathed pity, how could I reach my current heights?”

“With this sacred emblem, I can leave the Ghost World at any time and return to the Shadow Plane.”

“I win, Jian Gui.”

“You lost this battle due to my God’s power!”

Jian Gui sank into silence.

The moment he was unable to drive away Wei Ying from the ghost ship when he teleported to the Underworld River was the moment that decided the outcome of the battle.

True victory would have been if Wei Ying followed the teleporting ship, and without its protection, was immediately absorbed into the Underworld River.

But Jian Gui lost his only chance.

Bang.

Ku Feng kicked open a cabin door, rushing back over.

Xiao Wang Zi was standing to the side, like a foolish fowl. He had been severely injured, his backbone had nearly fractured, and he had lost half of his ribs.

But more importantly was his psychological trauma.

When he heard Wei Ying and Jian Gui talking, he realized Jian Gui had lost, causing his fighting spirit to subsequently be utterly lost too.

“We’re done for!” Xiao Wang Zi wanted to cry, but there were no tears.

Ku Feng took out a long blade to protect Xiao Wang Zi, his eyes locked onto Wei Ying.

Wei Ying merely glanced at him, and stood still, not bothering to engage him.

Outside the ship, the gold sea snake let out its last dying rasp.

When its body directly touched the Underworld River, it suffered boundless negative energy corrosion and quickly turned to snake bones, it had no strength to get out of it.

Wei Ying was still expressionless.

It was not his magic pet.
