Furthermore, the battle had already been determined, the gold sea snake had made its contribution.

Wei Ying continued, “I haven’t attacked yet, I want to give you a single chance, Jian Gui.”

“Sign a magic contract, serve me.”

“I can spare your life.”

Xiao Wang Zi’s body trembled, he who had lost all will to fight suddenly got a new hope.

Xiao Wang Zi was scared of death.

Despite being undead.

He was more afraid of never seeing his father and mother again.

Therefore, he wanted to survive.

But his open mouth didn’t say anything, because he knew that Jian Gui was the one who decided his end.

Ku Feng coldly snorted and was unmoved.

Compared to Xiao Wang Zi, he was one who endured millstones and was an experienced fighter.

Meanwhile, Cang Xu’s corpse was already cold, he was laying on the floor, his corpse swaying along with the ship.

After a good while, Jian Gui hoarsely replied, “Serve…so you wanted to capture me alive.”

Wei Ying smiled, “A living necromancer is naturally far more valuable than a dead one.”

“No need to doubt my sincerity, everyone knows that even the Sheng Ming Empire’s great noble clans are also covertly cultivating necromancers.”

Jian Gui went silent again.

The ghost ship was sinking.

The Underworld River’s water was flowing through gaps in the hull.

The ghosts inside the ship were fluttering in a panic, all avoiding the river water.

But as more river water poured in, some ghosts were taken in by the Underworld River, and they absent-mindedly sank into the river, and were assimilated by it.

Very swiftly, the Underworld River’s water also permeated the cabin where Cang Xu’s body was.

The river water touched Cang Xu’s corpse, rapidly rising from his feet and ankles, then into the hole in the middle of his back, permeating into his body.

A moment later, Cang Xu suddenly opened his eyes and sat back up.

Chapter 473: Walking on the River

“Cough! Cough!”

Cang Xu opened his mouth wide and took unprecedented breaths.

His eyes were filled with bewilderment.

“I…didn’t I die?”

The bewilderment waned, and Cang Xu realized something, quickly checking his chest’s space pouch.

In the bag, he discovered the Death Substitute Puppet.

The survivors had purchased it from Two Eyes Island’s auction.

The Death Substitute Puppet looked like a doll, it was made of dark green wood, had yellow straw hair, and its doll eyes and nose were very crooked, giving it an eerie beauty.

Now, the Death Substitute Puppet had crumbled, its limbs and torso were crushed, and the biggest remainder was the shattered half of its squashed head.

Cang Xu and Zi Di had purchased the Death Substitute Puppet for the dragonman youngster to use.

To the survivors, the dragonman youngster was the most crucial person among them.

But before Cang Xu attended the undead gathering, the dragonman youngster considered the hereunto risks and gave the Death Substitute Puppet to him.

“The Death Substitute Puppet revived me.”

“No, the commander saved my life!”

In his post traumatic stress, Cang Xu felt a spurt of warmth.

“This is the Underworld River’s water?!” After realizing his situation, he seemed to be shocked by electricity and suddenly stood up.

Previously, he had looked into the Underworld River, and nearly lost his life, and now he was touching the Underworld River, what kind of terrible consequences would occur?

Cang Xu had eaten a loss then and had incomparable fear of the Underworld River. He jumped onto a bed and completely got out of the water.

“Huh?” The mysterious black clothed man subconsciously tilted to look toward Cang Xu’s direction.

When Cang Xu came back to life and revealed his life aura, the black clothed man instantly detected it to an extent.

“He came back to life?” The black clothed person was astonished for a moment but then guessed why.

“It seems to be that Death Substitute Puppet.” The black clothed man had obtained ample intelligence.

Cang Xu and Zi Di had purchased many things from the auction, the Death Substitute Puppet was one of them.

Many people knew this information.

The black clothed man and Lady Sea Snake had been following Cang Xu’s trail and wouldn’t miss such important information.

The black clothed man turned back around and ignored Cang Xu.

A mere silver level was nothing to worry about.

The black clothed man pondered: if he could easily kill Cang Xu once, then he could do it a second time.

He was powerful and turned a deaf ear to Xiao Wang Zi and Ku Feng near him, he only had one real target.

It was Jian Gui!

“Jian Gui, what is your decision?” The black clothed man interrogated.

Jian Gui heavily sighed, “Ku Feng, put away your weapon.”

Ku Feng’s eyes narrowed.

Jian Gui continued, “I know you don’t fear death, but at this moment, unnecessary sacrifice is pointless.”

Ku Feng clenched his teeth, but ultimately still sheathed his blade.

Jian Gui weakly said, “I still don’t know where your distinguished self comes from, what power will I serve, or who you really are. If I surrender, what will I get for it?”

Those words made Jian Gui’s intention of surrendering very clear.

Xiao Wang Zi’s mind shook.

The black clothed man’s mouth quivered up into a smile.

The atmosphere in the ship cabin greatly eased.

Cang Xu, standing on a bed, was watching the Underworld River’s water quickly rising.

The ghost ship is leaking, it is sinking!”

“But Jian Gui isn’t repairing it.”

“What’s going on?”

“What will happen after I die?”

Cang Xu looked towards the cabin where the black clothed man, Xiao Wang Zi, and Ku Feng were.

Despite walls blocking his line of sight, he could still distinctly make out the three life auras.

“There is no fighting, it seems they are in a standoff?” Cang Xu still didn’t know the situation.

But that didn’t hinder him from acting first.

He took out a flying spell magic scroll and tore it open.

His body floated up, then he flew out, maintaining a cautious distance from the Underworld River’s water.

Cang Xu left the bed, pushed up the cabin door, then followed the ship’s ladder to the deck.

He fell onto the deck, ice-cold from head to toe with a spell of dark dizziness!

It was a simple flying spell, yet it had reduced Cang Xu to this state.

Although he returned to life, he was in an exceedingly terrible condition!

Cang Xu hurriedly examined himself.

He was horrified to find that his left chest had a hole in it, and that his heart had been obliterated by the black clothed man’s stab.

His entire body was full of intense negative energy.

It was very clear that this negative energy had come from the Underworld River’s water, and it was wildly eroding Cang Xu’s body and soul.

There was also a dark green power that was barely holding Cang Xu alive.

Most of the dark green power was lingering in Cang Xu’s left chest, substituting his heart, and keeping Cang Xu alive.

At the same time, there was also threads of green light stretching from his heart like tree roots and vines into Cang Xu’s internal organs, limbs, and bones.

“This is the Death Substitute Puppet’s power, the Voodoo Church’s divine spell’s power.”

After Cang Xu examined himself, he immediately noticed that the dark green power was visibly waning.

Although he had gotten out of the Underworld River’s water, that soaking had flooded Cang Xu’s broken body with a tremendous amount of negative energy.

“This negative energy will drag me into death’s embrace again!”

“What should I do?”

Cang Xu’s forehead filled with cold sweat, and he impatiently pondered how he should get out of this trouble.

He first thought of the Karma Reduction and Burden Alleviation spells.

They came from Jian Gui’s magician auxiliary book, which he still had in his hands, and wasn’t grabbed by the black clothed man.

But the Karma Reduction and Burden Alleviation spells inside it were already spent.

Cang Xu couldn’t cast them himself.

“Right, there is still medicine!” Cang Xu rushed his spirit into his storage bag and took out some medicine.

He drank the medicine or poured it on the hole in his heart.

But the medicine’s effects were quite terrible.

“There are too few medicines suitable for the undead.”

“Now that my body is full of negative energy, it has greatly reduced the effects of this medicine.”

The medicine Zi Di had refined were mostly bronze and iron level, with a few silver level medicine.

As for gold level medicine, she was currently unable to refine any.

The medicine did not alleviate Cang Xu’s terrible state much.

He took out some black crystals.

The crystals were long or short, big or small, but they all had the same strip shape. They were very dark and emitted an inexplicable chill, it was soul crystals.

It was very simple to use soul crystals.

Cang Xu simply grabbed one, then used his spirit to connect to them to receive the soul crystal’s nourishment.

The soul crystal in his hand rapidly shrunk like an ice cube in heat, before fading into nothing.

Cang Xu used several soul crystals, and his soul was greatly nourished, forcefully stirring his spirit.

“I feel a bit better.” Cang Xu’s dizzy spell was greatly weakened.

But the problem of negative energy hasn’t been solved yet.

Despite the soul crystals strengthening his soul’s foundation, it didn’t solve his peril.

At that moment, a skeleton magician flew over to Cang Xu.

“Newcomer, I have come to help you!” The one who came was Xiao Wang Zi.

After Cang Xu had revived, he could sense the others’ life auras, and of course, Xiao Wang Zi could sense Cang Xu’s life aura too.

Cang Xu’s life aura was very weak, like a candle in the wind.

Xiao Wang Zi was under immense psychological pressure near Wei Ying, and felt quite unwell, so he might as well courageously try to fly out of the ship cabin and assist Cang Xu.

When Cang Xu saw Xiao Wang Zi, he quickly asked about the battle.

Xiao Wang Zi’s tone was heavy, “The enemy is too strong, but we won’t die by his hands. They are currently negotiating with Lord Jian Gui over his surrender.”

Cang Xu’s eyes flashed.

The circumstances didn’t surprise him, and he guessed what was going on.

“If Jian Gui reaches a compromise and surrenders, then I will become a captive too.”

“Alas, I am still too weak.”

The situation was beyond him.

Cang Xu quickly cast off his distracting thoughts, now was not the time to be worrying about the future, if his problem wasn’t dealt with, then death would be before him.

Necromancy spell – Karma Reduction spell!

Necromancy spell – Burden Alleviation spell!

Xiao Wang Zi continuously used magic on Cang Xu.

Cang Xu’s descent into the abyss of death slowed greatly, but his trend towards it didn’t change.

Xiao Wang Zi was very grave, “Not good, newcomer, you touched the Underworld River’s water, there is too much negative energy.”

“This energy has not just permeated your body, actually most of it has poured into your soul.”

“If you hadn’t transformed your body, then you would have been assimilated by the Underworld River already!”

“In light of the speed of this, I cannot save you.”

“Lord Jian Gui, help me, save the newcomer.” Xiao Wang Zi raised his head and shouted, calling for help as candidly and proficiently as always.

In the ship cabin.

When Wei Ying heard Xiao Wang Zi’s call for help, he laughed, “Jian Gui, do you want to save him? Then sign my magic contract first.”

If Jian Gui acted and used magic to aid Cang Xu, then he’d have to show himself.

When that happened, Wei Ying could act as he wished.

Wei Ying treated Cang Xu as a bargaining chip to blackmail Jian Gui, “I heard that normally, you typically treat the undead by your side very well and with consideration.”

“Anyways, you are an orthodox undead, of You Shen’s direct line, and keeping an eye on other undead is what orthodox people like yourself should do.”

Jian Gui interrupted Wei Ying, “Who are you actually, sire, how do you know my identity?”

Wei Ying sneered, “You have held an undead gathering on Weeping Pine Island no less than ten times already.”

“A lot of information on it was quite easy to obtain.”

“That you could take out soul crystals to sell and have a steady external supply proves that you know how to make soul crystals.”

“This is the most distinct trait of orthodox undead.”

“Don’t ask about my history and identity again. I won’t tell you anything but this, my force is far more powerful than yours!”

Jian Gui immediately responded, “My founder, Lord You Shen is a legend level magician, and although Fire Beard is also a legend level too, he is just a single fighter. The necromancer’s power is not as outward as the Fire Beard Pirates, but its hidden strength far exceeds imagination!”

Wei Ying coldly snorted, “Unnecessary to carry out such pointless prying.”

“Jian Gui, my patience is spent.”

“Come out.”

“Agree and sign my contract, and I will spare your life.”

“Otherwise, I will smash your ghost ship and destroy your people!”

Jian Gui deeply sighed, “I am, in the end, a gold level caster, and also a necromancer. If surrendering and becoming your slave is worse than death, then I am better off simply dying in battle.”

Wei Ying gave off a strong killing intent, “You still want to discuss conditions with me?”

“I will destroy your ghost ship first, and let you recognize reality!”

“Reality?” Suddenly, a strange voice could be heard in the distance, “It is you who should recognize that.”

In the wake of that voice, Cang Xu, Xiao Wang Zi, and the others on deck were shocked to see a silhouette walking on the Underworld River.

Chapter 474: Flying Head Spell

The transcendent walking on the river had short silver-gray hair and deep blue eyes.

He appeared ordinary, had gray skin, and black surgical thread scattered across his skin. Regardless of whether it was his check, nape, arm, palm or any other parts of his body, all of it had been stitched together with thread long and short.

His clothes were an ashen gray magician gown, the gown had two long sleeves, wide cuffs, and as he walked, his arms naturally tugged at the cuffs, swinging them about.

A saint level aura was brimming all about and covering everything.

Cang Xu was shocked, he had sunk into a life threatening crisis when he touched the water, while this person was walking across its surface.

“A saint level necromancer!”

“It is said that a saint level transcendent understands laws and can form their personal domain that can vie with nasty external influences.”

“Who is he really?”

A moment later, Xiao Wang Zi’s shout answered the puzzlement in Cang Xu’s thoughts.

Xiao Wang Zi pliantly shouted, “It’s Lord Doctor Sui Shi!”

The one who had come was You Shen’s personal apprentice, the saint level necromancer – Doctor Sui Shi.

Ku Feng had been brought back as an undead by his hands.

What was odd was that when Ku Feng saw Doctor Sui Shi, he didn’t match Xiao Wang Zi’s feelings, instead his eyes exposed both pleasant surprise and also other feelings.

In the cabin, Wei Ying’s face radically shifted, and he was no longer as calm, “You were stalling for time?”

Jian Gui sneered, “You know I am an orthodox undead, of You Shen’s direct line. But it seems you didn’t really understand the meaning of those two things.”

“Very well, you’ve escaped this time, wait and see until next time!” After Wei Ying coldly threw out those words, he activated the Shadow Monk Sacred Emblem.

A moment later, space fluctuated, showing a half meter long chink.

From that chink came dense shadows.

Wei Ying’s entire being sank into the shadows.

The shadow rapidly flowed back into the spatial chink and Wei Ying vanished without a trace.

A second later, the spatial chink was completely gone, and space was returned to normal.

Doctor Sui Shi arrived at the ghost ship, then lightly jumped aboard onto the deck.

“He actually runs pretty fast.” Doctor Sui Shi sensed the spatial fluctuation and Wei Ying’s aura completely vanishing.

“Lord Doctor Sui Shi!” Xiao Wang Zi yelled, even though a skull couldn’t show facial expressions, his adoration and esteem were exhibited in his words.

As one of the twelve saints, Doctor Sui Shi just appearing was enough to scare away the enemy that had nearly taken them all captive.

That kind of style and disposition was something Xiao Wang Zi bowled over for.

From Doctor Sui Shi’s body, one could glimpse a trace of Founder You Shen’s graceful bearing.

Doctor Sui Shi nodded to Xiao Wang Zi, looked at Ku Feng and Cang Xu, then froze his gaze on the ghost ship’s deck.

After that, he began to chant and use necromancy.

The magic raised a billow with vast grandeur.

The magic’s power all flowed into the ghost ship.

The ghost ship was damaged beyond reckoning, but the magic quickly restored it.

The ship’s holes were all patched and no longer leaked.

So much so that the Underworld River’s water was also pulled out of the ship by Doctor Sui Shi and back into the Underworld River.

Doctor Sui Shi used three spells, none of which Cang Xu was able to discern what was going on.

After Doctor Sui Shi stopped casting, Jian Gui’s main body appeared on the deck.

Most of his injuries had been healed, and he was no longer near death.

Jian Gui deeply saluted Doctor Sui Shi, “Lord Doctor Sui Shi, many thanks for your help this time. Please save us one more time, the ghost ship’s magic power reservoir is empty, and I cannot replenish by myself.”

The magic power reserves were inadequate, and the ghost ship was unable to use Ghost World Traversal to return to the Main Plane.

The ultimate ending waiting for them was to be lost in the Ghost World, completely forfeit their original consciousness, and turn into muddleheaded beings of the Ghost World.

Doctor Sui Shi laughed, “That can be done.”

He extended his palm, exposing a black threaded seam in the center of his palm.

Then, he extended his other hand’s index finger and softly cut the black thread with his sharp fingernail.

The black thread was untied, forming a wound, from which ashen flesh quickly opened up on either side, showing not the bones of the hand, but rather a pitch-black space.

Doctor Sui Shi aimed his palm at the deck and mobilized his magic power.

A moment later, a majestic magic power flowed from his palm cut and directly into the ghost ship.

The magic power even lifted a gale with astonishing presence.

“Full, full!” Jian Gui called out a few seconds later.

The saint level magician had astonishing magic power reserves, and filling up a silver level magic ship was the flick of a finger for Doctor Sui Shi.

Jian Gui expressed his gratitude again.

Doctor Sui Shi nodded faintly, “Your luck’s pretty good, I have been exploring the Ghost World for over three months already and was planning to leave when I received your distress call.”

“Although I had your ghost ship’s spatial coordinates, long-distance teleportation spells are not very precise.”

“Luckily, you weren’t harmed that much.”

Thanks to Doctor Sui Shi’s timely assistance, although the necromancers had lost, they didn’t lose that much, by contrast, the pirates had enormous losses.

Xiao Wang Zi shook his arm in cheer, “Lord Doctor Sui Shi, if you came even a moment too late, we would have had a tragic end.”

Ku Feng saluted, “Your Lordship.”

Doctor Sui Shi looked at Ku Feng, “It seems you have been getting by decently these last years.”

Ku Feng expressed his gratitude, “Thanks to your Lordship’s actions, I was given a second life that allowed me to keep pursuing the path of the blade.”

Doctor Sui Shi shook his head, “You were designed in the spur of the moment and can only be considered mediocre. What a pity…I lacked some materials then, leading to your subpar aptitude, otherwise, you could have become an orthodox undead too.”

Ku Feng bitterly laughed, “To be able to regain my life is enough to satisfy me, chasing the path of the blade is my life’s purpose, regardless of the final outcome.”

Finally, Cang Xu got a chance to interject, “I say everyone, can you help me? I am dying!”

Xiao Wang Zi cried out in alarm, and he asked Jian Gui for help, “Lord Jian Gui, you have to save the newcomer now, I was unable to.”

Compared to Doctor Sui Shi, Xiao Wang Zi was closer to Jian Gui and didn’t dare to ask for the saint necromancer’s help

Jian Gui nodded and didn’t refuse the request.

Although he had treated Cang Xu and Xiao Wang Zi as bait during the battle, the situation had been at a desperate moment.

Looking after himself and abandoning others was human nature.

Now that the situation had calmed down, Jian Gui was no longer as stingy.

However, just as he was about to cast, Doctor Sui Shi prevented him, “Wait a moment.”

Jian Gui paused.

Doctor Sui Shi sized up Cang Xu with interest, gazing at his chest, “Your condition is a little rare, you have sparked my inspiration.”

“Let me remake you.”

Jian Gui was slightly startled.

Xiao Wang Zi replied with a cheer to congratulate Cang Xu, “Newcomer, I didn’t expect you to have a blessing in disguise! Being remade by Lord Doctor Sui Shi will make you stronger!”

Cang Xu also felt pleasantly surprised but quickly noticed Jian Gui’s and Ku Feng’s amiss expressions.

Cang Xu rushed to ask, “May I ask, what is the transformation success rate?”

Doctor Sui Shi excitedly shrugged his shoulders, saying irresponsibly, “I haven’t done any tests, this will be the first time, I can’t give an estimate.”

Xiao Wang Zi stared at him.

Jian Gui and Ku Feng held even stronger faces of agony.

Cang Xu hurriedly declined, “I want to be more cautious compared to getting stronger.”

Cang Xu had a prudent disposition and naturally didn’t want to take risks. He avoided Doctor Sui Shi’s gaze and looked at Jian Gui.

But Jian Gui avoided his eyes.

Doctor Sui Shi shook his head and smiled, “No one will come and save you, little magician.”

“On your own, you’re as good as dead.”

“You only have one road, that is to obediently work with me and become my test subject.”

Cang Xu’s heart sank into the depths, and resentment boiled out, but he quickly calmed down.

Cang Xu knew he had no other choice.

He nodded and weakly replied, “Then please do it Lord Doctor Sui Shi!”

Doctor Sui Shi lightly raised a hand, and with his spirit power, bound Cang Xu and raised him into the air.

Doctor Sui Shi extended his other hand and moved his fingers.

Magic was used in a second and hadn’t been stored, it was a silent casting technique.

The five fingers’ sharp aura lightly cut Cang Xu’s body everywhere from a distance.

A moment later, Cang Xu’s four limbs were severed and fell to the floor.

Cang Xu blankly stared, then opened his mouth and exploded with a miserable scream.

Pain, so painful!

He had never experienced such pain before, one far worse than pain that penetrated the soul, it was hard to keep himself composed and he could only vent it through his wretched howls.

Xiao Wang Zi was dumbstruck, and his heart went cold.

He saw that not only did Cang Xu’s severed limbs fall to the floor, but his soul had also been severed too, sealed inside each of the limbs.

It was this soul cutting that caused such unimaginably terrible pain.

“A little noisy.” Doctor Sui Shi was apathetic and lightly cut with his fingers again.

Cang Xu’s head left his neck and fell to the deck, tumbling in a circle.

Doctor Sui Shi did not care about Cang Xu’s head and cast a spell in passing.

Despite Cang Xu still mournfully howling, his voice was already gone and no longer spread.

“The impurity is removed, only the main material is left.” Doctor Sui Shi clicked his tongue, carefully taking measure of Cang Xu’s body with interest.

Soon after, a treasure was spat out of the wound in his palm.

This treasure looked like a frog statue, it was the size of a washbowl, and it emitted a gold level aura.

It was a sacred item of the Voodoo Church!

A moment later, Doctor Sui Shi used magic to crush it into slag.

Without the sacred item, the divine energy hidden inside it became visible.

The large dark green divine energy was spiritually guided by Doctor Sui Shi into Cang Xu’s body.

The dark green energy inside Cang Xu’s body was massively replenished, and a split second later, the negative energy inside it was eliminated and Cang Xu’s torso swelled.

Cang Xu’s toro swelled further, like a balloon, it seemed full enough to burst apart.

Doctor Sui Shi slightly raised his eyebrow, “Little magician, your aptitude is too low.”

Doctor Sui Shi had no choice but to add an extra investment and took out a palm from his wound.

This severed palm looked like a claw, it had exposed veins and was very sinister, it too emitted a gold level aura.

Doctor Sui Shi poured in his magic power, and the paw immediately reacted, its appearance grew fuzzy, and it rapidly melted into a dripping green.

Doctor Sui Shi used spiritual control to drip the green drops onto Cang Xu’s torso.

Then, Cang Xu’s limbs received drops.

The green drops extended across the body, forming a viscous green coating that stopped the torso from exploding from inflation.

Doctor Sui Shi cast once again.

After the transformation continued for a few minutes, he used his spirit to lift up Cang Xu’s limbs and attached them back.

Once connected back, the four limbs also began to rapidly swell, full of the voodoo divine energy.

Very quickly, there was a giant balloon floating in the air, a balloon with four smaller balloons attached to it.

“Next is the most crucial step.” Doctor Sui Shi exposed a serious look for the first time.

He took out another thing from his palm.

It was a zombie head.

“Open.” Doctor Sui Shi undid the head’s seal, revealing that the zombie head’s original size was gigantic, the size of a carriage.

“This is one of my collector’s items, a gold level voodoo zombie.”

“Killing this kind of this is very easy but cutting off its head and keeping its soul and the voodoo divine energy inside it isn’t as easy.

Doctor Sui Shi sighed and placed the gigantic voodoo zombie head onto Cang Xu’s body.

The gigantic zombie’s eyes were originally closed, then the instant it was connected, its eyes suddenly opened with cold killing intent!

But Doctor Sui Shi suppressed it at the same time as that, and the gigantic zombie’s eyes had no choice but to close again.

The dense voodoo divine energy inside the gigantic zombie head and Cang Xu’s body quickly formed a link.

Cang Xu’s body continued to swell, but this time, with the gigantic zombie head’s influence, it no longer looked like a balloon, but rather an enormous body and limbs.

“A great success!” Doctor Sui Shi burst into laughter.

Jian Gui and the others congratulated him.

Doctor Sui Shi looked at Cang Xu’s head and lightly snapped his fingers.

Magic was used, and Cang Xu’s soul calmed down and no longer felt pain, while simultaneously stopping the harm the Underworld River’s water brought.

In addition, knowledge passed through spiritual power was poured into Cang Xu.

“This is a small result of my research.”

“Consider it a reward for me taking your body.”

Cang Xu was dazed, and in the moment of unconsciousness, swiftly looked at the knowledge Doctor Sui Shi was imparting onto him.

“Flying Head spell…”

Chapter 475: Giant Type Intelligence Potion

The sky was azure, and the seawater was dark green.

The air temperature was very low.

The sky had snow fluttering in it, and mercenaries were trembling with cold.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ fleet had finally reached Ice Sculpture Island.

“It seems that we need to procure warm clothes.” Said the dragonman youngster.

He had the Flame Dragon King bloodline, and his strong and tough dragon scales maintained his body temperature well.

Zong Ge sized up the harbor ahead of them.

He also didn’t feel much with regards to the temperature. He looked human, but that was just the Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spells at work.

Zong Ge was a half beastman, and had a lion-man bloodline, his thick hair was a natural protection against the cold.

This was Snow Bird Harbor, one of Ice Sculpture Island’s eight harbors.

Snow Bird Harbor had a flat terrain that stretched as far as the eye could see.

The harbor was a bustling sight, ships of all sizes were anchored there, and the many standing masts permitted flocks of white feathered snow birds to sit on them.

Zong Ge’s gaze mainly focused on the harbor’s military defenses.

The harbor’s embankments had a row of artillery, the biggest main cannon was gold level, while the rest were uniformly silver level.

A small distance from the harbor stood a lofty magician tower.

The magician tower was an impressive gold level too!

With that magician tower, Snow Bird Harbor’s defensive power far exceeded Two Eyes Island’s.

Laborers towed the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ships slowly into mooring positions.

Anchors dropped, and after the ships were berthed appropriately, the laborers boarded to receive their payment.

The anchorage fee was very expensive.

The small ship anchorage fee was three times the normal price.

The medium ship anchorage fee was four times the normal price.

The large ship anchorage fee was unexpectedly five times the normal price!

“Excuse me, may I ask why the ship, ship anchorage fee is so expensive?” Fei She asked a laborer.

The laborer answered: it is due to a recent adjustment.

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom intentionally raised the anchorage expense of foreign ships by a lot.

While the citizen’s ships’ anchorage fees were kept at the same low price.

After Fei She heard this, he faintly nodded and looked pensive.

The high ship anchorage fee was nothing to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. They cared more about the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s attitude towards outsiders.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had already made the following arrangements.

Zong Ge would continue overseeing the fleet and defend the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ships.

Almost all of the mercenaries would remain on the ships.

If it was any other mercenary corps, the mercenaries would be in an uproar. But in the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, one trained by Zong Ge and with strong military discipline, it wouldn’t happen.

The dragonman youngster and a group split off and were the first to enter the harbor.

Men Shi and Tripleblade were responsible for reporting to the Mercenary Guild Branch. The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was registered as a regular mercenary corps, and in every region they moved to, they could register at the local Mercenary Guild Branch to gain some support.

Meanwhile, Chi Lai and Lan Zao needed to go to the Thief Guild Branch to purchase more information.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps wanted detailed and reliable information on the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, this was a convenient and fast way to do so. Of course, it had its shortcomings – buying information cost money.

The third group was the dragonman youngster and Zi Di, they wanted to investigate the marketplace.

Snow Bird Harbor’s marketplace was obviously rigorously mapped out, it was divided into 5 parts, and the biggest among them was the seafood market.

Of course, the seafood market was not their first destination, they were entering the most highly detailed alchemy market.

The alchemy market had great numbers of stores and brightly colored signs.

The shop buildings were of a unified design, and consequently, they warmed the heart and delighted the eyes.

Red buildings, white walls, yellow roofs.

Zi Di recognized them and pointed a finger at the yellow roofs, saying to the dragonman youngster, "That is Light Clear Potion, that potion is fellow and can be used to coat roofs. In the day, it can absorb sunlight, then at night, it can illuminate the rooms inside.”

“Although a lot of potion is applied in the early stages, its usage cycle is long, and the cost spread out over that time makes it a bargain.”

The roof coating was just one thing; the entire alchemy market had many practical applications of alchemy throughout it that functioned during the day.

The alchemy market had streams of people moving through it, it was mainly composed of elves, followed by humans, then dwarfs, beastmen and other races were comparatively rare.

Thus, the dragonman youngster was quite eye-catching as he walked.

Pedestrians often looked at him.

The dragonman youngster and Zi Di walked together.

They had seldom moved alone like this, and when they looked at each other, they could sense the others’ affection.

Seeing Zi Di at his side, the dragonman youngster’s finger twitched, and in his heart, the impulse to pull Zi Di’s hand along rose.

Despite Zi Di’s outward identity being Hong Teng, and that if the mercenary commander and a subordinate magician loving each other and holding hands together was seen in public, it would have a great adverse influence.

A small girl decided to walk in front of them, holding a snow bird figurine, “Big older brother, purchase a snow bird for your girlfriend.”

The dragonman youngster stared at them.

The small girl added, “Snow birds represent pure love in our Ice Sculpture Kingdom.”

The dragonman youngster and Zi Di looked at each other, making out the others’ feelings.

But the dragonman youngster refused, “You are mistaken, girl, we don’t have that kind of relationship.”

The small girl didn’t give up and still tried, “Not now, but that doesn’t mean it won’t happen in the future. To be exact, the snow bird represents the nascent arrival of pure love!”

The dragonman youngster couldn’t help but look at Zi Di.

But Zi Di didn’t look at him, instead she turned her gaze away.

The small girl giggled and smiled.

The dragonman youngster slowly shook his head and fished out 10 copper coins.

The small girl’s eyes opened, “Mr. Dragonman, that’s not enough money!”

The dragonman youngster replied, “This money isn’t for the snow bird figurine. If you can help us familiarize ourselves with the stores here and tell us everything you know, then these 10 copper coins will be yours.”

The small girl’s eyes narrowed, and didn’t agree to that, instead after some pondering, she asked, “So, may I ask how long Mr. Dragonman wants to hire me?”

The dragonman youngster didn’t reply, instead he looked at Zi Di.

Zi Di gave her assessment, “In my opinion, two hours with you will be more than enough.”

The girl nodded, “In that case, Mr. Dragonman, Miss Magician, this snow girl is ready to serve you.”

The small girl temporarily joined the two.

Whenever the two visited a store, the small girl would tell them some information outsiders didn’t know about it.

“This store looks small, but its business is very good. Natives regularly visit!”

“This inn’s boss is very sinister and stingy; they often are behind on salaries and also like to scam outsiders.”

“This is our biggest medicine shop, unfortunately, it is not one of our Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s stores.”

Finally, the dragonman youngster and Zi Di stopped in front of the biggest store.

The store’s sign had four words written on it – Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance!

One of the Sheng Ming Empire’s six great merchant alliances.

Not only was the Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance here on Ice Sculpture Island, the other five great merchant alliances also had many branches across the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. On the way here, the dragonman youngster and Zi Di had seen the Gem and Scale Merchant Alliance stores.

The three entered the Secret Medicine store.

The store employees inside the store were nearly all snow elves, but higher up positions in it were all staffed by humans.

Zi Di’s mind stirred, and she silently and attentively how the Secret Medicine branch was arranged, what kind of merchandise was on sale, along with at what scale and at what price.

Currently, although the survivors had a lot of gold, they lacked a steady income.

Selling medicine should be the most probable way to bring about that channel of earnings.

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s medicine market was always of an impressive scale.

On Two Eyes Island, Zi Di had improved prescriptions and sold medicine to make a considerable profit.

This kind of success could be duplicated.

Zi Di was confident that with the Deep Sea Monster Fish’s magic potion production capacity and War Merchant’s vast database of recipes, no matter which type she produced in, it would be competitive in the marketplace.

The dragonman youngster attracted the attention of the salespeople when he walked in.

His tall and sturdy body, silver level aura, and his female magician companion drew attention to his potentially formidable purchasing power.

Seeing a snow elf salesperson wearing a smile, and walking over, the dragonman youngster deliberately said to Zi Di, “Hong Teng, you are the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ expert on making medicine, can you take a look at what the mercenaries need from here? Purchase a lot, the mercenary corps’ funds are to be used in this aspect.”

“You two, please come here!” After the snow elf salesperson heard that, their smile grew more cordial.

Zi Di came to the sales counter, carefully observing all kinds of medicine while asking the price, comparing it to the market price, understanding and analyzing the concrete market situation.

The Secret Medicine store had five floors.

Every floor had large amounts of potions for sale.

On the first floor, Zi Di even saw some barbarian race herbal medicine, making clear that the Secret Medicine store offered a large and comprehensive selection of medicine.

After Zi Di bought some, she went to the second floor and saw tons of iron level medicine.

Then on the third floor, the medicine on sale was silver level on average.

On the fourth floor, the quantity of the magic potions was far lower than the third, but the quality was gold level!

Hun Tong had a flourishing commerce on Two Eyes Island, but if one wanted to buy a gold level potion, it was only possible during an auction.

In the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, gold level medicine was in steady supply.

“Heavens there really is a giant type intelligence potion! Lord Bing Xiao, I want this potion as my birthday present.”

“Very good, that type of intelligence potion isn’t common, and it can be used on your giant slave.”

These words were heard by the dragonman youngster and Zi Di.

The two were immediately interested and rushed over.

One was a human girl; the other was an old snow elf man.

They were the two that wanted to buy the giant type intelligence potion.

The salesperson serving them was just about to hand the carrot thick medicine bottle over to the old man.

The dragonman youngster walked up to them, “We also need that kind of intelligence potion.”

But the salesperson shook their head, “So sorry, Mr. Dragonman, we only have one in stock.”

The dragonman youngster was stunned.

Zi Di rushed over after him and smiled, “How much is that intelligence potion, we will purchase it!”

When the girl heard that, she immediately cried out, “Hey, we wanted to buy it.”

Zi Di laughed again, “But you haven’t paid yet, right? Since it hasn’t been sold yet, we have a chance.”

The old man coldly snorted, and his brows tightly creased, saying with annoyance, “Who are you to think of interrupting my purchase, do you know what the consequences are of that?”

Chapter 476: Obtaining the Potion

The old elf man showed a vile attitude, somewhat more than what the dragonman youngster and Zi Di expected.

Normally, annoyance was certain, but it was unnecessary to show such strong hostility.

Zi Di was faintly startled, and she looked at the old man, her face layered with a smile as she asked, “Senior, may I ask what your name is?”

The old elf man coldly snorted and arrogantly refused to answer.

His human companion held up her head and looked at Zi Di and the dragonman youngster as if they were bumpkins, “You don’t know about Lord Bing Xiao? Where did you come from? Who are you?”

Zi Di’s smile did not fall, and she replied, “We just arrived at Snow Bird Harbor.”

The dragonman youngster looked at the small girl with them.

The small girl avoided his gaze and cowed out an answer, “That is Lord Bing Xiao, the previous leader of the Bayonet Gang.”

“Bayonet Gang?” Zi Di was puzzled.

The small girl continued, “The Bayonet Gang is the biggest local gang.”

The dragonman youngster felt a bit surprised, this was the first time he had come across a gang.

Thinking about it carefully, the past islands the survivors landed on were quite small.

Snake Rat Island hadn’t been developed for long and hadn’t formed any gangs on it yet.

As for Two Eyes Island, Feudal Lord Hun Tong’s faction was the strongest, and he had complete control over his territory. There was only one religion in it, it was the Wealth Church’s parish. Adding in Two Eyes Island’s special nature, where half of it would sink into the ocean or float back up every so often, it was quite difficult for a gang to develop and expand on its soil.

Ice Sculpture Island was different.

It had grown for a very long time, and those settled here not only included a traditional royal family, various officials, a standing army, and more, but also there were mercenaries, thieves, and other types of people moving through the street corners.

When Zi Di learned of this information, her eyes could help but narrow, and she pondered to herself: we’re fresh off the boat, it is inadvisable to offend the local tyrant.

Zi Di laughed, “We really need that intelligence potion, we didn’t intend to clash with you two…”

Before Zi Di could finish speaking, the old elf man’s female companion interrupted, “My giant slave needs it more! We’ll be taking this intelligence potion!”

Zi Di slightly grinded her teeth and looked at the salesclerk, “Do you know if we can place an order?”

The salesclerk blurted out, “You can, you can do that. The producer of this potion is Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s famous Master Alchemist Hua Ni. Our merchant alliance has a firm relationship with her and has maintained close connections. As long as you place an order, you can get one in half a month.”

“Half a month?” Zi Di pursed her brows.

The salesperson honestly replied, “That is the fastest time possible.”

Zi Di laughed, “We can add money.”

The salesperson looked distressed, “Master Hua Ni cannot be rushed, guest, you are also a magician. You presumably know more of a master’s temperament.”

The old elf man Bing Xiao was utterly impatient, “Ok, stop dillydallying. I still want to catch tonight’s opera and continuing this will sour my mood. You can get lost, if you want to buy anything, you can come tomorrow, I don’t want to see you anymore.”

The human female echoed that, “Heard that? Why aren’t you leaving already?”

Then he disdainfully sized up the dragonman youngster and Zi Di, “Bumpkins who have never seen the world, how can money move Master Hua Ni’s temperament?”

Zi Di’s face sank.

The dragonman youngster suddenly chuckled, “Makes sense.”

“Since that is so…” The dragonman youngster dragged out his tone, “Then I should purchase the one before me, isn’t that correct?”

The dragonman youngster looked at the salesperson, “You are the Secret Medicine branch store, how much is this potion?”

“If you are afraid of Lord Bing Xiao and won’t dare to sell it to another, then that’s fine.”

“You?!” The human female glared at the dragonman youngster with shock.

The old elf man’s expression was gloomier than Zi Di’s.

The salesperson was a snow elf and was a little scared of the dragonman youngster’s deliberate bullying and didn’t dare to speak.

But he quickly responded with, “I will ask the store manager for instructions!”

The store manager’s voice could be heard, “No need to ask, I’m already here.”

The store manager looked ordinary, but he had a silver level fighter aura. He smiled and said, “Our Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance is one of the six great merchant alliances of the Sheng Ming Empire, and as always, our merchant alliances’ goods are sold impartially to the one who offers the highest price. This giant-type intelligence potion’s price is 360 gold. Anyone present can buy it. This is the potion’s detailed medicinal effect: it can substantially increase a giant’s intelligence for a short time. As time passes, the medicine will weaken until it expires entirely. However, the giant who takes this potion has a certain probability of improving their intelligence’s basis permanently.”

“Alright then.” The dragonman youngster looked at Zi Di, using his eyes to ask whether 360 gold was a fair price.

The two had an unspoken understanding and had used their eyes to communicate many times.

Zi Di nodded faintly to the dragonman youngster, indicating that the price was reasonable.

A gold level intelligence potion had a lower price to a healing potion of the same level. Because the latter was almost tantamount to a second life in critical moments.

300 gold could buy the vast majority of high quality silver level armor.

Therefore, this price didn’t shame its gold level quality.

The dragonman youngster didn’t hesitate, “Since that is the case, I will offer 360 gold.”

His gaze changed from the store manager to the old elf man.

The female human reacted by screeching, “You really dare to buy it, you really have the nerve. You actually don’t care about Lord Bing Xiao!”

Bing Xiao sneered, and sized up the dragonman youngster again, “Young outsider, you are very courageous.”

Then, the old elf man glanced at the store manager and bid, “400 gold.”

The dragonman youngster followed a split second later, “500 gold.”

The female human’s heart jumped, she didn’t expect the dragonman youngster to add on 100 gold.

Bing Xiao deeply said, “600 gold.”

Likewise, he too raised the price by 100 gold.

The dragonman youngster softly chuckled, he crossed his arms and calmly said, “800 gold.”

This time, he raised the price by 200 gold, which caused the store manager to raise an eyebrow.

The female human was frightened, and she looked at the old elf man with worry.

The female human hugged the old elf man’s arm and squeezed it to her chest, softly rocking the old man as she threw a tantrum, “Lord Bing Xiao, I want that potion.”

Bing Xiao gloomily looked back, and gently clapped the back of her hand, “900 gold!”

The dragonman youngster smiled, and pressured calmly, “1,200 gold.”

300 gold!

He had increased the base price by 900 gold coins!

The female human turned pale, the price of 1,200 gold was enough for her to completely recognize the dragonman youngster’s strength, these two bumpkins were rich.

Bing Xiao rigidly stared at the dragonman youngster, his eyes not covering up his killing intent.

The dragonman youngster remained calm and used eyes filled with battle fervor and an upright chest to respond to Bing Xiao’s killing intent.

Bing Xiao finally cowered, a sinister smile now appearing on his face, “Young dragonman, you really are fiery and exuberant, and your wealth surprises me. Do you know how long you’ll be able to keep the potion you bought today safe?”

The dragonman youngster serenely responded, “Of course I can keep it safe, don’t trouble yourself about it.”

Bing Xiao coldly snorted, “We’re leaving.”

He had lost face and decided to leave with his human companion.

The store manager said to the salesperson, “See Lord Bing Xiao off.”

The salesperson hurriedly nodded and bowed, then left to see their customer out.

The dragonman youngster watched Bing Xiao and the others leave; his expression staunch from beginning to end.

If they wanted this potion in advance, they needed to wait at least half a month.

This was too long for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

The survivors didn’t have the ability to refine gold level medicine yet.

To Xiao Guai, this giant-type intelligence potion had to be gotten!

The dragonman youngster had an acute sense of looming danger in his mind at all times.

He hoped that he and the people at his side could grow and strengthen as quickly as possible.

Because they didn’t know when that gigantic, hidden crisis would burst out.

The survivors’ future was uncertain.

Of course, as a Justice God believer, the dragonman youngster didn’t have a good opinion of the old elf man.

It was rather difficult for a Justice God believer to think well of a gang member.

A simple and direct transaction.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was easily able to part with 1,200 gold.

“Finally got an intelligence potion.” Zi Di happily thought, thinking of the big guy’s bloodline, her heart filled with expectations as the store manager warned them, “You two should be more careful, especially at night, don’t go out then.”

“Perhaps, you can employ the Snow Cotton Mercenary Corps. The Snow Cotton is Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s biggest mercenary corps, their predecessor is the Kingdom’s Female Guard, they are very good at protecting people.”

The dragonman youngster shook his head, “No thanks, we are a mercenary corps.”

Chapter 477: Complete Battle Qi Cycle!

Snow Bird Harbor Mercenary Guild.

The vice branch president received Men Shi and Tripleblade.

“I have read about your mercenary corps, and as a small mercenary corps, you’re quite fearsome.”

“But you’re only ranked at one star, this rank is too low. Based on our rules and regulations, the Mercenary Guild can only offer limited support to you.”

“I don’t believe your corps should be at this star ranking.”

“If your corps can increase your star rating, then the support you will get will be considerable.”

“When you attain a five star rating, your corps can become a permanent resident of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. The treatment of your mercenaries will be nearly identical to that of our citizens. This Mercenary Guild branch will also provide a mercenary encampment here free of charge.”

Men Shi was moved somewhat by those words, so he asked, “How can we get our mercenary corps’ star rating to five stars?”

Men Shi didn’t know much about the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, and he really believed that he had joined an actual mercenary corps. At most he knew the mercenary corps had a rather deep background, and that it involved arms smugglers, something that should be mentioned excessively.

That question didn’t need to be answered by the vice branch president, Tripleblade could do that.

“The base standard to be a five star mercenary corps is to have a gold level transcendent. The second standard is that the mercenary corps needs to have at least two people.”

This was the world of the strong.

Regardless of any organization, when considering how their strength, it depended on the individual transcendents in them, the size was secondary.

When the vice branch president heard Tripleblade’s answer, they smiled, “Indeed.”

Apart from that, there is another short cut.”

“For example, recruiting a snow elf clan’s transcendent can obtain the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s royal family’s favor.”

“Through gaining the royal family’s special official approval, one can reduce the criteria in the Mercenary Guild Branch.”

“The Snow Cotton Mercenary Corps did that back in the day, at the time, they didn’t have a gold level, their head was only silver level. But through the royal family’s special approval, their just established mercenary corps became a five star mercenary corps.”

“Of course, nowadays, the Snow Cotton Mercenary Corps is worthy of being a five star corps. Their head and vice head are both gold levels.”

“Now, they are publicly known to be the strongest mercenary corps in all of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.”

Men Shi curiously asked, “Then, what are the requirements to become a six star mercenary corps?”

The vice branch president replied, “Very simple. Six star mercenary corps need to have a saint level transcendent.”

Men Shi clicked his tongue, “Is a saint level willing to be a mercenary?”

The vice branch president bitterly laughed.

Tripleblade answered, “That is the reason why six star mercenary corps are quite rare.”

Meanwhile.

The Snow Bird Harbor Thief Guild Branch.

Chi Lai, Lan Zao, and the vice branch president were conversing.

They were discussing the specific price of all kinds of information.

Chi Lai and Lan Zao were buying a large amount of information, and this naturally involved a great sum of money, causing the vice branch president to come out themselves to haggle over the price.

As both parties were talking, Chi Lai’s communication device rang.

After Chi Lai connected, the dragonman youngster gave him a new order.

“The commander wants us to gather information on the Bayonet Gang.” Chi Lai told Lan Zao.

The information trade began apace.

The dragonman youngster put away the communication device.

After they had left the Secret Medicine branch store, they immediately contacted Chi Lai, Men Shi, and Zong Ge, warning them to pay attention to possible trouble.

“Someone is shadowing us.” Said Zi Di.

The dragonman youngster nodded slightly, he looked around and saw three rogues stop in a second.

When the rogues saw that he had found them, they didn’t run. One even sneered at him and cut his hand across his throat in a threatening fashion.

It was clear these people had been dispatched by the old elf man, Bing Xiao.

They were all transcendents, two were bronze, one was iron level. When facing the silver level dragonman youngster, they didn’t fear him at all, brazenly tailing him with reckless abandon.

Zi Di couldn’t help but worry, “With this attitude, the Bayonet Gang seems to be a huge power in Snow Bird Harbor.”

The dragonman youngster’s gaze was calm, “No need to burden yourself, since the other side is this arrogant, it instead divulges that they won’t be attacking at least for the time being.”

Of course, the dragonman youngster wasn’t scared of a mere iron level and two bronze levels.

Zi Di wasn’t worried about her safety either.

Because she was a ghost, immune to physical harm. If it were not for the fact she was acting like a normal person, she could walk through walls.

Zi Di looked at the magician tower in the distance, regretfully saying, “We can’t attack either. Although there isn’t a magic prohibition environment here, that magician tower must be supervising the entirety of Snow Bird Harbor at all times. If we attack, magic images are bound to be recorded, and when it is investigated in the future, it will be impossible to disavow.”

“Since the Bayonet Gang is Snow Bird Harbor’s local tyrant, they must have secret relationships with officials large and small here.”

“We want to avoid conflict with Ice Sculpture Kingdom officials as much as possible, otherwise, we will be in a very passive state.”

The dragonman youngster nodded, “Let’s return to the ship first.”

The two smoothly returned to the dock.

Like the dragonman youngster surmised, the three following them tried to put more psychological pressure on them but never attacked.

To avoid further complications, the dragonman youngster decided to use the gold potion immediately.

He came to the ship bow house.

“Xiao Guai.” The dragonman youngster softly called out.

“Father.” The big guy happily said, but a moment later, he rushed to cover his mouth.

The dragonman youngster had told him he needed to pay attention to how he addressed him. Addressing him as father would easily expose the real relationship between them.

The big guy had forgotten this due to his delight.

“Whisper a bit.” The dragonman youngster hissed, fortunately, he had put down an acoustics case.

The alchemy device could prevent sound from spreading.

The big guy lowered his head and admitted his mistake, “Sorry, my mistake. Father, I won’t do it next time.”

The dragonman youngster took out the potion and gave it to the big guy, “Drink it.”

The big guy had complete trust in the dragonman youngster, and drank down the potion at once, till not even a trickle remained.

When the dragonman youngster saw this, a bit of worry sprouted in his relief.

“That won’t do, Xiao Guai.”

“If I was an enemy in disguise, you would be too easy to dupe.”

“We must set a countersign.”

“In the future, when it comes to important decisions and operations, you can use that countersign to state clearly who you are.”

“The countersign will also be changed every so often.”

The big guy nodded repeatedly, “Father, that is a good method, what is the countersign?”

After considering the big guy’s intelligence, the dragonman youngster said, “The first countersign is Hog’s Kiss.”

“Hog’s Kiss!” The big guy nodded, that was the ship he had spent many years on, and it naturally had a deep impression on him.

The dragonman youngster didn’t need to worry that he would forget the countersign.

However, to prevent accidents, the dragonman youngster made sure the big guy memorized the countersign.

“Now how do you feel?” The dragonman youngster reckoned that enough time had passed for the gold potion to take effect.

But the big guy still gave off the same impression as before.

The big guy clicked his tongue, “Feel, feel…a little sweet.”

When the dragonman youngster heard this, he suddenly became a bit apprehensive.

Why wasn’t the big guy feeling a bit smarter?

To be safe, the dragonman youngster decided to run a test.

He asked, “What does 33 multiplied by 7 equal to?”

“Huh?” The big guy scratched his head, showing perplexion, “Father, what is multiplied?”

The dragonman youngster: …

“I’ll change the question. How much is 48 plus 21?”

The big guy scratched his check and flicked his toes. After trying hard for a long time, he shamefully said to the dragonman youngster, “Father, Xiao Guai doesn’t know.”

The dragonman youngster took a deep breath, “12 plus 6?”

The big guy puffed hard, waggled his fingers and toes, and after a long while, he still looked confused.

Seeing this happening, the dragonman youngster’s heart chilled.

He contacted Zi Di to ask: did we really buy a gold level potion?

He felt like this was a fake!

“Impossible, I already tested it.” Zi Di denied the dragonman youngster’s guess.

Then at that moment, the big guy suddenly jumped up with glee, “It is 17, 17! Father, I calculated it!”

The dragonman youngster sank into a deep silence.

“Could the giant-type intelligence potion have been replaced after Zi Di tested it?” He couldn’t help but think.

But he soon rejected that notion in his heart, “Impossible. This intelligence potion has been in my hand for nearly the entire journey.”

“Then, there is only one possibility.”

The dragonman youngster profoundly gazed at the big guy, thinking, “How great is Xiao Guai’s intellect shortfall? That even a gold level intelligence potion specialized for giants is not enough to fill it?”

“Father, did I calculate it wrong?” The big guy impatiently looked at the dragonman youngster, looking forward to the latter’s affirmation.

The dragonman youngster sighed, “That wasn’t important. Let us officially begin.”

“Ok!”

After that trouble, the dragonman youngster’s ample confidence had waned to uncertainty.

He began to guide the big guy in cultivation.

The bug guy reclined on the ground, using his most comfortable sleeping position, and began to circulate battle qi.

His cultivated battle qi technique was called the Mindless Sleeping Posture Technique.

The dragonman youngster had thought about it very much and specifically chose this one very carefully.

This battle qi technique was very easy, so long as one successfully completed internal circulation several times, their body would remember it. When they slept, their body would act on its own, refining and circulating their battle qi.

The dragonman youngster had tried many times to guide him, but the outcomes were less than ideal.

The big guy couldn’t remember or understand the fundamentals, and as of now, he was never able to complete a battle qi cycle.

The dragonman youngster took a deep breath, prepared himself mentally, and began hands-on instruction.

Despite not cultivating the Mindless Sleeping Technique, because he had taught the big guy many times, he almost knew it by heart.

After five minutes, the big guy had surmounted the first difficulty.

The dragonman youngster was pleasantly surprised.

Although the big guy had broken through this difficulty before, it usually took half an hour. This time it only took 5 minutes to break through, a truly outstanding achievement!

“Xiao Guai, you’re doing pretty good. A good beginning is half a success.”

The second difficulty was surmounted at 11 minutes.

“Xiao Guai, you’re doing great. You are making far more progress than ever before.”

The big guy starched his head and giggled gently, his father praising him this way made him feel very happy.

Then at 24 minutes, the third difficulty was broken through.

The dragonman youngster was more excited than the big guy, grabbing the big guy’s arm and shaking it, “You did it, Xiao Guai! You have never done this before.”

The fourth difficulty was surmounted.

“Quickly, Xiao Guai, avail yourself while you’re still intelligent and waste no time.”

As the dragonman youngster guided him, he noticed that the big guy’s comprehension and ability to remember was clearly declining.

The gold potion’s intelligence boosting effects was decreasing as time passed.

Then at 37 minutes, the fifth difficulty was crossed by the big guy.

But he was injured.

“Xiao Guai, how are you? Don’t give up, persevere with all you got.” The dragonman youngster encouraged.

He became irascible, and his guidance also became a little impatient.

The big guy’s comprehension and memory decline was growing more severe, and this caused battle qi to batter against his internals, consequently causing him to cough up blood.

“Father, I can do it! I can still persevere.” The big guy was unwilling to give up, and the dragonman youngster’s mood had infected him, he knew they were doing it all for his own good.

The dragonman youngster didn’t give the big guy a healing potion.

Healing potions might affect the effect of the intelligence potion.

Right now, the big guy had to bear with it.

With the big guy’s tough flesh, such an injury did not endanger his life.

Time passed, and the rate of progress grew slower.

From morning till dusk, the dragonman youngster and the big guy did not eat or drink, nor did they get any rest.

As the sunset quietly vanished from the ship bow house’s window, the dragonman youngster embraced the big guy, nearly crying tears of joy, “Success! Xiao Guai, you finally accomplished your first complete battle qi cycle!”

The big guy yelled in delight.

The giant sound wave was perfectly restrained by the acoustics case and didn’t get out.

“Cough, cough.” The big guy coughed out blood.

The dragonman youngster took out a healing potion and let the big guy drink it down.

The big guy’s injuries were swiftly stabilized.

The dragonman youngster, restless and on edge, once again had him circulate his battle qi completely.

The big guy was obedient and immediately lay down to circulate his battle qi.

“Pfff.” After a half minute, he spat out blood, the battle qi diverged, and he failed again.

The dragonman youngster’s heart skipped a beat, and he looked grave.

The big guy tried again and failed again.

The dragonman youngster suddenly felt his heart go cold.

“Father…I’m too stupid…” The big guy looked very solemn, he lowered his head and didn’t dare to look the dragonman youngster in the eyes out of shame.

“Xiao Guai, I’m sorry.” The dragonman youngster suddenly said.

“Huh?”

The big guy was just about to look up when the dragonman youngster knocked him out with a slap.

Not long after the big guy sank into deep slumber, his internal battle qi spontaneously circulated.

The anxious dragonman youngster gave his full attention to observing him.

Before too long, the big guy’s battle qi completed a complete circulation.

The dragonman youngster kept waiting patiently.

The big guy’s battle qi successfully did 2, 3, then 4 cycles.

The dragonman youngster let out his breath. Only now did he truly confirm that the big guy had successfully cultivated the Mindless Sleeping Posture Technique!

“So difficult, so ridiculously difficult.” The dragonman youngster relaxed and sat down on the planks.

He was exhausted, feeling more tired than the times he had fought gold level cultivators!

“It wasn’t easy, it really wasn’t easy, but it was all worth it.” Zi Di had been closely following the guidance secretly, sighing ruefully.

“When the big guy cultivates knowingly, he will still fail. But when he sleeps, he can succeed. This proves that his body is far more reliable than his mind, it is worthy of possessing at least a legend level bloodline!”

The dragonman youngster stood up, gratefully looking at the sleeping big guy, “In that case, from now on, let him sleep more. Based on his bloodline quality, when he sleeps, his progress will far exceed that of an ordinary person.”

The sleeping big guy was confused.

The other people on board also didn’t know the truth.

The only two who knew the truth of his bloodline was the dragonman youngster and Zi Di. They understood how bright the big guy’s future was, one they awaited with bated breath!

“This joyous news really needs to be shared with Cang Xu, I wonder how he’s doing.” The dragonman youngster lamented.

In the ghost ship.

Now just a head, Cang Xu slowly opened his eyes.

The big guy sank into unconsciousness as he regained consciousness.

Chapter 478: Cang Xu’s Bloodline Mutation

Cang Xu’s indistinct vision gradually cleared, and he recalled everything.

“I was experimented upon by Doctor Sui Shi.”

“My body…is gone.”

He hung down his eyelids and gazed down.

As expected, there was nothing past his neck.

He looked around, noticing that he was lying in a bed. The scene around him was one he was familiar with; it was the cabin he had been staying in.

Cang Xu took a deep breath and began to examine his condition.

He noticed that the erosion from the Underworld River was completely gone.

He was no longer in life threatening danger.

But he had landed in an unprecedented all time low, because he was just a head now.

“How terrible, my storage bag was in my chest.” Cang Xu’s heart sank when he suddenly thought of that.

Cang Xu’s storage bag had been disguised as skin and sewn into his chest. It hid it while simultaneously making it easy to get things out of it.

Now, Cang Xu’s body had been taken away by another, including his storage bag which had followed with it.

The storage bag had all of Cang Xu’s accumulated belongings!

Those included the alchemy treasures that he and Zi Di bought from the auction: the Fresh Water Infusion Bottle, the palm compass, and the gold level water path magic book.

Before Cang Xu had joined the undead gathering, the dragonman youngster had given him the Death Substitute Puppet.

“I used the Death Substitute Puppet.”

“But the Fresh Water Infusion Bottle, palm compass, and the water path magic book were still in my bag.”

Although the water path magic tome was badly damaged, it still had two pages.

Those two pages held the Water Snake spell and the Thick Fog spell. Those two spells were gold level and were Cang Xu’s trump cards.

It was an utter pity to lose them.

Compared to those losses, Cang Xu’s bronze level corpse cloth was nothing.

Apart from those alchemy items, the storage bag also had many magic scrolls.

Zi Di had also prepared many magic potions for Cang Xu too.

There was also the casting materials he had accumulated with great difficulty, such as the Dead Sea Shell fragments, the Grimace Mushrooms, the Ghost Spider legs, and much more that were gone now.

Finally, there was a large amount of food, water, clothes, and other items needed to live.

“That’s right, my fingers also had the five fire path rings. Along with the magic conduction bracelet.”

“The magic recovery mask was on my face and actually wasn’t lost.”

Cang Xu calculated the losses, his mood becoming ever more terrible.

He fully sensed the sorrow of the weak.

As a weak person, he was unable to control even his own body, while the strong was able to process it without restraint, treating it as experiment material.

“Too weak, I’m still too weak!”

Cang Xu’s heart roared, stimulating the desire to get stronger, more intense and pressing.

However, relying on the want and strong desire to become strong wouldn’t do, one still needed to show potential and that was decided by the lifeform’s bloodline.

“In the future, with the Bloodline Purification formation and the Bloodcore, I will raise my aptitude, I will truly become strong.”

“Doctor Sui Shi, this humiliation is one I will personally reciprocate!”

Cang Xu inwardly vowed.

“Newcomer, you’re awake!” At that moment, the cabin door suddenly opened.

The skeleton magician Xiao Wang Zi, the wandering ronin Ku Feng, and Jian Gui walked in together.

The three came to the side of Cang Xu’s bed.

Ku Feng said, “You were out for one day and night.”

Jian Gui took out a zombie from his storage device and placed it straight up on the floor, “I have prepared this for you. Xu Jia, you can make do with it for a short time.”

Cang Xu weakly thanked him.

Jian Gui sighed, “No need for thanks. We are comrades, mutually helping each other is just doing my part.”

The necromancers had experienced this perilous war together and fostered the camaraderie of comrades.

Cang Xu looked around and asked, “Where is my companion?”

Cang Xu was naturally talking about the wind path magician Qing Xin.

Xiao Wang Zi answered him, “When we found her, she was already unconscious, negative energy had eroded her seriously and her body’s magic power was dry, she is still unconscious as of now.”

“We guess that after we entered the Ghost World, it was likely her spirit was addled, then when she was unable to keep her magic shield up, she was eroded by negative energy.”

The Ghost World was a paradise for undead and did not welcome normal lifeforms.

Ku Feng said, “The Ghost World really doesn’t suit normal living beings, and the Underworld River has the highest concentration of negative energy. You don’t need to worry too much, Xu Jia. Qing Xin is already stable. Then, after we returned to the Main Plane, she will get much better, and there will probably be no problems with her coming to.”

Cang Xu wanted to unconsciously nod, causing him to notice that he couldn’t do such a gesture.

He could only blink to state that he knew what was going on.

He looked at Jian Gui, “May I ask where Lord Doctor Sui Shi is? I want to meet him and give him my…thanks.”

Xiao Wang Zi replied, “Ah, Lord Doctor Sui Shi left yesterday.”

“What?” Cang Xu was startled.

Ku Feng used a deliberate tone of warning, “Xu Jia, your body now belongs to Lord Doctor Sui Shi’s collection. There is little chance of getting it back.”

Cang Xu mumbled out, “Apart from my body, there was also my storage bag! It was sewn into my chest.”

As he said that, he looked pleadingly at Jian Gui. Of the necromancers present, only Jian Gui was qualified to seek out Doctor Sui Shi.

But Jian Gui bitterly laughed and shook his head, “Xu Jia, I advise you against having hope. Lord Doctor Sui Shi is a saint level necromancer, since I was able to see your chest’s storage bag, how could he not be able to see it too?”

“Since he left and didn’t leave behind your storage bag, that indicates that it was a trifle he didn’t have on his mind. Even if I could contact him now, he wouldn’t return here because of such a trivial matter.”

Cang Xu remained silent and dumbfounded for a moment.

Ku Feng comforted him, “As a matter of fact, you’re a lucky one. You still have the most valuable thing of all, your freedom, isn’t that right? Because of my insufficient aptitude at the time, Doctor Sui Shi abandoned me. Otherwise, I would have become one of his collectibles. Just like the giant zombie he made from your body, no longer a free being.”

Cang Xu slowly closed his eyes.

Jian Gui said, “We have sailed through the Underworld River long enough. Doctor Sui Shi had been protecting us, but the Underworld is full of hidden dangers, so we need to return to the Main Plane as soon as possible. Based on the plan, we will stay at one of my safehouses to rest for a time.”

Cang Xu hearing the intent of parting from these words, and he opened his eyes and asked, “In that case, what about future undead gatherings…”

Jian Gui replied, “From now on, undead gathering locations will be altered, but we haven’t determined it just yet, it is too early for that.”

“We plan to abandon Weeping Pine Island.”

“We leaked too much information there. The enemy knew too much about us.”

Cang Xu’s heart suddenly grew anxious.

Hearing Jian Gui’s tone, he planned to separate from Cang Xu and Qing Xin when they returned to the Main Plane.

This wouldn’t do!

The reason Cang Xu had taken a risk to get close to Jian Gui was because he was scheming for the latter’s undead inheritances.

Whether it was the soul crystal making method or how to use memory crystals, all of it was important to Cang Xu, no, to all of the survivors.

However, if Cang Xu suggested that he remain and keep in contact with Jian Gui, that would be too direct.

It might provoke suspicions.

Cang Xu thought on it, then immediately chose a strategy, with a trace of unwillingness, he deliberately asked Jian Gui, “Lord Jian Gui, how possible will it be for me to get back my body?”

Jian Gui was silent, then solemnly soon spread across his face, “Xu Jia, I advise you to give up this intention, don’t play with your small life. You are only silver, and that will be the extent of your life. Your body no longer belongs to you, and you made a deal with Doctor Sui Shi, he saved your life, and you gave him your body as a reward.”

Cang Xu bitterly laughed, “Not just my body, but also everything in my storage bag.”

He still didn’t give up, “I don’t dare to oppose Lord Doctor Sui Shi, but is it possible to make a trade back for it?”

Jian Gui slowly shook his head, “You don’t know Lord Doctor Sui Shi well, he has always been obsessed and serious with adding things to his collection. On the whole, he will never sell off his collection, at least I have never seen that happening. Even under unusual circumstances in the future, what you can offer will be far less than the price tag he puts on his collection.”

“As far as you are concerned, such a transaction is unfair, and the gains don’t make up for the cost.”

“Rather than doing that, I sincerely suggest that you change your body.”

“For us necromancers, changing our bodies is a simple affair. Did you not change your body in the past too? Actually, your corporeal body, as a cultivator of the undead faction’s soul aspect, is not that important.”

Cang Xu deeply sighed, his face filled with dense misery.

Xiao Wang Zi lowered his head and deeply sighed too.

He sympathized greatly with Cang Xu.

Not just him, Ku Feng and Jian Gui were the same too.

They had worked to fend off powerful enemies and had the friendship of comrades.

Cang Xu had helped out Jian Gui a lot, and on Weeping Pine Island, he had warned Jian Gui, allowing the latter to realize what was going on, leading to him calling the ghost ship to resolve his plight.

After sailing the Underworld River for some time, Jian Gui had given him his magic tome and treated Cang Xu as bait.

It was an emergency, and for his own survival, Jian Gui had no qualms of putting another into danger.

But now, they were safe.

Apart from sympathy for Cang Xu, Jian Gui also felt ashamed of how much he exploited him.

But in reality, Cang Xu wasn’t as attached to his body as they envisioned.

Not long after Cang Xu had come to, he had recognized reality and accepted it.

The reason why he said these things was to evoke sympathy from the others.

“But to just this level is still not enough.” As a noble steward, Cang Xu had come into contact with every social class and had an incisive understanding of the hearts of men.

He then purposely looked at the zombie.

This was the zombie Jian Gui had just taken out; the new body prepared for Cang Xu.

This zombie was also male, and its height was about the same as Cang Xu was before, furthermore, it had a silver level aura.

In terms of quality, Cang Xu had made a windfall.

Because Cang Xu’s main body’s life level was only iron level. Being a silver level was a facade Deceptive Disguise had produced.

Cang Xu looked at the zombie as he said, “Lord Jian Gui, if I am not remembering wrongly, according to the soul aspect inheritance – when changing one’s body, bloodline needs to be considered. If my head is incompatible with this zombie’s bloodline, they will interfere with each other and produce a bloodline rejection. After a time, the bloodline rejection will cause more vile consequences.”

Jian Gui’s face stiffened for a bit, then he nodded, “You weren’t mistaken, that is true. Therefore, finding a body one can use long term still had a degree of difficulty. Do you at least know what your current bloodline is? What did you find the bloodline of your body to be?”

Cang Xu deliberately looked troubled, “I need bloodline testing…Lord Jian Gui, can you really not teach me the spell that can test undead bloodlines?”

“Although I lost everything I had, as long as I contact the Wisteria Merchant Alliance, I can still reward you generously.”

Jian Gui shook his head, “Sorry, that is the bottom line of the orthodox undead. That inheritance cannot be taught as one wishes, one has to pass aptitude and loyalty evaluations. Especially right now, when every power is researching the undead faction and our starting advantages are quickly dwindling.”

Cang Xu deeply sighed, then asked, “In that case, Lord Jian Gui, what is my bloodline?”

Since Jian Gui had provided Cang Xu a body, then based on Cang Xu’s speculation, he should know what Cang Xu’s bloodline is.

However, a moment later, Jian Gui shook his head, “I still don’t know yet.”

Cang Xu was astonished.

Jian Gui said, “While you were unconscious, I used undead bloodline detection magic on you. They were present for that too.”

“Our goal was to choose a suitable body for you.”

“But the outcome actually went beyond everyone’s expectations.”

“You are in the process of a bloodline mutation.”

Cang Xu slightly widened his eyes, showing a bit of disbelief, “A bloodline mutation?”

He knew of bloodline mutation.

Bloodlines certainly could mutate.

Otherwise, how could there be so many bloodlines in the world?

However, bloodline mutation often happened when lifeforms reproduced and created newborns that contained a different kind of bloodline.

The result of mixing bloodlines, apart from deformities, malforms, and death, was that there was an extremely infinitesimal part of offspring that blended their bloodlines together to form an entirely new bloodline.

Cang Xu was in that very situation now and was naturally not a newborn.

It was because external stimulation had triggered a bloodline mutation!

Jian Gui explained, “Initially, you transformed your own body into that of a living dead person, half alive, half dead. This situation actually isn’t that good, because you haven’t completely changed into an undead lifeform. You still had a large amount of your living bloodline. Although your body could resist negative energy corrosion, it also hindered you from getting stronger.”

“During the battle, your body was soaked in the Underworld River’s water. After you were revived by the Death Substitute Puppet, Voodoo divine power also entered into your head. After that, Lord Doctor Sui Shi used magic on your head to save your life, while simultaneously exerting a formidable influence on it.”

“With all of those elements stacking on each other, and perhaps other factors that were overlooked, an unforeseen change has happened to your bloodline.”

Cang Xu slowly asked, “In that case, Lord Jian Gui, was the outcome of this bloodline mutation good or bad?”
