“I cannot say, I cannot determine it.” Jian Gui shook his head again, :I am only a necromancer, not a priest of the Life Church. Furthermore, your bloodline mutation is still in progress, far from done. Perhaps after it finishes, you will have the ability to learn what it ultimately ends up as.”

Cang Xu went silent again.

Ku Feng comforted him, “Don’t worry, normal people would worry for their lives when a bloodline mutation happens. However, you Xu Jia, are already an undead, the bloodline mutation probably won’t kill you.”

Xiao Wang Zi added on, “Your aptitude might rise, it might also fall. Actually, I envy you, newcomer. Perhaps you can improve your aptitude as a result of this and reach the criteria of learning undead inheritance!”

Cang Xu wasn’t excited in the slightest.

Because he knew he already had a better option.

“A bloodline mutation increasing my aptitude is too unlikely.” Cang Xu bitterly laughed.

This situation went a little beyond his expectations, but it didn’t hinder him from spouting his manipulative words.

Cang Xu looked at Jian Gui, his eyes full of pleading, “Lord Jian Gui, can you shelter me for a time?”

“My future is full of apprehension and emptiness.”

“Only by relying on your strength will it be more possible for me to obtain a body that truly fits me.”

“There is also my bloodline mutation…”

Jian Gui didn’t answer, instead he sank into deep thought.

Xiao Wang Zi and Ku Feng both looked at Jian Gui, deeply concerned.

Finally, Jian Gui nodded, “You can follow me around for a time. But I have to explain in advance, I fear we will welcome more fights, even against that gold level shadow monk again.”

Cang Xu stared, then replied, “He is my enemy too, I was reduced to this because of them! Lord Jian Gui, please let me contribute my strength, even if that strength is insignificant.”

Jian Gui nodded, he was very happy to see Cang Xu’s hatred was staked on Wei Ying and not the undead orthodox member Doctor Sui Shi.

To the side, Ku Feng smiled.

Xiao Wang Zi cheered, “Very good, Xu Jia, we can kill the enemy together.”

“We have already talked it over, after resting, we will go out to sea to save Qi Ling’s fishman clan.”

Cang Xu exposed a pondering look, “I understand, Qi Ling is not a sincere traitor. Otherwise, on Weeping Pine Island, she wouldn’t have revealed herself as a traitor and then stopped fighting me.”

“Her weak spot is her clansmen. As long as we save her fishman clan, Qi Ling will be free.”

Jian Gui responded with, “You’re not wrong.”

Xiao Wang Zi angrily hmphed, “Even if she has difficulties, I won’t forgive her. She made the mistake of betraying Lord Jian Gui, she bit the hand that fed her, harumph! Surrendering to the enemy means she didn’t have a bit of backbone, if it were me, I would have an unbreakable backbone and would never surrender.”

Ku Feng teased him, “Right, you have the toughest backbone, you are just bones.”

“Huh?” Xiao Wang Zi was perplexed, and for a moment, he didn’t know whether Ku Feng praised or ridiculed him.

Chapter 479: Heroic Duel

The gleam of sunset gradually dissipated, and the curtain of night gradually covered Snow Bird Harbor.

At the docks, on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ flagship, the Love Triangle, the survivors secretly gathered together to discuss their future projects and plans.

“That is roughly what the situation is.” Zi Di had just explained the conflict between them and Bing Xiao, giving a detailed account of it.

Everyone was silent.

Zong Ge put his hands on the intelligence reports.

These intelligence reports had been purchased from the Thieves Guild at a hefty price.

This intelligence report had detailed information on the Bayonet Gang.

The old elf Bing Xiao had come from the army.

He was once a gold level, but after being wounded on the battlefield, he was reduced to silver level.

After being discharged, he returned to his hometown, in other words, Snow Bird Harbor.

A year later, he joined the Bayonet Gang.

After living in the gang for some time, he had successfully nurtured a gold level giant slave.

With it, he had become the Bayonet Gang’s leader.

Three years later, another veteran returned to Snow Bird Harbor and decided to join the Bayonet Gang.

When he was promoted to become part of the core backbone of the gang, he challenged Bing Xiao to a heroic duel.

This veteran was the current leader of the Bayonet Gang – Teng Dong Lang.

From this, the outcome of said heroic duel was self-evident, Teng Dong Lang was the winner.

Bing Xiao had lost and so lost the position of leadership.

He then simply retired with his lovers.

Bing Xiao’s gold level slave was called Tie Tou Tong, and because it had participated in too many of the gang’s fights, their body was gravely injured and its strength greatly declined.

Anyways, in recent years, Bing Xiao had been cultivating new giant slaves.

He had some results in the current times.

He wanting to purchase a giant-type intelligence potion should be him striving to cultivate new giants.

Apart from Bing Xiao, the information on the Bayonet Gang gave a clear understanding of the rest of it.

This gang had almost a thousand people in it, and its current leader, Teng Dong Lang, was a genuine gold level fighter with rich battle experience.

He had more than twenty silver level subordinates, the vast majority of which were snow elves.

Elves had greater transcendent gifts compared to the humans and beastmen.

However, elves were always a sparse people, as they couldn’t reproduce as much as humans and beastmen.

Beastmen were better at reproducing, but of the three races, their transcendent gifts were the lowest.

Humans were the median of the three. Their reproduction and transcendent gifts were not the strongest, but with them, humanity’s total strength became the greatest over time.

The Sheng Ming Empire was a superpower on the Main Plane, and the greatest race of modern times was humanity, for the empire had the most territory of the Main Plane and the strongest transcendents.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was a small force, and the survivors didn’t even have a gold level.

Whether one had a gold level determined how large a force was determined to be.

Without a gold level, even if one had many silver levels, they would be merely a small power.

Zong Ge’s growth potential could be easily seen, and his battle qi already had a gold sheen, but so long as he wasn’t a gold level yet, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would remain a small sized force.

It was clear that the Bayonet Gang was a true medium sized power.

Zong Ge held the intelligence report in his hand and analyzed, “The Bayonet Gang’s strength far exceeds ours, but we have advantages they do not possess.”

“Such as in naval battles, we have a distinct dominance in that, for they don’t seem to have many ships.”

“In large scale battles, we also might hold a certain superiority. Gang members are better at fighting in cities in either groups of three to four, or alone.”

“What’s concerning is that the Bayonet Gang’s prior and current leader are from the army, we don’t know what things they brought into the gang from there.”

If they did what Zong Ge did to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, then the Bayonet Gang would have tight military supervision and training, that would add another level to the Bayonet Gang as a whole.

The dragonman youngster thought about it, “The intelligence report doesn’t mention any army drills.”

“If the Bayonet Gang did military administration, would Snow Bird Harbor’s officials sleep soundly at night?”

Zong Ge nodded, “That is indeed so.”

“However, we cannot launch a naval battle or wide scale warfare.”

“This is the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, Snow Bird Harbor is one of its eight harbors. The coast is lined with cannons, there is a magician tower, and everyone would see it.”

“The magician tower monitors the entire harbor at all times, if we assemble on a large scale, there would be no need to use the cannons, one or two of the magician tower’s large scale spells would inflict disastrous damage on us and even wipe out our organizational structure.”

Zong Ge’s intent was very explicit, forcing things was out of the question.

Zong Ge had finished speaking, and sitting at his side, Tripleblade spoke next, “On the face of it, the Bayonet Gang is very strong, however, the true strength they can muster is far less than what is on paper.”

Tripleblade was once a mercenary commander. He had dealt with merchant alliances, gangs, and all sorts of walks of life, thus, he had quite an understanding of gangs.

“Say for example.” Tripleblade continued to analyze, “The Bayonet Gang has twenty silver levels. But most of the silver level members cannot be directly dispatched, for many of them are figureheads in the gang.”

“The truly capable people depend on their specific status and position in the gang.”

“In my opinion, the genuine backbone only has 6 people, the 6 that the neighborhood gang leader controls.”

“Interestingly, of the 6 silver levels under this gang leader only has two snow elves, the rest are a human, a bear-man, a treeman, and even a demon-human hybrid, quite the mixed group.”

This was actually similar to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

A gang was like this, where dragons and snakes mingled, and as long as one was strong, they could usually rise to the top.

The backbone had many races, something actually quite common in gangs.

Especially in a harbor like Snow Bird Harbor, where there were many people of all sorts of races traveling through, making it even more necessary to designate some strong gang leaders for specific races.

Such leaders could gain their corresponding race’s respect, increasing the understanding in the hierarchy and greatly reducing supervision costs.

Tripleblade stopped analyzing and looked at the dragonman youngster, “Alas, we really didn’t need to clash with Bing Xiao, although the giant-type intelligence potion really suits the big guy, there was no need to make a rush for it.”

“If we preordered the potion, we could have gotten it within half a month.”

It was very clear that Tripleblade did not approve of the dragonman youngster’s and Zi Di’s decision at the Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance store.

To him it seemed irrational.

Zi Di and the dragonman youngster were silent and indifferent.

The bloodline testing results were only known by Zi Di, the dragonman youngster, and Cang Xu.

If Tripleblade knew what the big guy’s bloodline was, he wouldn’t be thinking what he did now.

But the bloodline testing results was something the dragonman youngster wouldn’t speak rashly about.

The dragonman youngster looked at Tripleblade and asked, “Based on what you see, what should we do, what is the best response to our current situation?”

Tripleblade shook his head, “Although we have considerable strength, we can still be easily eaten up by a gang.”

“The reason is simple; we lack elite stealth units. That is to say, thieves and assassins.”

“In Snow Bird Harbor, large scale warfare is impossible.”

“The Bayonet Gang can’t do it either. Their most likely operations will be dispatching stealth units to spy on us, steal from us, and attack us.”

“It is also possible that they will kidnap and blackmail people we dispatch.”

“From this evening onwards, we will be fully vigilant to prevent enemy infiltration, theft, and damage.”

“For the next several days, we will restrict our people as much as possible, keep them on the ships. But this can only be done for so long.”

“In the long term, the sailors will grow agitated and displeased. Sooner or later, we will have to let them out.”

“Also, only by letting them out will we be able to take the most mercenary missions to develop and expand the entire Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.”

Apart from the Thieves Guild in a city, gangs were the force with the most thieves and assassins.

The survivors really lacked such talents.

The closest to that was Tripleblade. But his battle skills weren’t able to hide his traces, and his fighting style was through agility and rapid movement within a small distance to directly grapple with the enemy and seize any gaps to throw the enemy formation into confusion.

In short, Tripleblade’s fighting style made him a competent mercenary good at conventional warfare.

Expanding this criterion to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, the best of them in sneaking was Xu Ma.

Xu Ma cultivated the Dark Spider technique, and cultivated compatible battle skills for stealth and eavesdropping, along with being able to fight. But Xu Ma’s bloodline was not outstanding, and he was an iron level nowadays, the limit of his aptitude.

Tripleblade continued, “If our luck is good, we can recruit one or two silver level infiltrators that can alleviate the current situation.”

“In fact, we need such talents for investigative work. Although we have many alchemy puppets, in many situations, alchemy puppets are no substitute for people, such as making intelligence reports.”

Tripleblade’s words were reasonable, and the dragonman youngster nodded repeatedly at them.

At the same time, the dragonman youngster’s thinking was growing, nonchalantly looking at Que Shan.

He thought of Que Shan’s bloodline.

Que Shan had a dark elf (Drow) bloodline, and the dark elf race could be said to have the most amount of rogues on the Main Plane.

“However, cultivating Que Shan takes too much time. Distant water cannot quench thirst now.” The dragonman youngster thought.

Zi Di then opened her mouth, “We don’t need antagonism, perhaps we can offer amends and apologize? Since the Bayonet Gang is Snow Bird Harbor’s local bully, building a good relationship with them can be beneficial to us and will help our future business activities.”

From Zi Di’s point of view, since they had taken the giant-type intelligence potion and used it on the big guy, then it was fine to bow their heads to the Bayonet Gang for the time being.

Even if they had to pay some compensation, it was a drop in the ocean for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ 60,000 gold coins of assets.

If both sides could reach a settlement, with the Bayonet Gang’s protection and contact network, Zi Di’s business would go smoothly here.

Looking at the long term, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ hefty investment would earn much more in the future!

However, everyone met her proposal with silence.

Even the dragonman youngster declined to comment.

The dragonman youngster looked at the rest, “Anyone can speak, what is your opinion?”

Lan Zao answered first, “My Lord, you are our commander, your decision is how we will operate!”

Lan Zao was a powerful supporter of the dragonman youngster, and since he started following the dragonman youngster, his position never wavered from the latter’s.

Fei She said, “On Mystifying Mon, Monster Island. If it wasn’t for the big guy’s protect, protecting our ship, everyone would have died un, under the beast tide. Before the old boatswain died, he ga, gave the big guy to the commander, the big guy treated the com, commander as his new father. And a father buying his son a potion is completely within reason, a mat, matter of course.”

Fei She’s words were more tactful and pleasant to listen to than Lan Zao’s.

He also explicitly expressed his support for the dragonman youngster.

Mu Ban scowled, “I am more worried about other things.”

“Since the Bayonet Gang has entrenched itself here for many years, they must have many hidden connections with the local government of Snow Bird Harbor, such as public officials and the city guard.”

“If they borrow the city guard’s hand to deal with us, then that will be more troublesome. If it isn’t dealt with well, we will antagonize the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s government, that will be exceedingly detrimental to us.”

Que Shan said, “If nothing else can be done, at worst we can leave Snow Bird Harbor for good. This harbor is only one of eight in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. The Ice Sculpture Kingdom is vast; we don’t need to be entangled in one place. We are free and our situation is very strained, the longer time drags on, the worse it will get for us.”

Everyone had said their piece, except Bai Ya.

The dragonman youngster looked at Bai Ya, “Do you have an opinion, Bai Ya?”

Bai Ya was somewhat nervous, “I, should I really speak? I don’t really have anything to say.”

The dragonman youngster smiled at him, “Have the courage to speak, mistakes don’t matter, we are all the same here.”

Bai Ya muttered to himself, then decided to change the subject, “Everyone knows that I am a hunter.”

“When I was old enough to shoot arrows and hunt rabbits, my father took me into the forest mountain to hunt.”

“One winter, when the first snow fell, my father and I were preparing to hunt.”

“Just as we entered the forest mountain, we saw a mess in the woodland, discovering that a ferocious beast had left blood traces, talon marks across trees, and footprints in the snow from its fight.”

“My father called off the hunt on the spot.”

“I felt very puzzled and asked my father why he did that.”

“My father said to me that this forest mountain only had one fierce tiger. The marks at the scene, however, showed that two fierce tigers had fought there.”

“It was very clear that one of the tigers was an outsider. It had intruded in and clashed with the master of the forest, so we had to wait for the fight to finish, for them to determine the outcome of the battle.”

“We would wait until the forest mountain grew tranquil again, and after it stabilized, we would go hunting again.”

“My father warned me: we are ordinary people, and even fully armed, we couldn’t fight a magic beast. Don’t even think of taking advantage of another’s misfortune to pick up a steal. A wounded and hungry magic beast is far more dreadful than it is normally! Transcendents are different, and we will never be their match.”

“We merely live on this forest mountain, killing only the prey we need to eat.”

“Don’t take risks, hunting in of itself is already a great risk. What is the difference between taking a risk and seeking death? We are men, and if we die, what will happen to our family?”

When Bai Ya said that he came back to the topic at hand, declaring his opinion, “I believe that our Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is a ferocious foreign beast. We need to have a true battle so the ferocious beasts in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom acknowledge us, that we are qualified to truly have a footing here.”

“Snow Bird Harbor isn’t our only option.”

“But everywhere else is the same.”

“Don’t tell me the other harbors don’t have gangs? Like that forest mountain, there will always be magic beasts entrenched there that divide up their respective territories.”

“This time, we provoked the Bayonet Gang, actually, if we didn’t provoke them, wouldn’t they provoke us instead?”

“Even if there wasn’t such a conflict, there will always be another, other powers will make things difficult for us and shove us aside.”

When Bai Ya finished, the people around him looked at him with newfound respect.

The dragonman youngster pumped up his fist and loudly encouraged, “Well spoken, correctly spoken, Bai Ya!”

Bai Ya scratched his head in bemusement; the praise made his face somewhat red.

The dragonman youngster looked around and raised his voice, “Just as Bai Ya said, that is the situation we are in.”

“The Ice Sculpture Kingdom is different from Snake Rat and Two Eyes Island. It has been developed for a very long time. It is full of all kinds of people of all sorts of professions. This is not vacant land. And to obtain sufficient space to survive, we will inevitably tussle with the natives here.”

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps needs an adversary so we can prove our power to others.”

“The Bayonet Gang is very strong, but it is not insurmountable.”

“If we defeat them, the fruits of victory we will reap are sure to be far more ample than the ones from a puny enemy!”

“Furthermore, this is not the naval battles of Two Eyes Island and Weeping Pine Island, nor is it surviving on Mystifying Monster Island. The only contradiction between us and the Bayonet Gang is a gold level potion.”

“This battle doesn’t need to determine life and death, but it does need an outcome.”

“As long as we display our true strength, the Bayonet Gang will inevitably fear us, and if that happens, we can vie for ground to make a footing on.”

Zong Ge looked pensive, observing the dragonman youngster, “In that case, what is the plan?”

The dragonman youngster faintly smiled, “Early tomorrow, I will move to publicly challenge the Bayonet Gang’s leader to a heroic duel!”

Those words caused a variety of expressions to appear on the others.

Zi Di couldn’t sit still, “Lord Commander, you want to fight Teng Dong Lang? He is a veteran gold level.”

The dragonman youngster’s smile grew, flowing with battle intent, “Gold level...it’s quite exciting to fight against such an opponent.”

The dragonman youngster had fought Er Gua Zi at the Battle of Two Eyes Island.

But the fight ended too soon and didn’t satisfy his craving.

The dragonman youngster was exuberantly interested at this chance.

Tripleblade approved, “A good method. Even if you lose, Commander, it’s not that bad. You’re a silver level after all. But as long as your fight is majestic and enough for people to sit up and take notice, we can display our Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ strength, so others won’t dare have contempt for us from then on.”

Zong Ge once again gazed at the intelligence report, “This battle can be fought.”

“Teng Dong Lang does not fight like Hun Tong, he is from the army, his moves are terse and clear, and his battle style should be frontal and direct. A most suitable opponent for you, Commander.”

The dragonman youngster nodded, “That’s right, I think so too.”

Unconsciously, he reached out and rubbed the Grand Arena Medal he wore.

Chapter 480: Teng Dong Lang’s Scheme

Teng Dong Lang was sitting at a dining table, enjoying his breakfast.

The breakfast he liked the most was sashimi.

This was the freshest way to dine on fish, caught from the ocean at first light.

Teng Dong Lang was a middle aged man.

As a snow elf, he looked attractive, but the wrinkles around his eyes revealed the ups and downs of his life.

Once part of the army, he not only sat very straight, but he also had a majestic presence far beyond an ordinary person.

Teng Dong Lang ate his sashimi while listening to a subordinate’s report.

The Bayonet Gang was the top gang in Snow Bird Harbor, and naturally had many affairs, so Teng Dong Lang was a true busybody that couldn’t be idle while dining.

Suddenly, Teng Dong Lang raised an eyebrow, “Oh, Bing Xiao wanted to dispatch gang members to deal with a mercenary corps? Why?”

The subordinate replied, “According to Lord Bing Xiao’s account, this mercenary corps doesn’t know good from bad, and in the Secret Medicine store, they outbid him for a giant-type intelligence potion. This potion would have been a great help for Lord Bing Xiao’s training of giants; thus, Lord Bing Xiao wants to get this potion back.”

Teng Dong Lang faintly nodded, his mind perceiving Bing Xiao’s intent.

Although Bing Xiao had been forced from the position of leader, he still retained a group of people, those who were his confidants and diehards.

Such as his gold level giant slave.

But Bing Xiao didn’t attack immediately, instead he made a request to the Bayonet Gang and informed Teng Dong Lang ahead of time.

This kind of attitude and conduct made the current leader, Teng Dong Lang feel good.

At once, Teng Dong Lang was trending towards giving Bing Xiao a favor and offering some help.

However, Teng Dong Lang was a naturally cautious person.

Before officially deciding, he asked, “What is the state of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps?”

The subordinate immediately took out a piece of paper and handed it to Teng Dong Lang, “This is the intelligence report of our inquiry.”

Teng Dong Lang laid the paper on the dining table, putting jam into his mouth as he read through the intelligence report.

The report stated:

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps were a small sized power and didn’t have a gold level powerhouse.

Their head was Long Fu, and their vice head was Shi Qi, both silver levels. Apart from them, there was also the silver level Chi Lai and Men Shi.

They had two magicians, the iron level Lao Zhong and the bronze level Hong Teng.

The mercenary corps had many races in it, including humans, dogmen, water pigmen, a dragonman, and a half giant.

The mercenary corps had three magic ships. The flagship the Love Triangle, along with the Iron Lump and the White Bandage. The first was iron level, and the other two were bronze level.

Apart from those three, the rest were ordinary ships, they had no large ships, and their 13 ships were mostly medium and small sized ships.

The fleet had two gold level components, the Feather Snake Sail and the gold level cannon. The latter was damaged and couldn’t be used for the time being.

The Bayonet Gang deserved to be the local bully, the information they obtained was detailed and reliable.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had entered the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s harbor just two days ago, and the Bayonet Gang had gotten so much information on them.

That was because when the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was on the sea, the patrol fleet had inspected them. Then after mooring at the docks, they specifically registered information on their ships.

The Bayonet Gang’s tentacles were long, and they had penetrated into many departments, so obtaining this information was no challenge.

Teng Dong Lang looked at the information report while thinking out his assessment.

“A group of outsiders…”

“Although a small sized power, their strength is decent.”

“They also have some potential…”

“There are also many hidden dangers.”

Teng Dong Lang believed that a hidden danger was the mercenary corps’ complex racial mix.

He understood the hidden troubles of this.

Because the Bayonet Gang was in a similar situation. The six people under him all belonged to different races, and he had eaten plenty of suffering when dealing with them.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps wasn’t as strong as the Bayonet Gang.

After Teng Dong Lang finished the report, he was trending even more towards helping Bing Xiao.

“Although Bing Xiao is retired, he was once the Bayonet Gang’s leader and has an inseparably close relationship with it. If he can train a second gold level giant slave, it will be favorable news for the entire Bayonet Gang.”

“Anyways, since Bing Xiao wants to deal with these people, based on his conduct style, this information report won’t satisfy him.”

“He will make further inquiries.”

“Since he is seeking the gang’s help, this information should be sent to him.”

Teng Dong Lang was from the army, and his main military position was as a staff officer, thus, his thoughts were very meticulous and he was an expert administrator.

He turned to look at his subordinate, “Is there anything else to report?”

As expected, the subordinate had prepared another intelligence report and handed it to him.

“This information report has been specially provided by Lord Bing Xiao.” The subordinate reported.

Teng Dong Lang scanned the intelligence report as he kept on eating.

Very quickly, his chewing slowed, and his face exposed a cautious look.

The information gathered by Bing Xiao did not describe what people the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was composed of, or what kind of ships, weapons, and equipment it had, instead, he spoke of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ prior military achievements and history.

As a result, Bing Xiao learned that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had participated in the battle for Two Eyes Island.

The dragonman youngster had fought a gold level fishman pirate outright and only at a slight disadvantage.

Their three magic ships had all once been another’s.

After the battle for Two Eyes Island, they chased down a group of necromancers to Weeping Pine Island and clashed with the fleet Lady Sea Snake had assembled.

Finally, they rescued some hostages and also captured the Iron Lump and the Love Triangle.

Because time was limited, Bing Xiao was unable to learn of what the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps did on Snake Rat Island.

But that didn’t hinder how Teng Dong Lang thought of them, on the contrary, this information greatly increased his assessment of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

“These people are bold and fierce in battle. After the battle of Two Eyes Island, they decided to pursue and attack the necromancers fearlessly.”

“Although Commander Long Fu is a silver level, his body is extremely fine, he can surprisingly withstand a gold level directly and can be regarded as a quasi-gold, he cannot be looked down on.”

Teng Dong Lang couldn’t help but grow vigilant.

These two information reports had given him ample knowledge into the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ strength.

He couldn’t help but inwardly warn himself, “I cannot underestimate the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, and when helping Bing Xiao’s search for the giant-type intelligence potion, I need to pay attention to our behavior and cannot overstep too much.”

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps didn’t want to go to war with the Bayonet Gang, and surprisingly, Teng Dong Lang felt the same.

Even though the Bayonet Gang was clearly stronger than them.

An enraged Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps could inflict disastrous casualties on the Bayonet Gang.

“How should this matter be handled?” Teng Dong Lang sank into thought.

Negotiation and trade were two methods that Teng Dong Lang threw away first.

Although these two methods had hope of recovering the potion, Teng Dong Lang knew he couldn’t do either.

His position and identity restricted many of his choices.

He was not an ordinary person; he was the leader of the Bayonet Gang. If he did those things, it was tantamount to the Bayonet Gang giving into a small sized mercenary corps, then how would others look at him?

If the Bayonet Gang was considered weak, then that would be too terrible.

A gang’s reputation was always important.

Only by displaying power constantly to cow the people into fear could they collect protection money and other such things smoothly.

“The conflict between the two of them occurred at the Secret Medicine branch store. This news will be disseminated widely over the next several days.”

“As of now, there are certainly many resolute people already waiting for Bing Xiao’s and the Bayonet Gang’s reaction.”

Teng Dong Lang understood the situation.

The Bayonet Gang not only needed to recover the potion but also give the other party a lesson. Only by doing that would they be satisfied.

“The most direct method is stealing it back.”

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ships are at the dock, and after this contention, they will surely have added more precautions.”

“The dock is easy to infiltrate; the pivotal thing is the magician tower.”

“The gold level magician tower keeps watch over all of Snow Bird Harbor at all times, and anchored ships at the dock is an important district that is supervised even more.”

“If we want the magician tower to turn a blind eye, then we need to bribe them. But that’s expensive!”

“Apart from that, I also have to put up a favor.”

Thinking of that, Teng Dong Lang couldn’t help but shake his head.

That method wasn’t good; it was too costly.

His mind turned, then thought of another way, “Then I’ll look for an opportunity to kidnap one of the important members of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. Then when I negotiate with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, I can demand they return the giant-type intelligence potion in exchange for the hostage.”

Teng Dong Lang was making his plans, but then his subordinate looked distressed and reported to him, “Lord Gang Leader. After the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps entered the harbor, they restricted their entire crew, almost none of them got off the ship. Yesterday, only a few of their ringleaders acted off the ships. If those people don’t come out, we won’t find a good chance to kidnap them.”

Teng Dong Lang was flabbergasted, “Huh? They chose to do that?”

He soon sneered, “Just a trick, as time goes one, those mercenaries will kick up a fuss and even stir up a rebellion.”

“But…” Teng Dong Lang’s sneer gradually vanished, for he felt trouble.

He didn’t believe the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps could restrict its mercenaries for long.

Under normal circumstances, Teng Dong Lang didn’t need to act. He only needed to keep calm and for changes, to wait for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to lash out.

But the problem was that Teng Dong Lang couldn’t delay.

Now that the intelligence potion was in the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ hands, Bing Xiao had asked for help, explicitly pointing out that time was an issue.

The more time passed, the greater the possibility that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would use the potion.

When that came to pass, even if the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps conceded, the potion would be gone, the Bayonet Gang would have left them alive for a long time, the original goal would be unattainable, and it would all be a waste of effort.

Chapter 481: One Fist to Victory

After Teng Dong Lang deliberated for a moment, he decided that recovering the potion was the top priority.

“The first thing is to steal the potion back!”

“I have no choice but to ask Hua Tang for help. He still owes me a favor.”

“He will be able to avoid the magician tower’s surveillance.”

“Hiring him will also be far cheaper than bribing the magician tower.”

“Not bad, this will do!”

Teng Dong Lang had just made his decision when another subordinate hastily intruded into the dining room and reported to him.

“Lord Gang Leader, a dragonman is blocking our door.”

“He is carrying an ice chunk and has made it known that he wants to duel you, Gang Leader!”

“Right now, the downstairs is packed with pedestrians.”

“A dragonman?” Teng Dong Lang suddenly felt his heart jump, for a split second, the name Long Fu flashed through his mind.

“He brought ice over; does he want to have a heroic duel with me?” Teng Dong Lang guessed.

“Precisely so.” The subordinate bowed their head.

To bring ice to a battle meant one wanted a heroic duel.

This was a tradition that came from a story from the barbarian race’s continent.

It was similar to what happened in the Sheng Ming Empire, where humans would throw their glove to ask for a duel.

When Teng Dong Lang learned of this news, he couldn’t sit still.

He got up and looked down from the second story window.

A moment later, he saw the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster was tall and robust, his red dragon scales stuck to his body like armor, and everyone who saw it knew of its astonishing defense.

“It is indeed unforgettable at first sight.” Teng Dong Lang thought to himself.

Sensing movement by the second floor window, the dragonman youngster raised his head and his amber dragon eyes gazed at Teng Dong Lang.

Teng Dong Lang gradually narrowed his eyes; they were glistening with a cold light.

Their stares collided in the air, nearly creating sparks.

The dragonman youngster had Lan Zao next to him.

He strongly requested that he serve as the dragonman youngster’s attendant for this. He sincerely felt honored to follow the dragonman youngster like this.

Noticing that there was a middle aged elf at the second floor window that matched the intelligence report, Lan Zao knew it was Teng Dong Lang and yelled loudly, “Distinguished Teng Dong Lang, we are from the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. This is our Commander, Lord Long Fu. We have heard of your power and have intentionally come here to challenge you to battle, to carry out a heroic duel!”

The dragonman youngster and Lan Zao had already grabbed the attention of spectators, and there was three rings of them both behind and in front of them.

This was the first time they had seen the dragonman youngster and didn’t know who he was. But they were well acquainted with the person he wanted to challenge!

At this moment, the crowd saw Teng Dong Lang show his face and immediately made a big fuss.

“Lord Teng Dong Lang has appeared!”

“This dragonman really wants to challenge Lord Teng Dong Lang, he wasn’t lying.”

“A silver level actually wants to challenge a gold level; he really has guts!”

“Hmph, challenging Lord Teng Dong Lang is simply embarrassing himself. This duel has no suspense to it!”

“Lord Ten Dong Lang, you must make this young man eat a bitter defeat!

Teng Dong Lang looked at the scene downstairs, his complexion gradually growing gloomy.

He felt a bit prickled.

From their interlocked gazes, he adequately felt the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ confidence and their brimming fighting spirit.

“Interesting, before I could deal with them, they decided to find trouble with me first.”

Teng Dong Lang’s train of thought changed, “Such confidence, don’t tell me he has a trump card dedicated to dealing with a gold level?”

Heroic duels did not restrict the usage of items.

It wasn’t impossible for a silver level to be victorious over a gold level. Especially when many items were unconventional and outstanding.

Teng Dong Lang thought of the information report, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ fleet had two gold level modules.

“The patrol fleet’s inspection was not able to analyze all of their storage devices. It is very possible that they have other trump cards.”

“They have confidence by means of these trump cards, something that allows a silver level to defeat a gold level in battle.”

“I cannot be impulsive; I have to be careful!”

Thinking of this, Teng Dong Lang’s originally low battle intent sharply declined.

He put his hand on the window sill and raised the other, indicating to the crowd downstairs that he was going to speak.

Everyone downstairs suddenly stopped speaking, showing Teng Dong Lang’s prestige in this place.

After waiting until the crowd downstairs calmed down, Teng Dong Lang opened his mouth, “Commander Long Fu, I am very happy to see you, and I welcome you to Snow Bird Harbor.”

“I can’t help but say, although this is the first time meeting, you have left a profound impression on me.”

“I never fear any challenge, and I am quite happy that you proposed to have a heroic duel with me.”

“However, you are only a silver level, and an outsider who has just arrived at Snow Bird Harbor.”

“I am a gold level; I surpass you by one level. If I were to take advantage of your travel weary state and do battle with you now, that be too inelegant.”

The dragonman youngster’s expression slightly shifted, and when he heard those words, he suddenly sensed something going wrong, so he quickly taunted, “Teng Dong Lang, what are you afraid of?”

Teng Dong Lang laughed, “Please understand, if every silver level here wanted to duel me, then how would I have time to do anything else? Without time and energy, I wouldn’t be able to engage in more important matters.”

“If you were a gold level, I would have agreed to a contest immediately, but you aren’t.”

“You have the courage to challenge a gold as a silver, but not necessarily the strength. You must prove that first.”

“Defeat the Bayonet Gang’s six famous block leaders, then I will acknowledge your strength and deem you worthy of dueling me.”

After saying that, Teng Dong Lang turned around to leave, vanishing from the window before the dragonman youngster could reply.

“Hey, don’t leave!” Lan Zao hastily shouted.

The dragonman youngster bade him to stop.

Lan Zao turned around, looking anxious, “Lord Commander, what should we do?”

“It doesn’t matter, this was within our predictions, isn’t that so?” The dragonman youngster smiled, “We’ll do as he demands. He can’t run away.”

The dragonman youngster turned around and looked to the northwest, “If I am remembering correctly, the closest Bayonet Gang ringleader should be Ban Lan Gen?”

“Yes, Lord Commander.” Lan Zao recalled it and immediately nodded.

“Teng Dong Lang, remember your promise! I will go find your subordinates, wait until I defeat them one by one.” After the dragonman youngster threw that out, he left in a swift and decisive manner.

The spectators lining the street that witnessed the conversation between the dragonman youngster and Teng Dong Lang bubbled with turmoil.

“A good show.”

“Catch up, keep pace!”

“Don’t disappoint the people, I still want to see Lord Teng Dong Lang teach that dragonman a lesson.”

“Did I mishear Lord Teng Dong Lang, why didn’t he agree to fight? His opponent is just a silver level!”

“That dragonman needs to beat all of the Bayonet Gang’s six ringleaders to be qualified to challenge Teng Dong Lang. I think that is reasonable.”

“Can that outsider really beat them? Everyone knows the six ringleaders are no simple silver levels.”

“He will go challenge the six ringleaders, I want to see how strong he is, what boldness does he have?”

The large crowd of pedestrians followed behind the dragonman youngster, looking ready to see a good show.

Ban Lan Gen was the name of a treeman.

When his subordinate notified him of this matter, he was eating breakfast too.

Branches spread across his head, and gaudy tree leaves were rolled out as much as possible, so he could absorb as much sunlight as he wanted.

His ten long fingers and toes stretched out, forming the shape of tree roots that dug into a bathtub in the ground.

The bath tub was filled with clear and slightly sweet spring water.

Ban Lan Gen was absorbing sunlight and water, lounging and unwilling to budge.

Upon hearing the dragonman youngster was rushing over to him, Ban Lan Gen curled his lip and paid the dragonman youngster no heed.

“Year after year, there is someone who wants to challenge the gang leader, but a silver level doing so is rather rare.”

“This year it is a dragonman, how strong are they?”

An underling urged him, “Boss, you can’t be negligent, since the other person dared to challenge our gang leader, they must be capable.”

The rate of the bathtub’s draining water level visibly decreased.

Ban Lan Gen slowly shut his eyes and rocked his recliner.

This was far from the first time someone had acted similarly to the dragonman youngster.

Many transcendents used heroic duels to display their majesty and to raise their renown.

Once, there was a silver level who deliberately challenged Teng Dong Lang and did their best to leave a profound impression on him, then joined the Bayonet Gang, it was quite the scheme.

“The Lord Gang Leader even transmitted an order that you will fight properly, boss.” The underling reminded him again.

Ban Lan Gen nodded, “I know what the gang leader intends. He is a gold level and feels it beneath his dignity to take advantage of the small. Therefore, we are to act and give that outsider a good lesson on his behalf.”

The dragonman youngster traveled quite quickly.

Not long after, Ban Lan Gen saw the dragonman youngster and Lan Zao with his own eyes.

“I know why you have come.”

“I’m actually quite bothered by your type. The Lord Gang Leader has his business, and I also have my own affairs I need to deal with.”

“Do you know how much of my time this challenge wastes?”

“However, since the Lord Gang Leader has given the order, then I will reluctantly fight you.”

“But let’s make it simple, don’t take too much time.”

“This is what’s going to happen, I will stand here, not attacking or defending. You will throw one fist, and so long as you knock me back, you will win. How about it, do you dare agree to my challenge?”

“Huh?” The dragonman youngster’s face twitched.

He recalled some information on Ban Lan Gen.

This treeman was an expert at defense, especially when he stood motionless and had his roots dug deep into the earth. His defense and recovery abilities were rather astonishing then.

Although Ban Lan Gen’s suggestion seemed arrogant, it actually demonstrated his strengths and was full of scheming.

If he won, he would have face on both ends.

“But is it fine for you to underestimate me like that?” The dragonman youngster laughed inwardly, but on the face of it, he revealed a hint of anger.

He chided Ban Lan Gen, “You are a silver level, and so am I. Suggesting this kind of dueling requirement makes it seem you rather look down on me.”

“You should give respect to one on the same level as yourself.”

“Since you decided to propose such a method of competition, then I accept, receive my fist!”

Just as he said that the dragonman youngster came at him.

He mobilized his Bursting Explosion battle qi, while simultaneously activating the Bloodcore to bring about the spurting smoke dragon transformation.

In a split second, dragon ring mounds appeared on the dragonman youngster’s elbows, arms, and on the back of his hands.

Then the dragonman youngster took a deep breath, one which caused Lan Zao to hear the rustling of the wind.

Battle skill – Surge!

Battle skill – Demolition Fist!

The dragonman youngster had practiced these two battle skills to the deepest level, and he could use them on instinct and as second nature.

Meanwhile, Ban Lang Gen had just placed his roots on the soil.

“Oh no, I didn’t have time to get ready!” Ban Lan Gen internally cried out in fear.

He didn’t anticipate the dragonman youngster to attack so soon after speaking and was forced into a race against time to use his defensive battle skills.

Battle skill -

It was too late!

A moment later, the dragonman youngster vanished from where he stood, and abruptly appeared in front of Ban Lan Gen, ruthlessly filling the latter’s entire field of view.

Then with a tick and massive dragon fist, he ferociously struck Ban Lan Gen in the chest.

Bang!

With a great boom, Ban Lan Gen soared like a ball, smashing through three full streets, until finally crashing into a hotel, collapsing it.

After the smoke and dust cleared, Ban Lan Gen was seen lying in the ruins, his eyes rolled back to white and his mouth spitting out foam, he was unconscious.

“Boss!” The Bayonet Gang members all cried out in alarm and rushed around him to give him a helping hand.

Ban Lan Gen had lost all fighting strength!

Everyone around turned to look at the dragonman youngster with astonishment.

Some people around came over.

They stood outside the crowd, on their tippy toes to look inward, asking, “What just happened?”

“Where did that loud explosion come from?”

“Did a fight start? Why is no one moving?”

Chapter 482: Spring Boxer

The crowd was in an uproar.

“What did I just see?”

“This guy! He launched Ban Lan Gen with a single fist?”

“Oh dear, Ban Lan Gen was too careless.”

“Hey, what just happened?”

After they were told, their eyes widened with incredulousness.

Those who knew the ropes were also shaken.

Even though Ban Lan Gen hadn’t used his true strength, to be knocked out by a single fist meant the commander of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was most certainly no simpleton.

“He has the courage to challenge Lord Teng Dong Lang, naturally he has to be capable.”

The dragonman youngster loosened his fist, and slowly stood up, purposely asking Lan Zao loudly, “One down, who is the second?”

The dragonman youngster looked calm, while Lan Zao was more excited.

The latter loudly replied, “Lord Commander, the next block leader is Spring Boxer.”

The dragonman youngster nodded, “Very good, let’s go to him next.”

The two advanced, and the crowd automatically parted for them.

The dragonman youngster’s strength had swept away those questioning him, and in exchanged was now looked at with disbelief, curiosity, and reverence.

Teng Dong Lang quickly received news of this fight.

Blue veins showed across his forehead as he clenched his teeth in anger, “Ban Lan Gen, this guy!”

He had intentionally reminded Ban Lan Gen and didn’t expect him to be beaten like this.

A headache.

Teng Dong Lang clutched his forehead, he had implicitly ordered Ban Lan Gen to entangle the dragonman youngster as much as possible, to probe into him and at best, scout out his trump cards.

In the end though, Ban Lan Gen didn’t understand that hidden meaning and remained arrogant, leading to the dragonman youngster to dropping him in a single fist.

Besides contributing to the dragonman youngster’s renown, Ban Lan Gen was completely useless.

“In any case, my plan is sound. Long Fu has already fallen into it, and in light of the plan, he still needs to beat 5 other people. Even if Ban Lan Gen wasn’t able to, the next 5 people should be able to force out his trump cards.”

Teng Dong Lang handled this matter very prudently.

He had the burden of his position; he couldn’t lose this war rashly.

If he lost, he would not only lose his prestige, but that of the entire Bayonet Gang, and even impair their interests.

“Long Fu will be going to Xiao Tan next…”

“Xiao Tan…I fear he isn’t Long Fu’s match.”

The Xiao Tan that Teng Dong Lang had his heart on was Spring Boxer.

The latter was a young man, not just the only snow elf among the six ringleaders, but also the son of his former comrade.

On the battlefield, his comrade of his had heroically sacrificed himself to take a shot for Teng Dong Lang. On his deathbed, he asked Teng Dong Lang to look after his sole kin in the world.

Teng Dong Lang took extreme care of Xiao Tan, however, gunfire on the battlefield had no eyes.

In the first battle, Xiao Tan was taken captive and narrowly avoided being eaten by the enemy.

Although Teng Dong Lang hastily risked his life to rescue Xiao Tan, Xiao Tan’s forearms and lower legs had been chopped off, crippling him.

Too much time had passed, and a priest was unable to heal him.

Xiao Tan consequently sank into despondence for a good while.

Teng Dong Lang then spent a huge sum to modify Xiao Tan’s body alchemically, molding him into the Spring Boxer, one of the six ringleaders of the Bayonet Gang.

But Teng Dong Lang knew in his heart that Spring Boxer’s strength was considered mid-level among the six ringleaders, and he would be unable to stop Long Fu’s advance.

After pondering it over, Teng Dong Lang suddenly said, “Someone, give my order to Xiao Tan, he will fight Long Fu well and see the ins and outs of his skills, the more information, the better.”

The underling hurried to follow and pass on the order.

This time, Teng Dong Lang learned from his prior experience and made his order clear and obvious.

Spring Boxer would definitely understand it!

Spring Boxer first received news of Ban Lan Gen’s defeat, then he received Teng Dong Lang’s order.

This young snow elf didn’t dare to neglect his duty, so he decided to sally over and intercepted the dragonman youngster and Lan Zao midway.

The dragonman youngster sized up Spring Boxer.

The young snow elf’s hands were both yellow boxing gloves. They wrapped all the way around and emitted a silver level aura.

Apart from that, Spring Boxer’s ankle boots were also silver level equipment.

What was most notable was his elbows and knees, which had all been replaced by thick and enormous elastic springs.

His body had been remolded by alchemy, and that was where his nickname, Spring Boxer, came from.

“Long Fu, you want to challenge Lord Teng Dong Lang, then you’ll have to get through me first! I will stop you with all my strength.” Spring Boxer was solemn and respectful.

The dragonman youngster slyly smiled, commending him, “Not bad, you have a far more proper attitude than Ban Lan Gen. Giving your opponent respect will usher in their respect for you.”

“It is with the greatest respect that I wholeheartedly engross myself in this battle with you!”

“Then come and fight!” Spring Boxer roared, and without fooling around, charged at Long Fu.

Halfway through the charge, the springs that were now his knees firmly compressed, then vigorously sprung out, bestowing him great motive force.

Spring Boxer accelerated massively from this.

The dragonman youngster held his fists up and stood his ground, prepared for battle.

He didn’t move, yet Spring Boxer felt an intense oppression from him.

“This guy…” Spring Boxer secretly clenched his teeth and sensibly altered his direction.

Whoosh!

Spring Boxer circled around the dragonman youngster, shooting from both sides of the street in all directions

His speed grew faster, becoming a shadow that an ordinary person had a hard time distinguishing.

The black streaking silhouette interweaved into a net that gradually hemmed in the dragonman youngster. The dragonman youngster seemed to be like an insect falling into a web that was made of Spring Boxer’s silhouette shooting in all directions.

Suddenly, Spring Boxer launched at the dragonman youngster’s back, throwing a fierce fist.

The dragonman youngster didn’t move.

“It hit!” Spring Boxer excitedly thought, and both of his fists were about to pound into the dragonman youngster’s back.

Suddenly, a red shadow attacked, blowing a strong wind.

Spring Boxer quickly blocked it with his left fist.

Pow.

A moment later, the dragonman youngster’s dragon tail slammed into Spring Boxer’s left arm.

The enormous power seriously perturbed Spring Boxer’s boxing glove connector.

His offensive immediately crumbled.

The dragonman youngster took advantage of that to turn around and grab Spring Boxer’s left boxing glove.

Spring Boxer fought back but found it hard to struggle free.

The dragonman youngster suddenly raised his foot and ruthlessly kicked Spring Boxer’s abdomen.

Ding.

With a dull echo, Spring Boxer’s frail body nearly folded into two.

The instant he suffered that heavy blow, his eyes nearly flew out of their sockets.

Battle skill – Reverse Spring Leg!

Spring Boxer’s elastic spring legs firmly compressed again, and with silver battle qi washing into them, he successfully used his battle skill.

A moment later, his legs kicked the dragonman youngster’s chest.

Reverse Spring violently launched his body backwards.

At the same time, his right boxing glove used a battle skill – Spiral Fist Wind!

This battle skill was supposed to help his attacks, but now, Spring Boxer was using it to throw off the dragonman youngster’s grapple.

Spring Boxer’s elastic spring arms twisted and compressed, then extended back open.

His left arm suddenly launched forward with an intense cyclonic force.

The dragonman youngster’s left hand was holding Spring Boxer’s fist.

But when the latter’s fist began to spin instantly, the friction across his palm caused the dragonman youngster to let go.

He didn’t have the appropriate battle skill to obstruct Spring Boxer’s battle skill.

Spring Boxer’s arm was freed as a result, and with the help of Reverse Spring Legs’ momentum, he was able to struggle free from the dragonman youngster’s grasp and jump far away, putting space between him and the dragonman youngster.

“Cough, cough, cough!” Spring Boxer squatted on the ground, coughing intensely.

The dragonman youngster’s kick was so powerful that even if he had battle qi protecting him, he wouldn’t have been able to withstand it.

At this moment, pain was spreading and intensifying across his abdomen.

Spring Boxer’s entire face gradually went flush, and matching his squatting hunched stoop, his entire likeness looked like a boiled curled up lobster, a sorry figure indeed.

“I fell into his trap!”

“Long Fu, this guy, he really is strong.”

Spring Boxer looked up at the dragonman youngster, his face showing a trace of terror.

The Flame Dragon King bloodline was legend level, and it gave the dragonman youngster impressive dynamic vision and powerful hearing.

Normal people wouldn’t be able to see Spring Boxer clearly, but the dragonman youngster had locked onto Spring Boxer’s position.

Then, the dragonman youngster purposely exposed a weak point and didn’t look at where Spring Boxer really was, using his back to withstand Spring Boxer’s charge.

Spring Boxer, as expected, walked right into the dragonman youngster’s trap.

The first contest of testing had concluded in a flash.

“How dangerous, luckily I struggled free! If he grappled me, the consequences would be unthinkable.” Spring Boxer had lingering fears.

The dragonman youngster looked at his palm with interest, sensing the intense friction that felt boiling hot.

Then the dragonman youngster slowly looked up towards the squatting Spring Boxer.

A smile emerged from the corner of his mouth, and he praised, “How interesting, you got free of my grab by using an agility battle skill. You are a talented combatant and aren’t rigidly adherent to how battle skills are supposed to be used.”

Spring Boxer coldly snorted, “Don’t think a few words of praise can make me throw the match!”

He stood back up and catapulted back into it.

With the help of the walls, ground, and other things around him, Spring Boxer once again became as fast as before, becoming a black phantom.

“No, not fast enough!” Spring Boxer grinded his teeth secretly and used more force to reach the limits of his speed.

The dragonman youngster was still standing there, watching Spring Boxer bounce all over the place.

Gradually, Spring Boxer’s shadow slowly drew near to the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster remained motionless, as steady as Mt. Tai.

Due to his prior loss, Spring Boxer didn’t act so blindly this time.

Many times, he moved behind the dragonman youngster but didn’t attack rashly.

Because he knew this was a weak point the dragonman youngster was deliberately flaunting off to him.

Chapter 483: Come, Use Your Strength, Hit Me!

The dragonman youngster waited for a long time, and when he saw Spring Boxer wasn’t attacking, he couldn’t help but ask, “Why aren’t you attacking?”

“Be a bit courageous!”

Spring Boxer turned a deaf ear.

“You’re always jumping about; don’t you hate getting tired?”

Wasting stamina like this was unwise.

Spring Boxer feigned ignorance.

The dragonman youngster deeply sighed, “This battle is taking too long. Get a move on, I still plan on challenging the other ringleaders.

Spring Boxer burst into laughter, “Commander Long Fu, do you think such simple insults are enough to entrap me?”

“Today’s battle is foreordained to be a battle of attrition!”

Spring Boxer remembered Teng Dong Lang’s order, he wanted to fight a prolonged battle, thereby learning more of how the dragonman youngster fought.

The dragonman youngster gazed at the bouncing Spring Boxer and suddenly suggested, “Come hit me, young snow elf, I won’t retaliate.”

“As long as you can make me bleed, you will win. My challenge will end here, and I won’t challenge the other ringleaders, let alone your gang leader.”

The onlookers’ expressions changed.

The dragonman youngster’s words felt very familiar to them. Previously, Ban Lan Gen had said a similar thing to the dragonman youngster.

“Huh?” Spring Boxer’s mind shook.

He continued to launch in all directions while pondering: what was this dragonman youngster plotting?

“Long Fu clearly wants to resolve this in the quickest way possible. He has great faith in his defense, and the Lord Gang Leader told me repeatedly that I had to test how he fights. I cannot give the enemy what they want!”

Spring Boxer shouted, “I won’t agree to that. I want an honest fight, use all of your strength and skills to carry out an honest duel.”

“Your proposal looks down on me too much!”

The dragonman youngster chuckled, and ignored Spring Boxer’s refusal, “Just do as I say.”

“Come.” He put his hands behind his back, stood still very straightly, and left himself wide open, “I will only block and won’t counterattack.”

When Spring Boxer saw his posture, his heart jumped again, feeling more that this was a trap.

He increasingly didn’t dare to act thoughtlessly.

However, this was all within the dragonman youngster’s expectations.

Very quickly, the onlookers grew impatient.

To them, it seemed that Spring Boxer was just jumping around and not attacking, what was this dillydallying?

“Hit him now.”

“What is all that jumping?”

“Aiya, this makes me so anxious. What is the meaning of this procrastinating?”

The crowd who watched for fun usually didn’t like such things.

When Spring Boxer heard these words, he grit his teeth harder.

He was aware things were far from good: if he kept delaying, it would make the people mistakenly believe that he feared his opponent and didn’t dare attack him directly.

His name and the Bayonet Gang’s name would be implicated as a result.

Left with no other choice, Spring Boxer was forced to eventually attack.

Battle skill – Spring Shot Fist!

Both of Spring Boxer’s arm springs compressed, then suddenly released. The powerful elastic force pushed his fists like an artillery shell being fired.

The glittering silver glowing boxing gloves smashed into the dragonman youngster’s chest.

However, the dragonman youngster was as motionless as a mountain range.

Spring Boxer’s pupils contracted, and with a great shout, he used the battle skill again.

He pulled back his arms, brought together his fingers, and merged them into a singular fist.

His elbow springs compressed again, then discharged.

·

Battle skill – Double Shot Fist!

Spring Boxer’s eyes glistened with the will to win.

This was his strongest attack!

He once tried this attack on a gold level shield.

Under the double fist bombardment, the gold level shield cracked a tiny bit.

“Fall for me, Long Fu!” Spring Boxer shouted internally.

But in reality, the dragonman youngster’s body only shook a bit.

The hard and heavy fists did not a wisp of harm to his chest, nor were any of the dragon scales broken.

“Interesting.”

“But not good enough.”

“Come again!” The dragonman youngster laughed to Spring Boxer.

Spring Boxer was shocked, which soon gave way to more intense rage.

He brought back both arms, and without a moment to breathe, he charged once more.

·

Battle skill – Double Shot Fist!

The heavy fists struck the dragonman youngster’s chest again, causing his body to subsequently sway.

“Not strong enough, again.” The dragonman youngster growled.

·

Thus, Spring Boxer used Double Shot Fist a third time.

And this time, the dragonman youngster’s body shook far less than the two previous times.

“Do you not have the strength? Why are you getting weaker and weaker?” The dragonman youngster deliberately yelled with disappointment.

Spring Boxer gasped for air.

That was his most powerful attack, and he couldn’t use it in succession.

It was an immense burden on his body and elbow springs.

“I don’t believe your defense can remain this sturdy forever!” Spring Boxer howled.

Pushed by anger, he stepped in front of the dragonman youngster.

Battle skill – Reckless Fist.

Ding, ping, ding…

Spring Boxer wildly swung his fists, his rapid punches lifting a furious storm of attacks.

Because the dragonman youngster wasn’t dodging, every single one of Spring Boxer’s fists hit him.

The dragonman youngster held up his head and chest high, had both arms behind his back, and strictly abided by his words, he did not retaliate and was always on the defensive.

This kind of attitude provoked Spring Boxer into further frenzy.

The rate of his attacks accelerated, causing his battle qi consumption to reach an unprecedented level.

The power of Reckless Fist was able to force the dragonman youngster to take some steps backwards.

But the dragonman youngster’s scales weren’t even chipped.

The dragonman youngster was forced back by Spring Boxer’s attacks, yet he appeared to be smiling excitedly, constantly encouraging his opponent.

“Good, very good.”

“Keep attacking!”

“You’re finally doing something.”

“If this keeps up, perhaps you might really succeed in making me bleed.”

When Spring Boxer heard those words, he was unable to restrain his anger, and a roar rumbled from his mouth.

The fists became more frantic.

The dragonman youngster continued to take several steps back, then he began to feel Spring Boxer’s offensive weakening.

He immediately asked, “Are you getting tired?”

“Did you eat breakfast?”

Spring Boxer was furious, and his punching force increased, even surpassing his prior limits.

But such things didn’t last forever, and very quickly, Spring Boxer weakened again.

The dragonman youngster used a despairing tone to assess him.

“Your stamina is too low.”

“Use all of your strength against me, don’t stay your hand, attack me with everything you have.”

“Is that it? Is that all you have?”

“Use your strength, use all of your strength!”

“Is this your full strength?”

“Should you go eat something? Or perhaps, drink some milk?”

“Ah, ah, ah…” Spring Boxer angrily bellowed, his voice hoarse.

He had been full of youthful vigor.

The dragonman youngster’s words really angered him.

But that intense ire couldn’t make the dragonman youngster cough up blood.

Spring Boxer’s stamina and battle qi also couldn’t be replenished by the wrath filling his heart.

The battle skill, Reckless Fist, gradually ceased.

Spring Boxer’s fists quickly slowed.

Gradually, the ordinary people watching could see his movements again.

Finally, his fists became so slow, it seemed like there were shot puts tied to them.

“Huff, huff, huff…”

He panted, focused solely on taking in air, and no longer able to bellow.

His fists stopped punching, and even his arms went slack.

But his eyes were still furiously glaring at the dragonman youngster, exposing his unyielding fighting spirit.

The dragonman youngster looked at Spring Boxer and smiled, “You being able to force me back so many steps is actually pretty good.”

Those words were like an incorporeal iron awl that deeply drilled into the young snow elf’s mind.

Spring Boxer was so angry his vision was growing dark, and his sweat plastered body was violently swaying, nearly causing him to fall on his buttocks.

“A bit painful.” The dragonman youngster wiped his chest clean.

The red dragon scales looked as they had before, not a single scratch on them.

This attracted the gaze of everyone.

The onlookers all exclaimed in unison.

“A phony?”

“Heavens, how is he not even injured in the slightest?!”

“What did I just witness?!”

“What monstrous defense, I pity Spring Boxer a little.”

All kinds of comments wafted into Spring Boxer’s ears, and his glowering eyes at the dragonman youngster were gradually replaced by bewilderment.

The dragonman youngster really wasn’t injured in the slightest, but this wasn’t because of his dragon scales.

His legend level Flame Dragon King Bloodline wasn’t fake, but it was incomplete.

The dragonman youngster’s dragon scales were comparable to gold level armor, but Spring Boxer’s all-out attack couldn’t be looked down upon and its strength was considerable. Ordinary gold level armor would crack after taking so many blows.

If the dragonman youngster was relying just on his dragon scales, the scales on his chest would have been damaged.

The reason why this didn’t happen was because the dragonman youngster was wearing the Gold Silk Chainmail!

This gold level armor was something the survivors had purchased from Two Eyes Island.

The dragonman youngster wanted to challenge the gold level Teng Dong Lang and naturally made some preparations for it.

The survivors had put the Deceptive Disguise and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spells on the Gold Silk Chainmail, making it completely “invisible” to any normal person’s eyes.

Even when Spring Boxer was constantly punching the dragonman youngster, he wouldn’t detect anything wrong with how it felt.

Deceptive Disguise’s casting material was the god level material, the Pearl Bubbles. Under normal circumstances, this magic could be maintained through external attacks.

Therefore, the dragonman youngster protected himself not only with his dragon scales, but also with the Gold Silk Chainmail.

With double the gold level armor, he was very confident in his defenses.

Actual combat also validated the dragonman youngster’s confidence.

Spring Boxer’s firepower was quite impressive, but even after depleting his body’s strength, he couldn’t harm the dragonman youngster. The dragon scales and the Gold Silk Chainmail prevented the dragonman youngster from being scratched; him coughing up blood was out of the question.

Chapter 484: Accepted Wholeheartedly

“This monster!”

“How can there be a dragonman with such defenses?”

“Spring Boxer attacked him wildly yet didn’t leave a scratch! If I didn’t see it with my own eyes, I would have never believed it.”

“Apparently, an amazing figure has come to our Snow Bird Harbor.”

“It’s no wonder he wants to challenge Lord Teng Dong Lang!”

“With that defense, I think he is qualified.”

The onlookers wagged their tongues.

Public opinion was gradually shifting.

At first the people were very skeptical of the dragonman youngster.

But after the dragonman youngster knocked Ban Lan Gen unconscious with a single punch, the people began to look at him squarely.

Now, the dragonman youngster had taken Spring Boxer’s attacks head on and was never scratched. Such a marvel caused the people to universally recognize his strength.

The dragonman youngster focused on Spring Boxer, somewhat astonished.

Last night, when he read the intelligence report on Spring Boxer, he discovered that their fighting style was similar to his.

For instance, their assault methods in battle was almost identical.

The difference was that the dragonman got driving force from battle qi explosions while Spring Boxer did so through elastic force produced by his springs.

The battle skills Spring Boxer displayed in succession were also similar to the dragonman youngster’s Demolition Fist.

“That final battle skill, Reckless Fist is actually suitable for me to cultivate.”

“He punched many times in a row, and the Grand Arena Medal should have absorbed a lot of experience.”

The dragonman youngster would have half the effort and twice the results cultivating Reckless Fist.

That was why the dragonman youngster provoked Spring Boxer constantly and decided to not retaliate, it was all to entice them into attacking him constantly to display his fighting methods as much as he liked.

Spring Boxer was not just an opponent for the dragonman youngster, he was more so a mineral deposit.

“This deposit can still be excavated.” The dragonman youngster didn’t plan on defeating Spring Boxer.

But Spring Boxer was at a loss and despairing, he had lost all of his fighting spirit and didn’t seem he was going to keep fighting.

The dragonman youngster said, “What’s with you?”

“Why did you stop?”

“Are you out of stamina?”

“You are one of the Bayonet Gang’s ringleaders, is that all you have?”

“It seems Teng Dong Lang doesn’t have an eye for talent; he actually promoted a waste like yourself to ringleader.”

Spring Boxer had been indifferent, but when he heard that last sentence, he suddenly hissed like a cat with its hair standing up, “Shut your mouth, I won’t allow you to insult Lord Teng Dong Lang!”

“Oh?” The dragonman youngster’s eye shined upon finding a new way to provoke Spring Boxer.

“Then come and fight, keep punching with your fists.”

“Prove Teng Dong Lang made the right choice!”

“Ah, ah, ah!” Spring Boxer bellowed in rage; he used all he had to raise his right arm and punch the dragonman youngster’s chest.

The dragonman youngster didn’t move even a bit, and the reaction force caused Spring Boxer to fall backwards.

A moment later, he really was sitting on the ground.

The dragonman youngster shook his head with disappointment, “You are too tired, your stamina is gone, and you have lost.”

After saying that, the dragonman youngster turned around to leave.

Spring Boxer shouted soon after, “Don’t leave, you wretched man. I can still…still fight.”

The dragonman youngster stopped walking and looked back to see Spring Boxer in the process of climbing to his feet.

But he really was exhausted, his limbs were aching and limp, and everyone passing by could see his arms and thighs constantly trembling.

After spending a good deal of effort, he managed to stagger back up.

Spring Boxer had remembered Teng Dong Lang’s order.

“I will fight with all my strength, to probe into that young dragonman’s methods!”

“This guy is no ordinary silver level, he is a monster, a gold level threat.”

“The more information I piece out, the greater Lord Teng Dong Lang’s odds of success.”

Seeing the swaying Spring Boxer, Lan Zao sneered, “What are you thinking? Going to pester us? As a ringleader of the grand Bayonet Gang, do you not have the forbearance to admit defeat?”

But a moment later, the dragonman youngster stopped Lan Zao from further berating.

He took the measure of Spring Boxer with interest, stating, “I have to say, Spring Boxer, you have made me respect you on a whole new level.”

“That kind of fighting spirit is deserving of respect.”

“So sorry. I take back saying you were a waste. As long as you retain that kind of fighting spirit and toughness, you will never be a waste!”

“You are very strong, unfortunate that you happened to meet me.”

“Right, those eyes exactly, that is the expression of a fighter who will never surrender, who will never give up.”

“Spring Boxer, you are excellent.”

“I promise you, as long as you don’t give up, I will keep fighting you today until the end!”

Spring Boxer panted for air, his tired eyes were half shut, for it was hard to keep them open.

The dragonman youngster’s words of praise gave him a new impetus.

Because the dragonman youngster’s words were pleasant to listen to, especially the phrase “as a fighter, one should never surrender, never give up”, those words simply spoke what Spring Boxer thought from the bottom of his heart.

Spring Boxer couldn’t help but have a strong sense of like-mindedness with the dragonman youngster.

Then when he heard that the dragonman youngster would “fight with him until the end”, he immediately laid down his worries, feeling that there was still a chance that her could accomplish Teng Dong Lang’s order.

However, he was exhausted, and there was no way he could keep fighting. How could he force the dragonman youngster to use his trump cards?

Just as Spring Boxer sank into panic, he heard the dragonman youngster say, “You are too tired.”

“Give yourself time to rest, it isn’t necessary to rush back into the fight.”

Spring Boxer’s body shivered, and he scowled at the dragonman youngster again.

He didn’t know if it was all in his mind, but it seemed the dragonman youngster had grown loftier in his eyes.

“What?”

The onlookers felt somewhat surprised.

Truth be told, heroic duels were a common sight in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

But allowing an opponent to rest during a duel was a rare sight indeed.

“Since that is so, then I will rest!” Spring Boxer sat back down, then shouted to an underling, “Get me some water to drink!”

He had gasped too heavily, and his throat was burning, as if his lungs were baking.

The underling quickly brought over a cup of water and Spring Boxer drained it a single gulp.

Another brought medicine.

Spring Boxer hesitated for a moment, then glanced at the dragonman youngster to see him smiling.

Spring Boxer coldly snorted, and with a mild blush, drank down the medicine and swallowed it.

The dragonman youngster patiently waited.

An hour quickly passed.

The spectators gradually grew impatient, and some began to grumble: how much longer did they need to wait?

The dragonman youngster was in no rush.

He was very patient for the imminent battle experience.

Spring Boxer couldn’t endure the comments and stood up again.

“I can keep fighting now.”

The dragonman youngster grinned, “Since you’ve rested enough, then come at me.”

They both began to fight each other once more.

To be more accurate, Spring Boxer unleashed his fists upon the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster didn’t retaliate, still remaining on the defense.

A few minutes later, Spring Boxer was half dead from exhaustion once again.

“You can stop and rest for a bit.” The dragonman youngster suggested.

Spring Boxer lowered his head, not daring to look at the crowd’s eyes, he considered giving up, but when he thought of the mission Teng Dong Lang had given him, and in the end, he forced himself to stay in it and rest.

His underlings gave him water, and another massaged his muscles to loosen them sufficiently, so he could more quickly recover his stamina.

The dragonman youngster came to Spring Boxer, and the Bayonet Gang member vigilantly watched him actually give him a potion.

“My potion will be quite good for you. Not only can it restore stamina, but it can also help you create battle qi faster.”

Spring Boxer stared blankly at it.

When the dragonman youngster saw his hesitation, he tutted, “Drink it if you’re brave enough!”

That provocation was enough for Spring Boxer to take the potion.

“I’m not brave enough?” Spring Boxer coldly snorted and glowered at the dragonman youngster.

He popped the potion’s cork and drained it instantly, slapping away his underlings that tried to stop him.

A moment later, he changed color slightly and blurted out, “It really is a good potion.”

The medicine took effect quickly.

Spring Boxer clearly felt his stamina quickly being restored, while simultaneously feeling his internal battle qi slowly rise without the need for a specific cultivation method.

The dragonman youngster’s potion was undoubtably good, it had been specially refined for him by Zi Di.

The potion’s recipe had come from War Merchant’s database and was of excellent quality.

Using it on Spring Boxer was equal to trading the potion for his battle experience.

If Zi Di was present, she would commend this great deal!

When the spectators saw this, they were dumbfounded.

Today’s event had broadened their horizons.

Many people had never seen a heroic duel where one let the other rest during it, nor had they seen one give their opponent a recovery potion while they rested.

After drinking the potion, Spring Boxer recovered at a faster rate.

He once again took to battle and dueled the dragonman youngster.

A short while later.

He was paralyzed from fatigue again…

Seeing the dragonman youngster standing straight, still untouched, Spring Boxer was puzzled, “Why is Long Fu’s chest feeling harder?”

The Grand Arena Medal was quietly taking effect.

It not only absorbed an opponent’s battle experience but also gave it to the wearer.

Furthermore, during the battle, the more eyes on the wearer, and the longer those eyes paid attention to them, the more the medal amplified the wearer.

This amplification included offense, defense, restoration, speed, and more.

“Abominable…such abnormal defense, how can I ever break through it?” Spring Boxer gazed at the dragonman youngster and couldn’t help but despair.

If this was his first time losing, Spring Boxer might not have accepted it.

The second time, perhaps he still felt it was a fluke from the bottom of his heart.

But now the dragonman youngster had let him rest again and again and let him try as much as he pleased.

Spring Boxer was convinced that he had used everything he had, and consequently, completely understood the immense disparity between him and the dragonman youngster.

He…was convinced.

“But I haven’t accomplished Lord Teng Dong Lang’s command, what can I do?” Spring Boxer was deeply disturbed.

Teng Dong Lang looked after Spring Boxer well, and Spring Boxer was very grateful for that, and always thought of ways to repay Teng Dong lang.

“What should I do?” Suddenly, Spring Boxer came up with an idea.

He looked up and glared at the dragonman youngster, “Long Fu, I have discovered your plan!”

The dragonman youngster was startled, “Oh? What are you saying?”

Spring Boxer deeply replied, “Is your speed inadequate?”

“You’ve been hiding your weakness.”

“You can’t actually match my speed, and when I am moving at high speed, you cannot attack me effectively.”
