“Therefore, you deliberately stood still, suggested that you would defend without counterattacking, and tricked me into abandoning my strengths and getting into a direct confrontation with you!”

That inference caused the spectators to stare blankly.

Upon careful thought, Spring Boxer’s words had some sense to it.

The dragonman youngster really didn’t display any speed.

For a moment, a great amount of suspicious and probing looks were cast on the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster’s face blossomed with another smile, “Since that is so, then let me win you over. Next, we shall change our manner of contest, we shall compete in terms of speed.”

“Good, that will do!” Spring Boxer made a fist, stirring himself.

He took off first, kicking his legs and launching himself forward.

The dragonman youngster watched him move and didn’t pursue hurriedly.

After Spring Boxer launched himself many times, his speed became lightning fast, and his body became a line of shadow, one hard for people to see.

“How about it, can you chase me down?” Spring Boxer arrogantly provoked the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster flatly replied, “Since you are ready, then I’m coming for you.”

Spring Boxer’s heart jumped, and he coldly snorted as he sped up further, faintly wanting to exceed his recorded limits.

But a moment later, a gigantic shadow enveloped him.

Spring Boxer looked up suddenly, and to his shock, he saw the dragonman youngster somehow appear right above him!

The dragonman youngster’s massive body covered the sun, and the heavy shadowing being cast on the young snow elf oppressed him with incomparable power.

Like an eagle catching a rabbit, the dragonman youngster grabbed the back of Spring Boxer’s neck with his dragon talon.

Ding.

He took advantage of that pressure to push Spring Boxer into the ground, smashing the floor tiles in the street.

“Fast, too fast! He is so quick and aggressive!” Spring Boxer’s face retained its dense shock as the dragonman youngster pinned him by the neck to the ground.

The dragonman youngster lightly released his dragon talon, and slowly stood up, looking down at Spring Boxer. He gently asked, “Want to go again?”

Spring Boxer returned to his senses; he reverently looked up at the dragonman youngster and wanted to shake his head.

But he thought of Teng Dong Lang’s order, so he had to clench his teeth and purposely feign denial, shouting, “I am unconvinced!”

“Then let’s do it again.” The dragonman youngster nodded, “I did say I would give you the greatest honor of accompanying you until the end.”

The dragonman youngster was glad to see Spring Boxer not conceding and still wanting to compete.

This kind of catapulting dash movement method was very similar to his Surge battle skill.

That kind of experience always suited the dragonman youngster and was very valuable to him.

Thus, the same scene constantly played out.

In the spectators’ point of view, there was one dark shadow and one red shadow flying through the street and tangling with each other, wherever one went, the other followed.

The street tiles and walls they trampled on were shattered.

The result of every competition was the same.

Spring Boxer always lost.

As the sun set in the west and gradually fell into the flat ocean, Spring Boxer couldn’t last any longer, despite how thick his face was.

The spectators had also become few.

Spring Boxer gave in, candidly saying to the dragonman youngster, “Commander Long Fu, you are the strongest silver level I have ever met. I feel that you are completely qualified to challenge our gang leader. I lost to you, I accept my loss wholeheartedly!”

The dragonman youngster encouraged him: your potential isn’t limited to this, in the future, perhaps we can compare notes with each other again.

Finally, with complex emotions, Spring Boxer watched the dragonman youngster and Lan Zao leave.

He was exhausted, completely limp, and his battle qi had dried up many times.

“Lord Teng Dong Lang, I really tried this time.”

“What a pity, even though I used all of my strength, I was unable to force him to use any trump cards.”

“I only proved that Long Fu is very strong, mindboggling strong!”

“Another thing…”

“Long Fu is not only a strong person, but he is also a venerable fighter and possesses a very noble character.”

This sort of transcendent was one Spring Boxer had accepted wholeheartedly.

Chapter 485: Speak, Don’t Give Constant Praise

Teng Dong Lang was looking at a magic image and sank into a long silence.

The magic image showed the entire fight between the dragonman youngster and Spring Boxer, along with the reactions and comments of the spectators.

“The situation is a little troublesome.” Teng Dong Lang pursed his brows.

He realized that the situation had subtly changed, and things had taken a turn for the worst for him and the Bayonet Gang.

On the one hand, it was because of the dragonman youngster’s incomprehensible fighting strength. On the other hand, it was also due to the crowd’s reaction.

The people universally expressed positive emotions about the dragonman youngster, such as esteem and curiosity.

Spring Boxer had always been esteemed and respected by the people.

But the dragonman youngster was in a completely different situation.

The people universally had a favorable impression of the dragonman youngster.

“How many days has he been in Snow Bird Harbor?”

Teng Dong Lang analyzed to himself the reason why this happened, it was mainly due to how the dragonman youngster behaved during the battle.

The dragonman youngster not only didn’t retaliate, but he also let his opponent rest during the duel and even gave them a potion so they would recover faster.

These actions were rarely seen, and both amazed the people in a novel manner, along with thoroughly displaying the dragonman youngster’s unique svelte as a transcendent.

Teng Dong Lang was certain that tonight, taverns large and small were sure to circulate the dragonman youngster’s deed.

Then, so long as the dragonman youngster’s challenge continued, his deeds would be disseminated like the wind.

The dragonman youngster’s dramatic rise in renown and influence troubled Teng Dong Lang.

“I was a little careless, no matter if it was his intention or this was all accidental, I cannot treat Long Fu or the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps like an ordinary power.”

The more Teng Dong Lang thought, the less he could sit still.

He decided to order all six ringleaders to convene for a meeting.

Very quickly, Ban Lan Gen, Spring Boxer, Xiong Ju, and the others showed up.

Teng Dong Lang, “Everyone should know about the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps matter. How should we deal with them? Tell me your ideas.”

The six ringleaders looked at each other for a moment, then gradually, everyone shifted their eyes towards Ban Lan Gen and Spring Boxer.

Spring Boxer was somewhat red.

Ban Lan Gen the treeman had a wooden expression.

After a moment of silence, Ban Lan Gen spoke first, “I was too careless. I didn’t expect Long Fu to attack right after he finished speaking, I didn’t have any time to use my defensive battle skills. Don’t be fooled by his magnanimity, he is actually very treacherous.!”

Spring Boxer suddenly pursed his brows and retorted, “I don’t believe that is the case, the main reason was because you didn’t give him the respect he deserved.”

“Commander Long Fu is the paragon of a transcendent. It’s just a fact you suffered a crushing defeat by his hands!”

“Even if you use your defensive skills, could you really defeat him?”

“Hehe, are you saying I don’t know your strength?”

“You are now slandering him to exonerate yourself, that makes you appear more despicable.”

Spring Boxer had accepted the dragonman youngster wholeheartedly and defended him subconsciously.

Ban Lan Gen immediately bellowed, “How much better did you do? You couldn’t even break a single one of his dragon scales, an utter loss of face!”

Spring Boxer coldly snorted, “At least I fought him for a while, you? How many seconds did you last?”

Ban Lan Gen angrily stood up, “Did you actually fight him? He gave you water, right? I would rather fight to the death and wouldn’t rest during the duel, let alone accept his potion. Where is your face? Did you feel no shame in doing that?”

For a moment, neither Ban Lan Gen nor Spring Boxer gave way, and all sorts of insults wrangled their way from their mouths.

Teng Dong Lang inwardly had a headache.

The meeting just began, and his own people already had an internal conflict.

The other four ringleaders didn’t participate contrary to expectations.

They saw Ban Lan Gen and Spring Boxer had very different opinions on Long Fu; this inevitably made them more curious – what kind of person was Long Fu?

Teng Dong Lang coughed twice, “Stop quarreling.”

He stopped the dispute between the two with a light sentence, revealing his immense prestige.

“We need to think over how we should deal with Long Fu.” Teng Dong Lang ran his eyes over them, his gaze quickly streaking past Ban Lan Gen.

Ban Gen Leng had been defeated too swiftly; his opinion didn’t have any value.

Teng Dong Lang’s eyes settled on Spring Boxer. “I want your thoughts.”

Spring Boxer had fought Long Fu for a long time, and of the people present, he was most qualified to speak on the matter.

Spring Boxer tightly scowled, then after a moment of deliberation, he said, “Long Fu is very strong! He definitely is no ordinary silver level.”

“His greatest strength is his defense. I have never met a silver level who possessed such terrifying defense. I attacked him with my full strength yet didn’t leave a scratch on him!”

“Truth be told, if someone told me about it before the battle, I would have believed it to be a gargantuan joke. But now, after personally experiencing it, I would no longer laugh at it.”

“Thinking back now, I was very dismayed, frustrated, and even felt hopeless!”

As a matter of fact, it wasn’t just Spring Boxer. When the other five ringleaders saw the magic image, they didn’t laugh either.

The dragonman youngster’s observable defense shocked them.

Spring Boxer continued, “Commander Long Fu caused the main focus to be on his defense, but everyone please, don’t underestimate his other attributes as a result. I believe that Long Fu is a holistically formidable fighter, he has no obvious shortcomings.”

“At minimum, his mobility is very strong, completely above mine.”

The five ringleaders were silent.

They had all seen the magic image; some had done so several times.

They had seen the dragonman youngster’s speed utterly trounce Spring Boxer’s.

But they didn’t understand that this was actually after the dragonman youngster had been amplified by the Grand Arena Medal.

This medal and the Gold Silk Chainmail were both made “invisible” by Deceptive Disguise.

Spring Boxer added on, “I believe that Commander Long Fu absolutely has other skills. I am just not strong enough and was unable to force them out. Think carefully everyone, he is a dragonman, he most definitely isn’t lacking in recovery capabilities.”

“I have a strong feeling that when he and I fought, he never truly displayed his offensive capability either.”

“Because I had no way of dealing with his defense.”

No one spoke.

The atmosphere was saturated with pressure.

Ban Lan Gen might be the one under the least pressure.

Because he had lost already.

The other four ringleaders, along with Gang Leader Teng Dong Lang, would be challenged by the dragonman youngster.

Spring Boxer looked at Ban Lan Gen and sneered, “There is nothing shameful in admitting one’s defeat, nor is recognizing an opponent’s power.”

“Commander Long Fu is very strong.”

“His strength makes people respect him.”

“Like he said to me, so long as others respect him, he will give them equal respect!”

“He obviously could have defeated me quickly, but he acknowledged my fighting determination and kept fighting me until dusk. He strictly abided by the promises he made during the fight and never violated them.”

“He is an adversary worthy of respect. I look forward to the fierce battle between him and the Lord Gang Leader!”

Spring Boxer’s opinion was clear; he believed that Long Fu was qualified to challenge Teng Dong Lang.

Teng Dong Lang couldn’t help but cough again, sighing to himself, “Xiao Tan…I want you to speak about your experiences, not constantly praise him and foster the enemy’s imposition.”

“Alright. Your praise of your opponent ends here, tell me about his weak points.” Teng Dong Lang ordered.

But Spring Boxer just stared at him, then held out his hands, “Lord Gang Leader, I don’t think Long Fu has any weak points.”

This immediately annoyed the human ringleader.

“Enough, Spring Boxer.”

“You losing and being unable to force out Long Fu’s trump cards does not mean he doesn’t have flaws.”

“I watched your fight with him, and I have found at least two flaws.”

“The first flaw is his dashing. Are you telling me you didn’t notice? He moves similarly to you, a pause before exploding with speed, that is the biggest weak point of you both.”

“The second possible flaw is that Long Fu lacks long range attacks. When faced with you jumping all over the place, he first lured you into attacking him, then used swift movements to close the gap with you.”

Only when a fighter reached the gold level could their battle qi exist outside of them. This was known by all.

“Hehehe, you aren’t wrong.” The dwarf ringleader laughed, “Long Fu has one other flaw.”

“He doesn’t use a weapon.”

“In the intelligence report, he only used his fists and feet at the Battle of Two Eyes Island.”

“He might not have specialized weapons training, rather he has spent most of his time training unarmed fighting.”

The demon hybrid ringleader nodded, approving the dwarf ringleader’s words.

He said, “Long Fu is very young, and managing the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps takes a lot of his energy.”

“How much time does he have?”

“Being able to train his martial arts is already pretty good.”

“However, since he has the guts to challenge our gang leader, this indicates that he has trump cards capable of battling a gold level! As for what those trump cards are, we don’t know yet. The intelligence reports don’t have any clues.”

Those final words hit a nail deep into Teng Dong Lang’s heart.

Teng Dong Lang tapped the table with a finger, “Next, how do you plan on taking on Long Fu?”

The human ring leader tossed his opinion out casually, “Very simple. We contact a security official and have them make a trap and frame the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. Get them to jail their people.”

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ members are still on their ships, unable to freely move about.”

“But what’s the use of that?”

“Their ships are berths at the dock and are under Snow Bird Harbor government supervision.”

“So long as a security official makes a move, it will be very easy to frame them.”

“Every year we deliver them many benefits and now is a good chance to get something back from all the money we sent them.”

“You want to frame Commander Long Fu? Out of the question!” Spring Boxer objected to it at once.

He was disgusted by the human ringleader’s method and sneered, “Your small tricks outside the arena are not what a transcendent does, and it divulges your cowardice and fear of battle.”

The human ringleader raised his eyebrows and replied with annoyance, “I say, Spring Boxer, could it be that after Long Fu let you off, let you rest, and also gave you medicine that you now completely support him now? Don’t forget yourself, you are one of the Bayonet Gang’s ringleaders, what’s with your demeanor?”

Spring Boxer coldly snorted, “It was because I am a gang ringleader that I consider the Bayonet Gang’s name in its entirety! If this happens, what will the people think? Many people will know it in their hearts, and even if there isn’t proof, the perceptive will know it was our plot!”

“Do we not have any shame? If we can’t beat them and resort to assassination and entrapment, how will the people see our Bayonet Gang?”

The human ringleader was shocked when he heard that.

He stared at Spring Boxer with outrage, “Hey, have you gone soft in the head? We are the Bayonet Gang, a gang, we are not the army you came from! Besides, when the army fights, isn’t victory what comes first, and for that, any strategy and method can be used and considered!”

“You go so far as to consider our Bayonet Gang’s reputation, simply ridiculous! What need does our Bayonet Gang have for a good reputation?” The human ringleader didn’t conceal his reproach from Spring Boxer.

Spring Boxer was furious, he couldn’t refute the human ringleader, but he still showed his serious objections.

Ban Lan Gen seized the opportunity to kick him while was on the backfoot, saying curiously, “Some people who have lost to Long Fu hope that others also taste defeat, thus, making their own defeat not so conspicuous.”

Spring Boxer exploded in anger, he was just about to spit insults when Teng Dong Lang stopped him on time, “Enough, do you still want to argue?”

Spring Boxer and the others quickly stopped talking.

Teng Dong Lang muttered to himself, “We are a gang, not an army, that statement isn’t wrong.”

“But it is a bit early to use such a trick.”

“We are a medium sized power, while the other side is a small sized power.”

“Using this kind of trick outside the arena proves that you believe you are not confident in facing Long Fu and even feel that you will lose.”

“Long Fu has wanted to make this a big deal.”

“After today, many people will know about it, and there will be more eyes on us.”

“The best way of handling this for the Bayonet Gang is to defeat Long Fu outright.”

“I don’t need to get involved; you can force him to withdraw yourselves!”

When Teng Dong Lang said that, he stooped, and after looking around at them, he continued, “We must seize the initiative.”

“We cannot allow Long Fu to keep dueling us one by one.”

“If he beats you six, the Bayonet Gang’s reputation will be greatly damaged.”

“Therefore, tomorrow, I need one of you to challenge Long Fu and defeat him!”

When Teng Dong Lang said that the six ringleaders were silent once again.

Using their eyes to communicate with each other, all of them eventually settled their gazes on the beastman ringleader Xiong Ju.

Of the six bosses, Xiong Ju was first among them in terms of military strength.

The human ringleader spoke first, breaking the silence, “Xiong Ju is the strongest among us, if Long Fu can defeat him, then the rest of us have no hope of winning.”

“I fully support Xiong Ju!”

After saying that, the human gang leader gave him the epaulet he wore.

“This is my strongest equipment; I will loan it to you Xiong Ju for tomorrow’s fight.”

Everyone’s look changed at this.

The human gang leader’s epaulet was gold level equipment!

It was called the Flowing Wave Armor, and the human ringleader had relied on it to stop many assassination attempts and it was his life saving trump card.

But now, he had simply loaned it to Xiong Ju.

The human ringleader frankly said to Xiong Ju, “This is the best thing I can give you, the rest are worse than what you currently possess.”

Teng Dong Lang gave him an appreciative look.

The dwarf ringleader muttered to himself for a moment, then took out a short war hammer.

“This is my weapon that I meticulously forged, as you can see, it actually isn’t finished yet.”

The war hammer emitted a mixed and ununified aura.

The hammer’s head and shaft were silver level, while the gem inlaid into it emitted a gold level aura and sparkled with lightning.

“This is my recreation of the legendary Great Thunder God’s Flying Hammer – the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer.”

“Xiong Ju, I know your main weapon is very powerful, but you can treat this Small Thunder God Flying Hammer as a secondary weapon.”

Xiong Ju shook his head, “I have little experience with war hammers, and my hacksaw needs to be wielded by both of my hands, this weapon doesn’t fit me.”

Chapter 486: Teng Dong Lang’s Pills

Faced with Xiong Ju’s refusal, the dwarf ringleader shook his head, “The Small Thunder God Flying Hammer is very easy to use, you just need to throw it in advance, then with you as its center, it can hit any enemy within its circle of effect.”

“You can treat it as a quasi-gold level heavy flying dart.”

“I can help you train with it later, and coach you in its usages. It will take about a half hour for you to be completely proficient with its usage.”

“When the time comes, you can use your spirit to guide the hammer and force a truly close bond and coordination with it.”

Xiong Ju nodded, “In that case, it’s pretty good.”

The demon hybrid ringleader exclaimed in admiration, “You both took out such good things, it seems I too need to fish out my own thing.”

He suddenly turned his head upwards, took a deep breath, and then something came from his stomach, up his rolling throat, and then a damp eyeball spat out of his mouth.

The eyeball was as large as a fist; it was full of blood wisps and also had fine fleshy feelers.

“An Evil Eye?!” When everyone saw that eyeball, they were equally disturbed.

The eyeball fell onto the long table, and after bouncing twice, it extended its fleshy feelers and “stood up”.

Soon after, under the demon hybrid’s spiritual control, the eyeball used its fleshy feelers as legs to walk over to Xiong Ju.

Xiong Ju looked at the saliva doused eyeball, obviously detesting it.

The demon hybrid ringleader giggled maliciously, “Xiong Ju, you need to swallow it, it can grow in and fuse into anywhere in your body, as if it was a natural organ.”

“Besides giving you an expanded field of view, the most important thing it can do is shoot three spiritual rays in a short timeframe.”

“Barring any special defensive skills, it can even take down a gold level target. As long as one’s spiritual power is weaker than the Evil Eye’s, the target will go unconscious, experience strong dizziness, or hallucinate. The specific result will depend on how strong the target’s spiritual power is.”

Xiong Ju reluctantly nodded when he heard that.

Although he loathed the eyeball, he knew its value.

The demon hybrid ringleader was a little uneasy and took him to take care, “Take note of this, don’t use it too many times during battle. If it shoots more than three rays in a short amount of time, the Evil Eye will be unable to sustain itself and it might fall apart. And if the Evil Eye falls apart, as the host, your spirit will be greatly shaken, and your body will also be severely injured. Remember that at all costs, at all costs!”

The human, dwarf, and demon hybrid ringleaders had all handed over their trump cards, so Xiong Ju couldn’t help but look at Ban Lan Gen and Spring Boxer.

After a moment of self-deliberation, Ban Lan Gen took out a syringe as thick as a baby’s arm.

“After some consideration, this is the thing that most suits you. You all should be familiar with this treasure, it is the Battle Qi Syringe.”

“As a fighter, we can store our battle qi into it. During a fierce battle, when our battle qi is nearly spent, injecting this into yourself can greatly replenish your battle qi in a short amount of time.”

“The amount it can store is about equal to the total sum of my battle qi reserves. Xiong Ju, if you use it, I estimate it can restore 150% of your total battle qi.”

“How wonderful!” Xiong Ju exclaimed in admiration, taking the Battle Qi Syringe.

Don’t be fooled by the fact that the Battle Qi Syringe was just silver level, it was very practical and had a great impact during combat.

Xiong Ju finally looked at Spring Boxer.

Spring Boxer shrugged his shoulders, “You know me, I haven’t been here, nor have I been a ringleader for that long. Normally, my savings are all used for cultivation. The strongest things I have are my spring components, how can I give them to you?”

Xiong Ju coldly snorted with dissatisfaction, “You don’t need to give me those springs.”

Spring Boxer was also resentful that these people were colluding with each other, but he didn’t say anything.

For one, he was a Bayonet Gang ringleader, and was part of them, he would never side with an outsider.

Secondly, they weren’t going against dueling regulations.

Seeing Spring Boxer not offering something, Teng Dong Lang decided to broker a compromise.

He took out a transparent medicine bottle, and inside, one could see there were three pills inside it.

These pills were the size of an adult human’s thumb, and the surface gleamed with a slick black.

“These are cultivation pills made by the Empire’s eastern monks, after taking one, a fighter’s battle qi will gain Penetration Force.”

Teng Dong Lang serenely introduced it.

This resulted in every ringleader gasping.

“Oh, it’s actually Penetration Force!”

“This pill is very rare. I’ve only heard of it and never seen it with my own eyes.”

“The eastern monks refined this pill to cultivate Penetration Force. But making this pill is very costly, and apart from needing a brilliant pharmacist, one also needs to have a saint level who cultivated Penetration Force participating in its refinement the entire time.”

“In the abbey, this kind of pill never meets demand. Don’t be fooled by its silver level, it does not have a market price and often reaches a gold level price during auctions!”

“Boss Gang Leader, how did you get hold of these pills?”

The ringleaders were all excited.

Because they were all fighters.

Magic usage had techniques. Such as quicken casting, consecutive casting, and heightened casting.

Likewise, battle qi usage also had techniques.

Among them was Piercing Force, Slashing Force, and Penetration Force, the three great classical battle qi attack techniques.

A master of Penetration Force could give defense penetration to their battle skills.

If the dragonman youngster grasped Penetration Force, then each of his Demolition Fists would be imbued with penetration. Regardless of whether the explosion or Penetration Force came first, grasping it would double his attack’s might.

It could be said that mastering any kind of force would cause a fighter’s strength to rise dramatically.

But mastering this kind of battle qi technique was always a challenging affair.

Cultivating it not only needs a vast volume of laborious efforts, but also comprehension.

Of the Sheng Ming Empire fighters, the Eastern Monastery’s monks were the best at cultivating this battle qi technique.

They were not only good at cultivating it but also compiling information on it and developing many helpful educational materials that allowed others to more easily master this force.

Even so, there were few monks that could learn Penetration Force.

Of the three classical battle qi attack techniques, Penetration Force was rarer than the Piercing and Slashing Force, harder to master, and also more applicable.

Piercing and Slashing Force could be used with battle skills, in place of a weapon, resulting in piercing and slashing damage.

But Penetration Force was very difficult to supplant (of course, there were rare battle skills that had penetration).

Its specific effect was that an offensive battle skill could penetrate outer defenses and go directly into the enemy’s body.

It was undoubtably the bane of armor!

Therefore, the ringleaders were very excited to learn about this.

Of the six ringleaders present, none of them knew how to use any force.

Then Teng Dong Lang suddenly took out a pill bottle. Those pills were able to help the user temporarily gain force in their attacks.

Increasing their military strength for a short time was the most unremarkable effect of the pills.

Their greatest impact was helping a fighter understand how Penetration Force felt earlier.

This was a great aid to a fighter mastering this force.

In fact, the eastern monks had developed this pill as a precious teaching aid, made to instruct the younger generation that was learning about Penetration Force!

Teng Dong Lang looked at Spring Boxer and sighed, “I haven’t mastered any force, but this pill was not mine to take. A few years ago, I requested this from the army for the younger generation, and after many difficulties, I was finally able to obtain three.”

“Now, I will use them to deal with Long Fu, I believe that is an appropriate use of them.”

“Don’t underestimate our adversary this time. After they had a clash with Bing Xiao, they struck first and gained the initiative, they are not simpletons!”

“Now that the entire gang has been challenged, we need to unite as one and dedicate ourselves sincerely.”

“Because we are one gang, one body, one people.”

“Do you believe that, Xiao Tan?”

Spring Boxer couldn’t help but be discolored, he felt both touched and ashamed. He lowered his head and admitted his mistake to Teng Dong Lang, “Lord Gang Leader, I will memorize your words. I too believe…that it is appropriate that these three pills be given to Xiong Ju.”

The other ringleaders were silent.

Teng Dong Lang and Spring Boxer’s relationship was known to them.

Teng Dong Lang had taken extremely good care of Spring Boxer, and in fact, Spring Boxer being able to become a gang ringleader was because Teng Dong Lang was backing him.

Teng Dong Lang’s three pills had clearly been for Spring Boxer.

However, Spring Boxer was too young, too high on himself, and made mistakes.

His biggest mistake was not being unable to defeat the dragonman youngster, nor was it thinking highly of him, but rather it was his terrible attitude that all of the gang members could recall from memory.

He actually opposed his own people and supported an external adversary!

Xiong Ju showed his discontent, and were the other four ringleaders not the same too?

Of course they were.

They were merely constrained by Teng Dong Lang and didn’t show their true feelings.

Luckily Teng Dong Lang was here.

He cleaned up Spring Boxer’s mess and publicly made up for his mistake.

The three treasured pills were given to Xiong Ju.

Xiong Ju’s dissatisfaction vanished.

Spring Boxer also got a lesson and understood his error.

The other ringleaders also increased their approval of Teng Dong Lang. Their gang leader’s handling of this gained their respect.

Spring Boxer’s “loss” also greatly offset their discontent.

Due to Teng Dong Lang’s actions, the hidden danger of internal strife was eliminated from the Bayonet Gang’s leadership

They were once again united, and most crucially, Teng Dong Lang was their core!

Teng Dong Lang finally looked at Xiong Ju, “Now, how sure are you of tomorrow’s fight?”

Xiong Ju, with equipment and medicine in hand, burst into laughter and clapped his chest in oath, “Long Fu’s defense is very formidable, but my hacksaw has never been bothered by such enemies. With the treasures I received, I don’t believe a silver level like him is stronger than me. I am certain of victory!”

Chapter 487: Absorbing Experience and a Dragon Corpse

The nightly threads enveloped Snow Bird Harbor’s docks, and the sea waves were as gentle as a mother’s hand, softly patting the hulls as if placating a baby to sleep.

In the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ flagship the Love Triangle, the dragonman youngster was training alone in a room and fiddling with his Grand Arena Medal.

Different from last time, the Grand Arena Medal emitted a small white light, giving him a feeling of fullness.

The dragonman youngster surmised, “This should be caused by the large amount of battle experience it absorbed during today’s fight.”

He gripped the Grand Arena Medal and used his spirit to draw from the large amount of battle experience stored in it.

He was familiar with this step.

He had used the Grand Arena Medal many times before.

What was different was that there was an unprecedented amount of experience being poured into him this time.

It took a full 5 minutes for him to absorb all of the experience.

Then after resting for 3 minutes, his mind slowly came to.

This battle experience was another’s and not the dragonman youngster’s.

Therefore, when he absorbed external experience, it would have an impact on himself.

Using the Grand Arena Medal needed both tension and relaxation, it couldn’t be misused.

This was not a shortcoming of the Grand Arena Medal, because almost everything in the world needed tension and relaxation. Overuse would cause harm; money was the same too.

The Grand Arena Medal’s absorption mechanism made the user incapable of activating it between the scant seconds between fights.

This was the reason the dragonman youngster was unable to improve himself with the Grand Arena Medal during his battle with Spring Boxer.

The dragonman youngster sat cross-legged on the floor, slowly closed his eyes, and carefully comprehended the experience he just absorbed.

There was very little experience from the fight with Ban Lan Gen, that fight went too quickly and he wasn’t able to absorb any defensive battle skills, as the dragonman youngster had knocked him out in a single punch.

Almost all of the battle experience came from Spring Boxer.

Roughly 60% of it was his dashing and movement experience.

The other part concerned battle skills like Spring Shot Fist, Spiral Fist Wind, Reckless Fist, and Reverse Spring Legs.

Finally, the final last bit was about how he used his alchemically modified spring body. This part was quite worthless to the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster recalled this experience, and it seemed like his body had been remodeled by alchemy and his elbows and knees had been replaced with alchemy springs.

This was an obvious conflict between his memories and self-perception.

Luckily, the impact this memory had on him was rather small, and it didn’t cause him to think he was another, blurring the lines between experience and reality.

“Let’s try sudden movement first.”

The dragonman youngster stood up and began to practice martial arts in the room, moving all over the place.

The martial arts training room was considered to be the most spacious room on the Love Triangle.

The Applaud Pirates’ commander Hu Li once used this room.

The training room had an alchemy puppet in it; it was tough and thick, able to absorb magic power from the reservoir to increase its defense. Not only that, but it could also repair itself and had been used as a boxing dummy by Hu Li.

The dragonman youngster didn’t use the alchemy puppet, instead he sprinted around the room.

Bang, thump, dink.

The dragonman youngster’s body was strong and heavy, and whenever his feet hit the floor, it would create a dull drum like noise.

But this sound didn’t ring outwards, because the practice room was soundproofed.

The simple dash movements felt different to the dragonman youngster.

He was constantly reflecting on the experience he had absorbed.

Spring Boxer’s experience was like an old yellow photograph gradually becoming colored, distinct, and vivid.

The dragonman youngster continuously adjusted and rapidly improved.

Spring Boxer and the dragonman youngster had both worked hard in this movement type, and the dragonman youngster absorbed that experience with his robust foundation very easily and at a high conversion rate.

After a dozen minutes, the dragonman youngster stopped moving.

He had made this part of the experience wholly his to use.

The improvement it gave him was also clear to the naked eye.

If he was merely familiar with it before, now he was perfect through practice.

Before, he didn’t have any energy to do anything else while dashing. Now, it was familiar and easy, and he could stop calmly and more easily shift his direction.

The dragonman youngster began to use the Surge battle skill.

Bang, thump, boom!

This time, the dragonman youngster made ten times the noise as before.

But the practice room was of rather high quality and was made entirely of silver level materials. It could not only absorb sound, but also intense vibrations too.

While using Surge, the dragonman youngster kept absorbing battle experience and adjusting himself.

His progress was visible to a layman’s eyes.

If anyone had been witnessing this, they would clearly notice the improvements in the dragonman youngster’s dashing movements.

Before, the explosions he wielded launched the dragonman youngster’s body like an artillery shell.

But now, his movements and jumps had become more graceful and lithe.

Although his speed hadn’t increased, his flexibility and control had vastly improved.

The dragonman youngster was gradually engrossed in his practice.

After a half hour, he sensed that his progress was no longer improving at an obvious rate, so he regrettably stopped.

He had completely absorbed that portion of experience.

The dragonman youngster sank into reflection.

“Next, I need to practice Surge more and learn how to control its explosive might more.”

“Before, growth was average and in agreement with each other, now that my posture has improved by a large amount, this part has become a shortcoming.”

“So long as this portion is improved, my dashing and turning movements will be substantially improved.”

“Is there anyone who cultivates the Surge battle skill?”

It was easy to go from frugality to extravagance, but not the other way around. The dragonman youngster had gained a large amount of experience from the Grand Arena Medal in such a convenient and fast way, and the first thing he thought of was that he really wanted to find a suitable opponent to fight so he could gain their experience.

“Next up, Spring Boxer’s battle skills.”

The dragonman youngster began to try to practice Spring Boxer’s four battle skills.

These battle skills were widely circulated and had a detailed description in War Merchant’s database.

Spring Boxer came from the military and didn’t have familial relationships.

These battle skills came from transcendents of the Rubber bloodline.

As they cultivated, they created battle skills like the Spring Shot Fist, Reckless Fist, Reverse Spring Legs, and other battle skills.

Spring Boxer just chose these battle skills because he was compatible with them.

Although this was the first time the dragonman youngster had cultivated these battle skills, he quickly had results.

The difficulty of mastery was very low, and from beginning to end, the dragonman youngster never failed. It was as if he had practiced this before, and merely took a break for a while, then began to practice them again.

The most difficult part was the start, and after constant practice, his body remembered it.

The dragonman youngster didn’t practice them deeply, only a superficial session before stopping.

The dragonman youngster’s body structure was different from Spring Boxer’s and he didn’t have the Rubber bloodline either.

“This most suitable battle skill for my dragonman form is Reckless Fist.”

“Even so, this battle skill is more suitable for Spring Boxer and has a low compatibility with me.”

The dragonman youngster planned to practice another battle skill – Machine Gun Fist!

The actual combat effects of both were very similar, raising the frequency of punches to increase attack power.

The internal force application and mechanisms of both battle skills were also alike.

This was because Machine Gun Fist was the improved version of what Spring Boxer was using.

A transcendent that was fond of red waistcoats who had the Rubber bloodline had improved the basic skill Reckless Fist into Machine Gun Fist.

His inspiration had been machine guns.

This was a newly innovated type of weapon that had just popped up in the arms market in recent years.

Compared to Reckless Fist, Machine Gun Fist was better suited to the dragonman youngster, for he was in a contrary situation to Spring Boxer.

The dragonman youngster began to evaluate the key points to practice for Machine Gun Fist.

He quickly mastered it, and after using it several times in a row, he became very proficient at it, as if he had trained it for 3-5 years.

As of now, he had processed most of the battle experience.

The other parts were rather worthless to him, and he wisely chose to discard them.

“In the future, if there is a chance, I want to spar with Spring Boxer again.”

The Grand Arena Medal had improved the dragonman youngster by so much. Thus, the dragonman youngster had saved a considerable amount of time and energy.

Upon finishing his cultivation, the dragonman youngster opened the practice room’s door and secretly traveled to another cabin.

Zi Di was waiting for him there.

Today, the dragonman youngster spent the daylight hours fighting.

Zi Di meanwhile, had been visiting Snow Bird Harbor’s great markets to purchase a large amount of alchemy materials.

The Battle of Two Eyes Island had given the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps a lot of gold. Snow Bird Harbor was one of the great harbors of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, and after some market research yesterday, Zi Di found that there were full stocks of materials here, both quality and quantity far surpassed Two Eyes Island.

“Lord Commander, today I purchased three dragon corpses.”

“They are all offshoots of the Flame Dragon King Bloodline based on the bloodline archive map.”

“This is a red tamed dragon, this is a hammer dragon, and this is a fat ocean dragon flank.”

The red tamed dragon tail was slender, it was the smallest of the three, and it was only slightly bigger than a normal sized cart.

The hammer dragon was short and stout, its dragon skin was yellow-green, and its most distinct trait was its ball shaped protrusion on its tail section; it looked like a circular hammer head.

The fat ocean dragon was the biggest of the three, despite just being a flank, it was far bigger than the other two corpses combined.

From the flank, one could see the fat ocean dragon’s deep blue dragon skin. It didn’t have scales, instead it had very smooth skin.

The dragon skin was 0.7 meters thick.

Under the dragon skin was ample layers upon layers of fat that bundled up viscera and seeped grease.

It is said that some giant tribes and titans domesticated the red tamed dragons for the purpose of becoming their hunting dogs.

The hammer dragon was a grass eating sub-dragon species, it had a lukewarm temperament, and only when its life was in danger would it lash out its dragon tail and erupt with strong and stalwart attacks.

The fat ocean dragon reproduced and dwelled in the deep sea, only a seldom few ventured into the shallows, thus, catching them was very hard, but people still kept on trying to devise all sorts of ways to fish them up.

Because they had massive economic value.

The fat ocean dragon’s fat could be cooked and refined into a unique kerosene, a widely used energy source in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom that was even more important to the kingdom.

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom had been always looking for how to breed fat ocean dragons on a massive scale, offering a large bounty to every great power for the last five hundred years.

The dragonman youngster absorbed each of the three dragon corpses.

The red tamed dragon gave him a lower leg and foot transformation.

The dragonman youngster turned his lower legs and feet into that of a red tamed dragon.

Compared to his previous thick and solid feet, the red tamed dragon feet were thinner. The toes spread open like petals and each of the toes had a bent hooked talon on them.

The dragonman youngster tried them out and found there was nothing to write home about them.

The dragonman youngster liked his prior form more, it could fully display his strength.

The bones of the red tamed dragon’s lower legs and feet were also slimmer, and were incompatible with the dragonman youngster’s upper body, and it felt really weird to him.

To speak plainly, the red tamed dragon toes’ hooked talons actually made it easy to climb and tear apart things.

An adult red tamed dragon could use their bent toes to grab hold of rocky surfaces and climb freely up precipices.

But this point of usefulness was not something the dragonman youngster needed currently.

The hammer dragon corpse gave the dragonman youngster a dragon tail transformation. He could now turn the tip of his tail into a hammer shape.

The dragonman youngster tossed his tail and noticed that this transformation had quite the growth potential, but it needed practice and a lot of time for diligent training.

Before that happened, recklessly transforming would instead hinder himself.

The fat ocean dragon corpse just gave him fat; it was not worth talking about something so negligible.

The dragonman youngster inherently didn’t have much hope for this and wasn’t disappointed at all.

To him, the true worth of these three dragon corpses was that it could raise the percentage of a complete Flame Dragon King bloodline.

After absorbing the three dragon corpses, the dragonman youngster’s Flame Dragon King bloodline concentration was elevated to 34%.

“Today I also purchased a lot of excellent alchemical and medicinal ingredients. Tonight, I will refine some good potions that should help you in your Lordship’s fight tomorrow.”

The dragonman youngster nodded, “Although I had two victories today, the Bayonet Gang will absolutely have a response to that, tomorrow will be a challenge!”

Zi Di added on, “In addition, we have more information pertaining to Teng Dong Lang.”

“His bloodline is the Holly bloodline, he simultaneously possesses an ice and a wood attribute, though his battle skills trend more towards the ice attribute.”

“I suggest that before you challenge him, Lord Commander, absorb some white dragon bloodlines.”

The ice Sculpture had some white dragon communities in its territory, along with a large amount of white dragon subspecies.

The most populous of the white dragon subspecies was the hoar frost dragon.

If the dragonman youngster absorbed white dragon bloodlines, it could greatly improve his ice resistance, very helpful for dealing with Teng Dong Lang.

“Finally, I also inquired about some talents in Snow Bird Harbor.” Zi Di handed the dragonman youngster a list of several names composed mainly of alchemists and one outstanding silver level thief.

The dragonman youngster carefully looked at each name and their brief introduction, “You can contact them and investigate them further.”

Zi Di reminded him, “Lord Commander, if you display your strength and grace as much as possible in the coming fight, the difficulty in recruiting these talents will lower accordingly.”

The dragonman youngster smiled, “I will do my best!”

Chapter 488: The Wind Rises in Snow Bird Harbor

Early morning.

The morning sun had just risen amidst the residual coldness of night.

The ice-cold daybreak wind brushed across the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ flag, fluttering it.

A ship plank clacked down, and the dragonman youngster disembarked his ship.

Unlike yesterday, the dragonman youngster did not have just Lan Zao following behind, but also Chi Lai and Men Shi, two silver levels.

The three people sensed many eyes just as they got off the ship.

Following one’s perception of these eyes, one would see some were brazenly staring, while others were hiding in some unremarkable corners.

Chi Lai slightly detected the aura of these people; there were at least 5 silver levels.

Things had shifted greatly.

This was because the dragonman youngster challenged two of the Bayonet Gang’s ringleaders yesterday.

During those battles, he had shown his unique graceful bearing.

Yesterday, the dragonman youngster had just begun his public challenge, this sudden matter had been followed by many ordinary onlookers and not many transcendents.

Then after a day and night of simmering, especially last night, Snow Bird Harbor’s taverns large and small had circulated the dragonman youngster’s story.

It could be said that the conflict between the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and the Bayonet Gang had already become a gale that had spread through the entirety of Snow Bird Harbor.

Snow Bird Harbor’s mainstay powerhouses were 4 famous gold levels. The city Lord, the Seabird God bishop, the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance branch head, and the Bayonet Gang’s leader.

The dragonman youngster challenging the Bayonet Gang’s leader, Teng Dong Lang to a heroic duel was undoubtably destabilizing Teng Dong Lang’s authority. If the latter didn’t deal with this matter well, the dragonman youngster would affect the equilibrium standoff between the upper levels of Snow Bird Harbor.

It wasn’t that no one challenged Teng Dong Lang before.

But the dragonman youngster was a rare powerful challenger, someone other people considered capable of rattling Teng Dong Lang’s position.

Seeing the dragonman youngster slowly leaving the dock, four silver levels gathered on an open balcony and talked with each other.

They were all highly respected people in Snow Bird Harbor, like a mercenary commander, a large shop owner, and a fishing ship captain.

A bearded dwarf watched the dragonman youngster with envy, “He has a dragonman bloodline, his body is far stronger than that of humans, snow elves, and dwarfs. At the silver level, he is capable of challenging a gold level!”

The dragon race was a higher race; this was reflected in the creation myth and every race related to the dragons was mostly not found lacking.

An old human man, with forlorn features, saw himself in the dragonman youngster, smoky clouds flashing in his memories, “It isn’t easy to make a living in Snow Bird Harbor.”

“This place already has a stable authority and social classes; there isn’t any space for foreigners.”

“Thinking back to those years, when I led my mercenary corps here, I had to pay visits to everyone, cut relationships, and build a network. In the end, I still had to spar with my rivals to be acknowledged.”

“Now the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is doing what I did back then. Only by proving their power will the surrounding people acknowledge them. Only by proving oneself a ferocious beast will the other beasts concede a hunting ground for you.”

The longbeard dwarf shook his head, “The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is only a small power, and it wanting to use a heroic duel to become famous and prove their strength is a good move. But they didn’t poke the right opponent, why did they go against the Bayonet Gang? In fact, as long as they challenge another small power, that will be able to obtain the same approval and have the qualifications to live in Snow Bird Harbor.”

Someone laughed at those words.

It was a silver level female snow elf.

The female snow elf wore black frame spectacles. Her eyes narrowed, “It seems that you haven’t gathered enough information, and you don’t know how things stand with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. They are not as simple as they appear to be, I have gathered many rumors and learned they are actually a front for a gang of arms smugglers.”

“It is merely happenstance that they established the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, to make selling weapons easier. They being a small power is true, but we cannot look at just that because of their patron behind them.”

The longbeard dwarf became slightly discolored, he knew a patron capable of selling weapons was clearly remarkable.

He couldn’t help but suddenly realize, “It’s no wonder Long Fu seems so rash, it seems he’s very confident.”

The snow elf with the black glasses continued to analyze, “Not only that, I believe that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps has an even deeper interest they are demanding for with this move.”

The old human paused to comprehend the truth, “Are you saying that they want to sell their weapons in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom?”

The snow elf nodded, “That should be it, otherwise why would they challenge the Bayonet Gang? Also, only by fighting such an opponent will they be able to win enough space and tolerance, allowing them to establish their arms business smoothly afterwards.”

“So that’s how it is.” The longbeard dwarf muttered.

“Everything is about business!” The old human sighed ruefully.

The longbeard dwarf clapped his hands together, “Now that I recall, the conflict between the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and Bing Xiao is actually rather complex!”

“Who normally want a giant-type intelligence potion? Also, only Bing Xiao trains giant slaves, and has a strong demand for gold level potions of that type, such a demand is very easy to learn about.”

“In that case, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps must have deliberately manufactured this conflict into being.”

The other silver level snow elf felt perplexed, “If it was purposely manufactured, how could they be certain of their success? Was Bing Xiao guaranteed to find trouble with them? If Bing Xiao exercised a step of forbearance, wouldn’t their plan come to nothing?”

The glasses wearing snow elf smiled, “If that plan failed, then they could implement another one. The Bayonet Gang has dominated the dark side of Snow Bird Harbor for many years; it is actually quite easy to provoke such a colossus.”

“I believe that if Bing Xiao is provoked, he will inevitably issue a response. Because as the Bayonet Gang’s former leader, he is in an awkward spot.”

“After being the Bayonet Gang’s leader for many years, he has definitely offended many people. Therefore, after he retired from leadership, he still retained a portion of devoted underlings and also his gold level giant slave, and still to this day continues to train new giant slaves.”

“For one, he has to pay attention to his own strength and restrain his growth. If his strength expands to surpass some line, it will break his mutual understanding with the current gang leader, and the first person to deal with him would be said gang leader, Teng Dong Lang.”

“After Bing Xiao was provoked, he didn’t act himself, rather he asked the Bayonet Gang Leader Teng Dong Lang for help. This was precisely caused by his awkward situation, because he both needed to display his strength while also being worried that his strength would arouse Teng Dong Lang’s disdain and fear. Therefore, he directly asked Teng Dong Lang for help, to both reassure himself and also show outwardly that he and the Bayonet Gang had an inseparably close connection.”

“Teng Dong Lang also clearly knows why Bing Xiao is asking for help and is very willing to preserve his common understanding with Bing Xiao.”

“Therefore, the current situation exists, where a conflict undoubtably happened between Bing Xiao and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. But the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps tried to find trouble with the Bayonet Gang directly. And so, the Bayonet Gang is also willing to take on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.”

When the glasses wearing snow elf’s analysis reached this point, the other people sank into silence.

In their silence, they sensed the ripples of the treacherous games of power underpinning this battle.

After a good while, the old man broke the silence first, “Actually, I will be happier if the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps won.”

“You know my mercenary corps has clashed many times with the Bayonet Gang.”

“This gang is too big, and takes up too much space, I don’t believe you don’t think the same.”

“Another important thing: I hope I can buy better weapons; the current arms market really isn’t worth looking at.”

Those words immediately solicited a strong resonance from the others.

The longbeard dwarf grumbled, “Yes, also I don’t know what happened to the arms black market, what happened to it? A good few big arms dealers suddenly went missing and there is no stock.”

“Arms smuggling is illegal, and every nation cracks down on it. Arms merchants that suddenly run out of stock means they have been eliminated. But for so many arms merchants to collectively run out of stock and vanish without a trace is quite rare.”

“Wasn’t there news that the Sheng Ming Empire was responsible for this crack down?”

“That has been stated, but there is no evidence.”

“Who needs proof? Doing this is to the Sheng Ming Empire’s advantage. They are invading the Beastman Continent, and their weapons and equipment outclass the beastmen’s, they really hold a massive advantage there. Of course, they are very worried that arms smugglers and merchants will sell large quantities of excellent weapons to the beastmen, therefore, they mopped them up in advance.”

“The Sheng Ming Empire is too overbearing, they can invade wherever and whenever they want! This time they are encroaching on the Beastman Continent, and the official justification for it makes me sick. This human empire also wants to turn our Ice Sculpture Kingdom into a vassal state, such rapacious designs. I really hope they faceplant in the Beastman Continent. Historically, those who launch an ocean spanning expedition usually aren’t victorious.”

The silver levels discussed amongst themselves.

The survivors could actually answer their questions about the arms market.

The answer was that War Merchant was dead.

War Merchant held a comparatively large share of the arms market, and he played many roles in it.

These roles was War Merchant pretending to be different merchants to guard against attracting too much attention to himself.

With War Merchant’s death, both he and his various identities died together, and naturally could no longer do business.

It had already been two months since Mystifying Monster Island had been destroyed.

The arms market had been greatly affected. Especially powers in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, which was quite close to Mystifying Monster Island.

As the silver levels continued to converse, one of the bespectacled woman’s subordinates came over to the elf and whispered a report to them.

Then the subordinate quietly withdrew.

The glasses wearing woman got up, “The breakfast ends here.”

“I have received the latest news, the Bayonet Gang has dispatched Xiong Ju to meet Long Fu head on.”

When the other silver levels heard that, they showed different expressions.

The old human muttered to himself, “Of the Bayonet Gang’s six bosses, the strongest is Xiong Ju, that is publicly known.”

The longbeard dwarf laughed, “Yesterday I said that Xiong Ju getting involved was a certainty! Through the last two fights yesterday, Long Fu has already revealed his strength. If he was allowed to run roughshod over the six ringleaders, then the Bayonet Gang’s name would suffer immensely. Then even if Teng Dong Lang beat Long Fu, the people would still believe that only the Bayonet Gang’s leader was the only one who could fight. Based on Teng Dong Lang’s disposition, he will surely regain the initiative.”

The other snow elf looked interested, “Xiong Ju was chosen by them to stop Long Fu, he must have come prepared. Today’s fight is certain to be a spectacle.”

“Xiong Ju is also considered to be one of the strongest silver levels in Snow Bird Harbor.”

“Long Fu…he hasn’t shown his true strength yet. Despite his outstanding defense, don’t forget, Xiong Ju’s weapon is the gold level Gold Flower Hacksaw!”

“All I know is that today, we can see a good show. Let’s go together.” The black glasses wearing woman left first, “The Bayonet Gang has already made arrangements, Xiong Ju is currently at Snow Bird Dueling Arena. Long Fu and his people are going there right now.”

Chapter 489: Long Fu vs Xiong Ju

Not long after the dragonman youngster left the dock, he found the Bayonet Gang ringleader, Ban Lan Gen, before him.

The latter had come over to pass on a message to the dragonman youngster: Xiong Ju was waiting for him at the dueling arena.

The dueling arena was a common structure in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom was fond of martial tradition, no matter if it was the upper or lower class, they all habitually utilized the dueling arenas to solve daily disputes, great conflicts, and animosity as deep as an ocean of blood.

In Snow Bird Harbor alone, there were more than dozen dueling arenas of all sizes.

The Bayonet Gang chose a dueling arena called “Snow Bird Dueling Arena”, it was Snow Bird Harbor’s largest dueling arena.

Before the dragonman youngster even arrived, Snow Bird Dueling Arena was already packed with crowds.

All six of the Bayonet Gang’s ringleaders showed up, Spring Boxer, the dwarf ringleader, the human ringleader, and the rest had taken their seats already.

Snow Bird Harbor’s leaders or representatives of powers large and small also tried to get the best seats.

In addition to him, there were also many spectators and busybodies that walked in from the dueling arena’s doors.

The dueling arenas never charged for admission, and there were even magic images hanging outside the arena, constantly advertising the duel between Xiong Ju and Long Fu.

This attracted even more people to watch the duel.

“This time, the Bayonet Gang used the Snow Bird Dueling Arena, what a grand showing!”

“Last night, when I was drinking, I said that the Bayonet Gang won’t sit and wait for death.”

“The betting pool outside has already been set up; I bet that Commander Long Fu would win.”

“I put down 10 gold coins on Xiong Ju.”

The people conversed with each other in the lively atmosphere.

Duels were something the citizens of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom were familiar with, and they were fond of participating in the sport.

It was precisely with this base of people that heroic duels were always the optimal shortcut to expand one’s influence and notoriety.

Snow Bird Dueling Arena was shaped like an oval, it had more than a dozen stairways, and in the middle was a vast stretch of sand.

All kinds of peddlers were shuttling up and down the stairs, selling their food and beverages.

“Chicken kebobs, 1 copper coin for 5 skewers, snowflake candy, 1 copper coin for one.”

“Give me ten rock candies.”

“Want something to drink? This is orange juice from the Iron Magic Kitchen, 1 medium cup for only 1 copper coin, a large cup for only 2 copper coins, and an extra-large cup for 2.5 copper coins.”

“Give me a small cup of orange juice.”

“Sorry sir, we only have medium, large, and extra-large cups.”

“I said I want the smallest cup.”

“Sorry sir, this is the medium sized cup.”

“I don’t care how you classify it; I want this small cup, do you understand?”

“Sorry sir, this is the medium cup!”

Clack, clack, clack!

The customer was provoked to anger, causing them to suddenly stand up and shake their fist.

The small peddler selling drinks was slapped a few times, reacting to the attack with a great shriek and began to hit the customer back.

The two people’s scuffle drew the applause and cheers of others around them.

“Right, hit his face!”

“Kick his butt!”

“Oh oh oh! Bite him, bite his bottom!!”

No one stopped them.

The vast majority of the people here treated this spat as the appetizer to the banquet ahead.

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s martial spirit could be seen from this incident.

Suddenly, someone stood up and cheered, this attracted many eyes, and soon after more of them began to chant and yell in excitement.

A thousand pairs of eyes were concentrated on the dueling arena’s entrance.

There, the dragonman youngster, Lan Zao, Chi Lai, and Men Shi were ambling in.

Seeing the protagonist of the duel finally arrive, the customer and peddler immediately stopped fighting each other.

The small peddler’s beaten face swelled like a pig head; the customer had been obviously winning.

The small peddler covered his face, drawing in a cold breath, “You win, you’re the one in charge, so did you still want a drink?”

The customer nodded, taking out a single copper coin, “Give me a small cup of orange juice.”

The small peddler took out some orange juice and took the coin, then continued to sell his merchandise.

The dueling arena’s workers welcomed the dragonman youngster’s group.

Soon after, Lan Zao, Men Shi, and Chi Lai were seated in the area for friends and relatives.

The dragonman youngster walked alone into the center, where Xiong Ju was waiting.

The two approached each other.

The distance was 5 meters apart when the dragonman youngster stopped.

Neither spoke first.

Each of their pupils reflected the other.

The dragonman youngster sized up Xiong Ju.

He was a bear-man, a bear head on a human body, and a full mouth of teeth.

Long black hair filled his bear face, a face with a gigantic cross shaped blade scar.

Xiong Ju’s body was especially tall and sturdy, somewhat taller than the dragonman youngster.

This was not to say a bear-man was taller than a dragonman, rather it was due to the fact that the dragonman youngster was far younger than Xiong Ju.

The dragonman youngster was not fully grown, while Xiong Ju was a complete adult.

Contrasted with Xiong Ju’s gigantic body, the dragonman youngster looked thin for the first time.

The dragonman youngster observed Xiong Ju, his gaze lingering on the latter’s weapons and equipment.

Xiong Ju was wearing an epaulet on his arm.

His belt had a one handed iron hammer in it.

On his back he was carrying an enormous hacksaw, and if the sharp saw teeth lining the hacksaw were removed, it would look almost identical to a great sword.

Xiong Ju wore leather armor.

With his square body and tremendous strength, putting on mail plating was perfectly doable.

But Xiong Ju was a member of the Bayonet Gang, he lived on the streets and alleys daily.

Wearing plate mail all day would waste too much stamina, and in narrow spaces, it would be very inconvenient to move in.

Leather armor was good enough.

It balanced defense and agility.

Generally speaking, gang members all wore leather armor.

Compared to the fully equipped Xiong Ju, the dragonman youngster seemed destitute by comparison.

But his red dragon scales were very striking, and they served to highlight his strong and tenacious temperament, something the people didn’t dare to look down on.

The dragonman youngster did have some equipment.

On his lower back, he had two alchemy crossbows.

On his upper back he had a giant quiver filled with alchemy arrows.

The two alchemy crossbows were silver level and originated from War Merchant’s arsenal.

Of course, he also had the Grand Arena Medal and the Gold Silk Chainmail, but those had been hidden by Deceptive Disguise.

As the dragonman youngster sized up Xiong Ju, so did Xiong Ju likewise.

The normally forthright Xiong Ju currently looked solemn and serious.

Xiong Ju always treated the dragonman youngster cautiously.

Because he understood the heavy responsibility and burden he shouldered!

Although he was the third Bayonet Gang ringleader to be fought, as far as the Bayonet Gang was concerned, he would be the final one.

Because the other ringleaders, like the human, dwarf, and demon hybrid ringleaders, were weaker than Xiong Ju.

Xiong Ju had gotten everyone’s support, was armed to the teeth, and in peak condition. But if he lost this duel to the dragonman youngster, then the other three ringleaders would be unable to defeat this adversary.

They didn’t have the strength to do so.

“I have to win this battle, only by defeating you in front of everyone can I revitalize my Bayonet Gang’s martial glory!”

Chapter 490: Even a Gold Level Weapon Can’t Break His Defense?

Four silver levels were sitting in the VIP seats.

The seating room was cut off from the masses, forming a privacy that displayed its grand tranquility.

The walls around them used alchemy technology, and when a user used spiritual control, they could be made completely transparent or turn into a unidirectional lens.

Its main function was to turn into a magic image of the dueling grounds that was broadcast to the spectators inside the room.

The magic image could be big or small, far or close, whatever suited the spectators.

Outdoor seating didn’t need to buy tickets, but the VIP seating did.

The black glasses wearing elf didn’t lack wealth.

She owned three alchemy workshops and was the best alchemy master in Snow Bird Harbor.

When she came over from the dock, thanks to her status, she easily rented the VIP spectator room.

At this time, the biggest wall was transmitting a magic image.

The image had the dragonman youngster and Xiong Ju in it, as if they were right next to them.

The longbeard dwarf surveyed Xiong Ju, and couldn’t help but purse his brows, “I have never seen Xiong Ju so well equipped.”

The old human was more interested in sizing up the dragonman youngster, “Apart from Commander Long Fu’s two alchemy crossbows, he has nothing. This is too arrogant, he is not a martial monk, but an arms smuggler. How could he possibly lack weapons?”

The snow elf put her finger on her black glasses, guessing, “Perhaps Long Fu has a storage device on him, we’ll see what happens. Regardless of what he’s hiding, this duel will force out his trump cards.”

“Similarly, we’ll be able to observe whether Xiong Ju’s fighting strength has risen or fallen these last few years.”

The other snow elf approved of her points.

The dragonman youngster was a newcomer and had already shown off his powerful and graceful bearing, he will be a powerful competitor in Snow Bird Harbor soon.

While the Bayonet Gang’s Xiong Ju was a well known silver level fighter.

Every single person living in Snow Bird Harbor would unavoidably have a connection with the Bayonet Gang.

Therefore, no matter which of the two it was, both were worth watching carefully.

Xiong Ju ran his eyes over the audience, then fixed his eyes on the dragonman youngster’s face, cackling, “Long Fu, you challenge our gang leader, you’ve got guts, but you’re also really stupid! In today’s battle, I will give you a good lesson and let you all know that I will end your challenge here.”

He had a very arrogant and overbearing attitude.

The dragonman youngster brightly smiled and his eyes were overflowing with battle intent, eager to give it a try, “It seems today you will be fighting with all of your strength, I like that zeal, so no matter who wins or loses, it is good to maintain that enthusiasm.”

He was neither servile nor overbearing, which immediately caused Xiong Ju to boil with brutalness.

“You can speak well; I’m going to tear out your mouth first!”

Xiong Ju attacked first right then, rushing towards the dragonman youngster.

Both had been quite close to each other, and within a few steps, Xiong Ju came to the dragonman youngster.

He swung his fist at the dragonman youngster’s fist.

The dragonman youngster leaned to the side and raised his left arm to block Xiong Ju’s fist, while swinging his right fist at Xiong Ju beneath his rib.

Xiong Ju took a step back and threw a heavy punch with his other hand.

Ding!

With a dull echo, each other’s fists collided head on precisely in a firm blow.

Both fell back a step, each sharing the limelight.

Neither took a breath before they both attacked each other again.

At first there were dodges and blocks, but gradually, their punches became faster and their movements more violent.

Then, they stopped bothering to dodge and duck, now only focused on attacking and using their bodies to resist the punches and kicks of the other.

For a moment, fists were flying and the air screamed from punches.

When the audience in the dueling arena saw the flurry of fists and the contest of toughness, they couldn’t help but be excited.

The dueling arena began to fill with cheers and hoots.

After a short period of violence, Xiong Ju suddenly raised his leg and kicked a piece of floor tile at the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster’s field of view was covered by the floor tile, and knowing that was a bad sign, took a large step back.

As expected, a moment later Xiong Ju used a dirty trick, he deliberately lowered his body and aimed his right hand at the dragonman youngster’s lower extremities for a ruthless chop.

The dragonman youngster had retreated in a timely manner and didn’t fall for it.

When Xiong Ju saw his storm of blows couldn’t suppress the dragonman youngster, he started to use many despicable moves commonly used by street gangsters.

These battle arts were engraved into him at the deepest level.

As he grew, he fought in close quarters amongst alleyways a great number of times.

This was his inherent nature!

The dragonman youngster at first couldn’t adapt and seemed somewhat on the off foot.

But the more times Xiong Ju used his treacherous moves, the dragonman youngster gained experience and gradually adapted to them.

After another short period of fighting, Xiong Ju gasped for air, his heart filled with increasing irritation.

“Long Fu is indeed a thorn.” Xiong Ju had rarely encountered such an opponent.

In the past, few people wouldn’t drop like the wind from his violent storm of punches.

Generally speaking, Xiong Ju would always have the upper hand, forcing the other side to prioritize using battle skills in an attempt to recover the situation.

But this time, it was he who was using battle skills first.

Fierce Wind Punch, Accelerate Fists.

Xiong Ju’s fists glistened with a white silver luster, and his threat level rose dramatically.

With battle skills now in play, the probing was formally declared finished, while simultaneously implied that Xiong Ju was on the downturn during the probing phase.

The dragonman youngster had been prepared for this and ducked to avoid Xiong Ju’s battle skill.

Soon after, he too used his most proficient attack skill.

Battle skill – Demolition Fist.

Xiong Ju was struck by Demolition Fist, causing his body to soar into the ark and fly backwards.

The dragonman youngster pressed the attack, but Xiong Ju rapidly adjusted his posture in the air and smashed the dragonman youngster back with both his fists.

Battle skill – Hammer Punch!

The dragonman youngster raised his left palm, and an explosion detonating in it launched his body to the right.

Bang.

Xiong Ju’s fist pounded the floor tiles, and because of the battle skill increasing his attack power, it smashed a shallow hole into the ground.

He had just landed on the floor when the dragonman youngster charged at him again.

The dragonman youngster’s thin red figure was somewhat visible as he tangled constantly with Xiong Ju’s massive black body.

The two fought in the dueling arena, charging, retreating, and fighting with increasingly intense vigor.

The transcendents around the dueling grounds could already make out clues.

“Although Xiong Ju is powerful, Commander Long Fu is still somewhat on top, surprisingly.

“Comparing them, Long Fu is more flexible and is able to avoid most of Xiong Ju’s attacks. While Xiong Ju is bigger and heavier, and when faced with that young dragonman’s attack, is often forced into passive defense and has to eat many losses.”

“The main cause of this situation is Long Fu’s battle experience. Although he is young, his combat experience is nevertheless exceptionally rich, therefore, Xiong Ju’s many dirty tricks don’t work on him.”

Battle skill – Barbarous Collision.

Battle skill – Bear Pounce!

Xiong Ju opened his legs and charged forward.

He opened his arms wide, exposing himself, and with incomparable bravery, charged into the dragonman youngster.

While using those two battle skills, his legs and hands both glowed with silver radiance.

The dragonman youngster at first constantly drew back, then suddenly, he sprung from the floor and counter charged Xiong Ju.

Due to the shock, Xiong Ju unconsciously brought his arms to his chest.

But a moment later, the dragonman youngster used Surge and quickly broke out and cleverly neutralized Xiong Ju’s Bear Pounce.

Xiong Ju grabbed nothing.

The audience’s applause disseminated everywhere.

Feeling he was being played with, Xiong Ju angrily roared, and his eyes reddened, hating the dragonman youngster ever more.

In a battle like this, the slightest difference led to grave errors.

No matter how imposing and astonishing Xiong Ju was, if he didn’t hit his opponent, it would be all in vain.

Xiong Ju had expended a large amount of stamina and battle qi yet was unable to exchange it for a good battle result.

He grew more irascible, reaching out now to pull out his main weapon.

The gold level weapon – the Gold Flower Hacksaw!

Generally speaking, silver level fighters rarely had gold level equipment.

Because it was an extravagant act. The money used to purchase gold level equipment could be used to supplement many aspects, make sure a silver level didn’t have any shortcomings, and increase their overall strength.

If one just used their money to buy a gold level weapon, they were liable to have a great offense and a weak defense, an awkward situation an opponent could easily target, as such, the gains didn’t make up for the losses.

Xiong Ju, as the top boss of the Bayonet Gang’s six ringleaders, had a rather high status, and this genuine power also produced great profits capable of making up for the fundamental prerequisites and to pay the expensive price to buy a powerful weapon that surpassed his level.

The Gold Flower Hacksaw was as long as a broadsword.

With the hacksaw in hand, Xiong Ju’s attack range immediately doubled.

The dragonman youngster was within his attack range, dodging left and right as nimbly as a fish.

Xiong Ju’s silver battle qi poured into the Gold Flower Hacksaw, causing the hacksaw’s teeth to swiftly turn and the hacksaw’s embedded gemstone to emit an earthen yellow light.

The yellow light caused the dragonman youngster’s speed to slow by 50%, and his body suddenly sank, as if a heavy object was suddenly weighing him down.

Due to this disturbance, the dragonman youngster was unable to evade the Gold Flower Hacksaw slash across his chest.

The sharp teeth instantly tore apart the Gold Silk Chainmail.

The Gold Silk Chainmail was likewise gold level.

The contest of a gold level weapon against a gold level armor created dazzling sparks.

The sparks condensed in the air, forming a golden flower.

The gold flower’s points were sharp, and it spiraled in the wind like darts, cutting through the air in various arcs towards the dragonman youngster’s eyes, ears, nose, and other vulnerable points.

The dragonman youngster wasn’t surprised at this, because the intelligence report on him contained a detailed account of Xiong Ju’s main weapon’s effects.

The golden flower was very frail.

The dragonman youngster used Demolition Fist to explode it to bits in two to three hits.

Without the gold flower, the dragonman youngster could use his arm or dragon scales to block the attack.

But this undoubtedly slowed down his battle tempo.

Xiong Ju seized the opportunity to launch a stream of attacks.

Unlike the prior contests, this time, his attacks slashed the dragonman youngster again and again.

Every blow energetically swung down, causing the Gold Flower Hacksaw to spark abundantly across the Gold Silk Chainmail.

The brilliant dazzling sparks quickly turned into another gold flower that attacked the dragonman youngster all over.

The dragonman youngster was in a disadvantageous position.

The Gold Silk Chainmail was being quickly scared and even had some gaps in it.

Prior to this, the Gold Silk Chainmail was damaged somewhat in his fight against Spring Boxer.

But that damage was far less serious than what was occurring now.

Xiong Ju had managed to damage the Gold Silk Chainmail far more in a few minutes than anything Spring Boxer was ever able to.

The gold level weapon was indeed formidable!

Even though the dragonman youngster was psychologically prepared, faced with this kind of situation, he was very disturbed by it.

Xiong Ju wasn’t happy, rather he was full of shock!

“Long Fu, this guy, why is his defense so formidable?!”

He had never experienced such an opponent.

So many times, his Gold Flower Hacksaw had slashed Long Fu.

Yet none of his attacks pierced a single dragon scale!

If it was a saint level or a legend level, Xiong Ju would have conceded it, but the crucial thing was that the dragonman youngster was just silver level.
