Xiong Ju was beginning to have doubts that his own Gold Flower Hacksaw was a fake.

“What is his bloodline actually, to have such hard dragon scales?”

“I thought that Long Fu’s defense was exaggerated enough, I didn’t expect it to exceed the limits of my imagination.”

“Don’t tell me a gold level weapon can’t get through his dragon scales?”

“It’s no boast that it is impossible to break through his defense.”

The audience was in an uproar.

Not only were the ordinary people shocked, but the eyes of many transcendents couldn’t help but widen with utter flabbergast.

They were being swindled by Deceptive Disguise and didn’t know the dragonman youngster was wearing the Gold Silk Chainmail.

And the Gold Silk Chainmail was currently being sliced apart wildly by the Gold Flower Hacksaw.

Chapter 491: Contest of Equipment

“Long Fu! I don’t believe I can’t pry off a dragon scale. Get over here so I can cut it off!” Xiong Ju roared, his face full of ruthlessness as he raised an even more wildly fierce offensive.

The dragonman youngster chose to avoid his blade, deciding to fall back and in between dodging, took every chance and gap to unleash counterattacks.

He quickly adapted to Xiong Ju’s attacks and was instantly struck far fewer times.

The audience was awed by Xiong Ju’s offensive and also amazed by the dragonman youngster’s adept sidesteps.

“He seems to be dancing across the blade’s edge.” The longbeard dwarf exclaimed in admiration.

Spring Boxer deeply sighed, “I didn’t expect that Long Fu, even in terms of movement, hadn’t used his full strength when doing battle with me.”

He didn’t understand that this was actually his contribution.

Even though Ban Lan Gen hated the dragonman youngster deeply, he too had no choice but to concede that the latter was outstanding, “Long Fu’s use of the Surge battle skill surprisingly makes me feel lithe. Against Xiong Ju’s arrogant offensive, it is very difficult for someone to handling that blade with ease like him.”

The Grand Arena Medal really was practical, who would have thought that this would have been impossible for the dragonman youngster to do this yesterday?

With Spring Boxer’s battle experience, the dragonman youngster’s last minute preparations last night had given him a breakthrough in his dodging capabilities.

The female snow elf adjusted her black frame glasses, her tone serene, “Although Xiong Ju is currently winning, if this situation continues, it will be more advantageous for Long Fu.”

“That’s right.” The old human next to her agreed, “Although Xiong Ju’s offensive is fierce and shocks the people, if it doesn’t hit his opponent, it is utterly worthless. Considering their expenditures, Xiong Ju is using up far more than Long Fu.”

Xiong Ju hit the dragonman at a decreasing rate, making him incessantly vexed and impatient.

“Long Fu, you don’t have the guts to battle me outright. Are you a coward? You’re just avoiding me, what use is that!?”

The dragonman youngster calmly responded, “Xiong Ju, I am just cautiously watching your military strategy, this is giving you respect. If this is causing a misunderstanding with you, then I shall strike back.”

Xiong Ju was overjoyed to hear that, but a moment later, he saw the dragonman youngster pull back further, holding an alchemy crossbow in each hand.

The dragonman youngster proficiently nocked on arrows.

A moment later, an alchemy arrow shot out.

Xiong Ju rushed to evade it; however, he wasn’t very agile.

The dragonman youngster’s archery was very exquisite and hit almost every arrow.

After being shot a dozen times, Xiong Ju finally recognized reality.

He used the Gold Flower Hacksaw as a shield, placing it in front of his body, then used the battle skill Barbarous Collision to charge the dragonman youngster.

His tactics were obvious; he wanted to close the distance between him and the dragonman youngster.

Seeing Xiong Ju coming towards him, the dragonman youngster calmly fell back while shooting more alchemy arrows.

The arrows hit Xiong Ju, breaking out in showers of different elemental sparkles.

Xiong Ju’s speed wasn’t impeded that much.

But at this moment, the dragonman youngster suddenly shot a silver level slow arrow.

The arrow hit Xiong Ju dead center, instantly decreasing his speed by 30%.

Due to this speed decrease, the dragonman youngster began to pull away from him.

Xiong Ju roared loudly.

As if in response to his bellow, the dragonman youngster shot another silver level alchemy arrow.

It was a numbing electric arrow.

After Xiong Ju was shot by the arrow, electricity crackled across his body, causing his muscles to feel incomparably limp and numb all over.

In this split second of losing motive power, he came to a standstill and lightly shuddered.

The dragonman youngster seized that opportunity to put even more distance between him and Xiong Ju.

Xiong Ju’s tactics failed, and this also caused him to admit that he had to block the dragonman youngster’s alchemy arrows, otherwise, he wouldn’t have the chance to get close to them.

The dragonman youngster shot another three arrows.

These were silver level poison arrows.

After Xiong Ju was struck by those arrows, his field of view suddenly went dark, and with every breath, purple bubbles popped from the corner of his mouth.

Xiong Ju had just been paralyzed and slowed, and now because he was poisoned, he became dizzy too.

The audience was stunned to see this.

“I didn’t expect Long Fu to be adept at archery.”

Spring Boxer felt ashamed, “Lord Long Fu’s archery skills are amazing. When I fought him, I misunderstood what he was, believing that his long ranged abilities were weak, therefore, that was why he deliberately provoked me into close quarters combat.”

In fact, the dragonman youngster hadn’t shown off his archery skills much after leaving Mystifying Monster Island.

This was because his exquisite archery skills were inherent to a bloodline.

The Gold Needle Queen Bee was a legend level magic beast, and the dragonman youngster’s human form contained the Gold Needle Queen Bee’s legend level bloodline. It was that bloodline that gave him the gift of precision.

After leaving Mystifying Monster Island, the dragonman youngster had always remained in the dragonman form.

Due to this, the Flame Dragon King bloodline suppressed the Gold Needle Queen Bee bloodline, causing his archery skills to fade away.

That was the reason he didn’t do much archery.

The reason why he could use it now was because he was secretly using the Bloodcore.

Right now, he had two bloodlines augmenting each other.

This was one of the usages of the Bloodcore the dragonman youngster discovered on Mystifying Monster Island – compound transformations!

The transformations were mighty, but it had a massive systemic problem; it depleted the Bloodcore’s magic at an intense rate.

Due to the dragonman youngster’s arrow storm, Xiong Ju started to lose.

All of the spectators, without exception, were deeply moved.

“Who would have thought it would end up like this. For the longest time, most people favored Xiong Ju, who is currently taking a beating.”

“I heard the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ backer is an arms smuggling merchant. Putting that aside, look at his alchemy arrows’ actual combat effectiveness, they are indeed strong. If there is a chance, I want to purchase a large batch of them.”

Many spectators were watching the dragonman youngster creating this battlefield situation, seeing that his usage of alchemy arrows was a big element in doing so.

“Unfortunately, Long Fu isn’t a true archer, many of the arrows he shoots interfere with each other and are unable to display their maximum might.”

An archer was a fighter specialized at using long ranged weapons, they cultivated unique battle skills for continuous fire of arrows that didn’t offset each other, instead they would conform with and amplify each other, so that 1+1>2.

From a certain point of view, this was similar to spell casting.

Inside the VIP room, the Bayonet Gang’s human ringleader saw things were going south, and he couldn’t help but howl, “You idiot, use the Flowing Wave Armor I gave you!!”

The human ringleader used a battle skill, his voice ringing like a big bell.

The dueling arena didn’t strictly prohibit reminders from the audience, so the human wasn’t breaking any rules.

What was needed was that one’s voice had to rise above the noise of everyone present and reach the ears of the duelists.

Xiong Ju heard the human ringleader’s howl and immediately quivered in response.

The fretfulness he felt weakened somewhat, and he immediately used the Flowing Wave Armor.

The Flowing Wave Armor looked like an epaulet, and it was hanging on his shoulder. But after being imbued with battle qi, a large blue stream of water suddenly bubbled from the Flowing Wave Armor.

The stream looked like silk that quickly wrapped around Xiong Ju’s entire body; however, his eyes, nose, ears, and mouth were still exposed.

The alchemy master who designed this protection device had given sufficient consideration to how fighters really acted during wartime.

Many fighters during battle needed to use their senses to the fullest.

Apart from the eyes providing sight, the nose providing smell, and the ears giving hearing, many fighters also needed to have their mouths free to use their unique battle skills.

Xiong Ju’s entire body was wrapped with the stream.

The dragonman youngster’s alchemy arrows shot him again and were blocked by the water armor.

The alchemy arrows’ various elemental explosions caused flowery sprays to puff from the water armor.

The stream was rapidly depleted, and many parts of Xiong Ju’s body were temporarily exposed.

But a moment later, the Flowing Wave Armor’s epaulet produced more water that quickly plugged the gaps created.

This was a unique item, armor that could rapidly mend itself on demand!

When the dragonman youngster saw this, he immediately changed his alchemy arrows.

He no longer used elemental arrows, instead he used armor breaking and heavy arrows.

But after these arrows hit the water armor, the water armor flowed in a way that completely deflected the arrows.

The Flowing Wave Armor had amazing defense.

It was able to restrain those two types of alchemy arrows.

The dragonman youngster’s arrows were no longer able to menace Xiong Ju.

However, the dragonman youngster didn’t decide to stop firing, on the contrary, he increased his rate of fire.

The human ringleader had used the epaulet many times.

The Flowing Wave Armor had helped him get through many perilous moments.

Such important information about it had been acquired by the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

The dragonman youngster’s choice was undoubtedly wise.

Although Xiong Ju had remarkable defense, it was being depleted.

Although the Flowing Wave Armor was convenient, the stream of water that was constantly repairing the armor consumed large amounts of Xiong Ju’s battle qi.

By contrast, the dragonman youngster was only using a tiny bit of battle qi to fire his alchemy arrows.

This kind of exchange was obviously partial towards the dragonman youngster.

Of course, it wasn’t without its problems.

Every alchemy arrow was expensive, especially the silver level arrows.

In comparison, Xiong Ju was paying a lower monetary cost. His battle qi would be completely recovered in a day’s time.

Put into terms of exchange, the dragonman youngster was attacking with money to intentionally reduce Xiong Ju’s battle qi reserves.

“Long Fu, you wretched guy! Eat my hammer!!” After Xiong Ju used the Flowing Wave Armor, he had a spark of inspiration.

He took out the iron hammer in his belt and threw it.

This hammer was not something the dragonman youngster knew from intelligence reports.

Because this equipment was something the dwarf ringleader was quietly refining and hadn’t finished yet.

This had been lent to Xiong Ju for the duel, and it was the first time the hammer had been shown off in public.

The Small Thunder God Flying Hammer flew in a circle, then swung towards the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster easily dodged it.

But a moment later, the iron hammer was controlled by Xiong Ju to circle back around and swing at the dragonman youngster in the back of the head.

The dragonman youngster had to dodge it again.

After evading it again, he used Demolition Fist to ruthlessly pound the iron hammer into the ground.

Chapter 492: A Fierce Close Combat Battle

The Small Thunder God Flying Hammer was smashed into the ground by the dragonman youngster’s battle skill.

But it quickly flew back into the sky by Xiong Ju’s spiritual control.

The Small Thunder God Flying Hammer continued to harass the dragonman youngster as it circled around him.

If the dragonman youngster was careless, the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer would whack him. Besides the pain of the strike, the hammer would also shock him with electricity and cause paralysis.

Luckily, the dragonman youngster’s Flame Dragon King bloodline was pretty resistant to magic.

If it were someone else, the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer would not only pound their flesh into pulp but also paralyze them with electricity.

“Do you see it? That is the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer I forged!” The dwarf ringleader proudly exclaimed inside the VIP room.

Due to the flying hammer’s interference, the dragonman youngster couldn’t shoot as freely as before.

He had thought the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer was a close combat weapon and didn’t think it was a long ranged type weapon.

To withstand the flying hammer’s interference, the dragonman youngster was forced to shoot his arrows at it.

The alchemy arrows worked, continuously shooting down the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer to the ground.

But it quickly flew back up again.

The Bayonet Gang’s dwarf ringleader had used a large amount of precious materials to make this hammer, causing it to be very tough and tenacious.

The Small Thunder God Flying Hammer would only be scuffed black a bit by alchemy arrows and the dragonman youngster’s battle skills, barely taking any damage.

“This flying hammer is silver level, but unless I use gold level firepower, I won’t be able to get rid of it.” The dragonman youngster quickly recognized this fact.

He unavoidably landed into a conundrum.

At first, Xiong Ju was using the Flowing Wave Armor to block the alchemy arrows.

Now, the dragonman youngster had no choice but to divide his firepower to deal with the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer.

Pressure on Xiong Ju plummeted, and with a howl, he charged at the dragonman youngster.

The Flowing Wave Armor’s stream was also wrapped around Xiong Ju’s feet. While he moved, the stream under his feet acted in concert with him, bursting with a spray of water that accelerated him.

Xiong Ju’s charge was too obvious, and the dragonman youngster avoided it.

Xiong Ju’s Gold Flower Hacksaw hit air, and with a thump, smashed a giant hole in the ground.

The teeth frantically whirled, easily cutting through the tile.

Xiong Ju’s hacksaw also caused a great amount of rocky debris to fly everywhere in a split second.

The dragonman youngster retreated, trying to pull distance between him and Xiong Ju again.

But during this, the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer did its best to impede him, greatly perturbing him.

Xiong Ju charged at the dragonman youngster again.

The dragonman youngster hadn’t gotten far when the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer suddenly struck the dragonman youngster’s lower back from a tricky angle.

The dragonman youngster was consequently exposed.

Xiong Ju just happened to reach him then.

The beastman excitedly howled and precisely grabbed hold of this opening to swing the Gold Flower Hacksaw with both hands.

This time, the dragonman youngster didn’t dodge, he barely had enough time to raise his arms to block it.

A moment later, the Gold Flower Hacksaw ferociously slashed the dragonman youngster’s arms.

The dragonman youngster was flung back like an artillery shell.

At this time, both were quite close to the dueling arena’s oval walls.

The dragonman youngster was flung right into said wall.

Despite this, his arms safely shielded and protected his face and head.

Even though he was smashed into a wall, the dragonman youngster’s defense hadn’t been broken.

Xiong Ju gnashed his teeth when he saw that.

He had confidence in his heavy attack, but when he saw the dragonman youngster was unharmed, his heart suddenly sank.

To him, the dragonman youngster was as stubborn and unchanging as a tenacious coastal reef. His attacks were like heavy sea waves, yet no matter how many battered the reef, it was to no avail.

The dragonman youngster quickly got out of the wall, then threw away his alchemy crossbows.

Seeing Xiong Ju charging with the Gold Flower Hacksaw again, the dragonman youngster decided to not pull back again, instead he charged too.

Both duelists quickly crashed into each other, starting a fierce confrontation.

This time, the dragonman youngster chose to fight in close quarters, abandoning long range warfare.

The Small Thunder God Flying Hammer spun around the two, its hammer head spinning and humming in the wind, yet it was unable to attack.

Although the hammer the dwarf ringleader had lent to Xiong Ju could be easily controlled with one’s spirit, time was too short, and Xiong Ju had barely learned how to use it over the course of one night.

Due to the two fighting close to each other, their bodies constantly changing position, Xiong Ju was unable to accurately control the hammer in this fight.

“I guessed right!”

“The intelligence report didn’t mention Xiong Ju having this weapon.”

“This proves: this flying hammer is like the Flowing Wave Armor; it was loaned to him.” The dragonman youngster thought.

His close combat abilities were not inferior to Xiong Ju, he had just decided to use long ranged firepower to wear down Xiong Ju more and make things more advantageous for the dragonman youngster.

The battle situation was constantly shifting, and when Xiong Ju used the Flowing Wave Armor and Small Thunder God Flying Hammer, the dragonman youngster realized his prior tactics were no longer appropriate.

Under these circumstances, it was more favorable for him to choose melee combat.

With this choice of tactics, the Small Thunder Gold Flying Hammer temporarily became useless.

From this, the power of intelligence would be seen to be omnipresent.

Battle skill – Demolition Fist!

The dragonman youngster’s exploding fist struck Xiong Ju.

The Flowing Wave Armor showed its defensive function, and blew apart in a shower of water, counteracting Demolition Fist’s attack power.

Then a moment later, the hole was quickly mended.

Battle skill- Total Annihilation.

Xiong Ju swung the Gold Flower Hacksaw.

The gigantic hacksaw flew through the air in a stripe of golden light, its momentum staggering.

The dragonman youngster didn’t dodge it, instead he planted his dragon feet tenaciously, ready to block it.

The Gold Flower Hacksaw smashed into the dragonman youngster’s Gold Silk Chainmail.

The gold flowers that appeared throughout the air were unable to harm the dragonman youngster.

The battle sank into a deadlock, as both sides competed fiercely in terms of battle qi, stamina, military power, and equipment!

During this contest, Xiong Ju suddenly opened his mouth and used his racial magic – Bear Roar!

An explosion of sound.

Because both were so close together, the dragonman youngster was instantly hit.

His head went dizzy, and his entire body lost mobility for a split second.

Xiong Ju raised his leg and kicked the dragonman youngster in the chest, tossing him to the ground.

Xiong Ju seized the opportunity to raise his body and arms, lifting the Gold Flower Hacksaw.

Battle skill – Mountain Splitter!

The hacksaw smashed into the dragonman youngster’s chest with ruthless and incomparably violence.

The teeth wildly whirled, wanting to sever the dragonman youngster at the waist.

Large amounts of splattering sparks quickly formed a gold flower that filled the sky.

This resplendent and dazzling scene contained brutality and grave danger.

Countless people cried out in alarm simultaneously.

Some people even stood up anxiously.

Among them was Chi Lai.

Although Chi Lai had just joined the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, he had a strong bond with the dragonman youngster.

The main reason was that they both had the same religion.

On the contrary, Lan Zao was calmly sitting in his seat.

Lan Zao worshiped the dragonman youngster. In addition to that, he also knew much more about the dragonman youngster, thus he was more confident in him than others.

As a matter of fact, the dwarf Men Shi was also standing up.

But because he was very short, this wasn’t that apparent.

Seeing signs the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ people wanted to interfere, the dueling staff immediately took precautions and glared at the three like tigers.

Heroic duels did not permit outsiders to meddle, but they were allowed to interrupt it.

If an outsider interfered, it would tarnish age-old tradition, and the people would turn against them.

Even the Bayonet Gang would never desecrate the meaning of this rule.

Not at all!

If someone among them acted to break a heroic duel, the Bayonet Gang’s reputation would be thrown into the ditch. The entire Ice Sculpture Kingdom would spurn them, and they would no longer have room to survive.

But the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps were outsiders, they wouldn’t care about such things as much.

“Prepare yourself, if all else fails, we’ll save our commander!” Chi Lai grumbled.

Men Shi firmly modded, his face solemn, “I’ll cover you when the moment comes, you guys get out first.”

Lan Zao looked at the two silver levels with astonishment, increasing his opinion of them.

Lan Zao understood clearly: regardless of whether it was Chi Lai interfering in the duel or Men Shi covering their escape, both would be in enormous danger.

Even so, they still planned on rescuing the dragonman youngster.

Lan Zao rushed to soothe them, “Don’t worry, the Lord Commander won’t lose so easily. He hasn’t used his full strength just yet.”

As expected, a moment later the dragonman youngster cleared his head.

Battle skill – Demolition Fist.

Bang.

With an explosion, the dragonman youngster blasted the Gold Flower Hacksaw away from his abdomen.

Then he seized that opportunity to disengage.

Xiong Ju growled, hacksaw in hand as he pursued close behind.

The dragonman youngster did a fighting retreat as he examined himself.

The Gold Silk Chainmail around his abdomen suffered severe damage.

If a few more seconds had passed, a hole might have been cut into it.

To tell the truth, the Gold Flower Hacksaw was not a conventional weapon, when its teeth swiveled and turned, it could saw through many solid armors.

As long as Xiong Ju had an opportunity, the Gold Silk Chainmail would be grinded through.

Xiong Ju swung the Gold Flower Hacksaw in wide ranging sweeps.

Every slash that hit the dragonman youngster would cause a glittering golden flower to spark into being.

However, when these golden flowers attacked the dragonman youngster, it didn’t do much harm.

Because at this point in the fight, the dragonman youngster’s Grand Arena Medal was amplifying his overall attributes greatly, its steady growth throughout the fight now reaching an obvious level.

Seeing the dragonman youngster smoothly escaping, and once again doing battle with Xiong Ju, everyone watching cheered continuously.

Those watching for fun didn’t mind the trouble.

The audience wanted to see a more marvelous and protracted battle.

After seeing this happen and finding the dragonman youngster wasn’t that injured, Chi Lai and Men Shi let out a breath and sat back down.

Chapter 493: Nothing Stands Above Speed

The dragonman youngster put more attention to the Gold Flower Hacksaw.

This gold level weapon was the biggest threat to him.

The dragonman youngster at this time felt the Gold Silk Chainmail was reaching its limit.

“Xiong Ju’s attack power with the Gold Flower Hacksaw is on an entirely different level than without it. After this fight, perhaps I too should consider getting a weapon that fits me.” The dragonman youngster sighed to himself.

He couldn’t help but be perplexed.

If he got into melee with Xiong Ju, he had to face the Gold Flower Hacksaw.

In long ranged combat, although he could avoid the Gold Flower Hacksaw for a time, he was constantly being harassed by the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer. Furthermore, the Flowing Wave Armor obstructed most of his alchemy arrows’ power.

The dragonman youngster gradually attacked less and defended more.

Although the Gold Flower Hacksaw didn’t seem to be breaking his defenses, in actuality, the Gold Silk Chainmail was damaged in many places.

If this kept up, there would come a moment when the Gold Flower Hacksaw would cut the chainmail apart.

The situation was getting worse for the dragonman youngster.

“This cannot continue!”

The dragonman youngster was determined and prepared for the opportunity to meet the Gold Flower Hacksaw head on in the next clash.

Battle skill – Demolition Fist.

Battle skill – Surge.

His fist smashed into the Gold Flower Hacksaw’s flat half, producing a reaction force.

The two battle skills were used simultaneously, allowing him to do an explosive retreat.

Within a few seconds, he had put 30 meters between him and Xiong Ju.

Xiong Ju immediately pursued while simultaneously using his spirit to throw the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer at the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster took a deep breath, then shouted.

A roar shook the arena and the air rippled.

The Small Thunder God Flying Hammer fell to the floor, and for a brief moment, Xiong Ju also went stiff.

It was the young man’s racial magic – Dragon Roar.

Using the opportunity this racial magic created, the dragonman youngster took a deep breath and loudly declared, “Xiong Ju, let me show you my ultimate form.”

After saying this, he used the Bloodcore to transform many spurting smoke mounds across his body.

Xiong Ju was awed by the dragonman youngster’s words, he didn’t attack but instead stood there and waited sternly.

He had obtained information on this.

The dragonman youngster’s form had been revealed to the masses during the Battle of Two Eyes Island.

Since the Bayonet Gang had made discreet inquiries, information of his ultimate form at Two Eyes Island would not be overlooked.

Click, click, click.

The dragonman youngster had two gigantic ring mounds on his back that were spraying yellow flames.

A moment later, the dragonman youngster used Surge.

The battle skill’s force followed the ring mounds spraying fire, and in an instant, it gave him ferocious forward momentum.

Like an unleashed arrow, the dragonman youngster swiftly charged into Xiong Ju.

Xiong Ju took a deep breath and assumed a defensive and counterattack stance.

Both hands firmly held the Gold Flower Hacksaw, holding it to his left side.

If the dragonman youngster’s attack showed a weak point, he would grab hold of that opportunity to swing and slash the dragonman youngster to the ground.

“Faster, a bit faster, I can go even faster!” The dragonman youngster’s amber eyes burned with the fiery flames of battle intent.

As the dragonman youngster was about to reach Xiong Ju, 3 more mounds suddenly appeared on his back.

With these additional spurting smoke mounds working in tandem with Surge, the dragonman youngster’s speed increased further!

Xiong Ju was just about to adapt to the dragonman youngster’s increasing speed, but a moment later, the dragonman youngster sped up further, causing him to instantly lose track of his target.

“Damn it, where is he?”

“The right!!”

Just as Xiong Ju was reacting, the dragonman youngster had already dashed to his right side.

“Crap!” Xiong Ju glared in anger, and with both arms, swung the Gold Flower Hacksaw with all his might.

The Gold Flower Hacksaw was a two handed weapon and very heavy.

Xiong Ju’s defensive and counterattack stance had placed the Gold Flower Hacksaw on his left.

This was equal to Xiong Ju’s right side being a wide open gate left undefended.

However, this was not a problem with his defensive stance.

This movement was carefully selected; it wanted to leave the right side open to lure the enemy into attacking it.

When he was attacked, Xiong Ju would swing his Gold Flower Hacksaw and sweep across his right.

Because there was enough distance to give Xiong Ju sufficient time to exert himself and give this sweep ample power.

But the dragonman youngster’s burst of speed had surpassed Xiong Ju’s limit.

He even lost track of the dragonman youngster for a time!

It was too late for Xiong Ju to react, nor was there time to activate his painstakingly arranged trap.

The dragonman youngster’s sudden attack worked, turning the trap into Xiong Ju’s vulnerability!

Xiong Ju snarled to himself, and in his eyes, time seemed to slow.

He looked on helplessly as the dragonman youngster’s fist smashed into him, while the Gold Flower Hacksaw on his left just started to move!

Xiong Ju was a veteran silver level fighter, and he had rich combat experience, with every part of his body capable of being a weapon.

In this critical moment, Xiong Ju suddenly loosened his arms and tensed his right shoulder’s muscles, using his shoulder to take on the dragonman youngster.

But the dragonman youngster had already used Demolition Fist.

One was using a battle skill while the other didn’t have such time.

This time, the one who ate the loss was naturally Xiong Ju.

Boom.

Xiong Ju’s right shoulder felt pain, and his entire being toppled backwards due to his center of gravity being utterly destabilized.

He finally swung the Gold Flower Hacksaw

While swinging it, Xiong Ju cleverly used his weapon’s weight to steady his center of gravity.

It got instant results!

Xiong Ju was really experienced and had a strong martial skill foundation and was able to quickly make up for his weak points after the fact.

However, by the time he stabilized his center of gravity, his eyes had lost the dragonman youngster again.

He turned his neck and ran his eyes everywhere.

The dragonman youngster suddenly appeared behind him.

Battle skill – Demolition Fist.

The fist smashed into Xiong Ju’s lower back, pulverizing the Flowing Wave Armor into countless droplets.

Demolition Fist’s power was consequently decreased immensely.

Soon after, Xiong Ju’s leather armor was hit.

Xiong Ju’s leather armor was hard and thick, and the dragonman youngster’s demolition fist was unable to split it open.

He only felt pain and his battle qi being depleted.

Xiong Ju quickly turned around, but he could only see the dragonman youngster’s red silhouette.

Xiong Ju was unable to adapt to the dragonman youngster’s speed for a moment!

Xiong Ju subconsciously picked the best response.

He simply squatted in the ground and used the Gold Flower Hacksaw to shield his front.

His ears were filled with the dragonman youngster’s blasting echoes.

His eyes found it difficult to lock onto the dragonman youngster, and under most circumstances, he was only able to catch a glimpse of the red figure, nothing more.

The dragonman youngster used Demolition Fist repeatedly, constantly bombarding Xiong Ju.

Xiong Ju was wearing leather armor, along with the Flowing Wave Armor, with both of these combined, he also had outstanding defense.

The dragonman youngster’s attacks didn’t do much.

Xiong Jux successfully steadied himself and immediately used his spirit to move the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer.

The flying hammer pursued the dragonman youngster.

Despite his high speed, the flying hammer still locked onto him.

He then purposely pulled away and charged from behind Xiong Ju.

Xiong Ju quickly reacted, quickly adjusting his defense direction.

The dragonman youngster was being closely followed by the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer.

Just as the dragonman youngster reached Xiong Ju, he suddenly dodged out of the way and nimbly avoided the latter.

But the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer behind him didn’t have enough time to adjust, and it flew in a straight line right into Xiong Ju’s head.

Seeing this, the dwarf ringleader couldn’t help but cover his face.

Xiong Ju had only practiced with the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer for a single night.

Although the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer was easy to use, as the fight got fiercer, Xiong Ju’s basic usage of it was not enough.

His reckless and random use of it instead backfired on himself.

The dragonman youngster had guessed previously what Xiong Ju’s relationship with the Small Thunder Flying Hammer was, and so, he used this strategy.

He succeeded.

Xiong Ju was ruthlessly pounded by the flying hammer, instantly creating a weak point in his defense.

The dragonman youngster was prepared for this and instantly seized upon it to launch a wild attack.

Battle skill – Machine Gun Fist!

Bang, bang, bang, bang, bang, bang…

The dragonman youngster’s downpour of punches unceasingly bombarded Xiong Ju.

Xiong Ju’s Flowing Wave Armor blocked the dragonman youngster’s fists, splattering droplets everywhere.

Very quickly, there were more and more shadows of the dragonman youngster’s fists.

The hard dragon fists penetrated the water armor and bombarded Xiong Ju’s leather armor.

After the Flowing Wave Armor broke apart, it could be repaired, but it was best done when it wasn’t being attacked.

And now, Xiong Ju was being beaten by the dragonman youngster’s fists. The Flowing Wave Armor’s repair speed couldn’t compare to the dragon fists’ destruction rate.

Using water instead of conventional metal armor meant that it was easy to carry and had its hidden benefits, but it also meant it had its shortcomings.

When confronted with Machine Gun Fist’s high frequency of attacks, the Flowing Water Armor found it difficult to provide protection.

Xiong Ju’s leather armor was only silver level.

Although it blocked the vast majority of Machine Gun Fist’s might, it was still very unpleasant for Xiong Ju.

At this moment, he was deeply feeling the pain from this rain of fists.

“Now Commander Long Fu is winning!”

“Xiong Ju is being suppressed by his attacks.”

The audience was unable to take their eyes off it; the twists and turns of this wonderful duel was exciting to watch and very satisfying.

When Spring Boxer saw this, his eyes also shined, thinking to himself, “Commander Long Fu’s battle skill Machine Gun Fist is closely related to my Spring Shot Fist.”

“Thinking about it carefully, I have a very similar fighting style to Commander Long Fu.”

“When will I be able to hit Xiong Ju like him?”

Spring Boxer came from the army, and detested gang members like Xiong Ju.

Normally, he had much friction with Xiong Ju and the others. Contradictions large and small also piled up.

Compared to Xiong Ju, Spring Boxer was undoubtably more engrossed in watching the dragonman youngster.

After the dragonman youngster used the spurting smoke mounds transformation, his speed rose dramatically.

The imperial east had a martial warfare philosophy – in the world of battle skills, nothing stands above speed!

The only aspect the dragonman youngster had above Xiong Ju was speed, and he was able to completely overturn the fight, firmly take the initiative, and suppress Xiong Ju with his attacks.

Xiong Ju found it hard to catch the dragonman youngster’s figure and was even less capable of retaliating.

For a moment, he could only defend himself passively, becoming a wooden dummy that was subjected to the dragonman youngster’s painful punches.

Chapter 494: Enormous Advantage

The dragonman youngster’s accelerating speed grew even faster.

Because his elbows, back, and even the bottom of his feet had sprouted spurting smoke mounds.

This gave him forward thrust in every direction.

The him from before could never have done this.

Previously, he could only use two spurting mounds on his back, no more than that.

After ingesting Spring Boxer’s battle experience, the dragonman youngster’s movement abilities had an enormous upgrade.

This gave him enough energy to control a third spurting smoke mound.

Since the battle began, Xiong Ju had never sunk into such a predicament!

He was enduring incomparable resentment.

After the dragonman youngster’s speed rose dramatically, the battle tempo suddenly expanded.

Xiong Ju was unable to adapt to this kind of tempo, and under the dragonman youngster’s constant surprise attacks, he was unable to use battle skills.

This kind of battle dispirited him.

Xiong Ju angrily raised his head and roared, then ferociously stood up straight and swung the Gold Flower Hacksaw horizontally viciously.

He wanted to use this to force the dragonman youngster to pull back and give him space to regroup.

But it was obvious that he was overthinking things.

This large movement was full of mistakes in the dragonman youngster’s eyes.

A split second later, the dragonman youngster suddenly planted his body nearly flat to the tiles and then shot out like a gigantic arrow.

A moment later, the dragonman youngster struck Xiong Ju’s calf.

Xiong Ju’s struck calf flew backwards, causing him to suddenly lose his footing and be tripped by the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster seized the opportunity to use Demolition Fist to explode Xiong Ju into the air.

Xiong Ju’s legs flew two meters above the ground.

His body was very heavy and quickly fell back down.

But a moment later, the dragonman youngster appeared underneath Xiong Ju.

Xiong Ju realized things were going terribly, but in the air it was hard for him to adjust his body in time.

The dragonman youngster struck with his two fists.

Battle skill – Demolition Fist!

Although Demolition Fist’s fury was blocked by the Flowing Wave Armor, the remaining power was enough to keep the falling Xiong Ju in the air.

The dragonman youngster blasted yellow flames from his feet’s ring mounds with all he had.

The strong explosive force, along with his thigh and calf muscles, instantly rocketed his entire being into the air.

Xiong Ju had finally adjusted his posture in the air.

He looked down at the charging dragonman youngster, then with the Gold Flower Hacksaw firmly in his grasp, he lifted it up, then ferociously chopped down at the dragonman youngster.

This posture was very suitable for using the battle skill [Mountain Splitter].

But the dragonman youngster’s battle tempo was too fast, and if Xiong Ju forced himself to use a battle skill, it would certainly expose greater vulnerabilities.

However, even with an ordinary downward chop, as a gold level weapon, the Gold Flower Hacksaw still had immense attack power.

Faced with the chopping Gold Flower Hacksaw, ring mounds suddenly appeared on the dragonman youngster’s left arm.

A total of five ring mounds of various sizes flared, and the enormous kinetic energy produced pushed the dragonman youngster backwards in a flash.

The dragonman youngster easily avoided the Gold Flower Hacksaw’s swing, then vanished from Xiong Ju’s field of view.

When Xiong Ju relocated the dragonman youngster, the latter was already ambushing him from the right.

Xiong Ju was thrown towards the ground by the dragonman youngster once again.

The dragonman youngster, by contrast, used his ring mounds to ascend higher into the air.

In the air, he twisted his dragon tail to effortlessly and quickly adjust himself into a diving stance.

He put both hands behind his back, his palms facing backwards.

His perfectly straight body swooped down towards the falling Xiong Ju.

In order to produce the greatest driving force, the 3 ring mounds on the dragonman youngster’s back, along with the 4 on his thighs and palms were all constantly jetting with yellow flames.

The dragonman youngster accelerated further as a result.

He was like a shooting artillery shell whose speed stirred up the wind.

Xiong Ju heard the whistling wind approaching him, and quickly threw his arm, wanting to turn the Gold Flower Hacksaw around.

But a moment later, the dragonman youngster ruthlessly rammed him.

Battle skill – Demolition Fist!

The exploding fist smashed into Xiong Ju’s belly.

The Flowing Wave Armor burst with a massive spray of water and was cut into twain in the middle of Xiong Ju’s belly, exposing the greatest gap in his defense so far.

Luckily, he still had the silver level leather armor under it.

But Xiong Ju’s body was bent by the attack, his back protruding out.

The dragonman youngster took a deep breath, then used another battle skill – Machine Gun Fist.

Ding, ding, ding, ding, ding, ding…

For a moment, blurred dragon fists flew like the rain!

Xiong Ju’s field of view was completely filled with innumerable fists.

His head, chest, arms, legs, and abdomen were punched by those fists.

The pain and fear in his heart leapt up, replacing his irritation and rage.

Xiong Ju realized: if he couldn’t withstand this round of attacks, he would inevitably lose!

Battle skill – Black Armor!

At the moment of victory or defeat, Xiong Ju used all of his strength to use a defensive battle skill.

Under these circumstances, this was a massive risk.

Generally speaking, defensive battle skills were used in advance and shouldn’t be disturbed when used to make them safe and effective.

Xiong Ju’s entire body was being punched in critical areas, and the external force on his body, along with the pain was also strongly disturbing him.

Battle skills did not always work the first time used.

If the internal battle qi circulation wasn’t complete, it would fail.

At minimum, failing led to coughing up blood, worsening to internal injuries, near death, and even death!

Xiong Ju had no choice but to take this risk for the sake of victory.

Fortunately, his risk paid off.

Xiong Ju’s foundation was quite strong.

He had tempered many battle skills into instinct.

When crisis came knocking, his instincts would grow more powerful to forcibly resist formidable external interference and allow him to use his defensive battle skill.

In a split second, Xiong Ju’s skin turned black and became very hard.

When the dragonman youngster’s fists struck the skin, it unexpectedly produced sounds of metal being struck.

Meanwhile, Xiong Ju raised his farms to firmly protect his head and curled up as much as possible to reduce his area of attack.

Machine Gun Fist had a high frequency of attack, but in terms of instant output, it couldn’t compare to Demolition Fist.

The dragonman youngster was unable to break Xiong Ju’s defensive stance.

Very quickly, the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer whizzed over, once again pestering him.

The dragonman youngster was overflowing with battle intent and didn’t dodge or evade, continuing to fire away with Machine Gun Fist as the flying hammer kept pounding at him.

In this manner, the dragonman youngster assaulted Xiong Ju as they fell from the sky.

Xiong Ju fell to the ground, with the dragonman youngster standing on him, still beating him up!

“I was actually hit to the ground. Damn it, let me stand up!” Xiong Ju was incomparably resentful.

Against an opponent of the same level, he had seldom been put in such a difficult and passive situation.

He had always been on top, while his opponent was underneath him, when had he ever been at such a disadvantage!?

The dragonman youngster was actually the strongest silver level he had ever faced.

Against the dragonman youngster’s Machine Gun Fist, the Flowing Wave Armor revealed its design flaws, gaps kept on forming and it could no longer protect Xiong Ju.

Xiong Ju’s leather armor was also struck by Machine Gun Fist and became damaged in many areas.

He sensed the pain the rainstorm of punches caused, doing his best to maintain his defensive battle skill to protect himself.

Xiong Ju sank completely into a passive state.

If this continued, and he failed to turn the situation around, the dragonman youngster would beat him into submission.

However, the Gold Flower Hacksaw had fallen to the side.

Although the Black Bear Armor battle skill was strong, it took more than half of a body’s battle qi circulation to use.

Under these circumstances, Xiong Ju couldn’t use other battle skills.

But it didn’t matter, he still had his bloodline racial magic.

Racial magic – Bear Roar!

The dragonman youngster saw him open his mouth, already on guard. He suddenly sighed in exasperation and immediately pulled back.

Xiong Ju’s methods weren’t limited to just that.

He used his spirit to throw the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer.

After the flying hammer spiraled around, it went to stop the dragonman youngster.

But the dragonman youngster’s speed was far beyond what it once was, and after nimbly changing directions, he easily shook off the flying hammer.

A moment later, Bear Roar burst out.

The dragonman youngster was already out of range.

Despite taking the potion Zi Di had given him before the battle, it was not enough to take on Bear Roar head on.

Xiong Ju quickly crawled back up, gasped for breath, and finally freed himself from his predicament.

The Bayonet Gang members in the audience were watching the duel on edge, and when they saw Xiong Ju finally escaping, they all let out a breath, then shortly after, they let out loud cheers and hoots to encourage Xiong Ju’s moral, wanting him to ruthlessly take back the bout.

When Teng Dong Lang saw this, he also let out a breath surreptitiously.

When Xiong Ju was hit up into the air, then back down, his heart was thumping tensely.

Xiong Ju was only a silver level, he couldn’t fly.

When he was launched into the air, and didn’t have any footholds, he passively got thrashed.

In this aspect, the dragonman youngster and him had a distinct contrast.

Even though the dragonman youngster can’t fly either, his spurting smoke ring mounds could give him strong motive power that allowed him to exert himself even while in the air.

“Fortunately, Long Fu’s battle skill and strength was not enough to keep Xiong Ju in the air.”

“Xiong Ju’s defensive battle skill also activated at that crucial juncture.” Teng Dong Lang thought.

The situation just now was an enormous opportunity for the dragonman youngster.

But currently, the dragonman youngster was only strong enough to create this opportunity to get an advantage and was unable to turn this advantage into a victory.

Xiong Ju picked up the Gold Flower Hacksaw, lifted it up with both hands, and gathered himself to continue fighting the dragonman youngster.

The last clash cut too deeply, and now Xiong Ju was very cautious and made a supreme effort to prevent himself from being hit into the sky by the dragonman youngster.

Bear Roar had gotten him out of his beatdown without a hitch, and he thought about using again to restrain the dragonman youngster.

However, its actual combat effectiveness wasn’t that good.

The dragonman youngster was very wary of Bear Roar, and his absurd speed allowed him to smoothly dodge Bear Roar.

The Small Thunder God Flying Hammer was already having less of an impact.

“Damn it, how is his speed still increasing?” Sensing this phenomenon, Xiong Ju felt his heart slightly chill.

Chapter 495: To My Heart’s Content

The dragonman youngster’s speed was indeed increasing.

Despite his prior speed already being his limit.

But now, the dragonman youngster was constantly breaking his limits.

There was one reason for this phenomenon, the Grand Arena Medal.

If the Grand Arena Medal only improved speed, the dragonman youngster wouldn’t be able to adapt and would show weakness during this duel.

But the Grand Arena Medal amplified all attributes, it didn’t just increase the dragonman youngster’s speed, but also his defense, strength, dynamic vision, reaction speed, and so on.

Therefore, the dragonman youngster automatically mastered his limit breaking speed.

“Fast, too fast. How is Long Fu still speeding up?!” Many spectators were watching this with fright.

Fewer Bayonet Gang members were cheering and hooting.

Because they all discovered that although Xiong Ju had escaped, he was clearly at a disadvantage, and his attacks found it difficult to threaten the dragonman youngster.

Many times, Xiong Ju could only eat the dragonman youngster’s dust.

His moves were wide ranging, and after the dragonman youngster dodged them in a timely fashion, Xiong Ju’s weak points could be seen from anywhere.

“A chance!”

The dragonman youngster saw Xiong Ju had missed an attack and used Demolition Fist.

Xiong Ju staggered back, constantly being forced back by the force of Demolition Fist.

His defensive strategy was adjusted, paying particular attention to certain attacks from the dragonman youngster.

As long as he was standing on the ground and not hit into the air, his situation wouldn’t be as terrible.

Battle skill – Machine Gun Fist.

Battle skill – Demolition Fist.

The dragonman youngster used offensive battle skills one after the other, forcing Xiong Ju to retreat again and again.

The Bayonet Gang members gradually sank into silence.

All of them could see clearly that their ringleader Lord Xiong Ju was slowly losing the power to retaliate.

The dragonman youngster’s offensive was as unending as the tide.

He couldn’t help but roar again and again to display the appearance of strength.

The dragonman youngster felt this was to his heart’s content!

During the battle of Two Eyes Island, he had once battled the gold level fishman Er Gua Zi directly.

But regretfully, the situation then was dire and as the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ leader, the dragonman youngster had to put the entire group first.

Ultimately, he restrained his desire to duel the strong and chose to retreat at his own volition.

Although Xiong Ju was not a gold level transcendent, he had great strength and luxurious equipment on him, making him the strongest opponent the dragonman youngster had faced on this island so far.

Another crucial thing was that Xiong Ju’s skin and flesh were thick and tough, and along with his extremely high defense, he became an excellent boxing target.

The dragonman youngster seldomly had the chance to use his offensive methods as much as he liked. He couldn’t do that in his magic ship’s training room.

It always felt comfortable using one’s full strength.

As he attacked, the dragonman youngster brought forth his full power.

His spirit and body were highly unified.

His battle qi, battle skills, battle reflection, subconscious reactions, and other such elements in a fight harmonized and interweaved with each other.

During intense battle, all kinds of factors formed a marvelous chain reaction.

This improvement was not something normal training could give him.

Only in pitched battle where he was going all out could these elements appear in sufficient amounts, then be swiftly interweaved and become more harmonized with each other.

The result of this harmonization allowed the dragonman youngster to become faster and stronger in battle!

His mind and soul seemed to be flying out, like it was detaching themselves from the shackles of his physical body.

He had never had such a moment before. He could understand the vividness of his life and sense his own existence.

Every time he charged, every time his feet rebounded from the ground, every time he punched, every time he crisply felt his fist cave into Xiong Ju’s body…it all proved his existence.

He was himself!

He was not Zhen Jin, he was not Long Fu, those were just parts of him.

The Grand Arena Medal was also elevating the dragonman youngster’s attributes.

His current speed, strength, defense, and recovery had far exceeded his normal limits.

Surpassing one’s limits was novel, and the thrill was one people were unable to stop themselves from continuing.

The constant surpassing of limits was naturally also unending.

“So, my speed had swiftly reached this extent.”

“So, after my punch becomes this strong, this is how it feels to strike my enemy!”

The dragonman youngster was becoming stronger at every moment, transcending his limits every second.

The dragonman youngster’s offensive became as wild as the waves!

Xiong Ju had been beaten back by him repeatedly; he was dizzy and could only rely on his bodily instincts to weakly resist.

The Bayonet Gang’s ringleaders all looked heavy, some were even subconsciously holding their breaths.

Xiong Ju’s defeat was wholly visible.

If this continued, he would lose!

The demon hybrid ringleader was sitting in a VIP room alone, he inwardly grinded his teeth, looking at the dragonman youngster with dense determination.

He then watched Xiong Ju closely, mumbling to himself, “This damned guy, this idiot! Why aren’t you using the Demon Eye I gave to you?”

Xiong Ju forgot.

Such a situation was actually rather normal.

When normal people suddenly got caught up in a fight, their brains would usually have blanks, their glands would fire, their field of view became very narrow, and their body and mind were very tense and emotional.

Although Xiong Ju was not a normal person, and experienced countless battles, he forgot to use some of his equipment.

In fact, Xiong Ju wasn’t a fighter capable of calm analysis.

He was a real warrior.

During this battle, the greater his irritation and hatred were, the more formidable his fighting strength was.

His bodily instincts were stronger than his mind.

He was the one who shaped this powerful bodily instinct.

He had gone through innumerable arduous training and battles large and small throughout his life, gradually forming his current battle style.

This also led to the prior situation happening.

Although Xiong Ju had the Flowing Wave Armor epaulet and the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer hanging at his waist, he didn’t prioritize using them.

Because his bodily instincts were placed above his brain, it directed the fight.

It took the dwarf ringleader reminding Xiong Ju for him to use the Flowing Wave Armor and Small Thunder God Flying Hammer.

“Xiong Ju, use the thing I gave you now!” The demon hybrid ringleader finally couldn’t restrain himself, sharply shouting.

But Xiong Ju didn’t hear him.

The dragonman youngster had silently activated the acoustics case he was carrying.

This alchemy device could absorb sound and prevent it from spreading.

Using it inside, one could plot within it.

Using it outside, one could form a tranquil space.

The dragonman youngster had used it to prevent Xiong Ju from receiving reminders.

He didn’t know whether Xiong Ju had other borrowed equipment on him, but the Flowing Wave Armor had taught the dragonman youngster a bitter lesson.

Just in case, he decided to take precautions in advance.

To prevent Xiong Ju from getting more reminders.

He was different from Xiong Ju.

Despite carrying the Flame Dragon King Bloodline, using explosive battle skills and dashing in straight lines, he still fought with his brain.

Regardless of how intense the battle was going; he never ceased his pondering.

The acoustics case blocked the demon hybrid ringleader’s reminder.

While Xiong Ju didn’t notice this small alchemy device being activated.

His condition was worsening, dodging left and right, relying on his beast-like battle instincts to barely resist the dragonman youngster’s violent attacks.

“God damn it!” The demon hybrid ringleader saw his voice had no effect, and the determination in his eyes became denser.

“Xiong Ju cannot lose, let alone the Bayonet Gang!” The demon hybrid began to whisper to himself.

Although he was a fighter, he had a demon bloodline.

He had his bloodline’s racial magic!

This gave him the ability to cast to a certain extent.

Despite opening and closing his mouth, the demon ringleader didn’t say anything.

This was silent casting!

A powerful casting technique.

In terms of this aspect, the demon hybrid was stronger than the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster also had racial magics like the Life Scorching Dragon Breath. But every time he used Life Scorching Dragon Breath, it needed loud chanting of the draconic tongue.

After spending some time, the dragonman youngster could cast the magic. During this process, his aura was exposed, and his bombastic dragon speech was bound to attract enemy attacks.

The demon hybrid was in the VIP spectator room, and adding on his silent casting, he couldn’t be detected momentarily.

He had lent Xiong Ju the Demon Eye last night.

Before the battle, Xiong Ju had swallowed the eye, putting it into his stomach.

Therefore, when the demon hybrid used his racial magic, the Demon Eye within Xiong Ju’s body activated immediately.

The Demon Eye moved through Xiong Ju’s body and quickly emerged from it.

The leather armor around Xiong Ju’s chest already had holes torn into it by the dragonman youngster.

When the Evil Eye emerged, it came out of one of these holes.

But Xiong Ju’s outermost layer around his chest was still there, the Flowing Wave Armor.

This didn’t hinder the Evil Eye from locking onto the dragonman youngster.

Because the Evil Eye mainly relied on spiritual reactions, rather than line of sight to spy on things.

The dragonman youngster didn’t detect the Evil Eye being activated.

A moment later, the demon ringleader’s mouth curled in a smile, exposing his merciless killing intent.

He unleashed the Evil Eye.

The Evil Eye suddenly erupted with a cold gray ray.

The gray colored ray instantly hit the dragonman youngster.

Both sides had been very close to each other, and the dragonman youngster simply didn’t have time to react before being struck by the Evil Eye’s spiritual beam.

His soul ruthlessly shuddered, and his mind suffered serious damage, instantly on the edge of collapse.

It seemed that he was just about to use Machine Gun Fist, and by chance, this caused it to fail.

The battle skill’s failure caused it to backfire on him.

The dragonman youngster’s internal battle qi became chaotic, and he coughed out scarlet watery blood.

His nose, ears, and canthus all leaked blood as his entire person sank into an intense vertigo.

For a moment, he couldn’t even think.

His speeding shadow had come to a spontaneous stop, becoming completely visible.

At this moment, the dragonman youngster was so weak that he was covered in vulnerabilities.

Chapter 496: Crushing the Demon Eye

The dragonman youngster’s mind was heavily damaged, and for a moment his body swayed, barely able to stand on his feet.

Xiong Ju didn’t take this chance to attack the dragonman youngster.

Because he was also in a terrible state.

He had been miserably suppressed by the dragonman youngster, and now Xiong Ju finally got a pivotal chance to gasp for breath.

He knelt to the ground, leaning on the Gold Flower Hacksaw.

This gold level weapon had become the nightmare of many enemies of Xiong Ju, but now it had been reduced to a crutch.

Huff, huff, huff…

Xiong Ju’s mouth was wide open, doing his best to breathe. He was covered in sweat, his vision was fuzzy, and he felt extremely weak.

After a few seconds, he realized the dragonman youngster had stopped attacking.

He quickly looked for signs of his foe.

In his field of view, he was astonished to discover that the dragonman youngster had stopped moving and was on the verge of collapse.

“What’s going on?” Xiong Ju was puzzled.

When he looked down at his chest, he realized it.

“The Evil Eye.”

He had found the Evil Eye popping out of his chest.

“Abominable!” Immense anger leapt from Xiong Ju’s heart.

“It was that damned demon hybrid.”

“He meddled in this heroic duel!”

Xiong Ju’s rage was not aimed at the dragonman youngster, but rather the demon ringleader who had secretly helped him.

Xiong Ju’s grandfather was an immigrant, but his father had lived his entire life in Snow Bird Harbor.

Therefore, don’t be fooled by the fact that Xiong Ju was a beastman, he was actually a wholly indigenous inhabitant of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

From childhood until now, he had accepted the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s cultural influence.

Despite not having the money to enter college, he still obtained a cultural upbringing.

Heroic duels were always sacred to him.

To restate: interruptions were fine, but interference was absolutely out of the question.

“He sullied this sacred duel!”

“This guy lent me his Evil Eye and also concealed his method. Even if I didn’t use it, he could still do so remotely.”

“That shameless reprobate, I will make him pay!”

Xiong Ju’s fury rose.

Then at that moment, a spiritual fluctuation came from the Evil Eye.

The spiritual fluctuation transmitted into Xiong Ju’s mind.

Xiong Ju heard the demon hybrid’s voice saying, “Xiong Ju, I am helping you!”

The demon ringleader’s tone was quite proud, “Attack now! Long Fu has been hit, he is now at his most vulnerable.”

“Shut up.” Xiong Ju bellowed weakly, strong in appearance but weak in reality.

The demon hybrid sensed Xiong Ju’s mood through the Evil Eye, then after a pause, he quickly replied, “Xiong Ju, now is not the time to show off!”

“You must know that if you lose, the other ringleaders, me included, are not Long Fu’s match.”

“We lent you equipment so you could use it. That means we cannot use them again. If this equipment was used against Long Fu again, others will definitely ridicule us.”

“This will also cause outsiders to think: our Bayonet Gang doesn’t have much of a foundation. The ringleaders all had to use their full strength to resist Long Fu.”

“Therefore, you have to win, to thoroughly eliminate Long Fu, and at best kill him! That will restore our Bayonet Gang’s fighting prestige.”

The demon hybrid’s killing intent was leaking.

“So noisy!” Xiong Ju grew even more indignant, letting out a curse.

That brief time allowed him to calm down somewhat.

He was no longer gasping for air and his breathing gradually leveled out.

He also regained some stamina and was able to stand again.

The demon hybrid rang through his mind again.

“Get a move on, Xiong Ju! Long Fu is taking medicine; he is quickly recovering.”

“Wait a minute, I can shoot a second spiritual beam.”

“Long Fu has a weak defense in this respect; this is his weak point and also your chance for victory.”

“You must seize it, as I said last night, the demon eye can only be used three times in a battle.”

“Now there are only two left, and when they are depleted, you won’t have another chance.”

“Grab hold of this chance, and you can win!”

Then a moment later, Xiong Ju suddenly extended his left hand and resolutely thrusted it into his chest.

Plop.

With a pop, blood splattered everywhere.

Xiong Ju tore the Evil Eye out of his body!

The Evil Eye’s feelers that had taken root in his flesh were also ruptured by this.

Then Xiong Ju squeezed with his left hand.

The Evil Eye by itself had weak defenses, and simply couldn’t withstand Xiong Ju’s grip, bursting into a pile of mush in his hand.

Pop!

The moment the Evil Eye was destroyed, the demon ringleader’s body trembled, and he coughed out a lot of blood.

His mind shook, and he lost control of his body, falling backwards.

Luckily, he had been sitting.

The demon ringleader sat paralyzed in his chair, his eyes rolled white, his tongue lolling out his mouth, and his limbs limp. His mind had been severely damaged, and he sank into unconsciousness.

It was also very unpleasant for Xiong Ju to destroy the Evil Eye.

The wound in his chest dripped with a great amount of blood.

“It’s finally quiet.” He took a deep breath, grimacing with pain. The pain from the massive wound in his chest caused him to drip with cold sweat.

Xiong Ju fully mobilized his battle qi to seal off the wound, quickly staunching the bleeding.

Soon after, he took out some medicinal power, first sprinkling it on his chest, then swallowing the rest.

The dragonman youngster was also taking medicine.

After the Evil Eye’s attack, he was barely able to regain some of his mind.

After having the ability to move again, he immediately took Zi Di’s medicine.

His serious spiritual wounds became minor injuries.

The medicine also began to affect his body, improving his spiritual defenses for a short time.

Then, the dragonman youngster saw Xiong Ju pull out the Evil Eye and crush it.

The dragonman youngster was startled by this, his eyes flashing with understanding, then he looked at Xiong Ju again, with somewhat complex feelings.

Most of the audience were confused about the changes the Evil Eye had wrought and didn’t know what was happening.

The Bayonet Gang ringleaders knew some of the details and were stupefied by this.

When Teng Dong Lang noticed that Xiong Ju had destroyed the Evil Eye, his look immediately became downcast.

He was the current Bayonet Gang leader, and he had a deep understanding of his subordinates’ disposition.

At this moment, Teng Dong Lang couldn’t sit quietly, he immediately got up and quietly left his spectator room to make a beeline for the demon ringleader’s room.

Xiong Ju gulped down medicine, as did the dragonman youngster.

The fierce fighting suddenly gave way into a strange contest of who could take more medicine.

This heroic duel really had its twists and turns.

After the dragonman youngster took spirit improvement medicine, he swallowed some healing potions.

The medicinal effect quickly restored his battle qi reserves.

His fierce attacks had come at the cost of severely depleting his battle qi. Even with the Flame Dragon King Bloodline, it couldn’t match the expenditure rate of such ferocity.

Regardless of whether it was Surge, Demolition Fist, or Machine Gun Fist, they all depleted large amounts of battle qi.

Meanwhile, Xiong Ju took out a precious medicinal bottle that contained three pills.

Xiong Ju took out one and swallowed it, not bothering to even chew.

Then, he did something that shocked the audience – he tossed the Gold Flower Hacksaw away.

Teng Dong Lang had arrived at the demon ringleader’s viewing room’s door.

As he was pushing the door open, he saw Xiong Ju eat the pill.

Teng Dong Long felt relieved.

The Gold Flower Hacksaw was very heavy, and such a weapon couldn’t threaten the dragonman youngster, instead it was an encumbrance on Xiong Ju.

The hacksaw was Xiong Ju’s main way of fighting, and his strongest too.

If Xiong Ju stubbornly wallowed in his habits, then he would inevitably lose.

But at this moment, under the dragonman youngster’s pressure, he broke through his ego. He decided to abandon his deepest habits and most confident style of attack.

Xiong Ju raised his fists, his eyes gleaming with resolve.

Next, he decided to use wrestling techniques to continue the fight with the dragonman youngster.

This was undoubtedly the wisest choice!

Compared to the Gold Flower Hacksaw, Xiong Ju’s fists and feet would increase his battle tempo and allow him to contend with the dragonman youngster’s great speed.

“Xiong Ju, you’ve grown.”

“This battle, even if you ultimately lose, will give you immense gains.”

“Remember your choice…”

“It can remake you!”

Teng Dong Lang sighed out a few words, then forced open the door of the viewing room the demon ringleader was in.

The demon ringleader had regained consciousness and was currently healing himself.

The clothes around his chest and chin were stained with blood.

While healing, he harped on, “Xiong Ju, you’re done for!”

“You stupid bear, you dared to crush my eye.”

“I will tell the gang leader; I will make you pay a bitter price!”

“Gang leader?!”

When the demon ringleader saw Teng Dong Long bursting in, he was pleasantly surprised.

He instantly started to complain, “Lord Gang Leader, you should’ve seen…”

“I saw it.” Teng Dong Lang nodded, coming in front of the demon ringleader.

The demon ringleader stopped healing his wounds as he continued to speak, “Xiong Ju doesn’t know what’s good for him, I decided to help him, yet he unexpectedly did this, this…”

Pow.

Teng Dong Lang, with no expression, slapped him with his hand.

The demon ringleader covered his face, and fell onto his seat, foolishly staring up at Teng Dong Lang.

For a moment, he was struck stupid.

Teng Dong Lang’s eyes were ice cold, “You’re the idiot!”

“Behold your folly.”

“Gang, Gang leader, I was also doing it for the Bayonet Gang…” The demon ringleader’s eyes were red.

Teng Dong Lang took a deep breath, retraining the anger in his heart, “You thought you could win with underhanded means? Do you think the thousands of people in front of your eyes you’re defrauding are all idiots?”

“You aren’t even aware the City Lord is secretly in attendance!”

“You better get up right now and clean up this place.”

“The dueling arena’s investigators will be coming very soon.”

“If you are investigated, I cannot protect you!”

The demon ringleader knew the seriousness of this.

He couldn’t attend to his aching cheek as he rushed into action, doing his best to tidy up all of the traces in the VIP room.

Chapter 497: The Victor is Long Fu!

The dueling arena.

Xiong Ju and the dragonman youngster collided with each other once more.

Their fists and feet greeted each other’s body, rarely protecting themselves.

This was quite similar to how the fight began.

But it was also rather different, they were both using their battle qi fully.

Unlike when they were probing each other and holding back.

They had pressed their battle qi to their limits, amplifying the might of every punch.

The surface of both bodies had a faint silver glow.

“This is?!”

After crossing blows for a moment, the dragonman youngster decided to retreat.

He covered his hand with his chest, gazing at Xiong Ju with bewilderment as his mouth constantly leaked blood.

“Penetration Force.”

The answer flashed through the dragonman youngster’s mind.

Although he had amnesia, he still had common sense.

Penetration Force was one of the classical battle skill attack techniques, its characteristics were distinct and everyone knew of it.

“I didn’t expect that the Gold Silk Chainmail would lose its effectiveness before it broke.” The dragonman youngster sighed to himself.

After Xiong Ju took the pill, each of his attacks had Penetration Force in them.

His attacks pierced the dragonman youngster’s defense and reached into the latter’s body, injuring and damaging him.

This struck at the dragonman youngster’s weak points.

Because the dragonman youngster didn’t have a regular dragonman bloodline, but rather the Flame Dragon King Bloodline.

The Flame Dragon King bloodline was a dragon bloodline.

The young man assumed a dragonman form because the dragon bloodline was incomplete.

Right now, he only had a Flame Dragon King bloodline concentration of 33%.

Henceforth, as the bloodline concentration increased, the young man’s form would gradually distance itself from a humanoid form and shift closer to that of a dragon.

Eventually, he would transform into a gigantic dragon.

A 33% bloodline concentration had more of an effect on the young man’s external appearance, turning him into a dragon with a human body.

This meant the dragonman youngster’s internals were still ordinary and weren’t benefiting from the Flame Dragon King bloodline.

“My armor is ineffective against Penetration Force.”

“Strange, why didn’t Xiong Ju use Penetration Force when we tussled before?”

“Was it because he just had a breakthrough in understanding this battle qi technique?”

“The information report didn’t say Xiong Ju knows how to use Penetration Force. There aren’t even rumors of him training himself in Penetration Force.”

“Was he secretly cultivating this, always hiding this trump card, or did he have a sudden flash of inspiration and out did himself?”

“Or perhaps this is the effect of the medicine he just took?”

A few guesses quickly flashed through the dragonman youngster’s mind.

“Wait a moment, perhaps…I am not lacking methods that can restrain Penetration Force.” The dragonman youngster had a burst of insight.

Before the battle, he had absorbed three sub-dragon corpses.

One of them was the fat ocean dragon abdomen.

After absorbing it, the dragonman youngster gained some of the fat ocean dragon’s bloodline, along with the corresponding bloodline transformation.

That transformation could increase the young man’s internal fat.

At the time, that transformation was judged to be useless by the dragonman youngster.

But in the current situation, it seemed…it could help the dragonman youngster somewhat?

The dragonman youngster wasn’t entirely convinced it would.

But he did it anyway.

Bloodcore – compound transformation!

A thick layer of fat appeared around his internal organs.

The dragonman youngster charged Xiong Ju again.

Both sides fought in fierce close quarters combat.

Xiong Ju’s Penetration Force easily brought through the dragonman youngster’s outer defenses and reached his internal organs again.

But after that, the fat blocked it.

The fat ocean dragon’s blubber was different from normal animal fat.

Its fat balanced toughness and hardness.

The fat ocean dragon could rely on said fat to resist alchemy ballista arrows!

This was the defensive method the fat ocean dragon used to survive.

By virtue of being able to exist and reproduce in this extraordinary world, the fat ocean dragon’s blubber’s capabilities could be inferred as such.

When the Penetration Force was blocked by the fat ocean dragon’s blubber layer, its might greatly decreased.

The fat ocean dragon’s blubber layer jiggled when struck by the Penetration Force.

The dragonman youngster had a very low concentration of the fat ocean dragon bloodline.

The dragonman youngster could only use the Bloodcore to give himself more fat.

After the fat jiggled, it was unlikely that the attack would shake and batter the dragonman youngster’s internals, rather the Bloodcore would immediately turn it back into magic.

Soon after, the Bloodcore would transform a new layer of fat ocean dragon blubber.

There was only one systemic problem, the dragonman youngster had to divert his attention to control the Bloodcore, enough to constantly transform the fat ocean dragon blubber layers in and out of existence.

Battle skill – Wreathed in Silver.

Battle skill – Power Punch.

Battle skill – Demolition Fist.

Battle skill – Machine Gun Fist.

Xiong Ju and the dragonman youngster fought in close combat, reaching the climax of the duel.

Both sides used battle skills continuously.

Nearly face to face, their battle skills collided with each other and produced violent gusts of wind and tempestuous impact waves.

The audience loved watching this sincere combat of the flesh and excitedly clamored and shouted.

Xiong Ju gradually fell into a disadvantage in this fierce struggle.

But he tenaciously persevered and believed firmly in the strength of Penetration Force.

A fierce blow occurred between them, and both sides took advantage of the reaction force to pull back a large distance.

They looked at each other as they gasped for breath.

Xiong Ju’s muscles were aching and weary, and hot sweat was steaming off of him.

The dragonman youngster’s mouth was leaking blood, and despite his fat layers, because of how close the battle was along with the frequency of attacks, it was very difficult for him to use the Bloodcore.

Therefore, his internal organs didn’t have a refined defense.

His internal organs were quite injured, and he had a massive amount of internal bleeding, yet the dragonman youngster was standing straight with glowing eyes, the fighting spirit in his mind burning like a flame, and the more it did, the greater his exuberance.

Xiong Ju looked at Long Fu. His eyes were sunken, and his face was full of exhaustion, but his eyes too were filled with the same staunch fighting spirit!

After breathing for a few seconds, they charged at each other at the same time.

Another round of close quarters combat began anew.

Both sides’ battle qi, stamina, spirit, and willpower were being spent ardently.

The seven apertures bled, and inner organs were hurt.

The duel continued.

Ribs broke, and the pain drew out cold air.

The duel continued.

An eye was ruthlessly pounded, and it swelled completely, no longer able to see.

The duel continued.

Not enough stamina, and like an oil lamp running dry, punches were sinking to as slow as a tortoise.

The duel continued!

The battle between the two became deadlocked.

Their battle qi and stamina had reached their limits.

Whether it was attack or defense, neither were as fierce as before.

The two sluggishly punched and kicked, as if a thousand catty burden was on them. They were also staggering and swaying. If any suddenly fell to the ground, the audience would not feel surprised.

However, regardless of if it was the dragonman youngster or Xiong Ju, both were wholeheartedly dedicated to this duel.

Their sore bodies and exhausted minds seemed to have left them.

Their blood was boiling hot with the carefree pleasure this kind of battle brought.

Unconsciously, everyone present sank into silence.

In the last round of battle, the dragonman youngster’s acoustics case had been broken.

The entirety of Snow Bird Dueling Arena could clearly hear their every heavy breath, every thud their fists made when they hit each other, and the occasional creaking of their weak bodies.

The silent audience did not show the slightest impatience.

They looked at the two men battling each other fiercely in the arena with admiration and emotion.

“This is an amazing duel, a wonderful duel.”

“In my many years, I have never seen such a marvelous duel.”

“They are both in a difficult situation precisely because they embody their tenacious fighting spirit. No matter who loses or wins, it is enough for the people to admire them.”

“They never had a moment of weakness; Long Fu used his intelligence during the fight while Xiong Ju used his emotions to empower himself. They are both true fighters!”

Some transcendents had their craving for battle aroused, and one by one, secretly decided that after this duel finished, they would go look for someone to spar with.

Meanwhile, in the demon hybrid’s VIP viewing lounge.

The investigation staff was in the process of clashing verbally with Teng Dong Lang.

“We seriously suspect that your gang’s high-ranking member has meddled in today’s duel.” The two investigators gazed frostily.

“Think clearly of what you just said. Can you assume the responsibility and consequences of that sentence?” Teng Dong Lang coldly let out.

The investigation staff also took a tough stance, “We were ordained to come here and have to test this place. This is our Snow Bird Dueling Arena’s right, and even though you are the Bayonet Gang’s leader, you don’t have the authority to block us.”

“How impudent!” The demon ringleader held up his fists, forcing himself to get up and show his teeth full of malice.

The investigation staff held up their chests and didn’t take a step back, nor did their gazes waver, “I forgot to tell you Lord Teng Dong Lang, our investigation order did not just come from our dueling arena’s master but also Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord.”

Teng Dong Lang instantly reached out and pulled the demon ringleader over.

When the demon ringleader heard the City Lord was involved, he wanted to flee, but with Teng Dong Lang blocking him, he sat on his butt and coldly snorted, not continuing to kick up a fuss.

The two investigators were both snow elves. They first used magic, then many kinds of alchemy tools to obtain a verdict.

“Not long ago, someone used racial magic or a spell here. But the specific magic cannot be detected for the time being.”

When the investigator said that they looked at Teng Dong Lang and the demon ringleader deeply.
