Because their destruction of the scene was too obvious.

The demon ringleader laughed, “Oh? Did you say magic, I saw that this place was too dirty and decided to use a cleaning spell scroll.”

“Speaking of that, how exactly do you clean this place? You know, you gave me a terrible experience.”

The demon ringleader made bogus accusations.

The investigator sneered, “Our investigation has reached this result, which doesn’t mean the others will be the same. Our abilities are limited, but you should know that there are many more capable people above us.”

“No matter if it is the Lord Arena Master or the City Lord, both can be dispatched.”

The demon ringleader’s smile suddenly went stiff.

Teng Dong Lang’s brows deeply wrinkled, feeling great trouble.

At this time, a magic image appeared on the wall, showing the bitter struggle between Xiong Ju and the dragonman youngster.

It also had the quiet ambience of the audience.

When Teng Dong Lang saw this, his eyes twinkled with a sudden idea.

He took out an alchemy device from his pocket.

The Snow Bird Dueling Arena Master was also present.

Teng Dong Lang knew the arena master well, otherwise he wouldn’t be able to use this seating venue.

Teng Dong Lang easily contacted the arena master.

“You see, old friend, I am the one who provided you with this marvelous duel.”

“Look at the people’s reaction.”

This duel will be engraved into their memory and will be discussed enthusiastically for several years.

“Without the conflict between our Bayonet Gang and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, without us choosing Snow Bird Dueling Arena, this duel wouldn’t have happened.”

“Shouldn’t you be thanking me?”

“Old friend.”

The communication device was silent for a good while, then the dueling arena master slowly replied, “You’re not wrong.”

“I should thank you.”

“Let me recall my investigators.”

“This is my gift of appreciation, are you satisfied?”

Teng Dong Lang smiled, “Very satisfied.”

When the two investigators heard the communication device, they glanced at each other, then asked to withdraw.

With this crisis solved, the demon ringleader whistled.

But after Teng Dong Lang glared at him viciously, the demon ringleader quickly lowered his head and knew it was time to shut his mouth.

Teng Dong Lang had solved his problem and now looked at the dueling arena.

Racial magic – Bear Roar.

Racial magic – Dragon Roar!

Xiong Ju and the dragonman youngster roared at each other.

Both tilted their heads back, their vision went black, their ears buzzed incessantly, their bodies wavered, and both staggered backwards.

The dragonman youngster recovered first, but his spinning vision was incapable of finding where Xiong Ju was.

He simply closed his eyes and used his memory to punch blindly.

The fists struck Xiong Ju’s chin.

Xiong Ju’s head tilted, and he fell to the ground with a loud rumble. He went unconscious and didn’t rise.

The dragonman youngster had exerted himself too much and also fell to the ground.

But he soon used his hands to push himself up, and after gasping for a good while, he barely got to his feet.

A dueling arena staff member walked in, and after quickly inspecting Xiong Ju, loudly declared, “This victor is Long Fu!”

A moment later, the cheers of the entire audience swept through like a tsunami.

“Long Fu, Long Fu!”

Thousands of people shouted the dragonman youngster’s name simultaneously.

Many people sat up from their seats, shook their arms, shouted endlessly, far more emotional than the victorious dragonman youngster.

Through this duel, the dragonman youngster had established his prestige in Snow Bird Harbor completely.

In the seating area for friends and relatives, Chi Lai and Men Shi relaxed.

They felt sincere joy and excitement for the dragonman youngster’s win.

“The commander won!” Men Shi radiated with delight.

“This victory did not come easy. This was not just a showdown between two people, it was more so a confrontation between the Bayonet Gang and our Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.” Chi Lai sighed ruefully.

Xiong Ju had gotten a lot of help from his gang, while the dragonman youngster had information reports, Zi Di’s medicine and other things that had been contributed by the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps behind him.

“How could the Lord Commander lose?” Lan Zao’s eyes shined, like one who had foreseen this.

The VIP lounge.

Teng Dong Lang stared at Long Fu in the magic image, his narrowed eyes glistening with cold sharpness.

The demon ringleader’s lips felt dry, feeling an imperceptible pressure pushing onto him.

Most of all, he was thinking about when he had to face the dragonman youngster, when he had to fight such a monster.

He asked Teng Dong Lang for help, “Lord Gang Leader, I am not Long Fu’s match. I fear the others are the same. It doesn’t matter if we lose, the crucial thing is our gang’s prestige…”

Teng Dong Lang coldly glanced at him, then said something that pleasantly surprised the demon ringleader, “Following this, you guys don’t need to act, I will deal with him personally.”

The demon ringleader excitedly stuttered somewhat, “Lord Gang Leader, if you fight him, Long Fu has no hope for victory!”

Teng Dong Lang was expressionless, “I didn’t say I would fight him physically.”

Chapter 498: Blind Adoration

Night enveloped Snow Bird Harbor.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ships were still anchored at port in a row.

By Zong Ge’s order, the ships had mercenaries patrolling everywhere, every five steps was a person, and every ten steps was a sentry post, security was very tight.

Some mercenaries were rotated out, but they didn’t rest, instead they crowded around Lan Zao and listened attentively to how the duel played out.

Lan Zao gestured animatedly, enthusiastically describing the dragonman youngster’s dashing figure during the battle.

“The Bayonet Gang is so shameless, they knew our commander was formidable and put together the gang’s best gear for Xiong Ju to use.”

“Xiong Ju had at least three gold level equipment on him, while our commander was empty handed.”

“Xiong Ju’s Gold Flower Hacksaw is very powerful, and he is also quite strong himself. His bloodline’s racial magic Bear Roar is very difficult to guard against, and if one becomes dazzled by it, they will lose the ability to move.”

“The Lord Commander fell into his trap, and was knocked down for a time, with Xiong Ju using his Gold Flower Hacksaw to cut through our commander’s belly, filling the air around them with sparks.”

“The entire audience cried out in alarm, believing our commander was done for. Even Chi Lai and Men Shi also nervously stood up and planned to save the Lord Commander.”

“Hehe, they just joined our mercenary corps, and don’t have much faith in Commander Long Fu. This is understandable. Only an old fellow like me knows how strong the Lord Commander is.”

“Sure enough, when Bear Roar wore off, the commander smashed aside the Gold Flower Hacksaw with a single punch and easily escaped.”

“Xiong Ju was stupefied! He had painstakingly grinded with the Gold Flower Hacksaw for a time yet was unable to even break a single one of the Lord Commander’s scales. His whole heart chilled. Everyone present was also dumbstruck, they had never seen such powerful defense! You must know that this gold level weapon, the Gold Flower Hacksaw, is amazing at restraining armor.”

“After the Lord Commander used his final form, the duel lost its suspense. The Lord Commander’s final form had been used during the Battle of Two Eyes Island. Those who had the chance to see it should remember it?” Lan Zao deliberately questioned.

The mercenaries around Lan Zao listened attentively.

When they heard Lan Zao mention Long Fu’s final form, many mercenaries quickly raised their hand and shouted, “I saw it, I saw it with my own eyes.”

He excitedly continued, “On Two Eyes Island’s pier, our Lord Commander had a mystical transformation, becoming wilder and more ferocious! He fought the gold level fishman Er Gua Zi head on and wasn’t conceding one bit.”

The other mercenaries who hadn’t seen that looked at the talking mercenary with envy and curiosity.

The mercenary speaking raised his head and showed his pride.

Lan Zao continued onwards, “After our commander used final form, Xiong Ju was no longer his match. He couldn’t see where our commander was clearly. The commander was too fast, much too fast! Xiong Ju’s eyes couldn’t catch our commander, let alone attack with his heavy Gold Flower Hacksaw.”

“However, Xiong Ju is a long renowned silver level cultivator. He continued to persevere until the end, until our commander knocked him out with a single punch. Our commander is magnanimous and when he saw he lost the ability to resist, he didn’t deal the killing blow. If it were me, I would have eradicated evil completely, such a sinister and contemptible enemy is not easy to deal with and killing one’s opponent on the spot is wholly within dueling regulations.”

“After our commander won, the entire dueling arena audience of thousands erupted into cheers, all of them yelling our commander’s name. It really got people fired up. That kind of cheering was like a mountain high tsunami that could drown you in its waves. Only by being there could you truly sense our Lord Commander’s grandness!”

Lan Zao’s descriptions were mixed with his personal feelings; he strongly revered the dragonman youngster.

His story caused the mercenaries around him to look on with longing and yearning.

After Lan Zao finished his account, the mercenaries weren’t satisfied and didn’t leave, rather they discussed it with each other.

“During the Battle of Two Eyes Island, I knew Commander Long Fu was no simple silver level.”

“When we were in our ship cabin, our commander was fighting bravely and protecting us.”

“The Bayonet Gang is Snow Bird Harbor’s local bully, and if we are alone when we spend our free time in the harbor, I fear these people will be plotting against us, it is still safer to stay on the ships.”

“Scaredy chicken! I’d just fight the Bayonet Gang. It doesn’t matter that much, if we can’t beat them, we can set sail and leave the harbor.”

“Also don’t cause trouble for the Lord Commander. After all, the Bayonet Gang has a strong gold level.”

“Even against a gold level fighter, Commander Long Fu won’t have a trace of fear.”

“Don’t treat our commander as an ordinary silver level. I believe he is completely capable of defeating Teng Dong Lang, otherwise why would he publicly challenge him?”

“The Lord Commander has already defeated three of the Bayonet Gang’s ringleaders in a row, so there are three left. They are absolutely not Lord Long Fu’s match. So long as our commander prevails over Teng Dong Lang, our mercenary corps can walk through Snow Bird Harbor. Hehehe…”

The mercenaries thought of their beautiful future, while Lan Zao’s eyes hid a trace of anxiety.

Lan Zao had spent many years on a ship, traveling extensively and have a wide horizon. He knew the gap between the silver level and gold level.

This qualitative change was related to battle qi.

Silver level battle qi needed to attach itself to the body, weapons, or equipment. But at the gold level, battle qi could leave these shackles and could exist by itself.

Battle qi existing by itself could improve a gold level fighter’s range, attack methods, and more. Making it nearly analogous to magic.

Of course, the prerequisite was that the fighter knew the corresponding gold level battle skills.

If one lacked said battle skills, it would be very difficult for a gold level fighter to display their strength.

“But does Teng Dong Lang lack battle skills?”

“He is the grand Bayonet Gang leader, a position of high weight that controls many resources. He comes from the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s military, with his contacts, does he really lack battle skills?” Lan Zao shook his head internally.

It was even possible that Teng Dong Lang also knew how to use a gold level flying skill.

Flying battle skills were rare and most gold level fighters would search for them bitterly without avail.

It was unknown if Teng Dong Lang had one.

At present, it was not feasible to exclude this possibility.

If he could fly, he would have an invincible position and the dragonman youngster would find it hard to threaten him.

Just as Lan Zao was secretly having worries, Chi Lai and Men Shi were having their own discussion about today’s duel.

“Our commander is quite fearsome; he is absolutely a peak silver level.”

“The Bayonet Gang is also rather strong; we saw during today’s duel that Xiong Ju had no less than three treasures. Especially during the later stages, when he strangely mutilated himself to dig out that eyeball.”

Why did Xiong Ju destroy his own equipment?

They had looked into this question.

After Chi Lai and Men Shi discussed it, they believed that it was very possible that Xiong Ju lost control over the eyeball. Therefore, he destroyed it before it could backfire on him.

“The information report on Teng Dong Lang shows that the last time he acted was a year ago. It has been quite difficult to gather any more information on him from the Thieves Guild. We don’t have a good understanding of Teng Dong Lang, for he has been hiding in a veil of smoke. Although the commander won, all of the information on his military power has basically been exposed.”

“Alas, unless Commander Long Fu has more hidden trump cards, I don’t have good prospects for his duel with Teng Dong Lang.”

Chi Lai and Men Shi had very similar viewpoints.

They maintained a pessimistic opinion of the dragonman youngster’s duel with Teng Dong Lang.

Their bloodlines determined their upper limits, and the highest they’ll ever get in their life was just the silver level.

They had already been silver levels for a long time. So they had a clearer understanding of the disparity between silver and gold levels than Lan Zao and other people.

It was because of this understanding that they knew how difficult it was for a silver level to defeat a gold level.

“What should we do, how can we help Commander Long Fu?” Chi Lai looked dejected.

Men Shi stroked his silver level war hammer, sighing, “Commander Long Fu’s strength is actually beyond Xiong Ju’s, the reason why today’s fight was so difficult was mainly due to Xiong Ju’s exceptional equipment. Unfortunately, Commander Long Fu is an unarmed fighter, if he mastered a few weapons, then he could deal with Teng Dong Lang with more certainty.”

Chi Lai replied, “Weapons are out of the question, but perhaps we can buy some armor.”

The two had the same line of thought, both then looked for outside assistance.

Because a fighter increased their strength at a steady pace, there were hardly any sudden jumps.

Fighters relied on constantly producing battle qi and practicing battle skills to cultivate, continuously unearthing their body’s potential and slowly accumulating their progress over a long period of time.

Zi Di faintly coughed.

She had dived down to the Deep Sea Monster Fish with great difficulty.

On Two Eyes Island and Snake Rat Island, the survivors could secretly berth the Deep Sea Monster Fish on the seafloor near the islands.

They did the same here too.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish had nearly perfect camouflage and even though there was a magician tower right above it, it wasn’t discovered.

However, Snow Bird Harbor’s security was tight, making it very troublesome for the survivors to return to the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

It took Zi Di a lot of effort to sneak back into the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

She first went to the captain’s room.

There, she could mobilize the Deep Sea Monster Fish’s functions, command many investigation type alchemy puppets, along with being able to survey the pier and ships in all aspects.

On the one hand, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had Zong Ge on watch and many mercenaries making the rounds.

On the other hand, numerous investigation type alchemy puppets could help with scouting and ensure there were no gaps in surveillance coverage.

Through these alchemy puppets, Zi Di could hear what Lan Zao, and the others were saying, along with Chi Lai’s and Men Shi’s discussion.

“Compared to the bottom level mercenaries, the higherups’ understanding is closer to reality.”

“Although Chi Lai and Men Shi have just joined the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, the commander’s personal charisma has convinced them, and it can be seen now that their loyalty is not a problem.”

“The bottom level mercenaries are gradually starting to have blind adoration. They believe the commander can defeat a gold level transcendent, that belief comes from personal feeling and not from meticulous logical deductions and facts.”

Zi Di believed there were two reasons for this happening.

The main reason was that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had been led step by step to victory by Long Fu. Constant victory made the people complacent and confident, and the more confident one got, said confidence could turn into arrogance.

The second reason was because of Lan Zao, Bai Ya, and the others. They adored and respected the dragonman youngster. It came from the bottom of their hearts and was exhibited in their speech. Their every word and action daily showed their attitude and emotions, silently influencing and infecting the others around them. Of them, Lan Zao had the biggest impact.

Zi Di gradually noticed Lan Zao’s impact.

He not only helped the dragonman youngster on the individual level but was also capable of influencing the low level mercenaries’ feelings of the leadership above them.

Loyalty could be passed on.

Zi Di couldn’t help but think back to Mystifying Monster Island, when she greatly objected to the dragonman youngster taking in Lan Zao. Because for survival’s sake, Lan Zao had sacrificed his younger brother Huang Zao.

“Now it seems the Lord Commander is right.”

“In this aspect, I am not his equal.”

Zi Di was glad to see the changes Lan Zao was having in the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

The dragonman youngster needed people like Lan Zao, especially when Zong Ge and Tripleblade were forming their own small team.

The dragonman youngster needed someone to help keep the lower strata in check to consolidate his authority.

Both Zi Di and Cang Xu would find it difficult to accomplish this.

On the face of it, it was because they were necromancers, ones who people feared. The intrinsic reason was due to their background, experience, style of conversation, conduction, and values, such things were natural barriers with the lower class people.

After ensuring the dock was secure, and that the environment around the Deep Sea Monster Fish was also safe, Zi Di left the captain’s room and went into the alchemy cabin.

Today, she didn’t go watch the dragonman youngster’s duel, because she had more important business to attend to.

Standing in front of the alchemy formation, Zi Di took out a large pile of alchemy materials from her storage device.

Due to a lack of materials previously, she had reached a limit to her bloodline testing and was forced to suspend it.

But Snow Bird Harbor was flourishing and had abundant resources.

Zi Di searched for the entire day, buying many things she wanted.

“Tonight, I should test my bloodline and perhaps get an answer to Lan Zao’s bloodline too.”

Chapter 499: Zi Di’s Bloodline

The night was dark, but the Deep Sea Monster Fish’s alchemy cabin was brilliantly illuminated.

The ghost girl Di Zi floated behind the alchemy formation, her mood somewhat unstable.

“Tonight’s magic medicine blood measurement will finally tell me what my bloodline is.”

Bloodlines had an incomparably immense effect on an individual’s life, almost no other factor could compare to its impact.

For years, Zi Di had imagined countless times how much better things would be if her bloodline was just more outstanding.

But that was clearly impossible.

If bloodlines could be changed as one wished, then the world would be changed beyond recognition.

When Zi Di learned of the Bloodcore’s existence and about the Bloodline Purification Formation, she was very emotional.

Zi Di was constantly thinking about the Bloodline Purification Formation, of how it would give her a second chance to change her life!

This kind of chance for her was really impactful and significant.

It was so important that it might completely change her life, so much so that her entire world would seem to be altered.

Previously, her magic medicine blood measurement had gotten to a particular stage.

Zi Di’s bloodline had four possible answers.

They were the Bitter Mouth bloodline, Mix Up bloodline, Gunpowder Flower bloodline, and King Sunflower bloodline.

The alchemy formation slowly activated.

The formation once again produced a bright white mist.

Zi Di’s blood sample condensed into a small ball, floating above the center of the alchemy formation.

Due to practice, Zi Di was far more familiar with the process.

She threw in the prepared testing medicine.

The medicine flowed into the light mist and quickly dissolved completely, turning the light mist into a sandy yellow.

Zi Di’s blood in the yellow light gradually changed into a red ball of light.

At the beginning the ball of light had an irregular and bumpy surface and was constantly undulating.

After a moment, the ball of light gradually stabilized and turned into a completely smooth sphere.

When Zi Di saw this, she suddenly let out a breath.

This result had indicated that she didn’t have the Bitter Mouth bloodline.

If it were the Bitter Mouth bloodline, the ball of light would still be spherical, but its surface would end up being filled with sharp thorns.

But that was clearly not what happened.

Zi Di was hoping she didn’t have the Bitter Mouth bloodline.

The reason was simple: the Bitter Mouth bloodline was only iron level.

If she had that bloodline, then even if she used the Bloodline Purification Formation, nothing much would change.

Zi Di had already been an iron level during life, and the limit of the Bitter Mouth bloodline was likewise iron level.

At most, it would increase her strength somewhat, however, her lifelong achievements would still be restrained to the iron level.

Zi Di continued to throw in medicine.

The medicine completely evaporated in the alchemy formation, turning the yellow light mist back to white.

In this white mist, the red ball of light gradually changed again.

Something in the ball of light gradually shrank to the size of an eye.

Looking from the outside, the red ball of light looked like a plump eyeball.

The eyeball’s sclera was yellow, and the pupil was black.

Red, yellow, and back, these three colors were clearly separated, none of them mixed. This indicated that Zi Di’s bloodline was not mixed.

If it was the Mix Up bloodline, then the three colors would be mixed with each other, and like constantly stirred dye, there would not be a final blended color.

Zi Di was inwardly disappointed.

Because the Mix Up bloodline was a saint level bloodline.

A pharmacist with this bloodline was an expert at mixing different medicinal ingredients together and easily controlling their proportions, thereby reaching the planned reactions.

To Zi Di, the Mix Up bloodline was the one most suited to being a pharmacist of the options.

Unfortunately, she didn’t have it.

Zi Di’s third medicine was put into the alchemy formation.

Every testing medicine was different and had been specially refined by Zi Di.

This testing medicine was absorbed by the red eyeball, causing its form to slowly change.

The eyeball’s black pupil gradually turned into the shape of a flower.

This change was within Zi Di’s expectations.

Her bloodline now only had two possible choices, one was the Gunpowder Flower bloodline, and the other was the King Sunflower bloodline.

No matter which it was, both would produce this change.

If it didn’t have this change, then that would be terrible – it would prove a mistake happened during the testing procedure.

It was possible the testing medicine was wrong or that there was a problem with the alchemy formation. There were too many places where things could go wrong, and Zi Di had to start from the beginning and examine every step to see where the error occurred.

Bloodline testing was so troublesome.

Magic medicine blood measurement was tantamount to using a nonstandard gauge to weigh a mysterious object.

“The next change will determine whether it will be the Gunpowder Flower bloodline or the King Sunflower bloodline.”

Zi Di subconsciously held her breath.

At this moment, she nervously forgot that she was a ghost, she didn’t need to breathe at all.

The moment seemed to stretch on and on.

It was obviously just a few minutes, but Zi Di seemed to live through seven or eight days.

Zi Di unblinkingly stared at the flower in the eyeball sized ball of light as its color gradually changed.

The black turned to crimson.

Then the outline of the flower pupil also became soft and curved.

The crimson color stretched outwardly, slowly forming the shape of a flame.

It gave people who looked at it a feeling of warmth.

At this moment, the question of Zi Di’s bloodline had been answered.

“It is the Gunpowder Flower bloodline, and not the King Sunflower bloodline.”

Zi Di was very disappointed.

The King Sunflower bloodline was saint level, while the Gunpowder Flower bloodline was only gold level.

Of the two, Zi Di naturally admired the King Sunflower bloodline more.

But reality was not what you wanted it to be.

Reality was reality, it could not be altered by people’s mere thoughts. Therefore, saying that you hope one’s wishes come true is a throwaway blessing.

“Thinking carefully, this outcome is pretty good.”

“The King Sunflower bloodline is more suited for magicians that grow medicinal plants and engage in magical plantations.”

“The Gunpowder Flower bloodline is more suited for making compounds, especially gunpowder.”

Gunpowder was also a type of medicine, as medicine usually appeared as fine powder. It was used broadly in firearms, cannons, and other long ranged weaponry.

Zi Di tried to console herself, to little success.

“Even though the King Sunflower Bloodline is not specifically inclined towards pharmacists, when refining medicine, the bloodline is still very useful, and more rounded than the Gunpowder Flower bloodline. Compared to it, the Gunpowder Flower bloodline only has an advantage when making gunpowder, and even then, the King Sunflower bloodline is only a bit inferior to it.”

Zi Di deeply sighed, “Alas, I didn’t expect my bloodline to be the Gunpowder Flower bloodline.”

“Hold on, what am I going on about? To an iron level, this outcome is pretty good. Why am I not content with my lot?”

Zi Di could only bitterly laugh for a moment.

Mainly because the contrast was too distinct.

Saint level and gold level. The latter could be Lord of a territory, such as Hun Tong was. While the former could be the backbone of an entire region.

The two were in completely different social classes.

In society, the scenery one’s life had was poles apart with every social class one rose.

Zi Di’s mentality had a massive disparity, despite being mentally prepared.

Perhaps there was another reason.

During the last magic medicine blood measurement, she had tested the big guy’s bloodline to be at least legend level.

With such an example before her, Zi Di couldn’t help but fantasize how things would be if her bloodline was like that too.

But the facts proved she was overthinking.

Zi Di gradually calmed down and slowly accepted the facts.

She began to reflect on how to use this bloodline.

“Magicians with the Gunpowder Flower bloodline are experts at making gunpowder.”

Zi Di had known of many different types of gunpowder. They all had different characteristics and effects.

After she obtained War Merchant’s database, she found out that there were many more types of high end gunpowder, and that when classified by actual combat results, they were leagues apart from each other.

After cannons and firearms were invented, gunpowder had a fairly quick and violent development, and nowadays, there were over a hundred classifications and dozens of qualities of them.

“But at present, my group doesn’t need to refine large amounts of gunpowder.”

Zi Di understood clearly that they needed spirit type medicine.

Ones capable of increasing one’s spiritual strength to death with mental attacks and willpower tampering, among other methods.

But currently, despite having War Merchant’s database’s fine prescriptions, the spiritual medicines Zi Di could make had a mediocre effect.

During the duel, the dragonman youngster had taken this medicine beforehand, yet still became dizzy when hit by Bear Roar.

When dealing with the Evil Eye’s spirit rays, the medicine’s defensive effects were even more useless.

Zi Di understood that guarding against sprit type attacks was very difficult. To ensure real results, she had to use gold level medicine.

But as of now, she could only make small batches of silver level potions, and her success rate of refining gold level potions was very low.

“If it were the Mix Up bloodline or the King Sunflower bloodline, perhaps I could increase my gold level medicine refinement success rate. But it is the Gunpowder Flower bloodline, and it won’t help me much in the early stages.”

When Zi Di thought of that, she sighed again.

Outstanding bloodlines could step across the levels in many aspects.

For example, the dragonman youngster’s Flame Dragon King bloodline could give him dragon scales analogous to a gold level defense at the silver level.

Zi Di had very high hopes for her bloodline, part of the reason was that the entire Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps needed medicine manufacturing capabilities.

With an outstanding medicine manufacturing ability, Zi Di could sell medicine and establish a steady profit channel for the entire team and be able to improve the team’s unhealthy financial situation.

With outstanding manufacturing means, Zi Di could also refine cultivation accelerating medicine that could help the survivors strengthen themselves faster.

The Gunpowder Flower bloodline was something the majority of people could wholly accept as the outcome.

But when put into Zi Di’s body, and the current Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, reality’s answer did not meet expectations.

Chapter 500: Fight Analysis (1)

The dragonman youngster didn’t sleep.

In the Love Triangle’s training room, he was watching the recording of the duel between him and Xiong Ju.

Because of mature alchemy technology, people could easily obtain such recordings.

Lan Zao, Chi Lai, and Men Shi had joined the dragonman youngster at the Snow Bird Dueling Arena, and they had made recordings with alchemy devices.

But the dragonman youngster didn’t watch those recordings.

This recording was from Snow Bird Dueling Arena itself.

The dueling arena had more sophisticated alchemy devices, and their recordings were clearer and more comprehensive than normal alchemy devices. Furthermore, their viewing distance could be adjusted at will, making it convenient for watchers to observe each detail of the fight.

If it was a marvelous duel, then after it, the dueling arena would sell the fight recordings to make a tidy profit.

A portion of these profits went to the duelists, but most of it went into the dueling arena’s pocket to maintain the dueling arena itself, rebuilding facilities, doing improvements, and the like.

Apart from this, the dueling arena also profited from other sources. For example, they charged a fee on the constant flow of peddlers in the arena.

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom had all kinds of dueling arenas in it.

These dueling arenas were popular, not only through the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s culture of revering martial arts, but also due to the mature business models of these dueling arenas.

The dragonman youngster also profited from the duel.

But the dragonman youngster didn’t know the exact amount he would earn.

After all, this was his first time.

Also, he wasn’t focused on the money right now.

After the Battle of Two Eyes Island, the survivors had 70,000 gold coins in capital. For a short time, money was not a problem.

The dragonman youngster rewatched his fight, for the purpose of continuous study and review, so he could be stronger!

At the start of the fight, the dragonman youngster and Xiong Ju fought with their fists, neither using battle qi.

“Xiong Ju’s boxing is energetic and heavy, and his wrestling technique is quite solid.”

“He also had some dirty tricks from street brawls, they were very vicious, and at the time, I almost ate a loss and chose to scurry from a vanguard approach.”

When the dragonman youngster watched it, his memories of it became vivid.

From an observer’s point of view, the dragonman youngster quickly noticed that he actually had excellent solutions to Xiong Ju’s dirty tricks.

With these responses, he was able to hold more of the initiative during combat.

The recording continued to when Xiong Ju started to use his main weapon.

“After Xiong Ju took out the Gold Flower Hacksaw, his attack power rose dramatically, and his range also doubled in size. The team’s funds are sufficient enough, perhaps I should buy myself a gold level weapon.”

This thought appeared in the dragonman youngster’s mind again.

Next, he saw himself using the pair of alchemy crossbows, shooting Xiong Ju from range.

The dragonman youngster shook his head.

“Although I was relying on the Gold Needle Queen Bee bloodline, and once again possessed a gift for shooting, I am not a true archer.”

“I am not qualified to be one.”

“Many of the alchemy arrows counteracted each other. If I were a qualified archer, this wouldn’t have happened. An excellent archer could even use their arrow type battle skills to amplify the power of their arrows and strategically integrate their shots together so that their power would be greater than the sum of their parts.”

“Perhaps, I can choose an alchemy crossbow as my main weapon.”

The recording carried on to when the dragonman youngster found himself on the ground.

He had been stunned for a brief moment, and Xiong Ju was ferociously slicing at his abdomen with the Gold Flower Hacksaw.

What caused this situation was Xiong Ju’s racial magic Bear Roar.

In the prior clash, the dragonman youngster didn’t sense it, let alone was able to dodge it.

In that point in the fight, he from a spectator’s viewpoint quickly noticed that Xiong Ju had an indicator that showed when Bear Roar was about to be used.

However, that sign was not clear and also very brief.

“Remember that sign, if I cross blades with Xiong Ju again, perhaps I will be able to detect when he uses Bear Roar ahead of time.”

“Anyways, even if I notice Bear Roar, that ability activates too quickly. It would be nearly impossible for me to evade it completely.”

“Even with my current strongest form that gives me extreme speed, it is still hard to dodge.”

Magic was unlike fists and swords; they usually couldn’t be avoided through just evasion.

The dragonman youngster then saw himself use Machine Gun Fist on Xiong Ju.

Xiong Ju’s Flowing Wave Armor visibly melted away under Machine Gun Fist.

This peculiar armor had its own weak points; one the Machine Gun Fist could exploit.

“Although I learned Machine Gun Fist last night, it was thanks to Spring Boxer’s battle experience that allowed me to have a very deep mastery of it. Today, I was able to use it against Xiong Ju in real combat, with great results.”

Before learning Machine Gun Fist, the dragonman youngster only had one attack type battle skill, Demolition Fist.

Comparing the two battle skills was difficult, each had their own merits and shortcomings.

Demolition Fist was one hit with massive power. While Machine Gun Fist had far lower power in comparison to Demolition Fist, however its strong point was that it could constantly unleash a string of attacks.

When dealing with the Flowing Wave Armor, Demolition Fist didn’t do much, after all it was just a single strike. While Machine Gun Fist was actually able to restrain that kind of armor.

It could be said that by learning Machine Gun Fist, the dragonman youngster complemented a gap in his attack methods.

“How on, this is…” The dragonman youngster decided to pause the recording at a certain moment.

That moment was when his two fists smashed Xiong Ju into the air.

“What a waste!”

“If I was able to follow up that attack and keep Xiong Ju in the air, then I would have decided the duel then in a smoother fashion.”

“The dilly-dally and bitter struggle afterwards wouldn’t have happened.”

The dragonman youngster hadn’t detected this opportunity for victory during the battle.

Now that he was watching the recording, he immediately noticed that rare opportunity.

He repeated this section of the fight recording.

Then he noticed that keeping Xiong Ju in the air would have been challenging.

At the time, he had just attacked with all of his strength. It could be said that what happened was the best outcome.

“My offensive battle skills are limited, and I never had the methods that could keep Xiong Ju in the air.”

“Xiong Ju was also paying attention to that afterwards, and for the rest of the battle, he made sure both legs were on the ground, no longer giving another chance.”

Seeing that, the dragonman youngster had a new realization.

“Actually, when fighting a silver level fighter, if I am able to hit them into the air, I will hold a massive advantage.”

“There is nothing to gain a footing on in the air, and my opponent will be greatly limited. Unless they are able to alter their position with equipment or a skill.”

“As for gold level fighters, the air is no longer a weakness. Even if they don’t have a flying battle skill.”

Flying skills were rare, and not every gold level fighter had one.

But even if a gold level fighter didn’t have a flying skill, they could use their gold level battle qi to move through the air.

The reason was simple; gold level battle qi could exist by itself.

Therefore, so long as a fighter discharged some battle qi into the air, they could use them as a stepping stone.

“Perhaps I should look into a set of skills and choose a few suitable ones that I can use in succession.”

“Under that combination of battle skills, I am more likely to toss my enemy into the air.”

“I only know Demolition Fist and Machine Gun Fist, that is far from enough.”

By quickly switching to different battle skills in succession, an enemy would find it difficult to protect themselves.

If it was just Demolition Fist and Machine Gun Fist, an enemy would find it easy to protect themselves.

Xiong Ju in the recording was a living example.

Xiong Ju used his defensive battle skill Black Armor to avert danger.

“Xiong Ju has a good grasp of this battle skill.”

“Later when I absorb the Grand Arena Medal’s experience, his battle skill will be mine.”

“Machine Gun Fist couldn’t break that defensive skill, in fact, I doubt Demolition Fist could either.”

“It seems that although Demolition Fist is powerful, I lack a truly formidable attack method that can fix the tone in a single blow.” The dragonman youngster realized his inadequacy.

Demolition Fist was just his conventional attack method.

The dragonman youngster needed true offensive trump cards and using them might require a high price. When they were used, they could instantly erupt with great firepower and thereby smash the enemy’s defenses.

In this aspect, the dragonman youngster actually had one method.

It was the legend level Flame Dragon King bloodline’s racial magic – Life Scorching Dragon Breath.

But although this racial magic was formidable, and had once restrained Rou Zang, its conditions of usage was too harsh.

Normally, it was very difficult to find a chance to discharge it in actual combat, its battle success against Rou Zang was actually rather hard to duplicate.

Take today, when the dragonman youngster fought Xiong Ju. Both fought at an extremely rapid tempo, and the dragonman youngster never found an opportunity to let lose Life Scorching Dragon Breath.

At the end of the fight, when both were exhausted, the dragonman youngster didn’t have the strength to use that racial magic.

In fact, using something so powerful was wholly unnecessary.

The recording proceeded to the latter part of the duel.

The dragonman youngster relied on his final form and the speed boost it gave to miserably suppress Xiong Ju.
